  function ic(name, cls) { return '<svg class="w122ic' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">' + (IC[name] || IC.spark) + '</svg>'; }

  /* ---------- small utilities ---------- */
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function nowIso() { return new Date().toISOString(); }
  function dayKey(d) { var x = new Date(d); if (isNaN(x.getTime())) return null; return x.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (x.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + x.getDate()).slice(-2); }
  var MONTHS = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
  function fmtDate(d) { var x = new Date(d); if (isNaN(x.getTime())) return ''; return MONTHS[x.getMonth()] + ' ' + x.getDate() + ', ' + x.getFullYear(); }
  function fmtShort(d) { var x = new Date(d); if (isNaN(x.getTime())) return ''; return MONTHS[x.getMonth()].slice(0, 3) + ' ' + x.getDate(); }
  function ago(d) {
    var x = new Date(d).getTime(); if (!x) return '';
    var s = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - x) / 1000);
    if (s < 60) return 'just now'; if (s < 3600) return Math.round(s / 60) + ' min ago';
    if (s < 86400) return Math.round(s / 3600) + ' h ago'; if (s < 86400 * 2) return 'yesterday';
    if (s < 86400 * 30) return Math.round(s / 86400) + ' days ago'; return fmtShort(d);
  }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (n === 1 ? one : (many || one + 's')); }
  function mins(n) { n = Math.round(n || 0); if (n < 60) return n + ' min'; var h = Math.floor(n / 60), m = n % 60; return h + ' h' + (m ? ' ' + m + ' min' : ''); }
  /* the trainings text: escape first, then **bold**, *italic*, [text](url) and bare links */
  function md(s) {
    var t = E(s);
    t = t.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<b>$1</b>').replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\s][^*]*)\*/g, '$1<i>$2</i>');
    t = t.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:[^)\s]+)\)/g, '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$1</a>');
    t = t.replace(/(^|[\s(])(https?:\/\/[^\s<)]+)/g, function (m0, pre, url) { var short = url.replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?/, ''); if (short.length > 48) short = short.slice(0, 45) + '\u2026'; return pre + '<a href="' + url + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + short + '</a>'; });
    return t;
  }
  /* never show a password written into a training description */
  function redact(s) { return String(s || '').replace(/((?:pass(?:word)?|pwd)\s*[:=]\s*)(\S+)/ig, '$1\u2022\u2022\u2022\u2022\u2022\u2022 (kept in Important info)'); }
  function initials(n) { var p = String(n || '?').trim().split(/\s+/); return ((p[0] || '?')[0] + (p[1] ? p[1][0] : '')).toUpperCase(); }
  function lsGet(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function lsSet(k, v) { try { if (v == null) localStorage.removeItem(k); else localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function reduced() { try { return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function ring(pct, size, stroke, color, track, inner) {
    var r = (size - stroke) / 2, c = 2 * Math.PI * r, p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct || 0));
    return '<svg class="w122ring" width="' + size + '" height="' + size + '" viewBox="0 0 ' + size + ' ' + size + '" aria-hidden="true">' +
      '<circle cx="' + size / 2 + '" cy="' + size / 2 + '" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="' + (track || 'rgba(16,63,57,.10)') + '" stroke-width="' + stroke + '"/>' +
      '<circle class="arc" cx="' + size / 2 + '" cy="' + size / 2 + '" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="' + color + '" stroke-width="' + stroke + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="' + c.toFixed(2) + '" stroke-dashoffset="' + (c * (1 - p / 100)).toFixed(2) + '" transform="rotate(-90 ' + size / 2 + ' ' + size / 2 + ')"/>' +
      (inner || '') + '</svg>';
  }

  /* ---------- who and what they can see ---------- */
  function me() {
    var p = null; try { p = typeof wa28Me === 'function' ? wa28Me() : null; } catch (e) { p = null; }
    var email = ''; var full = '';
    try { email = String((USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); full = String((USER && USER.name) || ''); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var name = p && p.name ? p.name : (full ? full.split(' ')[0] : email.split('@')[0]);
    var dept = (p && p.department) || null;
    if (!dept) { try { if (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.department) dept = WA_ME.department; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    return { name: name || 'me', email: email, dept: dept, id: p && p.id ? p.id : null, full: full || name };
  }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : true; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function role() {
    try { if (typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board') return 'board'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) return WA40_MINE.role; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return 'member';
  }
  function isLeader() { var r = role(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader'; }
  function tabVisible(id) { var b = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]'); return !!b && b.style.display !== 'none'; }
  function tabLabel(id) { var b = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]'); var t = b ? String(b.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : ''; if (!t) { try { t = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[id]) || id; } catch (e) { t = id; } } return t; }
  function deptTab() { var d = me().dept; if (d && tabVisible(d)) return d; for (var i = 0; i < DEPTS.length; i++) if (tabVisible(DEPTS[i])) return DEPTS[i]; return null; }
  function people() { try { return (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' && WA28_PEOPLE) ? WA28_PEOPLE.filter(function (p) { return p && p.name && p.active !== false; }) : []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function personId() { var m = me(); if (m.id) return m.id; var pp = people(); return pp.length ? pp[0].id : null; }
  function projectId() {
    var list = []; try { list = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' && WA27_PROJECTS) || []; } catch (e) { list = []; }
    if (!list.length) return null; var d = me().dept;
    var mine = list.filter(function (p) { return p.department === d; });
    return (mine[0] || list[0]).id;
  }

  /* ---------- catalogue helpers ---------- */
  function loomId(t) {
    var cand = [t.ext_id, t.resource_url].map(function (x) { return String(x || ''); });
    for (var i = 0; i < cand.length; i++) {
      var m = cand[i].match(/loom\.com\/(?:share|embed)\/([0-9a-f]{32})/i); if (m) return m[1];
      if (i === 0 && /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/i.test(cand[i]) && (t.source === 'loom' || /loom\.com/.test(String(t.resource_url || '')))) return cand[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function kindOf(t) {
    if (!t) return 'read';
    if (t.kind && KIND[t.kind]) return t.kind;
    if (loomId(t) || t.source === 'loom') return 'video';
    if (/^https?:\/\//.test(String(t.resource_url || ''))) return 'doc';
    return 'read';
  }
  function minutesOf(t) {
    if (t.minutes) return t.minutes;
    if (t.duration_sec) return Math.max(1, Math.round(t.duration_sec / 60));
    var k = kindOf(t);
    if (k === 'read') return Math.max(2, Math.ceil(String(t.description || '').length / 900));
    return { video: 5, lesson: 10, doc: 5, tour: 2, sop: 7 }[k] || 5;
  }
  function xpFor(t) {
    if (t.xp) return t.xp;
    var k = kindOf(t), m = minutesOf(t);
    if (k === 'lesson') return 60; if (k === 'tour') return 20; if (k === 'sop') return 30;
    if (k === 'video') return Math.min(60, 20 + Math.round(m / 2));
    return 25;
  }
  function tourKeyOf(t) { var k = String(t && t.key || ''); return k.indexOf('tour:') === 0 ? k.slice(5) : null; }
  function visibleT(t) { if (!t || t.active === false) return false; var tk = tourKeyOf(t); if (kindOf(t) === 'tour') return !!(tk && TOURS[tk] && tourAvail(tk)); return true; }
  function embedOf(t) {
    var lid = loomId(t); if (lid) return 'https://www.loom.com/embed/' + lid + '?hide_owner=true&hide_share=true&hide_title=true&hideEmbedTopBar=true';
    var u = String(t.resource_url || ''), m;
    if ((m = u.match(/(?:youtube\.com\/watch\?v=|youtu\.be\/|youtube\.com\/shorts\/)([\w-]{6,})/))) return 'https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/' + m[1];
    if ((m = u.match(/vimeo\.com\/(\d+)/))) return 'https://player.vimeo.com/video/' + m[1];
    if ((m = u.match(/drive\.google\.com\/file\/d\/([\w-]+)/))) return 'https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + m[1] + '/preview';
    return null;
  }
  function previewOf(u) {
    u = String(u || ''); var m;
    if ((m = u.match(/docs\.google\.com\/(document|presentation|spreadsheets)\/d\/([\w-]+)/))) return 'https://docs.google.com/' + m[1] + '/d/' + m[2] + '/preview';
    if ((m = u.match(/drive\.google\.com\/file\/d\/([\w-]+)/))) return 'https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + m[1] + '/preview';
    if (/\.supabase\.co\/storage\/v1\/object\/public\/.+\.pdf(\?|$)/i.test(u)) return u;
    return null;
  }
  function hostOf(u) { var m = String(u || '').match(/^https?:\/\/([^/]+)/); return m ? m[1].replace(/^www\./, '') : ''; }
  function coverStyle(t) { var k = KIND[kindOf(t)] || KIND.read; return 'background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + k.c1 + ',' + k.c2 + ')'; }

  /* ---------- load ---------- */
  function applyTrainings(rows) {
    A.trainings = (rows || []).filter(function (t) { return t && t.active !== false; });
    A.byId = {}; A.byKey = {}; var cats = {};
    A.trainings.forEach(function (t) { A.byId[String(t.id)] = t; if (t.key) A.byKey[t.key] = t; if (t.category) cats[t.category] = 1; });
    A.cats = Object.keys(cats).sort();
  }
  function load(force) {
    if (A.loading) return A.loading;
    if (A.loaded && !force && Date.now() - A.loaded < 90000) return Promise.resolve();
    var m = me();
    A.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await Promise.all([
          db.from('trainings').select('*').eq('active', true).order('sort').order('id'),
          db.from('training_progress').select('*'),
          db.from('academy_paths').select('*').eq('active', true).order('sort'),
          db.from('academy_awards').select('*').order('earned_at', { ascending: false }).limit(600),
          db.from('wa_policies').select('*').eq('active', true).eq('required', true).order('sort'),
          m.email ? db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').select('policy_key, signed_at').eq('email', m.email) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] })
        ]);
        if (r[0].error) throw r[0].error;
        applyTrainings(r[0].data || []);
        A.prog = (r[1] && r[1].data) || [];
        A.paths = (r[2] && !r[2].error && r[2].data && r[2].data.length) ? r[2].data : null;
        A.awardsOk = !(r[3] && r[3].error);
        A.awards = (r[3] && r[3].data) || [];
        A.myAwards = {}; A.awards.forEach(function (a) { if (String(a.person_name).toLowerCase() === m.name.toLowerCase()) A.myAwards[a.award] = a; });
        A.pols = (r[4] && r[4].data) || [];
        A.signs = {}; ((r[5] && r[5].data) || []).forEach(function (s) { A.signs[s.policy_key] = s.signed_at; });
        try { if (typeof wa37Load === 'function') await wa37Load(!!force); } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
        try { if (window.w112Api && !window.w112Api.loaded()) await window.w112Api.load(); } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
        try { await loadAcks(); } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
        A.err = null; A.loaded = Date.now();
      } catch (e) { A.err = String((e && e.message) || e); }
      A.loading = null;
    })();
    return A.loading;
  }

  /* ---------- progress ---------- */
  function rowFor(tid, name) { var n = String(name || me().name).toLowerCase(); for (var i = 0; i < A.prog.length; i++) { var p = A.prog[i]; if (String(p.training_id) === String(tid) && String(p.person_name).toLowerCase() === n) return p; } return null; }
  function mine(tid) { return rowFor(tid); }
  function isDone(tid) { var r = mine(tid); return !!(r && r.status === 'done'); }
  function pctOf(t) { var r = mine(t.id); if (!r) return 0; if (r.status === 'done') return 100; return Math.max(0, Math.min(99, Number(r.pct) || (r.started_at ? 5 : 0))); }
  var KEEP = ['status', 'assigned_by', 'note', 'done_at', 'started_at', 'pct', 'last_step', 'score', 'seconds', 'data'];
  async function saveProg(t, patch, who) {
    var m = me(); var name = who || m.name;
    var cur = rowFor(t.id, name) || {};
    var row = { training_id: t.id, person_name: name };
    if (!who) row.email = m.email; else if (cur.email) row.email = cur.email;
    KEEP.forEach(function (k) { if (cur[k] !== undefined) row[k] = cur[k]; });
    Object.keys(patch || {}).forEach(function (k) { row[k] = patch[k]; });
    if (!row.status) row.status = 'started';
    row.updated_at = nowIso();
    /* optimistic: the local copy is the truth until the next load */
    var found = false;
    for (var i = 0; i < A.prog.length; i++) { var p = A.prog[i]; if (String(p.training_id) === String(t.id) && String(p.person_name).toLowerCase() === String(name).toLowerCase()) { Object.keys(row).forEach(function (k) { p[k] = row[k]; }); found = true; break; } }
    if (!found) A.prog.push(Object.assign({}, row));
    try {
      var res = await db.from('training_progress').upsert(row, { onConflict: 'training_id,person_name' });
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      try { if (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(WA36_PROG)) { var hit = WA36_PROG.filter(function (p) { return String(p.training_id) === String(t.id) && String(p.person_name).toLowerCase() === String(name).toLowerCase(); })[0]; if (hit) Object.keys(row).forEach(function (k) { hit[k] = row[k]; }); else WA36_PROG.push(Object.assign({}, row)); } } catch (e) { /* the classic view catches up on its next load */ }
      return true;
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save your progress. Check your connection and try again.'); return false; }
  }

  /* ---------- paths ---------- */
  function pathDefs() { return (A.paths && A.paths.length ? A.paths : DEFAULT_PATHS).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 100) - (b.sort || 100); }); }
  function pathByKey(k) { return pathDefs().filter(function (p) { return p.key === k; })[0] || null; }
  function pathItems(p) {
    return A.trainings.filter(function (t) { return t.path_group === p.name && visibleT(t); })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || (a.id - b.id); });
  }
  function pathOf(t) { if (!t || !t.path_group) return null; return pathDefs().filter(function (p) { return p.name === t.path_group; })[0] || null; }
  function pathState(p) {
    var items = pathItems(p); var done = 0, left = 0, next = null, last = null;
    items.forEach(function (t) { var r = mine(t.id); if (r && r.status === 'done') { done++; if (r.done_at && (!last || r.done_at > last)) last = r.done_at; } else { left += minutesOf(t); if (!next) next = t; } });
    return { p: p, items: items, done: done, total: items.length, pct: items.length ? Math.round(100 * done / items.length) : 0, next: next, minsLeft: left, complete: items.length > 0 && done === items.length, lastDone: last };
  }
  function forMe(p) { var d = me().dept; return !!(p.required || !p.dept || p.dept === d); }

  /* ---------- onboarding ---------- */
  async function loadAcks() {
    var o = orgMine(); if (!o || !o.completed_at) return;
    if (A.acksFor === o.id) return;
    var r = await db.from('onboarding_update_acks').select('*').eq('onboarding_id', Number(o.id));
    A.acks = {}; ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (a) { var k = String(a.template_id); if (!A.acks[k] || A.acks[k] < a.template_updated_at) A.acks[k] = a.template_updated_at; });
    A.acksFor = o.id;
  }
  function orgMine() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA37 === 'undefined' || !WA37.onbs) return null;
      var n = me().name.toLowerCase();
      return WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status !== 'cancelled' && String(o.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === n; })[0] || null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  function orgState() {
    var o = orgMine(); if (!o) return { has: false };
    var map = {}; try { map = wa37StepsFor(o.id) || {}; } catch (e) { map = {}; }
    var weeks = []; try { weeks = wa37Weeks() || []; } catch (e) { weeks = []; }
    var total = 0, done = 0, next = null, nextWeek = null, curWeek = null;
    weeks.forEach(function (w) { (w.steps || []).forEach(function (t) { var s = map[t.id]; if (!s) return; total++; if (s.done) done++; else if (!next) { next = t; nextWeek = w.name; } }); });
    if (next) curWeek = nextWeek;
    var pending = [];
    if (o.completed_at) {
      try { pending = (WA37.tmpl || []).filter(function (t) { if (!t.active || !t.updated_at) return false; if (String(t.updated_at) <= String(o.completed_at)) return false; var ack = A.acks[String(t.id)]; return !(ack && String(ack) >= String(t.updated_at)); }); } catch (e) { pending = []; }
    }
    /* wave285: the figure and the next step follow the one progress rule (real steps only); whether it is complete (and so the certificate) is the
       raw count, or window.w285.completeKnown: every real step done with the rule's facts known, never more generous than the facts */
    var rawT = total, rawD = done;
    try {
      var w285p = window.w285 && window.w285.plan ? window.w285.plan(o.id) : null;
      if (w285p && w285p.total) {
        total = w285p.total; done = w285p.done;
        var w285n = window.w285.firstOpen ? window.w285.firstOpen(o.id) : null;
        if (w285n) { var nt = (WA37.tmpl || []).filter(function (t) { return String(t.id) === String(w285n.tid); })[0]; if (nt) { next = nt; nextWeek = w285n.section; curWeek = w285n.section; } }
        else { next = null; curWeek = null; }
      }
    } catch (e) { total = rawT; done = rawD; }
    var known = false; try { known = !!(window.w285 && typeof window.w285.completeKnown === 'function' && window.w285.completeKnown(o.id)); } catch (e) { known = false; }
    var complete = o.status === 'completed' || (rawT > 0 && rawD === rawT) || known;   /* wave285: or every real step done with the rule's facts known */
    return { has: true, o: o, total: total, done: done, rawPct: rawT ? Math.round(100 * rawD / rawT) : (complete ? 100 : 0), pct: total ? Math.round(100 * done / total) : (complete ? 100 : 0), next: next, week: curWeek, complete: complete, completedAt: o.completed_at, pending: pending };
  }
  function hubState() {
    var api = window.w112Api;
    if (!api) return { has: false };
    try {
      if (!api.eligible()) return { has: false };
      var steps = api.steps() || [];
      var list = steps.map(function (s) { return { id: s.id, t: s.t, d: s.d, go: s.go, done: !!api.done(s.id), at: (api.row() && api.row().steps && api.row().steps[s.id]) || null }; });
      var done = list.filter(function (s) { return s.done; }).length;
      var row = api.row() || {};
      return { has: list.length > 0, loaded: api.loaded(), list: list, done: done, total: list.length, pct: list.length ? Math.round(100 * done / list.length) : 0, all: list.length > 0 && done === list.length, completedAt: row.completed_at || null };
    } catch (e) { return { has: false }; }
  }
  function polState() {
    var list = A.pols.map(function (p) { return { key: p.key, t: p.title, url: p.url, at: A.signs[p.key] || null }; });
    var signed = list.filter(function (p) { return p.at; }).length;
    return { has: list.length > 0, list: list, signed: signed, total: list.length, pct: list.length ? Math.round(100 * signed / list.length) : 0, all: list.length > 0 && signed === list.length };
  }
  function onbState() {
    var org = orgState(), hub = hubState(), pol = polState();
    var tot = 0, dn = 0;
    if (org.has) { tot += org.total || 1; dn += org.total ? org.done : (org.complete ? 1 : 0); }
    if (hub.has) { tot += hub.total; dn += hub.done; }
    if (pol.has) { tot += pol.total; dn += pol.signed; }
    var all = (!org.has || org.complete) && (!hub.has || hub.all) && (!pol.has || pol.all) && (org.has || hub.has || pol.has);
    var dates = [org.completedAt, hub.completedAt].concat(pol.list.map(function (p) { return p.at; })).filter(Boolean).sort();
    return { org: org, hub: hub, pol: pol, pct: tot ? Math.round(100 * dn / tot) : 0, done: dn, total: tot, all: all, cert: all && org.has, when: dates.length ? dates[dates.length - 1] : null };
  }

  /* ---------- stats, XP, level ---------- */
  function levelOf(xp) {
    var i = 0; while (i + 1 < LEVELS.length && xp >= LEVELS[i + 1][0]) i++;
    var floor = LEVELS[i][0], nxt = LEVELS[i + 1] ? LEVELS[i + 1][0] : null;
    return { n: i + 1, name: LEVELS[i][1], floor: floor, next: nxt, nextName: LEVELS[i + 1] ? LEVELS[i + 1][1] : null, pct: nxt ? Math.round(100 * (xp - floor) / (nxt - floor)) : 100, toNext: nxt ? nxt - xp : 0 };
  }
  function streaks(rows) {
    var days = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r) { [r.done_at, r.started_at].forEach(function (d) { var k = d && dayKey(d); if (k) days[k] = 1; }); });
    var keys = Object.keys(days).sort(); var best = 0, run = 0, prev = null;
    keys.forEach(function (k) { var d = new Date(k + 'T12:00:00'); if (prev && Math.round((d - prev) / 86400000) === 1) run++; else run = 1; if (run > best) best = run; prev = d; });
    var cur = 0; var probe = new Date(); probe.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
    if (!days[dayKey(probe)]) probe = new Date(probe.getTime() - 86400000);
    while (days[dayKey(probe)]) { cur++; probe = new Date(probe.getTime() - 86400000); }
    return { cur: cur, best: best, days: keys.length };
  }
  function stats() {
    var m = me(); var n = m.name.toLowerCase();
    var rows = A.prog.filter(function (p) { return String(p.person_name).toLowerCase() === n; });
    var doneRows = rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'done' && A.byId[String(p.training_id)]; });
    var s = { rows: rows, done: doneRows.length, videos: 0, tours: 0, lessons: 0, perfect: 0, doneList: [] };
    doneRows.forEach(function (p) { var t = A.byId[String(p.training_id)]; var k = kindOf(t); if (k === 'video') s.videos++; if (k === 'tour') s.tours++; if (k === 'lesson') s.lessons++; if (k === 'lesson' && Number(p.score) === 100) s.perfect++; s.doneList.push({ t: t, r: p }); });
    var st = streaks(rows); s.streak = st.cur; s.bestStreak = st.best; s.activeDays = st.days;
    s.onb = onbState(); s.hubAll = s.onb.hub.has && s.onb.hub.all; s.polTotal = s.onb.pol.total; s.polSigned = s.onb.pol.signed;
    s.paths = pathDefs().map(pathState);
    s.certs = s.paths.filter(function (ps) { return ps.complete; }).map(function (ps) { return { award: 'cert:' + ps.p.key, title: ps.p.cert_title || ps.p.title, detail: ps.p.title, when: ps.lastDone, meta: { path: ps.p.key, color: ps.p.color, icon: ps.p.icon, items: ps.total } }; });
    if (s.onb.cert) s.certs.unshift({ award: 'cert:onboarding', title: 'Welfare Avenue Onboarding', detail: 'Onboarding', when: s.onb.when, meta: { path: 'onboarding', color: GOLD, icon: 'seal' } });
    s.certCount = s.certs.length;
    s.badges = BADGES.filter(function (b) { try { return b.test(s); } catch (e) { return false; } });
    var xp = 0;
    s.doneList.forEach(function (d) { xp += xpFor(d.t) + (kindOf(d.t) === 'lesson' && Number(d.r.score) === 100 ? 20 : 0); });
    if (s.onb.org.has) xp += s.onb.org.complete ? 100 : Math.round((s.onb.org.rawPct != null ? s.onb.org.rawPct : (s.onb.org.pct || 0)) * 0.6);   /* wave285: XP is full when complete (completeKnown above), else the raw count, never more generous */
    if (s.onb.hub.has) xp += s.onb.hub.all ? 50 : s.onb.hub.done * 8;
    xp += s.polSigned * 10 + s.certCount * 100 + s.badges.length * 15;
    s.xp = xp; s.level = levelOf(xp);
    var req = s.paths.filter(function (ps) { return ps.p.required && ps.total; });
    var rt = 0, rd = 0; req.forEach(function (ps) { rt += ps.total; rd += ps.done; });
    s.reqPct = rt ? Math.round(100 * rd / rt) : 100; s.reqDone = rd; s.reqTotal = rt;
    var assigned = rows.filter(function (p) { return p.assigned_by && A.byId[String(p.training_id)]; });
    s.assigned = assigned.length; s.assignedDone = assigned.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'done'; }).length;
    return s;
  }
  function earnedList(s) {
    var out = [];
    s.certs.forEach(function (c) { out.push({ award: c.award, kind: 'cert', title: c.title, detail: c.detail, meta: c.meta, when: c.when }); });
    s.badges.forEach(function (b) { out.push({ award: 'badge:' + b.id, kind: 'badge', title: b.t, detail: b.d, meta: { icon: b.icon, color: b.c }, when: null }); });
    for (var n = 2; n <= s.level.n; n++) out.push({ award: 'level:' + n, kind: 'level', title: 'Level ' + n + ': ' + LEVELS[n - 1][1], detail: LEVELS[n - 1][0] + ' XP', meta: { n: n }, when: null });
    return out;
  }
  function certNo(a, when) {
    var code = { onboarding: 'ONB', foundations: 'FND', hub: 'HUB', tours: 'NAV', marketing: 'MKT', branding: 'BRD', ops: 'OPS', keela: 'KLA', governance: 'GOV' }[(a.meta && a.meta.path) || ''] || 'CRT';
    var d = new Date(when || a.earned_at || Date.now());
    return 'WA-' + code + '-' + String(d.getFullYear()).slice(2) + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2) + '-' + initials(me().full).replace(/[^A-Z]/g, '');
  }
  /* persist what is newly earned; returns the new ones (the celebration list) */
  async function evaluate(opt) {
    opt = opt || {};
    if (!A.loaded) return { fresh: [], s: null };
    var s = stats(); var m = me();
    var earned = earnedList(s);
    var fresh = earned.filter(function (a) { return !A.myAwards[a.award]; });
    var first = !A.evaluated; A.evaluated = true;
    var prevXp = A.lastXp == null ? s.xp : A.lastXp, prevLevel = A.lastLevel == null ? s.level.n : A.lastLevel;
    A.lastXp = s.xp; A.lastLevel = s.level.n;
    if (fresh.length && A.awardsOk && !A.busyAw) {
      var backfill = first && Object.keys(A.myAwards).length === 0;
      var lastAct = null; s.rows.forEach(function (r) { [r.done_at, r.started_at].forEach(function (d) { if (d && (!lastAct || d > lastAct)) lastAct = d; }); });
      var rows = fresh.map(function (a) { return { person_name: m.name, email: m.email, award: a.award, kind: a.kind, title: a.title, detail: a.detail || null, meta: a.meta || {}, earned_at: backfill ? (a.when || lastAct || nowIso()) : nowIso() }; });
      rows.forEach(function (r) { A.myAwards[r.award] = r; A.awards.unshift(r); });
      A.busyAw = true;
      try { var res = await db.from('academy_awards').upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'person_name,award', ignoreDuplicates: true }); if (res && res.error) throw res.error; } catch (e) { /* stays local for this visit; the next visit retries */ }
      A.busyAw = false;
      if (backfill) { if (fresh.length && lsGet('w122welcome:' + m.email) !== '1') A.welcome = fresh.length; return { fresh: [], s: s, backfilled: fresh.length }; }
    }
    if (first && !opt.celebrate) return { fresh: [], s: s };
    return { fresh: fresh, s: s, xpGained: Math.max(0, s.xp - prevXp), levelUp: s.level.n > prevLevel ? s.level : null };
  }

  /* ---------- the home page ---------- */
  function cardHtml(t, extra) {
    var k = KIND[kindOf(t)] || KIND.read, r = mine(t.id), done = r && r.status === 'done', pct = pctOf(t);
    var tags = [];
    if (extra && extra.tag) tags.push(extra.tag);
    if (r && r.assigned_by && !done) tags.push('<span class="tag gold">Assigned' + (r.assigned_by ? ' by ' + E(String(r.assigned_by).split(' ')[0]) : '') + '</span>');
    var p = pathOf(t); if (p && p.required && !done && !(extra && extra.noReq)) tags.push('<span class="tag purple">Required</span>');
    return '<button class="w122card" data-w122open="' + E(t.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="cv" style="' + coverStyle(t) + '">' +
      '<span class="big">' + ic(k.icon) + '</span><span class="kd">' + ic(k.icon) + ' ' + E(k.l) + '</span>' +
      (done ? '<span class="dn">' + ic('check') + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="bd"><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b><div class="m"><span>' + ic('clock') + ' ' + mins(minutesOf(t)) + (p ? ' &middot; ' + E(p.title) : (t.category ? ' &middot; ' + E(t.category) : '')) + '</span></div>' +
      (tags.length ? '<div class="tags">' + tags.join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      (!done && pct > 0 ? '<div class="pb"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>' : '') + '</div></button>';
  }
  function topHtml() {
    return '<div class="w122top"><span class="crumb">People &middot; Avenue Academy</span><span class="sp"></span>' +
      (TOURS.academy ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122tour="academy">' + ic('map') + ' Tour this page</button>' : '') +
      (isLeader() ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122add="1">' + ic('plus') + ' Add a training</button><a class="w122btn soft sm" href="#hrcentre/training">' + ic('people') + ' HR training board</a>' : '') +
      '<button class="w122btn ghost sm" data-w122classic="1" title="The previous Training page">Classic view</button></div>';
  }
  function onbHtml(o) {
    if (!o.org.has && !o.hub.has && !o.pol.has) return '';
    var m = me();
    if (o.all && !A.ui.onbOpen) {
      return '<section class="w122onb all" data-sec="onboarding"><div class="oh"><div class="done-seal">' + ic('check') + '</div><div><div class="eyebrow">Onboarding</div><h2>You are fully onboarded</h2>' +
        '<p>' + (o.when ? 'Completed ' + E(fmtDate(o.when)) + '. ' : '') + 'Your onboarding plan, the Hub first steps and your policies are all done.</p></div><span class="sp"></span>' +
        (A.myAwards['cert:onboarding'] ? '<button class="w122btn gold sm" data-w122cert="cert:onboarding">' + ic('seal') + ' Certificate</button>' : '') +
        '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122onbx="1">Review</button></div>' +
        (o.org.pending && o.org.pending.length ? '<div class="w122tracks" style="grid-template-columns:1fr"><div class="w122trk">' + pendingHtml(o.org) + '</div></div>' : '') + '</section>';
    }
    var h = '<section class="w122onb" data-sec="onboarding"><div class="oh"><div><div class="eyebrow">Start here</div><h2>Onboarding</h2>' +
      '<p>Everything for your first weeks at Welfare Avenue, in one place: your onboarding plan, the Hub first steps and the policies to sign.</p></div><span class="sp"></span>' +
      '<div class="big">' + ring(o.pct, 96, 10, GOLD, 'rgba(16,63,57,.08)') + '<b>' + o.pct + '%</b></div>' +
      (o.all ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122onbx="0">Collapse</button>' : '') + '</div><div class="w122tracks">';
    /* 1. the onboarding plan */
    var g = o.org;
    h += '<div class="w122trk" data-trk="plan"><div class="th"><div class="rg">' + ring(g.has ? g.pct : 0, 48, 5, GOLD) + '<i>' + (g.has ? g.pct + '%' : '\u2013') + '</i></div><div><h3>Your onboarding plan</h3><div class="m">' +
      (g.has ? (g.complete ? 'Complete' + (g.completedAt ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtShort(g.completedAt)) : '') : (g.done + ' of ' + g.total + ' steps' + (g.week ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(titleCase(g.week)) : ''))) : 'Week by week, set up by HR') + '</div></div></div>';
    if (!g.has) {
      h += '<div class="empty">HR has not assigned your onboarding plan yet. When they do, it appears here week by week' + (isLeader() ? '. Assign plans from the HR centre.' : ', so check back soon.') + '</div>';
      if (isLeader()) h += '<div class="foot"><a class="w122btn soft sm" href="#hrcentre/onboarding">' + ic('people') + ' Onboarding board</a></div>';
    } else {
      if (g.next) h += '<div class="nx"><div class="k">Next step</div>' + E(g.next.name) + '</div>';
      else if (g.complete) h += '<div class="nx"><div class="k">Done</div>Every step is ticked. Revisit the plan any time.</div>';
      if (g.pending && g.pending.length) h += pendingHtml(g);
      h += '<div class="foot"><button class="w122btn sm" data-w122onbopen="1">' + ic('list') + (g.complete ? ' View the plan' : ' Open my plan') + '</button>' + (isLeader() ? '<a class="w122btn soft sm" href="#hrcentre/onboarding">Board</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    h += '</div>';
    /* 2. the Hub first steps */
    var u = o.hub;
    h += '<div class="w122trk" data-trk="hub"><div class="th"><div class="rg">' + ring(u.has ? u.pct : 0, 48, 5, TEAL) + '<i>' + (u.has ? u.done + '/' + u.total : '\u2013') + '</i></div><div><h3>Hub first steps</h3><div class="m">' + (u.has ? (u.all ? 'All done' : 'Each one ticks itself when you do it') : 'Loading\u2026') + '</div></div></div>';
    if (u.has) {
      h += '<div class="lst">' + u.list.map(function (s) {
        return '<div class="it' + (s.done ? ' done' : '') + '"><button class="ck' + (s.done ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w122hub="' + E(s.id) + '" title="' + (s.done ? 'Mark not done' : 'Mark done') + '">' + ic('check') + '</button>' +
          '<div class="tx"><b>' + E(s.t) + '</b>' + (s.done ? '' : '<span>' + E(s.d) + '</span><div class="ia"><button class="go" data-w122hubgo="' + E(s.id) + '">' + E(s.go) + '</button></div>') + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    h += '<div class="foot"><button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122tour="chrome">' + ic('map') + ' Take the 2 minute tour</button></div></div>';
    /* 3. policies */
    var p = o.pol;
    /* wave279: the button waits for the reading time (wa279Pol, parts279/b_hooks.js): "Sign in N min", then "I have read it" */
    function polSignBtn(k) { var W = window.wa279Pol, v = W ? W.view(k, 'I have read it', true) : { dis: false, txt: 'I have read it' }; return '<button class="go dark" data-w122sign="' + E(k) + '"' + (W ? W.attrs(k, 'I have read it', true) : '') + (v.dis ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(v.txt) + '</button>'; }
    h += '<div class="w122trk" data-trk="policies"><div class="th"><div class="rg">' + ring(p.has ? p.pct : 0, 48, 5, PURPLE) + '<i>' + (p.has ? p.signed + '/' + p.total : '\u2013') + '</i></div><div><h3>Policies to sign</h3><div class="m">' + (p.has ? (p.all ? 'All signed' : 'Read each one, then sign off') : 'Nothing to sign') + '</div></div></div>';
    if (p.has) {
      h += '<div class="lst">' + p.list.map(function (x) {
        return '<div class="it' + (x.at ? ' done' : '') + '"><span class="ck' + (x.at ? ' on' : '') + '">' + ic('check') + '</span><div class="tx"><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><span>' + (x.at ? 'Signed ' + E(fmtShort(x.at)) : 'Your sign-off is recorded for HR with the date') + '</span><div class="ia">' +
          (x.url ? '<a class="go" href="' + E(x.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"' + (x.at ? '' : ' data-w279polopen="' + E(x.key) + '"') + '>' + (x.at ? 'Read again' : 'Open and read') + '</a>' : '') +
          (x.at ? '' : polSignBtn(x.key)) + '</div></div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    } else h += '<div class="empty">There are no policies waiting for you.</div>';
    h += '</div></div>';
    if (isLeader()) h += '<div class="hrrow"><span>HR tools:</span><a class="w122lnk" href="#hrcentre/onboarding">Onboarding board and master plan</a><a class="w122lnk" href="#hrcentre/training">Assign trainings in bulk</a></div>';
    return h + '</section>';
  }
  function titleCase(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/(^|\s)\S/g, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }); }
  function pendingHtml(g) {
    return '<div class="upd"><b>Updated since you finished (' + g.pending.length + ')</b>' + g.pending.map(function (t) {
      return '<div class="ur"><span>' + E(t.name) + '</span><button class="go" data-w122ack="' + E(t.id) + '" data-up="' + E(t.updated_at) + '">Got it</button></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function heroHtml(s) {
    var m = me(), lv = s.level;
    var hi = (s.done ? 'You have completed ' + plural(s.done, 'training') : 'Your learning starts here') + (s.streak > 1 ? ', and you are on a ' + s.streak + '-day streak.' : '.');
    var rings = '<svg width="180" height="180" viewBox="0 0 180 180" aria-hidden="true">' +
      ringArc(90, 80, 13, s.onb.total ? s.onb.pct : 100, GOLD) + ringArc(90, 62, 13, s.reqPct, '#5CD6B8') + ringArc(90, 44, 13, lv.pct, '#B3A8F5') + '</svg>';
    var h = '<section class="w122hero" data-sec="hero"><div><div class="eyebrow">Welfare Avenue</div><h1>Avenue Academy</h1>' +
      '<div class="hi">Hi ' + E(m.name) + '. ' + E(hi) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="lvl"><span class="n">' + lv.n + '</span>Level ' + lv.n + ' &middot; ' + E(lv.name) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="xpbar"><i style="width:' + lv.pct + '%"></i></div><div class="xpt">' + s.xp.toLocaleString() + ' XP' + (lv.next ? ' &middot; ' + lv.toNext + ' to ' + E(lv.nextName) : ' &middot; top level') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stats"><div class="st"><b>' + s.done + '</b><span>completed</span></div><div class="st"><b>' + s.certCount + '</b><span>' + (s.certCount === 1 ? 'certificate' : 'certificates') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="st"><b>' + s.badges.length + '<span style="font-size:13px;color:rgba(255,250,242,.6)">/' + BADGES.length + '</span></b><span>badges</span></div><div class="st fl"><b>' + s.streak + '</b><span>day streak</span></div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="rings"><div class="rs">' + rings + '<div class="ctr"><b>' + lv.n + '</b><span>level</span></div></div>' +
      '<div class="leg"><div><i style="background:' + GOLD + '"></i>Onboarding<b>' + (s.onb.total ? s.onb.pct + '%' : 'done') + '</b></div><div><i style="background:#5CD6B8"></i>Required learning<b>' + s.reqPct + '%</b></div><div><i style="background:#B3A8F5"></i>To level ' + (lv.next ? lv.n + 1 : lv.n) + '<b>' + lv.pct + '%</b></div></div></div></section>';
    if (A.welcome) h += '<div class="w122welcome">' + ic('sparkle') + '<div><b>Welcome to the Avenue Academy.</b> Your past work already counts: ' + plural(A.welcome, 'achievement') + ' unlocked.</div><span class="sp"></span><button class="w122btn sm" data-w122goto="trophies">See your trophies</button><button class="w122lnk" data-w122welcomex="1">Dismiss</button></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function ringArc(c, r, w, pct, color) {
    var cir = 2 * Math.PI * r, p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct || 0));
    return '<circle cx="' + c + '" cy="' + c + '" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,250,242,.12)" stroke-width="' + w + '"/>' +
      '<circle class="arc" cx="' + c + '" cy="' + c + '" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="' + color + '" stroke-width="' + w + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="' + cir.toFixed(2) + '" stroke-dashoffset="' + (cir * (1 - p / 100)).toFixed(2) + '" transform="rotate(-90 ' + c + ' ' + c + ')" style="transition:stroke-dashoffset 1.1s ' + EASE + '"/>';
  }
  function upNext(s) {
    var seen = {}, out = [];
    function add(t, tag) { if (!t || seen[t.id] || !visibleT(t) || isDone(t.id)) return; seen[t.id] = 1; out.push({ t: t, tag: tag }); }
    var n = me().name.toLowerCase();
    var mineRows = A.prog.filter(function (p) { return String(p.person_name).toLowerCase() === n && p.status !== 'done'; });
    mineRows.filter(function (p) { return p.started_at || p.pct; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.updated_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.updated_at || '')); })
      .forEach(function (p) { add(A.byId[String(p.training_id)], '<span class="tag">In progress</span>'); });
    mineRows.filter(function (p) { return p.assigned_by; }).forEach(function (p) { add(A.byId[String(p.training_id)]); });
    s.paths.filter(function (ps) { return ps.p.required; }).forEach(function (ps) { add(ps.next); });
    s.paths.filter(function (ps) { return !ps.p.required && ps.p.dept && ps.p.dept === me().dept; }).forEach(function (ps) { add(ps.next, '<span class="tag">For your team</span>'); });
    ['chrome', 'home', 'team'].forEach(function (k) { add(A.byKey['tour:' + k]); });
    return out.slice(0, 10);
  }
  function nextHtml(s) {
    var list = upNext(s);
    if (!list.length) return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="next"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Up next</h2><div class="sub">You are all caught up. Explore a Learning Avenue or the library below.</div></div></div></section>';
    return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="next"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Up next</h2><div class="sub">What you started, what was assigned to you, and what is recommended.</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w122scroll">' + list.map(function (x) { return cardHtml(x.t, { tag: x.tag }); }).join('') + '</div></section>';
  }
  function pathsHtml(s) {
    var mineP = s.paths.filter(function (ps) { return ps.total && forMe(ps.p); }), other = s.paths.filter(function (ps) { return ps.total && !forMe(ps.p); });
    function one(ps) {
      var p = ps.p, col = p.color || GREEN, cert = A.myAwards['cert:' + p.key];
      return '<div class="w122path" data-w122path="' + E(p.key) + '" role="button" tabindex="0"><div class="pc" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + E(col) + ',' + E(shade(col)) + ')"><span class="big">' + ic(p.icon || 'book') + '</span>' +
        '<div class="row">' + (p.required ? '<span class="chip">Required</span>' : '') + (p.dept && !p.required ? '<span class="chip">' + E(tabLabel(p.dept)) + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="chip">' + plural(ps.total, 'item') + '</span></div><h3>' + E(p.title) + '</h3></div>' +
        '<div class="pb2"><p>' + E(p.blurb || '') + '</p><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + ps.pct + '%;background:' + E(col) + '"></i></div>' +
        '<div class="ft2">' + (ps.complete ? '<span class="seal">' + ic('seal') + ' Certified' + (cert ? ' ' + E(fmtShort(cert.earned_at)) : '') + '</span>' : '<span>' + ps.done + ' of ' + ps.total + ' done' + (ps.minsLeft ? ' &middot; ' + mins(ps.minsLeft) + ' left' : '') + '</span>') +
        '<span class="sp"></span><span class="w122lnk">' + (ps.complete ? 'Review' : ps.done ? 'Continue' : 'Start') + ' ' + ic('arrow') + '</span></div></div></div>';
    }
    return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="paths"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Learning Avenues</h2><div class="sub">Short series with a certificate at the end.</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w122paths">' + mineP.map(one).join('') + '</div>' +
      (other.length ? '<div class="w122sech" style="margin-top:22px"><div><h2 style="font-size:19px">More Learning Avenues</h2><div class="sub">For other teams, open to everyone.</div></div></div><div class="w122paths">' + other.map(one).join('') + '</div>' : '') + '</section>';
  }
  function shade(hex) {
    var m = String(hex || '').match(/^#?([0-9a-f]{6})$/i); if (!m) return hex;
    var n = parseInt(m[1], 16), r = (n >> 16) & 255, g = (n >> 8) & 255, b = n & 255;
    function mix(c) { return Math.round(c + (255 - c) * 0.28); }
    return 'rgb(' + mix(r) + ',' + mix(g) + ',' + mix(b) + ')';
  }
  function toursHtml(s) {
    var list = A.trainings.filter(function (t) { return kindOf(t) === 'tour' && visibleT(t); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
    if (!list.length) return '';
    var done = list.filter(function (t) { return isDone(t.id); }).length;
    return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="tours"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Page tours</h2><div class="sub">A guided walkthrough of every page you can open, section by section. Each takes a minute or two, and a page opens for you once you have taken its tour.</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w122tourbar">' + ic('map') + '<b>' + done + ' of ' + list.length + '</b><span>toured</span><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + Math.round(100 * done / list.length) + '%"></i></div><span style="color:#797773">Tour them all for the Hub Navigator certificate.</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w122tours">' + list.map(function (t) {
        var k = tourKeyOf(t), d = TOURS[k] || {}, ok = isDone(t.id);
        return '<button class="w122tile' + (ok ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w122tour="' + E(k) + '"><span class="ti">' + ic(ok ? 'check' : (d.icon || 'map')) + '</span><span><b>' + E(String(t.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '')) + '</b><span>' + (ok ? 'Toured' : mins(minutesOf(t))) + '</span></span></button>';
      }).join('') + '</div></section>';
  }
  function libList() {
    var q = A.ui.q.trim().toLowerCase();
    return A.trainings.filter(function (t) {
      if (!visibleT(t)) return false;
      var k = kindOf(t);
      if (A.ui.kind !== 'all' && k !== A.ui.kind) return false;
      if (A.ui.cat && t.category !== A.ui.cat && t.path_group !== A.ui.cat) return false;
      if (q && (String(t.title) + ' ' + String(t.description || '') + ' ' + String(t.category || '') + ' ' + String(t.path_group || '')).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.category || '').localeCompare(String(b.category || '')) || (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || a.id - b.id; });
  }
  function libGridHtml() {
    var list = libList(); var shown = list.slice(0, A.ui.libN);
    if (!list.length) return '<div class="w122none">Nothing matches. Try another word or filter.</div>';
    return '<div class="w122grid">' + shown.map(function (t) { return cardHtml(t, { noReq: true }); }).join('') + '</div>' +
      (list.length > shown.length ? '<div class="w122more"><button class="w122btn soft" data-w122more="1">Show ' + Math.min(24, list.length - shown.length) + ' more of ' + list.length + '</button></div>' : '');
  }
  function libHtml() {
    var kinds = [['all', 'All'], ['video', 'Videos'], ['lesson', 'Lessons'], ['read', 'Reading'], ['doc', 'Documents'], ['sop', 'SOPs'], ['tour', 'Tours']];
    var groups = {}; A.trainings.forEach(function (t) { if (t.category) groups[t.category] = 1; });
    return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="library"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Library</h2><div class="sub">Every training: videos, lessons, reading and documents.</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w122lib"><div class="w122seg">' + kinds.map(function (k) { return '<button data-w122kind="' + k[0] + '" class="' + (A.ui.kind === k[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + k[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<select class="w122sel" data-w122cat="1"><option value="">All topics</option>' + Object.keys(groups).sort().map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + E(c) + '"' + (A.ui.cat === c ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(c) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label class="w122srch">' + ic('search') + '<input type="search" data-w122q="1" placeholder="Search the library" value="' + E(A.ui.q) + '"></label></div>' +
      '<div id="w122libgrid">' + libGridHtml() + '</div></section>';
  }
  function badgeMedal(b, earned) {
    return '<div class="w122bdg' + (earned ? '' : ' lock') + '" title="' + E(b.d) + '"><div class="md" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + b.c + ',' + shade(b.c) + ')">' + ic(earned ? b.icon : 'lock') + '</div><b>' + E(b.t) + '</b><span>' + (earned ? 'Earned ' + E(fmtShort(earned.earned_at)) : E(b.d)) + '</span></div>';
  }
  function trophiesHtml(s) {
    var certRows = [];
    var onbA = A.myAwards['cert:onboarding'];
    if (s.onb.org.has) certRows.push({ key: 'cert:onboarding', t: 'Welfare Avenue Onboarding', d: 'Finish your plan, the Hub first steps and your policies', a: onbA });
    s.paths.filter(function (ps) { return ps.total; }).forEach(function (ps) { certRows.push({ key: 'cert:' + ps.p.key, t: ps.p.cert_title || ps.p.title, d: ps.done + ' of ' + ps.total + ' in ' + ps.p.title, a: A.myAwards['cert:' + ps.p.key] }); });
    certRows.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.a ? 1 : 0) - (a.a ? 1 : 0); });
    return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="trophies" id="w122trophies"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Trophy case</h2><div class="sub">Certificates you can print or share, and badges for the way you learn.</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w122troph"><div class="w122shelf"><h3>Certificates</h3><div class="w122certs">' + certRows.map(function (c) {
        return c.a ? '<button class="w122mc" data-w122cert="' + E(c.key) + '"><span class="sl">' + ic('seal') + '</span><span class="k">Certificate</span><b>' + E(c.t) + '</b><span>Awarded ' + E(fmtDate(c.a.earned_at)) + '</span></button>'
          : '<div class="w122mc lock"><span class="sl">' + ic('lock') + '</span><span class="k">Certificate</span><b>' + E(c.t) + '</b><span>' + E(c.d) + '</span></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w122shelf"><h3>Badges &middot; ' + s.badges.length + ' of ' + BADGES.length + '</h3><div class="w122badges">' + BADGES.map(function (b) { return badgeMedal(b, A.myAwards['badge:' + b.id] || (s.badges.indexOf(b) >= 0 ? { earned_at: nowIso() } : null)); }).join('') + '</div></div></div></section>';
  }
  function awardVisual(a) {
    var meta = a.meta || {};
    if (a.kind === 'cert') return { icon: 'seal', color: meta.color || GOLD };
    if (a.kind === 'level') return { icon: 'bolt', color: PURPLE };
    var b = BADGES.filter(function (x) { return 'badge:' + x.id === a.award; })[0];
    return { icon: (b && b.icon) || meta.icon || 'star', color: (b && b.c) || meta.color || TEAL };
  }
  function feedHtml() {
    /* one line per achievement that matters: the highest level per person, and at most three lines per person */
    var topLv = {}; A.awards.forEach(function (a) { if (a.kind === 'level') { var k = String(a.person_name).toLowerCase(), n = Number((a.meta || {}).n) || 0; if (!topLv[k] || n > topLv[k]) topLv[k] = n; } });
    var per = {}, list = [];
    A.awards.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.earned_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.earned_at || '')); }).forEach(function (a) {
      var k = String(a.person_name).toLowerCase();
      if (a.kind === 'level' && (Number((a.meta || {}).n) || 0) !== topLv[k]) return;
      if (a.kind === 'level' && (Number((a.meta || {}).n) || 0) < 3) return;
      per[k] = (per[k] || 0) + 1; if (per[k] > 3 || list.length >= 10) return;
      list.push(a);
    });
    if (!list.length) return '';
    var colors = [GREEN, PURPLE, '#B9851B', TEAL, FLAG, STEM];
    return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="feed"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Recently earned</h2><div class="sub">Celebrate each other: what people across Welfare Avenue achieved.</div></div></div><div class="w122feed">' +
      list.map(function (a, i) {
        var v = awardVisual(a), nm = String(a.person_name || '');
        var verb = a.kind === 'cert' ? 'earned the <b>' + E(a.title) + '</b> certificate' : a.kind === 'level' ? 'reached <b>' + E(a.title) + '</b>' : 'unlocked <b>' + E(a.title) + '</b>';
        return '<div class="w122fr"><span class="av" style="background:' + colors[nm.length % colors.length] + '">' + E(initials(nm)) + '</span><div class="tx"><b>' + E(nm) + '</b> ' + verb + '<span>' + E(ago(a.earned_at)) + '</span></div><span class="aw" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + v.color + ',' + shade(v.color) + ')">' + ic(v.icon) + '</span></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div></section>';
  }
  function homeHtml() {
    if (!staffOk()) return '<div class="w122none">The Avenue Academy is available to staff accounts.</div>';
    if (A.err && !A.trainings.length) return topHtml() + '<div class="w122none">The Avenue Academy could not load (' + E(A.err) + '). <button class="w122lnk" data-w122reload="1">Try again</button></div>';
    var s = stats();
    return topHtml() + onbHtml(s.onb) + heroHtml(s) + nextHtml(s) + pathsHtml(s) + sopsHomeHtml() + toursHtml(s) + libHtml() + trophiesHtml(s) + feedHtml();
  }
  var paintQ = null;
  function paint() {
    var root = document.getElementById('w122'); if (!root) return;
    if (!A.loaded) {
      if (!root.__skel) { root.innerHTML = '<div class="w122none" style="margin-top:20px">Loading the Avenue Academy\u2026</div>'; root.__skel = 1; }
      load().then(function () { root.__skel = 0; paint(); after(); });
      return;
    }
    var active = document.activeElement, focusQ = active && active.getAttribute && active.getAttribute('data-w122q');
    var html = homeHtml();
    if (root.__h !== html) {
      root.innerHTML = html; root.__h = html;
      if (focusQ) { var inp = root.querySelector('[data-w122q]'); if (inp) { inp.focus(); try { inp.setSelectionRange(inp.value.length, inp.value.length); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    }
  }
  function paintSoon() { if (paintQ) return; paintQ = setTimeout(function () { paintQ = null; paint(); }, 60); }
  /* after the first paint: persist what the person has already earned, once */
  var afterDone = false;
  function after() {
    if (afterDone || !A.loaded) return; afterDone = true;
    evaluate({ celebrate: false }).then(function () { paint(); });
  }

  /* ---------- the sheet ---------- */
  function sheetEl() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w122sheet');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w122sheet'; el.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); el.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w122x="1"></div><div class="sh"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    return el;
  }
  function sheetOpen() { var el = document.getElementById('w122sheet'); return !!(el && el.classList.contains('on')); }
  function showSheet() {
    var el = sheetEl();
    if (!el.classList.contains('on')) {
      el.classList.add('on'); document.documentElement.classList.add('w122lock');
      requestAnimationFrame(function () { requestAnimationFrame(function () { el.classList.add('in'); }); });
    }
    return el.querySelector('.sh');
  }
  function closeSheet(silent) {
    stopTimer();
    if (R && R.t && R.secs > 3 && !R.saved) { var t0 = R.t, add = R.secs; R.saved = true; var cur = mine(t0.id); saveProg(t0, { seconds: (Number(cur && cur.seconds) || 0) + add }); }
    R = null;
    var el = document.getElementById('w122sheet');
    if (el && el.classList.contains('on')) {
      el.classList.remove('in'); document.documentElement.classList.remove('w122lock');
      setTimeout(function () { if (!el.classList.contains('in')) { el.classList.remove('on'); el.querySelector('.sh').innerHTML = ''; } }, 260);
    }
    if (!silent && /^#training\//.test(location.hash || '')) { try { location.hash = '#training'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    paintSoon();
  }
  function startTimer() { stopTimer(); if (!R) return; R.tick = setInterval(function () { if (R && document.visibilityState !== 'hidden') R.secs++; }, 1000); }
  function stopTimer() { if (R && R.tick) { clearInterval(R.tick); R.tick = null; } }

  function kindChip(t) { var k = KIND[kindOf(t)] || KIND.read; return '<span class="kd" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + k.c1 + ',' + k.c2 + ')">' + ic(k.icon) + ' ' + E(k.l) + '</span>'; }
  function headHtml(t, sub) {
    var p = R && R.path ? R.path : pathOf(t), idx = '';
    if (p) { var items = pathItems(p); var i = items.indexOf(t); if (i >= 0) idx = ' &middot; ' + (i + 1) + ' of ' + items.length; }
    return '<div class="hd"><div class="hi"><div class="kk">' + kindChip(t) + (p ? '<span>' + E(p.title) + idx + '</span>' : (t.category ? '<span>' + E(t.category) + '</span>' : '')) +
      '<span>' + ic('clock') + ' ' + mins(minutesOf(t)) + '</span><span>' + ic('bolt') + ' ' + xpFor(t) + ' XP</span></div><h2>' + E(sub || t.title) + '</h2></div>' +
      '<div class="acts">' + (isLeader() && t.id ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122assign="1">' + ic('user') + ' Assign</button>' : '') +
      (kindOf(t) === 'sop' ? (t.id && canEditSop(t) ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122sopedit="1">' + ic('edit') + ' Edit</button>' : '') : (isLeader() && t.id && kindOf(t) !== 'tour' && kindOf(t) !== 'lesson' ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122edit="1">' + ic('edit') + ' Edit</button>' : '')) +
      '<button class="xb" data-w122x="1" title="Close (Esc)">' + ic('x') + '</button></div></div>';
  }
  function railPath(t) {
    var p = R.path || pathOf(t); if (!p) return '';
    var items = pathItems(p);
    return '<div class="rail"><div class="rh">' + E(p.title) + '</div>' + items.map(function (x, i) {
      var ok = isDone(x.id);
      return '<button class="ri' + (x.id === t.id ? ' on' : '') + (ok ? ' ok' : '') + '" data-w122pitem="' + E(x.id) + '"><span class="n">' + (ok ? ic('check') : (i + 1)) + '</span><span>' + E(String(x.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '')) + '</span></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function footHtml(t, primary) {
    var r = mine(t.id), done = r && r.status === 'done';
    return '<div class="ft"><div class="st">' + (done ? '<span class="ok">' + ic('check') + ' Completed ' + E(fmtShort(r.done_at)) + '</span>' + (r.score != null && kindOf(t) === 'lesson' ? ' &middot; knowledge check ' + E(r.score) + '%' : '') + ' <button class="w122lnk" data-w122undone="1" style="font-weight:500;color:#9a9893">Mark as not complete</button>' : (r && r.assigned_by ? 'Assigned by ' + E(r.assigned_by) : (r && r.started_at ? 'Started ' + E(fmtShort(r.started_at)) : ''))) + '</div><span class="sp"></span>' + (primary || '') + nextBtn(t, done) + '</div>';
  }
  function nextInPath(t) { var p = R && R.path ? R.path : pathOf(t); if (!p) return null; var items = pathItems(p); var i = items.indexOf(t); for (var j = i + 1; j < items.length; j++) if (!isDone(items[j].id)) return items[j]; for (var k = 0; k < items.length; k++) if (items[k] !== t && !isDone(items[k].id)) return items[k]; return null; }
  function nextBtn(t, done) { if (!done) return ''; var n = nextInPath(t); return n ? '<button class="w122btn" data-w122pitem="' + E(n.id) + '">Next: ' + E(String(n.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '').slice(0, 42)) + ' ' + ic('arrow') + '</button>' : ''; }

  function openTraining(id, ctx) {
    var t = A.byId[String(id)];
    if (!t) { toastS('That training is not in the catalog any more'); if (/^#training\//.test(location.hash)) location.hash = '#training'; return; }
    stopTimer();
    R = { t: t, kind: kindOf(t), mode: 'view', ch: 0, quiz: null, path: (ctx && ctx.path) || (R && R.path && pathItems(R.path).indexOf(t) >= 0 ? R.path : pathOf(t)), secs: 0, saved: false, preview: false };
    var r = mine(t.id);
    if (R.kind === 'lesson' && r && r.last_step != null) R.ch = Math.max(0, Math.min((lessonBody(t).chapters || []).length - 1, Number(r.last_step) || 0));
    if (R.kind !== 'tour' && (!r || (r.status !== 'done' && !r.started_at))) saveProg(t, { status: r && r.status === 'assigned' ? 'assigned' : 'started', started_at: nowIso() });
    if (R.kind === 'lesson' && !isDone(t.id)) markSeen(t, R.ch);
    renderSheet(); startTimer();
  }
  function renderSheet() {
    if (!R) return;
    var sh = showSheet();
    if (R.mode === 'path') return renderPath(sh);
    if (R.mode === 'onb') return renderOnb(sh);
    if (R.mode === 'cert') return renderCert(sh);
    if (R.mode === 'form') return renderForm(sh);
    if (R.mode === 'sopform') return renderSopForm(sh);
    var t = R.t;
    if (R.mode === 'assign') return renderAssign(sh, t);
    var k = R.kind, html = '';
    if (k === 'lesson') html = lessonHtml(t);
    else if (k === 'video') html = headHtml(t) + '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + pctOf(t) + '%"></i></div><div class="bd">' + railPath(t) + '<div class="main"><div class="wrap wide">' + videoHtml(t) + '</div></div></div>' + footHtml(t, isDone(t.id) ? '' : '<button class="w122btn" data-w122done="1">' + ic('check') + ' I have watched it</button>');
    else if (k === 'doc') html = headHtml(t) + '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + pctOf(t) + '%"></i></div><div class="bd">' + railPath(t) + '<div class="main"><div class="wrap' + (R.preview ? ' wide' : '') + '">' + docHtml(t) + '</div></div></div>' + footHtml(t, isDone(t.id) ? '' : '<button class="w122btn" data-w122done="1">' + ic('check') + ' Mark as complete</button>');
    else if (k === 'sop') html = headHtml(t, sopTitle(t)) + '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + pctOf(t) + '%"></i></div><div class="bd">' + railPath(t) + '<div class="main"><div class="wrap wide">' + sopHtml(t) + '</div></div></div>' + footHtml(t, isDone(t.id) ? '' : '<button class="w122btn" data-w122done="1">' + ic('check') + ' I have read it</button>');
    else if (k === 'tour') html = headHtml(t) + '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + pctOf(t) + '%"></i></div><div class="bd">' + railPath(t) + '<div class="main"><div class="wrap">' + tourIntroHtml(t) + '</div></div></div>' + footHtml(t, '<button class="w122btn" data-w122starttour="' + E(tourKeyOf(t)) + '">' + ic('map') + (isDone(t.id) ? ' Take it again' : ' Start the tour') + '</button>');
    else html = headHtml(t) + '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + pctOf(t) + '%"></i></div><div class="bd">' + railPath(t) + '<div class="main"><div class="wrap"><div class="prose">' + articleHtml(t) + '</div></div></div></div>' + footHtml(t, isDone(t.id) ? '' : '<button class="w122btn" data-w122done="1">' + ic('check') + ' Mark as complete</button>');
    sh.innerHTML = html;
    var main = sh.querySelector('.main'); if (main) main.scrollTop = 0;
    if (!sh.getAttribute('tabindex')) sh.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1'); try { sh.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function videoHtml(t) {
    var src = embedOf(t);
    var v = src ? '<div class="vid"><iframe src="' + E(src) + '" title="' + E(t.title) + '" allow="autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture; encrypted-media" allowfullscreen loading="lazy"></iframe></div>'
      : '<div class="vid"><div class="ph">' + ic('play') + '<div>This video can\u2019t play inside the Hub.</div>' + (t.resource_url ? '<a class="w122btn white sm" href="' + E(t.resource_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open it in a new tab ' + ic('ext') + '</a>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    var desc = String(t.description || '').trim();
    return v + '<div class="meta2"><span>' + ic('clock') + ' ' + mins(minutesOf(t)) + '</span>' + (t.resource_url ? '<a class="w122lnk" href="' + E(t.resource_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + ic('ext') + ' Open in ' + E(/loom\.com/.test(t.resource_url) ? 'Loom' : hostOf(t.resource_url)) + '</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (desc ? '<div class="prose" style="margin-top:18px">' + articleText(desc) + '</div>' : '') + relatedTours(t);
  }
  function docHtml(t) {
    var u = String(t.resource_url || ''), pv = previewOf(u);
    var h = '<div class="docbox"><div class="i">' + ic('file') + '</div><div class="tx"><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b><span>' + E(hostOf(u)) + '</span></div>' +
      (u ? '<a class="w122btn" href="' + E(u) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the document ' + ic('ext') + '</a>' : '') + (pv ? '<button class="w122btn soft" data-w122preview="1">' + (R.preview ? 'Hide the preview' : 'Preview here') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (pv && R.preview) h += '<div class="pvw"><iframe src="' + E(pv) + '" title="' + E(t.title) + '" loading="lazy"></iframe></div><p class="snote">If the preview asks you to sign in or stays blank, use Open the document instead.</p>';
    var desc = String(t.description || '').trim();
    return h + (desc ? '<div class="prose">' + articleText(desc) + '</div>' : '') + relatedTours(t);
  }
  function articleHtml(t) {
    var desc = String(t.description || '').trim();
    return (desc ? articleText(desc) : '<p>This training has no written content yet.</p>') + relatedTours(t);
  }
  function articleText(text) {
    return redact(text).split(/\n{2,}/).map(function (p) {
      var s = p.trim(); if (!s) return '';
      if (/^try it:/i.test(s)) return '<div class="callout try">' + ic('bolt') + '<div><b>Try it</b>' + md(s.replace(/^try it:\s*/i, '')) + '</div></div>';
      if (/^done when:/i.test(s)) return '<div class="callout info">' + ic('check') + '<div><b>Done when</b>' + md(s.replace(/^done when:\s*/i, '')) + '</div></div>';
      if (/^goal:/i.test(s)) return '<p class="lead">' + md(s.replace(/^goal:\s*/i, '')) + '</p>';
      var lines = s.split('\n');
      if (lines.length > 1 && lines.every(function (l) { return /^\s*(?:[-\u2022*]|\d+[.)])\s+/.test(l); })) return '<ul>' + lines.map(function (l) { return '<li>' + md(l.replace(/^\s*(?:[-\u2022*]|\d+[.)])\s+/, '')) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>';
      return '<p>' + lines.map(md).join('<br>') + '</p>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function relatedTours(t) {
    var keys = (t.body && t.body.tours) || [];
    keys = keys.filter(function (k) { return TOURS[k] && tourAvail(k); });
    if (!keys.length) return '';
    return '<h3 style="font-size:17px;margin:26px 0 10px">See it for yourself</h3><div class="cards">' + keys.map(function (k) {
      var tt = A.byKey['tour:' + k];
      return '<div class="c" style="cursor:pointer" data-w122starttour="' + E(k) + '"><div class="i">' + ic(TOURS[k].icon || 'map') + '</div><b>' + E(tt ? String(tt.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '') : k) + '</b><p>' + E(tt ? tt.description : '') + '</p><span class="k">Page tour' + (tt && isDone(tt.id) ? ' \u00b7 done' : '') + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function tourIntroHtml(t) {
    var k = tourKeyOf(t), d = TOURS[k];
    if (!d) return '<p>This tour is not available.</p>';
    var steps = typeof d.steps === 'function' ? d.steps() : d.steps;
    return '<div class="prose"><p class="lead">' + E(t.description || '') + '</p><p>The tour opens the page and highlights each part in turn. Use the arrow keys or the buttons to move through it, and Esc to stop. It takes about ' + mins(minutesOf(t)) + '.</p></div>' +
      '<div class="tourlist">' + steps.filter(function (s) { return !s.hidden; }).map(function (s, i) { return '<div><i>' + (i + 1) + '</i><div><b>' + E(s.t) + '</b></div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- lessons ---------- */
  function lessonBody(t) { var b = t.body; if (typeof b === 'string') { try { b = JSON.parse(b); } catch (e) { b = null; } } return b && b.chapters ? b : { chapters: [] }; }
  function lessonHtml(t) {
    var b = lessonBody(t), chs = b.chapters || [], hasQ = !!(b.quiz && b.quiz.questions && b.quiz.questions.length);
    var r = mine(t.id), seen = (r && r.data && r.data.seen) || [];
    var total = chs.length + (hasQ ? 1 : 0), onQuiz = R.ch >= chs.length;
    var pct = isDone(t.id) ? 100 : Math.round(100 * Math.min(total, seen.length + (onQuiz ? 0 : 0)) / Math.max(1, total));
    var rail = '<div class="rail"><div class="rh">In this lesson</div>' + chs.map(function (c, i) {
      var ok = seen.indexOf(c.id) >= 0 || isDone(t.id);
      return '<button class="ri' + (i === R.ch ? ' on' : '') + (ok ? ' ok' : '') + '" data-w122ch="' + i + '"><span class="n">' + (ok ? ic('check') : (i + 1)) + '</span><span>' + E(c.t) + '</span></button>';
    }).join('') + (hasQ ? '<button class="ri' + (onQuiz ? ' on' : '') + (isDone(t.id) ? ' ok' : '') + '" data-w122ch="' + chs.length + '"><span class="n">' + (isDone(t.id) ? ic('check') : ic('star')) + '</span><span>Knowledge check</span></button>' : '') + '</div>';
    var body;
    if (onQuiz && hasQ) body = quizHtml(t, b.quiz);
    else {
      var c = chs[R.ch] || { t: '', blocks: [] };
      body = (R.ch === 0 && b.summary ? '' : '') + '<div class="chh">Chapter ' + (R.ch + 1) + ' of ' + chs.length + '</div><div class="cht">' + E(c.t) + '</div><div class="prose">' + blocksHtml(c.blocks || []) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="chfoot">' + (R.ch > 0 ? '<button class="w122btn soft" data-w122ch="' + (R.ch - 1) + '">' + ic('back') + ' Previous</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>' +
        (R.ch < chs.length - 1 ? '<button class="w122btn" data-w122ch="' + (R.ch + 1) + '">Next: ' + E(chs[R.ch + 1].t) + ' ' + ic('arrow') + '</button>' : (hasQ ? '<button class="w122btn gold" data-w122ch="' + chs.length + '">' + ic('star') + ' Take the knowledge check</button>' : (isDone(t.id) ? '' : '<button class="w122btn" data-w122done="1">' + ic('check') + ' Finish the lesson</button>'))) + '</div>';
    }
    return headHtml(t) + '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div><div class="bd">' + rail + '<div class="main"><div class="wrap">' + body + '</div></div></div>' + footHtml(t, '');
  }
  function markSeen(t, idx) {
    var b = lessonBody(t), c = (b.chapters || [])[idx]; if (!c) return;
    var r = mine(t.id) || {}; var data = Object.assign({}, r.data || {}); var seen = (data.seen || []).slice();
    if (seen.indexOf(c.id) < 0) seen.push(c.id);
    data.seen = seen;
    var total = (b.chapters || []).length + (b.quiz ? 1 : 0);
    var patch = { data: data, last_step: idx };
    if (!r || r.status !== 'done') patch.pct = Math.min(95, Math.round(100 * seen.length / Math.max(1, total)));
    saveProg(t, patch);
  }
  function blocksHtml(blocks) { return blocks.map(blockHtml).join(''); }
  function blockHtml(b) {
    switch (b.type) {
      case 'lead': return '<p class="lead">' + md(b.text) + '</p>';
      case 'p': return '<p>' + md(b.text) + '</p>';
      case 'h': return '<h3>' + E(b.text) + '</h3>';
      case 'quote': return '<div class="quote">\u201c' + E(b.text) + '\u201d' + (b.by ? '<span>' + E(b.by) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
      case 'stats': return '<div class="stats">' + (b.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<div><b>' + E(x.n) + '</b><span>' + E(x.l) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' + (b.note ? '<p class="snote">' + E(b.note) + '</p>' : '');
      case 'note': return '<p class="snote">' + md(b.text) + '</p>';
      case 'cards': return '<div class="cards">' + (b.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<div class="c"><div class="i">' + ic(x.icon || 'spark') + '</div><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><p>' + md(x.d) + '</p>' + (x.k ? '<span class="k">' + E(x.k) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
      case 'activities': return '<div class="acts8">' + (b.items || []).map(function (x) {
        return '<div class="a8" data-a8="' + E(x.n) + '"><button data-w122a8="' + E(x.n) + '"><span class="nn">' + E(x.n) + '</span><span><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><p>' + E(x.sum) + '</p></span><span class="cv">' + ic('chev') + '</span></button>' +
          '<div class="more"><b style="display:block;margin:0 0 4px">How we do it</b><ul>' + (x.how || []).map(function (h) { return '<li>' + md(h) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' +
          (x.teams ? '<div class="tm"><b>Who is involved: </b>' + E(x.teams) + '</div>' : '') + (x.extra ? '<div class="ex">' + E(x.extra) + '</div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
      case 'callout': { var icn = { info: 'compass', tip: 'sparkle', warn: 'flag', try: 'bolt' }[b.tone] || 'compass'; return '<div class="callout ' + E(b.tone || 'info') + '">' + ic(icn) + '<div>' + (b.t ? '<b>' + E(b.t) + '</b>' : '') + md(b.text) + '</div></div>'; }
      case 'compare': return '<div class="compare">' + (b.cols || []).map(function (c) { return '<div class="col ' + E(c.tone || 'neutral') + '"><h4>' + ic(c.tone === 'bad' ? 'x' : 'check') + E(c.t) + '</h4>' + (c.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<div>' + E(x) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
      case 'list': return '<ul>' + (b.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + md(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>';
      case 'steps': return '<div class="steps">' + (b.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<div class="s"><div><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><p>' + md(x.d) + '</p></div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
      case 'link': return linkCard(b);
      case 'links': return (b.items || []).map(linkCard).join('');
      case 'kinds': { var cols = { Partner: [GREEN, '#1d5a51'], Sponsor: ['#B9851B', '#D9A43A'], Collaborator: [PURPLE, '#8b80c9'] }; return '<div class="kinds">' + (b.items || []).map(function (x) { var c = cols[x.t] || [GREEN, TEAL]; return '<div class="k3" style="background:linear-gradient(145deg,' + c[0] + ',' + c[1] + ')"><span class="i">' + ic(x.icon || 'star') + '</span><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><p>' + E(x.d) + '</p>' + (x.e ? '<div class="e">For example: ' + E(x.e) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }
      case 'partners': return '<div class="pgroups">' + (b.groups || []).map(function (g) { return '<div class="pg"><h4>' + E(g.g) + '</h4><div class="pl">' + (g.items || []).map(function (x) { var st = String(x.s || ''); var cls = st.indexOf('New') === 0 ? 'New' : st; return '<div class="p"><b><span>' + E(x.n) + '</span><span class="w122st ' + E(cls) + '">' + E(st) + '</span></b><p>' + E(x.d) + '</p></div>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
      case 'stages': { var tc = { muted: '#9a9893', gold: GOLD, green: TEAL, purple: PURPLE, flag: FLAG }; return '<div class="stages">' + (b.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<div class="g"><i style="background:' + (tc[x.tone] || TEAL) + '"></i><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><span>' + E(x.d) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }
      default: return b.text ? '<p>' + md(b.text) + '</p>' : '';
    }
  }
  function linkCard(x) { return '<a class="lnk2" href="' + E(x.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class="i">' + ic('doc') + '</span><span><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><span>' + E(x.d || hostOf(x.url)) + '</span></span><span class="ar">' + ic('ext') + '</span></a>'; }

  /* ---------- the knowledge check ---------- */
  function quizHtml(t, q) {
    var qs = q.questions || [];
    if (!R.quiz) R.quiz = { i: 0, picks: [], shown: false, first: !(mine(t.id) && mine(t.id).data && mine(t.id).data.quizTries) };
    var Z = R.quiz;
    if (Z.i >= qs.length) {
      var right = Z.picks.filter(function (p, i) { return p === qs[i].c; }).length, pct = Math.round(100 * right / qs.length), pass = pct >= Math.round(100 * (q.pass || 0.8));
      return '<div class="quiz"><div class="res"><div class="rg">' + ring(pct, 150, 12, pass ? TEAL : FLAG) + '<b>' + pct + '%</b></div><h3>' + (pass ? (pct === 100 ? 'Perfect score!' : 'You passed') : 'Almost there') + '</h3>' +
        '<p>' + right + ' of ' + qs.length + ' right. ' + (pass ? 'This lesson is complete.' : 'You need ' + Math.round(100 * (q.pass || 0.8)) + '% to pass. Review the chapters and try again.') + '</p>' +
        (pass ? (isDone(t.id) ? '' : '<button class="w122btn" data-w122qfinish="' + pct + '">' + ic('check') + ' Complete the lesson</button>') : '<button class="w122btn" data-w122qretry="1">' + ic('refresh') + ' Try again</button> <button class="w122btn soft" data-w122ch="0">Review the lesson</button>') + '</div></div>';
    }
    var cur = qs[Z.i], picked = Z.picks[Z.i];
    var dots = qs.map(function (x, i) { return '<i class="' + (i < Z.i ? (Z.picks[i] === qs[i].c ? 'ok' : 'no') : i === Z.i ? 'cur' : '') + '"></i>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="quiz"><div class="dots">' + dots + '</div><div class="qn">Question ' + (Z.i + 1) + ' of ' + qs.length + '</div><div class="qq">' + E(cur.q) + '</div><div class="opts">' +
      cur.a.map(function (a, i) { var cls = picked == null ? '' : (i === cur.c ? ' right' : (i === picked ? ' wrong' : '')); return '<button class="op' + cls + '" data-w122opt="' + i + '"' + (picked != null ? ' disabled' : '') + '><span class="l">' + 'ABCDEF'[i] + '</span><span>' + E(a) + '</span></button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      (picked != null ? '<div class="why"><b>' + (picked === cur.c ? 'Right. ' : 'Not quite. ') + '</b>' + E(cur.why || '') + '</div><div class="chfoot"><span class="sp"></span><button class="w122btn" data-w122qnext="1">' + (Z.i + 1 < qs.length ? 'Next question' : 'See my score') + ' ' + ic('arrow') + '</button></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- path view ---------- */

  }
  function renderPath(sh) {
