    var html = '<div class="hd"><div class="hi"><div class="kk"><span class="kd" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + E(col) + ',' + E(shade(col)) + ')">' + ic('map') + ' Learning Avenue</span><span>' + plural(ps.total, 'item') + '</span></div><h2>' + E(p.title) + '</h2></div><div class="acts"><button class="xb" data-w122x="1">' + ic('x') + '</button></div></div>' +
      '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + ps.pct + '%"></i></div><div class="bd"><div class="main"><div class="wrap">' +
      '<div class="phero" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + E(col) + ',' + E(shade(col)) + ')"><span class="big">' + ic(p.icon || 'book') + '</span><h3>' + E(p.title) + '</h3><p>' + E(p.blurb || '') + '</p><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + ps.pct + '%"></i></div><div style="font-size:13px">' + ps.done + ' of ' + ps.total + ' done' + (ps.minsLeft ? ' &middot; about ' + mins(ps.minsLeft) + ' left' : '') + '</div></div>' +
      (cert ? '<div class="callout tip">' + ic('seal') + '<div><b>Certified</b>You earned the ' + E(p.cert_title || p.title) + ' certificate on ' + E(fmtDate(cert.earned_at)) + '. <button class="w122lnk" data-w122cert="cert:' + E(p.key) + '">View it</button></div></div>' : '<div class="callout info">' + ic('seal') + '<div><b>' + E(p.cert_title || p.title) + ' certificate</b>Finish every item to earn it.</div></div>') +
      '<div class="pitems">' + ps.items.map(function (t, i) {
        var ok = isDone(t.id), k = KIND[kindOf(t)] || KIND.read;
        return '<button class="pitem' + (ok ? ' ok' : '') + '" data-w122pitem="' + E(t.id) + '"><span class="n">' + (ok ? ic('check') : (i + 1)) + '</span><span class="tx"><b>' + E(String(t.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '')) + '</b><span>' + E(k.l) + ' &middot; ' + mins(minutesOf(t)) + (ok ? ' &middot; done' : '') + '</span></span>' + ic('chev') + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="ft"><div class="st">' + (ps.complete ? '<span class="ok">' + ic('check') + ' Learning Avenue complete</span>' : ps.done + ' of ' + ps.total + ' done') + '</div><span class="sp"></span>' + (ps.next ? '<button class="w122btn" data-w122pitem="' + E(ps.next.id) + '">' + (ps.done ? 'Continue' : 'Start') + ': ' + E(String(ps.next.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '').slice(0, 40)) + ' ' + ic('arrow') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    sh.innerHTML = html;
  }

  /* ---------- the onboarding plan ---------- */
  function openOnb() { stopTimer(); R = { mode: 'onb', secs: 0, open: {} }; renderSheet(); }
  function renderOnb(sh) {
    var g = orgState();
    if (!g.has) { sh.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><div class="hi"><h2>Your onboarding plan</h2></div><div class="acts"><button class="xb" data-w122x="1">' + ic('x') + '</button></div></div><div class="bd"><div class="main"><div class="wrap"><p class="prose">HR has not assigned your onboarding plan yet.</p></div></div></div>'; return; }
    var inner = ''; try { inner = wa37Checklist(g.o, true) || ''; } catch (e) { inner = '<p>The plan could not load.</p>'; }
    sh.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><div class="hi"><div class="kk"><span class="kd" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#B9851B,' + GOLD + ')">' + ic('list') + ' Onboarding</span><span>' + g.done + ' of ' + g.total + ' steps</span></div><h2>Your onboarding plan</h2></div><div class="acts"><button class="xb" data-w122x="1">' + ic('x') + '</button></div></div>' +
      '<div class="pr"><i style="width:' + g.pct + '%"></i></div><div class="bd"><div class="main"><div class="wrap"><div class="prose"><p>Work top to bottom and tick each step as you go. Big steps fold their details away; open a week to see everything in it. Lean on your onboarding channel whenever something is unclear.</p></div><div class="onbwrap">' + inner + '</div></div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="ft"><div class="st">' + (g.complete ? '<span class="ok">' + ic('check') + ' Complete</span>' : g.done + ' of ' + g.total + ' done') + '</div><span class="sp"></span><button class="w122btn" data-w122x="1">Done</button></div>';
    /* keep the weeks the person had open */
    sh.querySelectorAll('.onbwrap details.w37week').forEach(function (d, i) { if (R.open[i] != null) d.open = R.open[i]; d.addEventListener('toggle', function () { if (R && R.open) R.open[i] = d.open; }); });
  }

  /* ---------- certificates ---------- */
  function openCert(award) {
    var a = A.myAwards[award]; if (!a) { toastS('That certificate is not earned yet'); location.hash = '#training'; return; }
    stopTimer(); R = { mode: 'cert', award: a, secs: 0 }; renderSheet();
  }
  function certHtml(a) {
    var m = me();
    return '<div class="w122cert"><div class="c-top">WELFARE AVENUE &middot; AVENUE ACADEMY</div><div class="c-title">Certificate of completion</div><div class="c-sub">This certifies that</div><div class="c-name">' + E(m.full || m.name) + '</div><div class="c-sub">has completed</div><div class="c-path">' + E(a.title) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="c-meta"><span>Awarded ' + E(fmtDate(a.earned_at)) + '</span><span>Certificate ' + E(certNo(a, a.earned_at)) + '</span></div><div class="c-seal">' + ic('seal') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function renderCert(sh) {
    var a = R.award, shared = a.meta && a.meta.shared;
    sh.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><div class="hi"><div class="kk"><span class="kd" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#B9851B,' + GOLD + ')">' + ic('seal') + ' Certificate</span></div><h2>' + E(a.title) + '</h2></div><div class="acts"><button class="xb" data-w122x="1">' + ic('x') + '</button></div></div>' +
      '<div class="bd"><div class="main"><div class="wrap wide">' + certHtml(a) + '</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="ft"><div class="st">Earned ' + E(fmtDate(a.earned_at)) + '</div><span class="sp"></span>' + (shared ? '<span class="st">' + ic('check') + ' Shared with the team</span>' : '<button class="w122btn soft" data-w122share="' + E(a.award) + '">' + ic('megaphone') + ' Share with the team</button>') + '<button class="w122btn" data-w122print="' + E(a.award) + '">' + ic('print') + ' Print or save as PDF</button></div>';
  }
  function printCert(award) {
    var a = A.myAwards[award]; if (!a) return;
    var w = window.open('', '_blank'); if (!w) { toastS('Allow pop-ups for the Hub to print the certificate'); return; }
    var certCss = (document.getElementById('wa122css') || {}).textContent || '';
    w.document.write('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>' + E(a.title) + ' certificate</title><style>@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:12mm;}body{margin:0;background:#FFFAF2;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:100vh;}.w122cert{width:260mm;max-width:100%;}' +
      certCss + '</style></head><body>' + certHtml(a) + '<script>setTimeout(function(){window.print();},350);<\/script></body></html>');
    w.document.close();
  }
  async function shareAward(award, silent) {
    var a = A.myAwards[award]; if (!a || (a.meta && a.meta.shared)) return;
    var m = me();
    var text = a.kind === 'cert' ? m.name + ' earned the ' + a.title + ' certificate in the Avenue Academy.' : m.name + ' reached ' + a.title + ' in the Avenue Academy.';
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_shoutouts').insert({ kind: 'manual', source: 'academy', ref: 'award:' + award, person: m.name, member_id: m.id || null, team: m.dept || null, text: text, by: m.email });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      a.meta = Object.assign({}, a.meta || {}, { shared: true });
      try { await db.from('academy_awards').update({ meta: a.meta }).eq('person_name', m.name).eq('award', award); } catch (e) { /* the shout-out is what matters */ }
      if (!silent) toastS('Shared with the team. It shows in the HR centre shout-outs.');
      if (R && R.mode === 'cert') renderSheet();
    } catch (e) { if (!silent) toastS('Could not share it right now'); }
  }

  /* ---------- assign (leaders) ---------- */
  function renderAssign(sh, t) {
    var q = (R.q || '').toLowerCase();
    /* wave258 (Steel in the 15/10/26 Fundraising meeting: "Weird, I can't assign Alex to it"): search the email and the names people
       go by too, and say plainly when someone already finished the training (their box was greyed out with no reason) */
    var nickOf = function (p) { try { var m = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(p.id) || (p.email && String(x.email || '').toLowerCase() === String(p.email).toLowerCase()); })[0]; return m && Array.isArray(m.nicknames) ? m.nicknames.join(' ') : ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } };
    var ppl = people().slice().sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); }).filter(function (p) { return !q || String(p.name + ' ' + (p.role || '') + ' ' + (p.department || '') + ' ' + (p.email || '') + ' ' + nickOf(p)).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0; });
    var finished = ppl.filter(function (p) { var r0 = rowFor(t.id, p.name); return r0 && r0.status === 'done'; });
    R.pick = R.pick || {};
    sh.innerHTML = headHtml(t, 'Assign: ' + t.title) + '<div class="bd"><div class="main"><div class="wrap"><div class="prose"><p>Pick the people who should take this training. It shows up in their Up next with your name on it, and HR sees it on the training board.</p></div>' +
      (finished.length ? '<p class="w258finnote">' + E(finished.length === 1 ? finished[0].name + ' already finished this training, so their box is greyed out.' : finished.length + ' people already finished this training, so their boxes are greyed out.') + '</p>' : '') + '<label class="w122srch" style="max-width:none;background:#FFFAF2">' + ic('search') + '<input type="search" data-w122aq="1" placeholder="Search people" value="' + E(R.q || '') + '"></label><div class="ppl">' + ppl.map(function (p) {
        var r = rowFor(t.id, p.name), fin = !!(r && r.status === 'done'), st = r ? (fin ? 'Already finished' + (r.done_at ? ' ' + fmtShort(r.done_at) : '') : (r.assigned_by ? 'Already assigned' : 'Started')) : '';
        return '<label' + (fin ? ' class="w258fin" title="' + E(p.name + ' already finished this training, so there is nothing to assign') + '"' : '') + '><input type="checkbox" data-w122pick="' + E(p.name) + '"' + (R.pick[p.name] ? ' checked' : '') + (fin ? ' disabled' : '') + '><span>' + E(p.name) + ' <em>' + E(p.role || '') + '</em></span><em' + (fin ? ' class="w258fint"' : '') + '>' + E(st) + '</em></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div></div></div><div class="ft"><button class="w122btn soft" data-w122assignx="1">' + ic('back') + ' Back</button><span class="sp"></span><button class="w122btn" data-w122assigngo="1">' + ic('check') + ' Assign to ' + Object.keys(R.pick).filter(function (k) { return R.pick[k]; }).length + '</button></div>';
  }
  async function doAssign() {
    var t = R.t, names = Object.keys(R.pick || {}).filter(function (k) { return R.pick[k]; });
    if (!names.length) { toastS('Pick at least one person'); return; }
    var by = me().name, n = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) { var cur = rowFor(t.id, names[i]); if (cur && cur.status === 'done') continue; var ok = await saveProg(t, { status: cur && cur.status === 'started' ? 'started' : 'assigned', assigned_by: by }, names[i]); if (ok) n++; }
    toastS('Assigned to ' + plural(n, 'person', 'people'));
    R.mode = 'view'; R.pick = {}; renderSheet(); paintSoon();
  }

  /* ---------- add or edit a training (leaders) ---------- */
  function openForm(t) { stopTimer(); R = { mode: 'form', t: t || null, secs: 0 }; renderSheet(); }
  function renderForm(sh) {
    var t = R.t || {}, isNew = !R.t;
    var cats = A.cats.slice(); if (t.category && cats.indexOf(t.category) < 0) cats.push(t.category);
    var kinds = [['video', 'Video (Loom, YouTube, Vimeo or Drive link)'], ['doc', 'Document or link'], ['read', 'Reading (write it here)']];
    var paths = pathDefs();
    sh.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><div class="hi"><div class="kk"><span class="kd" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + GREEN + ',' + TEAL + ')">' + ic(isNew ? 'plus' : 'edit') + (isNew ? ' New training' : ' Edit') + '</span></div><h2>' + (isNew ? 'Add a training' : E(t.title)) + '</h2></div><div class="acts"><button class="xb" data-w122x="1">' + ic('x') + '</button></div></div>' +
      '<div class="bd"><div class="main"><div class="wrap"><div class="form">' +
      '<label>Title<input type="text" data-f="title" value="' + E(t.title || '') + '" placeholder="For example: Running a Sunday serving"></label>' +
      '<div class="row2"><label>Type<select data-f="kind">' + kinds.map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k[0] + '"' + (kindOf(t) === k[0] && !isNew || (isNew && k[0] === 'video') ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + k[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' +
      '<label>Minutes<input type="number" min="1" max="600" data-f="minutes" value="' + E(t.minutes || (t.id ? minutesOf(t) : '')) + '"></label></div>' +
      '<label>Link (video or document)<input type="url" data-f="resource_url" value="' + E(t.resource_url || '') + '" placeholder="https://www.loom.com/share/..."></label>' +
      '<div class="row2"><label>Topic<input type="text" list="w122cats" data-f="category" value="' + E(t.category || 'General') + '"><datalist id="w122cats">' + cats.map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + E(c) + '">'; }).join('') + '</datalist></label>' +
      '<label>Learning Avenue<select data-f="path_group"><option value="">None</option>' + paths.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + E(p.name) + '"' + (t.path_group === p.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.title) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label></div>' +
      '<label>Description or the reading itself<textarea data-f="description" placeholder="What people will learn. For a reading, write it here: blank lines make paragraphs, and lines starting Try it: or Done when: become highlighted boxes.">' + E(t.description || '') + '</textarea></label>' +
      '</div></div></div></div><div class="ft">' + (!isNew ? '<button class="w122btn ghost" data-w122retire="1">Retire it</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button class="w122btn soft" data-w122x="1">Cancel</button><button class="w122btn" data-w122save="1">' + ic('check') + ' Save</button></div>';
  }
  async function saveForm() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh'); if (!sh || !R) return;
    var f = {}; sh.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (el) { f[el.getAttribute('data-f')] = el.value; });
    f.title = String(f.title || '').trim(); if (!f.title) { toastS('Give it a title'); return; }
    var row = { title: f.title, kind: f.kind || 'read', minutes: f.minutes ? Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(f.minutes))) : null, resource_url: String(f.resource_url || '').trim() || null, category: String(f.category || 'General').trim() || 'General', path_group: f.path_group || null, description: String(f.description || '').trim() || null, updated_at: nowIso() };
    if (row.kind === 'video' && row.resource_url) { var lid = (row.resource_url.match(/loom\.com\/(?:share|embed)\/([0-9a-f]{32})/i) || [])[1]; if (lid) { row.source = 'loom'; row.ext_id = lid; } }
    if (!row.source) row.source = 'hub';
    try {
      var res;
      if (R.t) { res = await db.from('trainings').update(row).eq('id', R.t.id).select(); if (res.error) throw res.error; Object.keys(row).forEach(function (k) { R.t[k] = row[k]; }); toastS('Saved'); var keep = R.t; applyTrainings(A.trainings); openTraining(keep.id); }
      else {
        row.active = true; row.sort = 900;
        res = await db.from('trainings').insert(row).select(); if (res.error) throw res.error;
        var nt = (res.data && res.data[0]) || row; if (!nt.id) nt.id = 'tmp' + Date.now();
        A.trainings.push(nt); applyTrainings(A.trainings); toastS('Added to the library'); if (location.hash === '#training/t/' + nt.id) openTraining(nt.id); else location.hash = '#training/t/' + nt.id;
      }
      paintSoon();
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 80)); }
  }
  async function retire() {
    if (!R || !R.t) return;
    try { var res = await db.from('trainings').update({ active: false, updated_at: nowIso() }).eq('id', R.t.id); if (res.error) throw res.error; A.trainings = A.trainings.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== R.t.id; }); applyTrainings(A.trainings); toastS('Retired. It no longer shows in the Avenue Academy.'); closeSheet(); }
    catch (e) { toastS('Could not retire it'); }
  }

  /* ---------- completion and celebration ---------- */
  async function complete(t, extra) {
    var r = mine(t.id);
    if (r && r.status === 'done' && !(extra && extra.score != null && (r.score == null || extra.score > r.score))) return;
    var patch = Object.assign({ status: 'done', done_at: (r && r.status === 'done' && r.done_at) || nowIso(), pct: 100 }, extra || {});
    if (R && R.t === t && R.secs) { patch.seconds = (Number(r && r.seconds) || 0) + R.secs; R.secs = 0; R.saved = true; }
    await saveProg(t, patch);
    var res = await evaluate({ celebrate: true });
    celebrate(t, res);
    if (R && R.t === t && R.mode === 'view') renderSheet();
    paintSoon();
  }
  function celebrate(t, res) {
    res = res || {};
    var s = res.s || stats(), lv = s.level, fresh = (res.fresh || []).filter(function (a) { return a.kind !== 'level'; });
    var certs = fresh.filter(function (a) { return a.kind === 'cert'; });
    var old = document.getElementById('w122cel'); if (old) old.remove();
    var el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w122cel';
    var gained = res.xpGained || (t ? xpFor(t) : 0);
    var fromPct = Math.max(0, lv.pct - (lv.next ? Math.round(100 * gained / (lv.next - lv.floor)) : 0));
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w122celx="1"></div><canvas></canvas><div class="cc" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">' +
      '<div class="ck">' + ring(100, 92, 7, GOLD, 'rgba(16,63,57,.08)') + '<div class="in">' + ic(certs.length ? 'seal' : 'check') + '</div></div>' +
      '<h3>' + (certs.length ? 'Certificate earned!' : (t ? (kindOf(t) === 'tour' ? 'Tour complete' : 'Training complete') : 'Nice work')) + '</h3>' +
      '<p>' + (t ? E(String(t.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '')) : '') + '</p>' +
      (gained ? '<div class="xp">' + ic('bolt') + ' +' + gained + ' XP</div>' : '') +
      (res.levelUp ? '<div class="lvup">You reached<b>Level ' + res.levelUp.n + ': ' + E(res.levelUp.name) + '</b></div>' : '<div class="lv"><div class="t">Level ' + lv.n + ' &middot; ' + E(lv.name) + '<span>' + (lv.next ? lv.toNext + ' XP to ' + E(lv.nextName) : 'Top level') + '</span></div><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + fromPct + '%" data-to="' + lv.pct + '"></i></div></div>') +
      (fresh.length ? '<div class="aws">' + fresh.map(function (a, i) { var v = awardVisual(a); return '<div class="aw' + (a.kind === 'cert' ? ' cert' : '') + '" style="animation-delay:' + (0.25 + i * 0.12) + 's"><span class="md" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + v.color + ',' + shade(v.color) + ')">' + ic(v.icon) + '</span><div><b>' + E(a.title) + '</b><span>' + (a.kind === 'cert' ? 'Certificate' : 'Badge') + ' unlocked</span></div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      (certs.length ? '<label class="share"><input type="checkbox" data-w122shareck="1" checked> Share it with the team in the shout-outs</label>' : '') +
      '<div class="btns">' + (certs.length ? '<button class="w122btn gold" data-w122celcert="' + E(certs[0].award) + '">' + ic('seal') + ' View certificate</button>' : '') + nextAfter(t) + '<button class="w122btn' + (certs.length ? ' soft' : '') + '" data-w122celx="1">' + (T || (t && kindOf(t) === 'tour') ? 'Stay on this page' : 'Continue') + '</button>' + (t && kindOf(t) === 'tour' ? '<button class="w122btn soft" data-w122celhome="1">Back to the Avenue Academy</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    el.__certs = certs.map(function (a) { return a.award; });
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(function () { var bar = el.querySelector('.lv .bar i'); if (bar) bar.style.width = bar.getAttribute('data-to') + '%'; }, 300);
    if (!reduced()) confetti(el.querySelector('canvas'), certs.length ? 180 : 90);
  }
  function nextAfter(t) { if (!t || kindOf(t) === 'tour') return ''; var n = nextInPath(t); return n ? '<button class="w122btn" data-w122celnext="' + E(n.id) + '">Next up ' + ic('arrow') + '</button>' : ''; }
  function closeCel(opts) {
    var el = document.getElementById('w122cel'); if (!el) return;
    var ck = el.querySelector('[data-w122shareck]');
    if (ck && ck.checked) (el.__certs || []).forEach(function (a) { shareAward(a, true); });
    el.remove();
  }
  function confetti(cv, n) {
    if (!cv || !cv.getContext) return;
    var ctx = cv.getContext('2d'), W = cv.width = innerWidth, H = cv.height = innerHeight;
    var cols = [GOLD, TEAL, PURPLE, '#F4D27A', '#5CD6B8', '#FFFAF2', FLAG];
    var ps = []; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) ps.push({ x: W / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 120, y: H * 0.42, vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14, vy: -Math.random() * 15 - 5, r: Math.random() * 6 + 3, c: cols[i % cols.length], a: Math.random() * 6, va: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3, life: 0 });
    var t0 = performance.now();
    (function frame(now) {
      if (!cv.isConnected) return;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ps.forEach(function (p) { p.vy += 0.38; p.vx *= 0.992; p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy; p.a += p.va; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.rotate(p.a); ctx.fillStyle = p.c; ctx.fillRect(-p.r, -p.r / 2, p.r * 2, p.r); ctx.restore(); });
      if (now - t0 < 3200) requestAnimationFrame(frame); else ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    })(t0);
  }

  /* ---------- page tours: the steps (they point at the live DOM) ---------- */
  function S(sel, t, p, o) { var x = { s: sel, t: t, p: p }; if (o) Object.keys(o).forEach(function (k) { x[k] = o[k]; }); return x; }
  function H(re, up, t, p, o) { var x = { h: re, up: up, t: t, p: p }; if (o) Object.keys(o).forEach(function (k) { x[k] = o[k]; }); return x; }
  function C(t, p) { return { c: 1, t: t, p: p }; }

  };
  function tourAvail(key) {
    var d = TOURS[key]; if (!d) return false;
    if (d.avail) return !!d.avail() && (d.tab === 'dept' || !d.tab || d.tab === 'dashboard' || d.tab === 'create' || tabVisible(d.tab) || allowed(d.tab));
    if (d.tab === 'dept') return !!deptTab();
    return !d.tab || tabVisible(d.tab) || allowed(d.tab);
  }
  function allowed(tab) { try { return typeof wa40Allowed === 'function' && wa40Allowed().indexOf(tab) >= 0 && !!document.getElementById(tab); } catch (e) { return false; } }

  /* ---------- the tour engine ---------- */
  function wait(ms) { return new Promise(function (r) { setTimeout(r, ms); }); }
  function vis(el) { if (!el || !el.getClientRects || !el.getClientRects().length) return false; var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); if (r.width < 2 || r.height < 2) return false; var cs = getComputedStyle(el); return cs.visibility !== 'hidden' && cs.display !== 'none' && Number(cs.opacity) > 0.02; }
  function norm(s) { return String(s || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }
  function resolveStep(st) {
    if (!st || st.c) return null;
    if (st.s) {
      var alts = st.s.split('||');
      for (var i = 0; i < alts.length; i++) {
        var list; try { list = document.querySelectorAll(alts[i].trim()); } catch (e) { list = []; }
        for (var j = 0; j < list.length; j++) if (vis(list[j])) return st.up ? (list[j].closest(st.up) || list[j]) : list[j];
      }
      return null;
    }
    if (st.h) {
      var root = document.querySelector('.panel.active') || document;
      var hs = root.querySelectorAll('h1,h2,h3,h4,summary');
      for (var k = 0; k < hs.length; k++) if (vis(hs[k]) && st.h.test(norm(hs[k].textContent))) return st.up ? (hs[k].closest(st.up) || hs[k]) : hs[k];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function stepRect(st, el) {
    var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(), box = { l: r.left, t: r.top, r: r.right, b: r.bottom };
    if (st.range) {
      var tag = el.tagName, n = el.nextElementSibling, guard = 0;
      while (n && n.tagName !== tag && guard++ < 40) { if (vis(n)) { var q = n.getBoundingClientRect(); box.l = Math.min(box.l, q.left); box.r = Math.max(box.r, q.right); box.b = Math.max(box.b, q.bottom); } n = n.nextElementSibling; }
    }
    return box;
  }
  function waitFor(fn, ms) {
    return new Promise(function (res) {
      var t0 = performance.now();
      (function poll() { var v = null; try { v = fn(); } catch (e) { v = null; } if (v || performance.now() - t0 > ms || !T) return res(v || null); setTimeout(poll, 150); })();
    });
  }
  function goHash(h) {
    return new Promise(function (res) {
      if (!h || location.hash === h) return res(false);
      location.hash = h; if (T) T.navAt = performance.now(); setTimeout(function () { res(true); }, 650);
    });
  }
  function tourLayer() {
    var el = document.querySelector('.w122tp');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'w122tp';
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="catch"></div><div class="hole none"></div><div class="pop" role="dialog" aria-live="polite"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    return el;
  }
  function startTour(key) {
    var d = TOURS[key];
    if (!d || !tourAvail(key)) { toastS('That tour is not available for your account'); return; }
    if (d.w215 && window.w215 && w215.owns(key)) { closeSheet(true); endTour(true); w215.start(key); return; }   /* wave215: the hands-on walkthroughs run in their own coach */
    closeSheet(true); endTour(true);
    var steps = (typeof d.steps === 'function' ? d.steps() : d.steps).slice();
    T = { key: key, d: d, steps: steps, i: -1, max: 0, tr: A.byKey['tour:' + key] || null, busy: false, navAt: performance.now() };
    tourEvent('start', key);
    var tr = T.tr; if (tr) { var cur = mine(tr.id); if (!cur || cur.status !== 'done') saveProg(tr, { status: cur && cur.status === 'assigned' ? 'assigned' : 'started', started_at: (cur && cur.started_at) || nowIso(), last_step: 0 }); }
    tourLayer();
    var h = typeof d.hash === 'function' ? d.hash() : d.hash;
    goHash(h).then(function () { showStep(0, 1); });
  }
  async function showStep(i, dir) {
    if (!T) return;
    if (i < 0) i = 0;
    if (i >= T.steps.length) return finishTour();
    var st = T.steps[i]; T.i = i; T.busy = true;
    var pop = document.querySelector('.w122tp .pop'); if (pop) pop.style.opacity = '.4';
    if (st.hash) { var h = typeof st.hash === 'function' ? st.hash() : st.hash; if (h) await goHash(h); }
    if (st.go) { try { st.go(); T.navAt = performance.now(); } catch (e) { /* the step shows centred */ } await wait(1100); }
    /* right after a navigation the page is still drawing, so wait longer; otherwise a missing optional part is skipped quickly */
    var fresh = performance.now() - (T.navAt || 0) < 3500;
    var el = st.c ? null : await waitFor(function () { return resolveStep(st); }, fresh ? (st.opt ? 2600 : 4500) : (st.opt ? 450 : 1500));
    if (!T || T.i !== i) return;
    if (!el && st.opt && !st.c) {
      var ni = i + (dir < 0 ? -1 : 1);
      if (ni < 0) { dir = 1; ni = i + 1; }
      return showStep(ni, dir);
    }
    T.el = el; T.max = Math.max(T.max, i);
    if (el) {
      var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(), vh = innerHeight;
      if (r.top < 70 || r.bottom > vh - 30) {
        var top = scrollY + r.top - (r.height < vh - 220 ? Math.max(80, (vh - r.height) / 2 - 40) : 90);
        window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, top), behavior: reduced() ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
        await wait(reduced() ? 60 : 480);
      }
    }
    T.busy = false;
    renderPop(st); place();
  }
  function renderPop(st) {
    var pop = document.querySelector('.w122tp .pop'); if (!pop || !T) return;
    var n = T.steps.length, i = T.i;
    var tt = T.tr ? String(T.tr.title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, '') : T.key;
    var dots = n <= 16 ? '<div class="dots">' + T.steps.map(function (s, k) { return '<i class="' + (k === i ? 'on' : (k < i ? 'ok' : '')) + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="dots" style="font-size:12px;color:#797773">' + (i + 1) + ' of ' + n + '</div>';
    pop.innerHTML = '<div class="tk">' + ic('map') + ' ' + E(tt) + '<span class="sp"></span><button data-w122tx="1" title="End the tour (Esc)">' + ic('x') + '</button></div><h4>' + E(st.t) + '</h4><p>' + E(st.p) + '</p>' +
      '<div class="pf">' + dots + (i > 0 ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122tprev="1">Back</button>' : '') + '<button class="w122btn sm" data-w122tnext="1">' + (i >= n - 1 ? 'Finish' : 'Next') + '</button></div>';
    pop.style.opacity = '1';
    var nb = pop.querySelector('[data-w122tnext]'); if (nb) try { nb.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function place() {
    if (!T) return;
    var L = document.querySelector('.w122tp'); if (!L) return;
    var hole = L.querySelector('.hole'), pop = L.querySelector('.pop');
    var st = T.steps[T.i] || {}, el = T.el && T.el.isConnected ? T.el : null;
    if (!el && !st.c && T.el) { el = resolveStep(st); T.el = el; }   /* the page repainted under the tour: find the part again */
    var W = innerWidth, Hh = innerHeight, pw = Math.min(360, W - 24), ph = pop.offsetHeight || 200;
    if (!el) {
      hole.className = 'hole none';
      pop.style.left = Math.round((W - pw) / 2) + 'px'; pop.style.top = Math.round(Math.max(20, (Hh - ph) / 2)) + 'px';
      return;
    }
    var b = stepRect(st, el), pad = 8;
    var l = Math.max(6, b.l - pad), t = Math.max(6, b.t - pad), r = Math.min(W - 6, b.r + pad), bt = Math.min(Hh - 6, b.b + pad);
    if (bt - t < 24) { bt = t + 24; }
    hole.className = 'hole';
    hole.style.left = l + 'px'; hole.style.top = t + 'px'; hole.style.width = Math.max(24, r - l) + 'px'; hole.style.height = Math.max(24, bt - t) + 'px';
    if (W < 700) return;
    var x, y;
    if (r + 16 + pw <= W - 12) { x = r + 16; y = Math.min(Math.max(12, t), Hh - ph - 12); }
    else if (l - 16 - pw >= 12) { x = l - 16 - pw; y = Math.min(Math.max(12, t), Hh - ph - 12); }
    else if (bt + 14 + ph <= Hh - 12) { y = bt + 14; x = Math.min(Math.max(12, l), W - pw - 12); }
    else if (t - 14 - ph >= 12) { y = t - 14 - ph; x = Math.min(Math.max(12, l), W - pw - 12); }
    else { x = W - pw - 20; y = Hh - ph - 20; }
    pop.style.left = Math.round(x) + 'px'; pop.style.top = Math.round(y) + 'px';
  }
  var placeQ = 0;
  function placeSoon() { if (!T || placeQ) return; placeQ = requestAnimationFrame(function () { placeQ = 0; place(); }); }
  window.addEventListener('scroll', placeSoon, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('resize', placeSoon);
  function endTour(silent) {
    var L = document.querySelector('.w122tp'); if (L) L.remove();
    if (T && !silent && T.tr && !isDone(T.tr.id)) saveProg(T.tr, { last_step: T.max, pct: Math.min(95, Math.round(100 * (T.max + 1) / Math.max(1, T.steps.length))) });
    var was = T; T = null;
    if (was && !silent) toastS('Tour paused. Pick it up again from the Avenue Academy.');
    if (was) tourEvent('end', was.key, { silent: !!silent });
  }
  /* wave260: the page gate (train to unlock) listens: start, end (stopped before the last step) and finish (completion saved) */
  function tourEvent(kind, key, extra) {
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('w122tour', { detail: Object.assign({ kind: kind, key: key }, extra || {}) })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function finishTour() {
    if (!T) return;
    var tr = T.tr, key = T.key;
    var L = document.querySelector('.w122tp'); if (L) L.remove();
    T = null;
    /* complete() saves the local copy before its first wait, so the gate sees the tour done when it hears finish */
    var p = tr ? complete(tr, { last_step: TOURS[key] ? TOURS[key].steps.length : 0 }) : null;
    tourEvent('finish', key);
    if (p) await p;
    else celebrate(null, { s: stats() });
  }

  /* ---------- SOPs (wave125, 25/09/26) ----------
     Steel: "If we've done a similar event like the ones listed in the past please provide SOPs. I'm wondering if
     each department should have SOP on their home page or they should all be hosted in trainings center."
     Both: SOPs live here (one library, searchable, progress counts like any training), each team's overview
     shows its own ("Our SOPs"), and the events calendar links each event to its SOP (fund_events.sop_training_id).
     A trainings row with kind 'sop' carries its content in body: depts, owner, intro, glance [[label, value]],
     format [], editions [[label, when, text]], roles [[role, what]], phases [{when, title, steps []}], dayof [],
     money [], lessons [], confirm [], links [[title, url]], reviewed, sources. Leaders, and members of the SOP's
     teams, edit it in plain text. */
  var SOP_TEAMS = ['opsmtl', 'progdev', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board'];
  function isSop(t) { return !!t && t.active !== false && kindOf(t) === 'sop'; }
  function sopBody(t) { var b = t && t.body; if (typeof b === 'string') { try { b = JSON.parse(b); } catch (e) { b = null; } } return b && typeof b === 'object' ? b : {}; }
  function sopDepts(t) { var b = sopBody(t), d = Array.isArray(b.depts) ? b.depts : (b.dept ? [b.dept] : []); return d.filter(function (x) { return SOP_TEAMS.indexOf(x) >= 0; }); }
  function sopTitle(t) { return String((t && t.title) || '').replace(/^SOP:\s*/i, ''); }
  function teamName(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || tabLabel(d); } catch (e) { return d; } }
  function teamShortName(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.short && WA_TEAMS.short[d]) || teamName(d); } catch (e) { return d; } }
  function canEditSop(t) {
    if (!staffOk() || role() === 'board') return false;
    if (isLeader()) return true;
    var d = me().dept; if (!d) return false;
    if (!t) return true;
    var ds = sopDepts(t); return !ds.length || ds.indexOf(d) >= 0;
  }
  function sopSort(a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)); }
  function clip2(s, n) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1).trim() + '\u2026' : s; }
  function safeUrl(u) { u = String(u || '').trim(); return /^https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+$/i.test(u) ? u : ''; }
  function ymdToday() { var d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'); }

  /* ---------- the events that use an SOP ---------- */
  var SF = { rows: null, busy: null };
  function sopFundLoad() {
    if (SF.rows) return Promise.resolve(SF.rows);
    if (SF.busy) return SF.busy;
    SF.busy = Promise.resolve(db.from('fund_events').select('id, title, season, precision, start_date, end_date, timing, status, raised, sop_training_id').not('sop_training_id', 'is', null))
      .then(function (r) { SF.rows = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (f) { return f && f.sop_training_id != null; }); SF.busy = null; return SF.rows; },
        function () { SF.rows = []; SF.busy = null; return SF.rows; });
    return SF.busy;
  }
  function sopFundFor(t) { return (SF.rows || []).filter(function (f) { return t && String(f.sop_training_id) === String(t.id) && f.status !== 'cancelled'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.start_date || '9999').localeCompare(String(b.start_date || '9999')); }); }
  function fundDay(s) { var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(s || '')); if (!m) return null; var x = new Date(0); x.setFullYear(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])); x.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0); return x; }
  function fundWhen2(f) {
    var s = fundDay(f.start_date), e = fundDay(f.end_date);
    if (f.precision === 'day' && s) return MONTHS[s.getMonth()].slice(0, 3) + ' ' + s.getDate() + (e && e.getTime() !== s.getTime() ? ' to ' + (e.getMonth() !== s.getMonth() ? MONTHS[e.getMonth()].slice(0, 3) + ' ' : '') + e.getDate() : '') + ', ' + s.getFullYear();
    if (f.precision === 'month' && s) return MONTHS[s.getMonth()] + ' ' + s.getFullYear();
    if ((f.precision === 'range' || f.precision === 'season') && s) return String(f.timing || '').replace(/\s*\(.*\)\s*$/, '') + (/\d{4}/.test(f.timing || '') ? '' : ' ' + (e || s).getFullYear());
    return f.timing || (f.precision === 'flexible' ? 'Flexible' : 'Not yet scheduled');
  }
  function fundUpcoming(f) { if (f.status === 'done' || f.status === 'cancelled') return false; var e = fundDay(f.end_date) || fundDay(f.start_date); if (!e) return true; var t = new Date(); t.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); return e >= t; }
  function sopNextHtml(t) {
    var list = sopFundFor(t); if (!list.length) return '';
    var st = { idea: 'Idea', planned: 'Planned', confirmed: 'Confirmed', done: 'Done', cancelled: 'Cancelled' };
    return '<div class="sopnext"><div class="lb">' + ic('clock') + ' On the events calendar</div>' + list.map(function (f) {
      var up = fundUpcoming(f);
      return '<button class="ev' + (up ? ' up' : '') + '" data-w122sopfund="' + E(f.id) + '"><b>' + E(f.title) + '</b><span>' + E(fundWhen2(f)) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(st[f.status] || f.status || '') + (f.raised != null && f.status === 'done' ? ' \u00b7 raised $' + Number(f.raised).toLocaleString('en-CA') : '') + '</span>' + ic('arrow') + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function fillSopNext() {
    var el = document.querySelector('#w122sheet [data-w122sopnext]'); if (!el || !R || !R.t) return;
    if (String(el.getAttribute('data-w122sopnext')) !== String(R.t.id)) return;
    var h = sopNextHtml(R.t); if (el.__h !== h) { el.innerHTML = h; el.__h = h; }
  }

  /* ---------- reading an SOP ---------- */
  function sopUl(items, cls) { return '<ul class="' + (cls || 'bl') + '">' + (items || []).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + E(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>'; }
  function sopSec(id, title, inner) { return '<section class="sops" id="w122sop-' + id + '"><h3>' + E(title) + '</h3>' + inner + '</section>'; }
  function sopHtml(t) {
    var b = sopBody(t), ds = sopDepts(t), toc = [], h = '';
    if (!SF.rows) sopFundLoad().then(fillSopNext);
    var has = function (k) { return Array.isArray(b[k]) && b[k].length; };
    [['format', 'How it works'], ['editions', 'Past editions'], ['roles', 'Who does what'], ['phases', 'Step by step'], ['dayof', 'On the day'], ['money', 'Money'], ['lessons', 'What we learned'], ['links', 'Documents']].forEach(function (x) { if (has(x[0])) toc.push(x); });
    h += '<div class="w122sop">';
    h += '<div class="shero"><div class="chips">' + ds.map(function (d) { return '<span class="tm">' + E(teamShortName(d)) + '</span>'; }).join('')
      + (b.owner ? '<span class="own">' + ic('user') + ' ' + E(b.owner) + '</span>' : '')
      + (b.reviewed ? '<span class="rev">' + ic('check') + ' Reviewed ' + E(fmtDate(String(b.reviewed).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00')) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<p class="intro">' + E(b.intro || t.description || '') + '</p>'
      + (toc.length > 2 ? '<div class="toc">' + toc.map(function (x) { return '<button data-w122sopjump="' + x[0] + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (has('glance')) h += '<div class="glance">' + b.glance.map(function (g) { return '<div><b>' + E(g[0]) + '</b><span>' + E(g[1]) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    h += '<div data-w122sopnext="' + E(t.id) + '">' + sopNextHtml(t) + '</div>';
    if (has('format')) h += sopSec('format', 'How it works', sopUl(b.format));
    if (has('editions')) h += sopSec('editions', 'Past editions', '<div class="eds">' + b.editions.map(function (e) { return '<div class="ed"><div class="yr"><b>' + E(e[0]) + '</b>' + (e[1] ? '<span>' + E(e[1]) + '</span>' : '') + '</div><p>' + E(e[2] || '') + '</p></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>');
    if (has('roles')) h += sopSec('roles', 'Who does what', '<div class="roles">' + b.roles.map(function (r) { return '<div class="ro"><b>' + E(r[0]) + '</b><span>' + E(r[1] || '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>');
    if (has('phases')) h += sopSec('phases', 'Step by step', '<div class="phs">' + b.phases.map(function (p, i) {
      return '<div class="ph"><div class="phh"><span class="n">' + (i + 1) + '</span><div>' + (p.when ? '<span class="wh">' + E(p.when) + '</span>' : '') + '<h4>' + E(p.title || '') + '</h4></div></div>' + sopUl(p.steps, 'ck') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>');
    if (has('dayof')) h += sopSec('dayof', 'On the day', sopUl(b.dayof, 'ck'));
    if (has('money')) h += sopSec('money', 'Money', sopUl(b.money));
    if (has('lessons')) h += sopSec('lessons', 'What we learned', sopUl(b.lessons, 'ls'));
    if (has('confirm')) h += '<div class="todo"><b>' + ic('flag') + ' Still to confirm</b>' + sopUl(b.confirm) + '</div>';
    if (has('links')) {
      var ls = b.links.filter(function (l) { return safeUrl(l[1]); });
      if (ls.length) h += sopSec('links', 'Documents', '<div class="lnks">' + ls.map(function (l) { return '<a href="' + E(safeUrl(l[1])) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + ic('file') + '<span>' + E(l[0] || hostOf(l[1])) + '</span>' + ic('ext') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>');
    }
    h += '<p class="src">' + E(b.sources || 'Keep this SOP current: update it after every edition.') + (canEditSop(t) ? ' <button class="w122lnk" data-w122sopedit="1">Update it</button>' : '') + '</p>';
    return h + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- writing and editing an SOP (plain text in, structure out) ---------- */
  function linesOf(v) { return String(v || '').split(/\r?\n/).map(function (x) { return x.replace(/^\s*(?:[-\u2022*]|\d+[.)])\s+/, '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean); }
  function pairsIn(v, sep) { return linesOf(v).map(function (l) { var i = l.indexOf(sep); return i > 0 ? [l.slice(0, i).trim(), l.slice(i + sep.length).trim()] : [l, '']; }); }
  function pairsOut(a, sep) { return (a || []).map(function (x) { return (x[0] || '') + (x[1] ? sep + x[1] : ''); }).join('\n'); }
  function triplesIn(v) { return linesOf(v).map(function (l) { var p = l.split(' | '); if (p.length >= 3) return [p[0].trim(), p[1].trim(), p.slice(2).join(' | ').trim()]; if (p.length === 2) return [p[0].trim(), '', p[1].trim()]; return ['', '', l]; }); }
  function triplesOut(a) { return (a || []).map(function (x) { return [x[0] || '', x[1] || '', x[2] || ''].join(' | '); }).join('\n'); }
  function linksIn(v) { return linesOf(v).map(function (l) { var i = l.lastIndexOf(' | '); var t = i > 0 ? l.slice(0, i).trim() : ''; var u = i > 0 ? l.slice(i + 3).trim() : l.trim(); return [t || hostOf(u), u]; }).filter(function (x) { return safeUrl(x[1]); }); }
  function linksOut(a) { return (a || []).map(function (x) { return (x[0] ? x[0] + ' | ' : '') + (x[1] || ''); }).join('\n'); }
  function phasesOut(ps) { return (ps || []).map(function (p) { return '## ' + (p.when ? p.when + ' | ' : '') + (p.title || '') + '\n' + (p.steps || []).map(function (s) { return '- ' + s; }).join('\n'); }).join('\n\n'); }
  function phasesIn(v) {
    var out = [], cur = null;
    String(v || '').split(/\r?\n/).forEach(function (raw) {
      var l = raw.trim(); if (!l) return;
      var m = /^#{1,3}\s*(.*)$/.exec(l);
      if (m) { var parts = m[1].split(' | '); cur = { when: parts.length > 1 ? parts[0].trim() : '', title: (parts.length > 1 ? parts.slice(1).join(' | ') : parts[0]).trim(), steps: [] }; out.push(cur); return; }
      if (!cur) { cur = { when: '', title: 'Steps', steps: [] }; out.push(cur); }
      var s = l.replace(/^\s*(?:[-\u2022*]|\d+[.)])\s+/, '').trim(); if (s) cur.steps.push(s);
    });
    return out.filter(function (p) { return p.title || p.steps.length; });
  }
  function openSopForm(t, dept) { stopTimer(); R = { mode: 'sopform', t: t || null, dept: dept || '', secs: 0, kind: 'sop' }; renderSheet(); }
  function renderSopForm(sh) {
    var t = R.t, isNew = !t, b = t ? sopBody(t) : {};
    var ds = t ? sopDepts(t) : (R.dept && SOP_TEAMS.indexOf(R.dept) >= 0 ? [R.dept] : (me().dept ? [me().dept] : []));
    var ta = function (key, label, hint, val, rows) { return '<label>' + E(label) + (hint ? '<small>' + E(hint) + '</small>' : '') + '<textarea data-sf="' + key + '" rows="' + (rows || 4) + '">' + E(val || '') + '</textarea></label>'; };
    sh.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><div class="hi"><div class="kk"><span class="kd" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#8A5A0B,' + GOLD + ')">' + ic(isNew ? 'plus' : 'edit') + (isNew ? ' New SOP' : ' Edit SOP') + '</span></div><h2>' + (isNew ? 'Write an SOP' : E(sopTitle(t))) + '</h2></div><div class="acts"><button class="xb" data-w122x="1" title="Close (Esc)">' + ic('x') + '</button></div></div>'
      + '<div class="bd"><div class="main"><div class="wrap wide"><div class="form w122sopf">'
      + '<p class="snote" style="margin-top:0">Write it the way you would explain it to the next person running it. One item per line; the formats are shown under each box.</p>'
      + '<div class="row2"><label>Title<input type="text" data-sf="title" value="' + E(t ? sopTitle(t) : '') + '" placeholder="For example: Flag Football tournament"></label>'
      + '<label>Owner<input type="text" data-sf="owner" value="' + E(b.owner || '') + '" placeholder="For example: Fundraising, with Marketing"></label></div>'
      + '<label>One-line summary<small>Shown on the card in the Avenue Academy and on the team page.</small><input type="text" data-sf="description" value="' + E((t && t.description) || '') + '"></label>'
      + '<div class="teams"><span>Teams</span>' + SOP_TEAMS.map(function (d) { return '<label class="tg"><input type="checkbox" data-sfteam="1" value="' + d + '"' + (ds.indexOf(d) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + '>' + E(teamShortName(d)) + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + ta('intro', 'What it is and why we do it', 'A short paragraph.', b.intro, 3)
      + ta('glance', 'At a glance', 'Label: value, one per line. For example: When: a Saturday in August', pairsOut(b.glance, ': '), 5)
      + ta('format', 'How it works', 'Optional. One point per line.', (b.format || []).join('\n'), 3)
      + ta('editions', 'Past editions', 'Year | when | what happened, one per line.', triplesOut(b.editions), 4)
      + ta('roles', 'Who does what', 'Role: what they do, one per line.', pairsOut(b.roles, ': '), 5)
      + ta('phases', 'Step by step', 'Start each phase with ## when | title, then one step per line. For example: ## 5 months out | Lock the partner', phasesOut(b.phases), 14)
      + ta('dayof', 'On the day', 'One step per line.', (b.dayof || []).join('\n'), 5)
      + '<div class="row2">' + ta('money', 'Money', 'One point per line.', (b.money || []).join('\n'), 4) + ta('lessons', 'What we learned', 'One lesson per line.', (b.lessons || []).join('\n'), 4) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="row2">' + ta('confirm', 'Still to confirm', 'Optional. One question per line.', (b.confirm || []).join('\n'), 3) + ta('links', 'Documents', 'Title | https://link, one per line.', linksOut(b.links), 3) + '</div>'
      + '<label style="max-width:180px">Minutes to read<input type="number" min="1" max="120" data-sf="minutes" value="' + E((t && t.minutes) || 6) + '"></label>'
      + '</div></div></div></div>'
      + '<div class="ft">' + (!isNew && isLeader() ? '<button class="w122btn ghost" data-w122retire="1">Retire it</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button class="w122btn soft" data-w122sopcancel="1">Cancel</button><button class="w122btn" data-w122sopsave="1">' + ic('check') + (isNew ? ' Add the SOP' : ' Save') + '</button></div>';
    var main = sh.querySelector('.main'); if (main) main.scrollTop = 0;
    var first = sh.querySelector('[data-sf="title"]'); if (first && isNew) { try { first.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function slugOf(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '').slice(0, 40) || 'sop'; }
  var sopSaving = false;
  async function saveSopForm() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh'); if (!sh || !R || sopSaving) return;
    var f = {}; sh.querySelectorAll('[data-sf]').forEach(function (el) { f[el.getAttribute('data-sf')] = el.value; });
    var depts = Array.prototype.map.call(sh.querySelectorAll('[data-sfteam]:checked'), function (el) { return el.value; });
    var title = String(f.title || '').trim().replace(/^SOP:\s*/i, '');
    if (!title) { toastS('Give the SOP a title'); return; }
    if (!depts.length) { toastS('Pick at least one team'); return; }
    var old = R.t ? sopBody(R.t) : {};
    var body = Object.assign({}, old, {
      v: 1, depts: depts, owner: String(f.owner || '').trim(), intro: String(f.intro || '').trim(),
      glance: pairsIn(f.glance, ': '), format: linesOf(f.format), editions: triplesIn(f.editions), roles: pairsIn(f.roles, ': '),
      phases: phasesIn(f.phases), dayof: linesOf(f.dayof), money: linesOf(f.money), lessons: linesOf(f.lessons), confirm: linesOf(f.confirm),
      links: linksIn(f.links), reviewed: ymdToday(), edited_by: me().full
    });
    var row = { title: 'SOP: ' + title, description: String(f.description || '').trim() || null, kind: 'sop', category: 'Our SOPs', body: body, minutes: f.minutes ? Math.max(1, Math.min(120, Math.round(Number(f.minutes)))) : null, updated_at: nowIso() };
    sopSaving = true;
    try {
      var res;
      if (R.t) {
        res = await db.from('trainings').update(row).eq('id', R.t.id).select(); if (res.error) throw res.error;
        Object.keys(row).forEach(function (k) { R.t[k] = row[k]; }); applyTrainings(A.trainings); toastS('Saved'); var keep = R.t; openTraining(keep.id);
      } else {
        row.active = true; row.sort = 900; row.source = 'hub'; row.xp = 30; row.key = 'sop:' + slugOf(title) + '-' + Date.now().toString(36);
        res = await db.from('trainings').insert(row).select(); if (res.error) throw res.error;
        var nt = (res.data && res.data[0]) || row; if (!nt.id) nt.id = 'tmp' + Date.now();
        A.trainings.push(nt); applyTrainings(A.trainings); toastS('Added to Our SOPs');
        if (location.hash === '#training/t/' + nt.id) openTraining(nt.id); else location.hash = '#training/t/' + nt.id;
      }
      SC.rows = null; paintSoon(); setTimeout(function () { injectSopCard(true); }, 50);
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 80)); }
    finally { sopSaving = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- the academy home: Our SOPs ---------- */
  function sopTile(t) {
    var b = sopBody(t), ds = sopDepts(t), done = isDone(t.id), nx = sopFundFor(t).filter(fundUpcoming)[0];
    return '<button class="w122sopt' + (done ? ' ok' : '') + '" data-w122open="' + E(t.id) + '"><span class="tp"><span class="i">' + ic('list') + '</span>' + ds.slice(0, 2).map(function (d) { return '<span class="tm">' + E(teamShortName(d)) + '</span>'; }).join('') + (done ? '<span class="dn">' + ic('check') + ' Read</span>' : '') + '</span>'
      + '<b>' + E(sopTitle(t)) + '</b><span class="ds">' + E(clip2(t.description || b.intro || '', 140)) + '</span>'
      + '<span class="ft">' + ic('clock') + ' ' + mins(minutesOf(t)) + ((b.phases || []).length ? ' \u00b7 ' + plural(b.phases.length, 'phase') : '') + (nx ? '<em>Next: ' + E(nx.title) + '</em>' : '') + '</span></button>';
  }
  function sopsHomeHtml() {
    var list = A.trainings.filter(isSop).sort(sopSort), canAdd = canEditSop(null);
    if (!list.length && !canAdd) return '';
    if (!SF.rows && !SF.busy) sopFundLoad().then(paintSoon);
    var mine = me().dept;
    if (mine) list = list.filter(function (t) { return sopDepts(t).indexOf(mine) >= 0; }).concat(list.filter(function (t) { return sopDepts(t).indexOf(mine) < 0; }));
    return '<section class="w122sec" data-sec="sops"><div class="w122sech"><div><h2>Our SOPs</h2><div class="sub">How we run the things we do again and again: step by step, with what we learned last time. Your team\u2019s come first.</div></div><span class="sp"></span>'
      + (canAdd ? '<button class="w122btn soft sm" data-w122sopnew="' + E(mine || '') + '">' + ic('plus') + ' Write an SOP</button>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (list.length ? '<div class="w122sops">' + list.map(sopTile).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w122none">No SOPs yet. Write down how your team runs something it does again and again.</div>') + '</section>';
  }

  /* ---------- each team's overview: Our SOPs ---------- */
  var SC = { rows: null, busy: null, at: 0 };
  function sopCardLoad() {
    if (A.loaded) { SC.rows = A.trainings.filter(isSop); SC.at = Date.now(); return Promise.resolve(SC.rows); }
    if (SC.rows && Date.now() - SC.at < 120000) return Promise.resolve(SC.rows);
    if (SC.busy) return SC.busy;
    SC.busy = Promise.resolve(db.from('trainings').select('id, title, description, kind, body, sort, active, category, minutes, key').eq('kind', 'sop').eq('active', true).order('sort'))
      .then(function (r) { SC.rows = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(isSop); SC.at = Date.now(); SC.busy = null; return SC.rows; },
        function () { SC.busy = null; SC.rows = SC.rows || []; return SC.rows; });
    return SC.busy;
  }
  function teamRouteSop() { var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, ''); var p = h.split('/'); if (p[0] !== 'team' || !p[1] || SOP_TEAMS.indexOf(p[1]) < 0) return null; if (p[2] && p[2] !== 'overview') return null; return p[1]; }
  function sopCardHtml(d) {
    var list = (SC.rows || []).filter(function (t) { return sopDepts(t).indexOf(d) >= 0; }).sort(sopSort);
    var body = SC.rows == null ? '<p class="w116empty">Loading\u2026</p>' : list.length
      ? '<div class="w122soprows">' + list.map(function (t) { return '<a class="w122soprow" data-w122sopgo="' + E(t.id) + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="i">' + ic('list') + '</span><span class="tx"><b>' + E(sopTitle(t)) + '</b><span>' + E(clip2(t.description || '', 88)) + '</span></span>' + ic('chev') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      : '<p class="w116empty">No SOPs for this team yet. Write down how the team runs the things it does again and again, so the next person starts from what worked.</p>';
    return '<div class="w116card w122sopcard" id="w122sopcard" data-d="' + d + '"><h3>Our SOPs <span class="lnk" data-w122sopall="1">all SOPs</span></h3>' + body
      + (canEditSop(null) ? '<div class="w116addrow"><a data-w122sopnew="' + d + '">+ Write an SOP</a></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  var sopCardBusy = false;
  function injectSopCard(force) {
    if (sopCardBusy) return;
    var d = teamRouteSop(); if (!d || !staffOk()) return;
    var host = document.getElementById('w116host'); if (!host) return;
    var cols = host.querySelectorAll('.w116grid > .w116col'); var col = cols[1] || cols[0]; if (!col) return;
    var anchor = null;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(col.children, function (c) { if (c.id === 'w122sopcard') return; var h3 = c.querySelector('h3'); if (h3 && /^projects/i.test(String(h3.textContent || '').trim())) anchor = c; });
    var html = sopCardHtml(d), cur = document.getElementById('w122sopcard');
    var placed = cur && cur.parentNode === col && (anchor ? anchor.nextElementSibling === cur : true);
    if (cur && !force && placed && cur.__h === html) return;
    sopCardBusy = true;
    try {
      if (cur) cur.remove();
      if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', html); else col.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
      var nc = document.getElementById('w122sopcard'); if (nc) nc.__h = html;
    } finally { sopCardBusy = false; }
    if (SC.rows == null && !SC.busy) sopCardLoad().then(function () { injectSopCard(true); });
  }
  var sopCardT = null;

    var sec = document.getElementById('team'); if (!sec || sec.__w122sop) return;
    sec.__w122sop = true;

  }

  });

  /* ---------- onboarding actions ---------- */
  async function afterChange() {
    var res = await evaluate({ celebrate: true });
    if (res && res.fresh && res.fresh.length) celebrate(null, res);
    paint();
  }
  function toggleHub(id) {
    var api = window.w112Api; if (!api) return;
    var on = !api.done(id);
    if (api.set(id, on) === false) { toastS('One moment, the checklist is still loading'); return; }
    setTimeout(afterChange, 30);
  }
  function hubGo(id) { var api = window.w112Api; if (api && api.go) { try { api.go(id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  async function signPolicy(key, btn) {
    var m = me(); if (!m.email) return;
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true'); }
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').insert({ email: m.email, name: m.full || m.name, policy_key: key });
      if (r && r.error && !/duplicate|unique/i.test(String(r.error.message || ''))) throw { db: String(r.error.message || '') };
      A.signs[key] = nowIso();
      ['w105sign', 'w105signt'].forEach(function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.remove(); });
      toastS('Sign-off recorded, thank you');
      afterChange();
    } catch (e) {
      /* wave279: the database's refusal ("Read it for N more minutes first") shows as it is, and the count is read again */
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.removeAttribute('aria-busy'); }
      toastS((e && e.db) || 'Could not record the sign-off');
      if (window.wa279Pol) { window.wa279Pol.reload(); window.wa279Pol.refresh(); }
    }
  }
  async function ackStep(el) {
    var g = orgState(); if (!g.has) return;
    var tid = el.getAttribute('data-w122ack'), up = el.getAttribute('data-up');
    el.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('onboarding_update_acks').insert({ onboarding_id: Number(g.o.id), template_id: tid, template_updated_at: up, acked_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : me().name });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      A.acks[String(tid)] = up; toastS('Noted, thanks'); paint();
    } catch (e) { el.disabled = false; toastS('Could not save the confirmation'); }
  }
  var onbQ = null;
  function onbRefresh() {
    clearTimeout(onbQ);
    onbQ = setTimeout(function () {
      if (R && R.mode === 'onb') { var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh'); var main = sh && sh.querySelector('.main'); var top = main ? main.scrollTop : 0; renderSheet(); var m2 = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .main'); if (m2) m2.scrollTop = top; }
      afterChange();
    }, 700);
  }

  /* ---------- the Academy card on person pages ---------- */
  function personKey() { var m = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').match(/^#person\/(.+)$/); return m ? m[1] : null; }
  function personOf(key) {
    var pp = people(), k = String(key).toLowerCase();
    return pp.filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) === String(key); })[0] || pp.filter(function (p) { return String(p.name).toLowerCase() === k || String(p.email || '').toLowerCase() === k; })[0] || null;
  }
  function paintPerson() {
    var host = document.getElementById('w116kright'); if (!host || !staffOk()) return;
    var key = personKey(); if (!key) return;
    var p = personOf(key); if (!p) return;
    if (!A.loaded) { load().then(paintPerson); return; }
    var n = String(p.name).toLowerCase(), isMe = n === me().name.toLowerCase();
    var rows = A.prog.filter(function (r) { return String(r.person_name).toLowerCase() === n && A.byId[String(r.training_id)]; });
    var done = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'done'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.done_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.done_at || '')); });
    var todo = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.status !== 'done' && r.assigned_by; });
    var aw = A.awards.filter(function (a) { return String(a.person_name).toLowerCase() === n; });
    var certs = aw.filter(function (a) { return a.kind === 'cert'; }), badges = aw.filter(function (a) { return a.kind === 'badge'; });
    var lvN = aw.filter(function (a) { return a.kind === 'level'; }).reduce(function (mx, a) { return Math.max(mx, Number((a.meta || {}).n) || 0); }, 1), lvName = LEVELS[Math.max(0, lvN - 1)][1], lvPct = 0;
    if (isMe) { var s = stats(); lvN = s.level.n; lvName = s.level.name; lvPct = s.level.pct; }
    var sig = key + '|' + done.length + '|' + todo.length + '|' + aw.length + '|' + lvN;
    var cur = document.getElementById('w122pk');
    if (cur && cur.getAttribute('data-k') === sig && cur.parentNode === host) return;
    var html = '<div class="w116card" id="w122pk" data-k="' + E(sig) + '"><h3>Avenue Academy <a class="lnk" href="#training">' + (isMe ? 'open' : 'open yours') + '</a></h3>' +
      '<div class="lvrow"><div class="rg">' + ring(isMe ? lvPct : 100, 58, 6, GOLD, 'rgba(16,63,57,.08)') + '<b>' + lvN + '</b></div><div class="tx"><b>Level ' + lvN + ' &middot; ' + E(lvName) + '</b><span>' + plural(done.length, 'training') + ' completed' + (todo.length ? ' &middot; ' + todo.length + ' assigned' : '') + '</span></div></div>' +
      (certs.length ? '<div class="chips">' + certs.map(function (c) { return '<span class="chip">' + ic('seal') + E(c.title) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      (badges.length ? '<div class="bdgs">' + badges.map(function (b) { var v = awardVisual(b); return '<i title="' + E(b.title) + '" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + v.color + ',' + shade(v.color) + ')">' + ic(v.icon) + '</i>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="ln">' + (done.length ? 'Last completed: ' + E(String(A.byId[String(done[0].training_id)].title).replace(/^Page tour:\s*/, 'Tour: ')) + (done[0].done_at ? ', ' + E(fmtShort(done[0].done_at)) : '') : 'Nothing completed yet.') + '</div></div>';
    var tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = html; var card = tmp.firstChild;
    if (cur) cur.remove();
    var tr = document.getElementById('w116ktrain');
    if (tr && tr.parentNode === host) { tr.classList.add('w122hid'); host.insertBefore(card, tr.nextSibling); } else host.appendChild(card);
  }
  var personQ = null;
  function personSoon() { if (personQ) return; personQ = setTimeout(function () { personQ = null; try { paintPerson(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 450); }

  /* ---------- mounting and routing ---------- */
  function classic() { return lsGet('w122classic') === '1'; }
  function classicBar(p) {
    if (document.getElementById('w122cbar')) return;
    var b = document.createElement('div'); b.id = 'w122cbar';
    b.innerHTML = ic('sparkle') + '<span>This is the classic Training page.</span><span style="flex:1"></span><button class="w122btn sm" data-w122classic="0">Back to the Avenue Academy</button>';
    p.insertBefore(b, p.firstChild);
  }
  function mount() {
    var p = document.getElementById('training'); if (!p) return;
    var root = document.getElementById('w122');
    if (!root) { root = document.createElement('div'); root.id = 'w122'; p.insertBefore(root, p.firstChild); }
    else if (root.parentNode !== p) p.insertBefore(root, p.firstChild);
    if (classic()) { p.classList.remove('w122on'); classicBar(p); return; }
    p.classList.add('w122on');
    var cb = document.getElementById('w122cbar'); if (cb) cb.remove();
    paint();
    if (A.loaded && Date.now() - A.loaded > 90000) load(true).then(paint);
    if (A.loaded) after();
  }
  function parseHash() { var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, ''); if (h !== 'training' && h.indexOf('training/') !== 0) return null; var p = h.split('/'); return { sub: p[1] || '', arg: p.slice(2).join('/') }; }
  function route() {
    var r = parseHash();
    if (!r) { if (sheetOpen()) closeSheet(true); return false; }
    if (!document.getElementById('training') || !staffOk()) return false;
    var act = document.querySelector('.panel.active');
    if (!act || act.id !== 'training') { try { showPanel('training'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    mount();
    var go = function () {
      if (classic() && r.sub) { lsSet('w122classic', null); mount(); }
      if (r.sub === 't' && r.arg) { if (!R || !R.t || String(R.t.id) !== String(r.arg) || R.mode !== 'view') openTraining(r.arg); }
      else if (r.sub === 'p' && r.arg) openPath(r.arg);
      else if (r.sub === 'tour' && r.arg) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#training'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } startTour(r.arg); }
      else if (r.sub === 'cert' && r.arg) openCert(r.arg);
      else if (r.sub === 'onboarding') openOnb();
      else if (r.sub === 'sopnew') { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#training'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } openSopForm(null, r.arg || me().dept || ''); }
      else if (r.sub === 'sops') { if (sheetOpen()) closeSheet(true); setTimeout(function () { var sec = document.querySelector('#w122 [data-sec="sops"]'); if (sec) sec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduced() ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }, 80); }
      else if (sheetOpen()) closeSheet(true);
    };
    if (A.loaded) go(); else load().then(function () { paint(); after(); go(); });
    return true;
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { try { route(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } try { if (personKey()) { watchPerson(); personSoon(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  if (typeof applyHash === 'function') {
    var __ah122 = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () { var r = parseHash(); if (r && r.sub && route()) return; return __ah122.apply(this, arguments); };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp122 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      var out = __sp122.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (id === 'training') setTimeout(mount, 0); else if (id === 'personpage') { setTimeout(function () { watchPerson(); personSoon(); }, 300); } } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa122 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var out = await __sa122.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (parseHash()) route(); if (personKey()) { watchPerson(); personSoon(); } } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }

    if (t.getAttribute('data-w37chk') && t.closest && t.closest('#w122sheet')) { onbRefresh(); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    if (t.getAttribute('data-w122q')) { A.ui.q = t.value; A.ui.libN = 24; clearTimeout(libQ); libQ = setTimeout(paintLib, 140); return; }
    if (t.getAttribute('data-w122aq') && R) { R.q = t.value; clearTimeout(aQ); aQ = setTimeout(function () { if (!R || R.mode !== 'assign') return; renderSheet(); var i = document.querySelector('#w122sheet [data-w122aq]'); if (i) { i.focus(); try { i.setSelectionRange(i.value.length, i.value.length); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } }, 160); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (document.getElementById('w122cel')) { if (e.key === 'Escape') { closeCel(); e.preventDefault(); } return; }
    if (T) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { endTour(false); e.preventDefault(); return; }
      if (T.busy) return;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || (e.key === 'Enter' && !(e.target && /INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(e.target.tagName)))) { showStep(T.i + 1, 1); e.preventDefault(); return; }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { showStep(T.i - 1, -1); e.preventDefault(); return; }
      return;
    }
    if (sheetOpen() && e.key === 'Escape') { closeSheet(); e.preventDefault(); }
  });

  /* ---------- for the harness ---------- */
  window.w122 = {
    A: A, tours: TOURS, badges: BADGES, levels: LEVELS,
    state: function () { return { R: R, T: T }; },

    sop: { html: sopHtml, form: openSopForm, card: injectSopCard, fund: sopFundLoad, body: sopBody, phasesIn: phasesIn, phasesOut: phasesOut }
  };

/* =====================================================================
   WA v123 Requests and the team inbox (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "you need to redo the Request tab, it's hard to navigate and I
   want to make sure requests are also sent to the department's channel
   as well as have an inbox on the department's overview page showing
   the requests that came in via requests and via Hub Inbox (jev decides
   whether to notify team or not)".

   The Requests page (#requests) becomes four simple views:
   - New request: pick the team, pick what you need (or "something
     else"), fill in a short form. A side panel says where it goes (the
     team inbox, the Slack channel, who picks it up) and how it moves.
   - My requests: everything you asked for, with a progress bar per
     request and the status the team set.
   - Team inbox: what came in for a team, in one list: requests, emails
     the Hub inbox filed for the team (with Jev's call on whether the
     team was pinged), and asks spotted in meetings to accept or dismiss.
   - All requests (leaders) and Slack settings (admins): each team's
     channel, fallbacks, test posts, and how emails are handled.
   Every request and email opens in a sheet: status steps, assign, done,
   notes (mirrored into the Slack thread), the Slack link, the activity.
   Each department's overview page gets a Team inbox card at the top.
   The team-inbox edge function does the Slack side from database
   triggers (wa123_requests_inbox): a post in the team channel for every
   new request, thread replies for status, done and notes, a DM to the
   requester when it is done, and Jev's decision for each filed email.
   Deep links: #requests/new[/team[/type]], /mine, /team[/team], /all,
   /settings, /r/<request id>, /m/<email id>.
   ===================================================================== */

  function asDate(v) { if (!v) return null; var s = String(v); var d = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s) ? new Date(s + 'T12:00:00') : new Date(s); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d; }
  function fmtDay(v) { var d = asDate(v); if (!d) return ''; return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); }
  function fmtLong(v) { var d = asDate(v); if (!d) return ''; return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); }
  function fmtWhen(v) { var d = asDate(v); if (!d) return ''; return d.toLocaleString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); }
  function ago(v) {
    var d = asDate(v); if (!d) return '';
    var m = Math.round((now() - d.getTime()) / 60000);
    if (m < 1) return 'just now'; if (m < 60) return m + ' min ago';
    var h = Math.round(m / 60); if (h < 24) return h + ' h ago';
    var dd = Math.round(h / 24); if (dd < 7) return dd + (dd === 1 ? ' day ago' : ' days ago');
    return fmtDay(d);
  }
  function daysUntil(v) { var d = asDate(v); if (!d) return null; var t = new Date(isoDay() + 'T12:00:00'); return Math.round((new Date(isoDay(d) + 'T12:00:00').getTime() - t.getTime()) / 86400000); }
  function dueText(v) {
    var n = daysUntil(v); if (n === null) return '';
    if (n < 0) return 'overdue by ' + (-n) + (n === -1 ? ' day' : ' days');
    if (n === 0) return 'needed today'; if (n === 1) return 'needed tomorrow';
    if (n < 7) return 'needed in ' + n + ' days';
    return 'needed by ' + fmtDay(v);
  }

  function reduced() { try { return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } }

  function hue(n) { var h = 0, s = String(n || ''); for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0; return ['#103F39', '#6F64AB', '#B9851B', '#2A8C7A', '#c87a2f', '#3a6ea5', '#B3402F'][h % 7]; }

  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- state ---------- */
  var R = {
    loaded: 0, loading: null, ver: 1,
    types: [], reqs: [], sugs: [], mail: [], notes: {}, logs: {}, bodies: {}, cfg: null, cfgDraft: null, bot: null, botErr: null,
    view: 'new',
    nw: { d: null, type: null, q: '', drafts: {}, sent: null },
    tm: { d: null, state: 'open', kind: 'all', mine: false },
    all: { d: '', state: 'open', q: '' },
    sheet: null
  };
  var MAILCOLS = 'id, internal_date, from_email, from_name, subject, title, summary, kind, department, amount, due_date, status, filed, attachments, notify, team_state, team_done_by, team_done_at, assignee, request_id, mailbox, ask, assignee_email, forwarded_by';   /* wave170: where it came in, the ask, the person, who sent it */
  var JEV_SINCE = '2026-09-25T17:00:00Z';

  /* ---------- who and where ---------- */
  function members() { try { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function me() {
    var email = ''; try { email = lower((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || ''); } catch (e) { email = ''; }
    var name = ''; try { name = (typeof who === 'function') ? String(who() || '') : ''; } catch (e) { name = ''; }
    var m = members().filter(function (x) { return email && lower(x.email) === email; })[0] || null;
    return { email: email, name: name || (m && m.name) || (email ? email.split('@')[0] : 'staff'), dept: m ? m.department : null };
  }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function isAdmin() { return role() === 'admin'; }
  function isLeader() { var r = role(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader'; }
  function allowedTeams() { var a = null; try { a = typeof wa40Allowed === 'function' ? wa40Allowed() : null; } catch (e) { a = null; } return INBOX.filter(function (d) { return !a || a.indexOf(pageOfTeam(d)) >= 0; }); }
  function teamLabel(d) { return (T.labels && T.labels[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; }
  function teamShort(d) { return (T.short && T.short[d]) || teamLabel(d); }
  function teamColor(d) { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function tint(hex, a) { var h = String(hex || '').replace('#', ''); if (h.length === 3) h = h.split('').map(function (c) { return c + c; }).join(''); var n = parseInt(h, 16); if (h.length !== 6 || isNaN(n)) return 'rgba(16,63,57,' + a + ')'; return 'rgba(' + ((n >> 16) & 255) + ',' + ((n >> 8) & 255) + ',' + (n & 255) + ',' + a + ')'; }
  function cv(hex) { return '--c:' + hex + ';--cb:' + tint(hex, 0.13); }
  function homeTeam() { var ds = allowedTeams(); var mine = me().dept; if (R.tm.d && ds.indexOf(R.tm.d) >= 0) return R.tm.d; if (mine && ds.indexOf(mine) >= 0) return mine; return ds[0] || T.order[0]; }
  function nameByEmail(em) { em = lower(em); var m = members().filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === em; })[0]; return m ? m.name : null; }
  function teamMembers(d) { return members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === d && m.active !== false; }); }

  /* ---------- request helpers ---------- */
  function typeOf(key) { if (!key) return null; for (var i = 0; i < R.types.length; i++) if (R.types[i].key === key) return R.types[i]; return null; }
  function typesFor(d) { return R.types.filter(function (t) { return t.department === d && t.enabled !== false; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || String(a.label).localeCompare(String(b.label)); }); }
  function typeLabel(r) { var t = typeOf(r.type_key); return t ? t.label : (r.type_key ? cap(String(r.type_key).replace(/-/g, ' ')) : 'General request'); }
  function typeIcon(key) { return TYPE_ICON[key] || 'spark'; }
  function statusesOf(r) { var t = typeOf(r.type_key); return (t && Array.isArray(t.statuses) && t.statuses.length) ? t.statuses.slice() : DEFAULT_STATUSES.slice(); }
  function stepOf(r) { var s = statusesOf(r); if (r.done) return s.length; var cur = lower(r.status), i = -1; s.forEach(function (x, k) { if (lower(x) === cur) i = k; }); return i < 0 ? 0 : i; }
  function pct(r) { var s = statusesOf(r); if (r.done) return 100; return Math.max(8, Math.round((stepOf(r) + 1) / s.length * 100)); }
  function statusText(r) { return r.done ? 'Done' : cap(r.status || statusesOf(r)[0]); }
  function pillCls(r) { if (r.done) return 'done'; return stepOf(r) === 0 ? 'new' : 'prog'; }
  function neededOf(r) {
    if (r.needed_by) return r.needed_by;
    var d = r.details;
    if (d && typeof d === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d)) { for (var k in d) { if (/needed by|send date|which event date|first day/i.test(k) && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(d[k]))) return d[k]; } }
    return null;
  }
  function whoAsked(r) {
    var s = String(r.requested_by || '');
    var m = s.match(/^Meeting AI \(accepted by (.+)\)$/); if (m) return m[1] + ' (from a meeting)'; if (window.waWho) s = waWho(s);
    var c = s.match(/^Chat \((.+)\)$/); if (c) return (nameByEmail(c[1].indexOf('@') > 0 ? c[1] : c[1] + '@welfareavenue.com') || c[1]) + ' (Ask the Hub)';
    return s || (r.requested_email ? (nameByEmail(r.requested_email) || r.requested_email) : 'someone');
  }
  function isMine(r) {
    var m = me(); var by = lower(r.requested_by);
    if (m.email && lower(r.requested_email) === m.email) return true;
    if (!m.name) return false;
    var n = lower(m.name);
    return by === n || by.indexOf('accepted by ' + n) >= 0;
  }
  function withMe(x) {
    var a = lower(x.assignee); if (!a) return false;
    var n = lower(me().name); if (!n) return false;
    return a === n || a.split(' ')[0] === n.split(' ')[0];
  }
  function urgentOrLate(r) { if (r.done) return false; if (r.priority === 'urgent') return true; var n = daysUntil(neededOf(r)); return n !== null && n < 0; }
  function mailOpen(m) { return !m.team_state || m.team_state === 'open'; }
  function mailRecent(m) { var d = asDate(m.internal_date); return !d || (now() - d.getTime()) < 30 * 86400000; }
  function chanFor(d, t) {
    if (t && t.channel) return String(t.channel).replace(/^#/, '');
    var c = (R.cfg && R.cfg.channels && R.cfg.channels[d]) || DEFAULT_CHANNELS[d] || [];
    if (R.bot && R.bot.teams && R.bot.teams[d] && R.bot.teams[d].channel && !(t && t.channel)) return R.bot.teams[d].channel;
    return c[0] || null;
  }
  function cfgOf(v) {
    v = v || {};
    var ch = {};
    INBOX.forEach(function (d) {
      var x = v.channels && v.channels[d];
      var arr = (Array.isArray(x) ? x : (x ? [x] : [])).map(function (s) { return String(s || '').replace(/^#/, '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      ch[d] = arr.length ? arr : (DEFAULT_CHANNELS[d] || []).slice();
    });
    return { channels: ch, post_requests: v.post_requests !== false, thread_updates: v.thread_updates !== false, dm_requester: v.dm_requester !== false, email_mode: Object.assign({}, v.email_mode || {}) };
  }

  /* one team's inbox: requests, emails the Hub inbox filed for the team, asks spotted in meetings */
  function teamItems(d, state) {
    var out = [];
    R.reqs.forEach(function (r) { if (r.department !== d) return; if (state === 'open' ? !r.done : r.done) out.push({ k: 'r', x: r, at: r.created_at, hot: urgentOrLate(r), soon: !r.done && (daysUntil(neededOf(r)) !== null && daysUntil(neededOf(r)) <= 3) }); });
    R.mail.forEach(function (m) {
      if (m.department !== d) return;
      var open = mailOpen(m) && mailRecent(m);
      if (state === 'open' ? open : !open) out.push({ k: 'm', x: m, at: m.internal_date, hot: false, soon: !!(m.due_date && daysUntil(m.due_date) !== null && daysUntil(m.due_date) <= 3 && open) });
    });
    if (state === 'open') R.sugs.forEach(function (s) { if (s.department === d) out.push({ k: 's', x: s, at: s.created_at, hot: false, soon: false }); });
    out.sort(function (a, b) {
      if (a.hot !== b.hot) return a.hot ? -1 : 1;
      if (a.soon !== b.soon) return a.soon ? -1 : 1;
      return String(b.at || '').localeCompare(String(a.at || ''));
    });
    return out;
  }
  function counts() {
    var mine = R.reqs.filter(function (r) { return !r.done && isMine(r); }).length;
    var d = homeTeam();
    return { mine: mine, inbox: d ? teamItems(d, 'open').length : 0, d: d };
  }

  /* ---------- loading ---------- */
  function q(p) { return Promise.resolve(p).then(function (r) { return r || { data: null }; }, function (e) { return { data: null, error: e }; }); }
