  async function load(force) {
    if (R.loading) return R.loading;
    if (R.loaded && !force && now() - R.loaded < 45000) return;
    R.loading = (async function () {
      var since = new Date(now() - 120 * 86400000).toISOString();
      var a = await Promise.all([
        q(db.from('request_types').select('*').order('sort').order('label')),
        q(db.from('dept_requests').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(800)),
        q(db.from('request_suggestions').select('*').eq('status', 'pending').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(60)),
        q(db.from('hub_mail').select(MAILCOLS).eq('status', 'filed').gte('internal_date', since).order('internal_date', { ascending: false }).limit(300)),
        q(db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'team_inbox').maybeSingle())
      ]);
      if (a[0].data) R.types = a[0].data.filter(function (t) { return t.enabled !== false; });
      if (a[1].data) R.reqs = a[1].data;
      if (a[2].data) R.sugs = a[2].data.filter(function (s) { return s.status === 'pending'; });
      if (a[3].data) R.mail = a[3].data.filter(function (m) { return m.department && m.kind !== 'noise' && m.status === 'filed' && ((m.filed || {}).type !== 'inbox'); });
      R.cfg = cfgOf(a[4].data && a[4].data.value);
      try { REQ_TYPES = R.types; REQ_CACHE = R.reqs; } catch (e) { /* older waves read these */ }
      R.loaded = now(); R.ver++;
    })();
    try { await R.loading; } catch (e) { R.loaded = R.loaded || now(); } finally { R.loading = null; }
  }
  async function loadThread(k, id) {
    var key = k + id;
    if (k === 'r') {
      var a = await Promise.all([
        q(db.from('request_notes').select('*').eq('request_id', id).order('created_at')),
        q(db.from('change_log').select('field, old_value, new_value, changed_by, changed_at').eq('entity_type', 'request').eq('entity_id', String(id)).order('changed_at').limit(80))
      ]);
      R.notes[id] = (a[0].data || []).filter(function (n) { return String(n.request_id) === String(id); });
      R.logs[key] = a[1].data || [];
    } else {
      var b = await q(db.from('change_log').select('field, old_value, new_value, changed_by, changed_at').eq('entity_type', 'hub_mail').eq('entity_id', String(id)).order('changed_at').limit(40));
      R.logs[key] = b.data || [];
    }
  }
  async function fx(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('team-inbox', { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) {
      var msg = r.error.message || String(r.error);
      try { var ctx = r.error.context; if (ctx && typeof ctx.json === 'function') { var j = await ctx.json(); if (j && j.error) msg = j.error; } } catch (e) { /* keep the first message */ }
      throw new Error(msg);
    }
    if (r && r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return (r && r.data) || {};
  }
  async function logChange(entity, id, field, oldV, newV) {
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: entity, entity_id: String(id), field: field, old_value: oldV == null ? null : String(oldV), new_value: newV == null ? null : String(newV).slice(0, 400), changed_by: me().name }); } catch (e) { /* audit only */ }
    var key = (entity === 'request' ? 'r' : 'm') + id;
    if (R.logs[key]) R.logs[key].push({ field: field, old_value: oldV, new_value: newV, changed_by: me().name, changed_at: new Date().toISOString() });
  }
  function bump() { R.ver++; try { REQ_CACHE = R.reqs; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }

  /* ---------- changes ---------- */
  async function createRequest(p) {
    var m = me(), t = p.type;
    var row = {
      type_key: t ? t.key : null, department: p.d, title: p.title, details: p.details,
      status: t ? (t.default_status || statusesOf({ type_key: t.key })[0]) : 'requested',
      requested_by: m.name, requested_email: m.email || null, needed_by: p.needed || null,
      priority: p.urgent ? 'urgent' : 'normal', source: p.source || 'requests', done: false
    };
    if (p.mail_id) row.mail_id = p.mail_id;
    if (p.requested_by) row.requested_by = p.requested_by;
    if (p.requested_email !== undefined) row.requested_email = p.requested_email;
    if (p.assignee) row.assignee = p.assignee;
    var r = await db.from('dept_requests').insert(row).select('*').single();
    if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
    var saved = (r && r.data && r.data.id) ? r.data : Object.assign({ id: -now(), created_at: new Date().toISOString() }, row);
    if (!saved.created_at) saved.created_at = new Date().toISOString();
    R.reqs.unshift(saved); bump();
    await logChange('request', saved.id, 'created', null, row.title);
    return saved;
  }
  async function patchReq(r, patch, okText, failText, logs) {
    var old = {}; Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { old[k] = r[k]; });
    patch.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); patch.updated_by = me().name;
    Object.assign(r, patch); bump(); repaint();
    var res = await q(db.from('dept_requests').update(patch).eq('id', r.id));
    if (res.error) { Object.assign(r, old); bump(); repaint(); toastS(failText || 'Could not save that'); return false; }
    for (var i = 0; i < (logs || []).length; i++) await logChange('request', r.id, logs[i][0], logs[i][1], logs[i][2]);
    if (okText) toastS(okText);
    repaint();
    return true;
  }
  function setStatus(r, st) {
    var s = statusesOf(r), last = lower(s[s.length - 1]) === lower(st);
    if (lower(st) === lower(r.status) && !r.done) return;
    var patch = { status: st };
    var logs = [['status', r.status, st]];
    if (last && !r.done) { patch.done = true; patch.done_at = new Date().toISOString(); logs.push(['done', 'false', 'true']); }
    if (!last && r.done) { patch.done = false; patch.done_at = null; logs.push(['done', 'true', 'false']); }
    return patchReq(r, patch, last ? 'Marked done' : 'Status: ' + cap(st), 'Could not change the status', logs);
  }
  function setDone(r, done) {
    return patchReq(r, { done: done, done_at: done ? new Date().toISOString() : null }, done ? 'Marked done' : 'Reopened', 'Could not save that', [['done', String(!done), String(done)]]);
  }
  function assign(r, name) {
    return patchReq(r, { assignee: name || null }, name ? 'Now with ' + name : 'Unassigned', 'Could not assign it', [['assignee', r.assignee || null, name || null]]);
  }
  async function addNote(r, text) {
    var m = me();
    var row = { request_id: r.id, author: m.name, email: m.email || null, body: text };
    var res = await q(db.from('request_notes').insert(row).select('*').single());
    if (res.error) { toastS('Could not add the note'); return false; }
    var saved = (res.data && res.data.id) ? res.data : Object.assign({ id: -now(), created_at: new Date().toISOString() }, row);
    if (!saved.created_at) saved.created_at = new Date().toISOString();
    (R.notes[r.id] = R.notes[r.id] || []).push(saved);
    toastS('Note added' + (r.slack && r.slack.ts ? ' and posted in the Slack thread' : ''));
    return true;
  }
  async function mailSet(m, opts) {
    var old = { team_state: m.team_state, assignee: m.assignee, request_id: m.request_id, team_done_by: m.team_done_by, team_done_at: m.team_done_at };
    if (opts.state) { m.team_state = opts.state; m.team_done_by = opts.state === 'open' ? null : me().name; m.team_done_at = opts.state === 'open' ? null : new Date().toISOString(); }
    if (opts.clear) m.assignee = null; else if (opts.assignee) m.assignee = opts.assignee;
    if (opts.request_id) m.request_id = opts.request_id;
    bump(); repaint();
    var res = await q(db.rpc('team_inbox_mail', { p_id: m.id, p_state: opts.state || null, p_assignee: opts.assignee || null, p_request_id: opts.request_id || null, p_clear_assignee: !!opts.clear, p_by: me().name }));
    if (res.error) { Object.assign(m, old); bump(); repaint(); toastS('Could not save that'); return false; }
    var key = 'm' + m.id; if (R.logs[key]) R.logs[key].push({ field: 'team inbox', new_value: (opts.state ? 'state ' + opts.state : '') + (opts.assignee ? ' assigned ' + opts.assignee : '') + (opts.clear ? ' unassigned' : '') + (opts.request_id ? ' request ' + opts.request_id : ''), changed_by: me().name, changed_at: new Date().toISOString() });
    return true;
  }
  async function convertMail(m) {
    var staffSender = /@welfareavenue\.com$/i.test(m.from_email || '');
    var details = {};
    details['From'] = (m.from_name ? m.from_name + ' ' : '') + '<' + (m.from_email || '') + '>';
    if (m.summary) details['Summary'] = m.summary;
    if (m.subject) details['Email subject'] = m.subject;
    if (m.amount != null) details['Amount'] = '$' + Number(m.amount).toLocaleString('en-CA', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
    var saved = await createRequest({
      d: m.department, type: null, title: clip(m.title || m.subject || 'Email request', 150), details: details,
      needed: m.due_date || null, urgent: false, source: 'email', mail_id: m.id,
      requested_by: m.from_name || m.from_email || 'Hub inbox', requested_email: staffSender ? lower(m.from_email) : null,
      assignee: m.assignee || null
    });
    await mailSet(m, { state: 'request', request_id: saved.id > 0 ? saved.id : null });
    m.request_id = saved.id; bump();
    return saved;
  }
  async function acceptSug(s) {
    var m = me(), by = m.name, iso = new Date().toISOString();
    if (s.kind === 'refine' && s.target_request_id) {
      var tgt = R.reqs.filter(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(s.target_request_id); })[0];
      if (!tgt) { var f = await q(db.from('dept_requests').select('*').eq('id', s.target_request_id).maybeSingle()); tgt = f.data; }
      if (!tgt) { toastS('The request it updates is gone'); return false; }
      var add = (s.details || s.title || '') + (s.quote ? '\n"' + s.quote + '"' : '');
      var det = tgt.details;
      if (det && typeof det === 'object' && !Array.isArray(det)) { det = Object.assign({}, det); det['Update from a meeting, ' + fmtDay(iso)] = add; }
      else det = (det ? String(det) + '\n\n' : '') + 'Update from a meeting: ' + add;
      var u = await q(db.from('dept_requests').update({ details: det, updated_at: iso, updated_by: by }).eq('id', tgt.id));
      if (u.error) { toastS('Could not apply it'); return false; }
      tgt.details = det;
      await q(db.from('request_suggestions').update({ status: 'accepted', request_id: tgt.id, decided_by: by, decided_at: iso }).eq('id', s.id));
      await logChange('request', tgt.id, 'refined', null, clip(s.title, 200));
      toastS('Added onto the open request');
    } else {
      var t = typeOf(s.type_key);
      var details = {};
      if (s.details) details['What was asked'] = s.details;
      if (s.quote) details['From the meeting'] = '"' + s.quote + '"';
      var saved;
      try {
        saved = await createRequest({ d: s.department, type: t, title: s.title, details: details, source: 'meeting', requested_by: by + ' (from a meeting)', assignee: s.assignee || null });
      } catch (e) { toastS('Could not file it'); return false; }
      await q(db.from('request_suggestions').update({ status: 'accepted', request_id: saved.id > 0 ? saved.id : null, decided_by: by, decided_at: iso }).eq('id', s.id));
      toastS('Filed to ' + teamLabel(s.department));
    }
    R.sugs = R.sugs.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== s.id; }); bump(); repaint();
    return true;
  }
  async function dismissSug(s) {
    var r = await q(db.from('request_suggestions').update({ status: 'dismissed', decided_by: me().name, decided_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', s.id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not dismiss it'); return; }
    R.sugs = R.sugs.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== s.id; }); bump(); repaint();
    toastS('Dismissed');
  }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  var MAST_P = 'Ask another team for something, follow it through to done, and see what came in for your team.';
  function host() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('requests'); if (!sec) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w123');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w123'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function mount() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('requests');
    if (!sec || !staffOk()) return false;
    sec.classList.add('w123on'); host();
    var p = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast > p'); if (p && p.textContent !== MAST_P) p.textContent = MAST_P;
    return true;
  }
  function onPage() { var a = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); return !!(a && a.id === 'requests'); }
  function paint() {
    var h = host(); if (!h || !mount()) return;
    var html = navHtml();
    if (!R.loaded) html += '<div class="w123card"><p class="w123empty">Loading requests...</p></div>';
    else if (R.view === 'mine') html += mineHtml();
    else if (R.view === 'team') html += teamHtml();
    else if (R.view === 'all') html += allHtml();
    else if (R.view === 'settings') html += settingsHtml();
    else html += newHtml();
    h.innerHTML = html;
    after();
  }
  var paintT = null;
  function paintSoon() { clearTimeout(paintT); paintT = setTimeout(function () { if (onPage()) paint(); }, 30); }
  function repaint() {
    if (onPage() && !typingInForm()) paint();
    if (R.sheet) renderSheet();
    injectCard(true);
  }
  function typingInForm() { var a = document.activeElement; return !!(a && a.closest && a.closest('#w123 form[data-w123form]')); }
  function after() {
    if (R.view === 'new' && R.nw.sent) watchSent(R.nw.sent);
    if (R.view === 'settings' && isLeader() && !R.bot && !R.botBusy) loadBot();
  }

  function navHtml() {
    var c = R.loaded ? counts() : { mine: 0, inbox: 0, d: homeTeam() };
    var items = [['new', 'New request', '#requests/new', 0], ['mine', 'My requests', '#requests/mine', c.mine], ['team', 'Team inbox', '#requests/team/' + (R.tm.d || c.d || ''), c.inbox]];
    if (isLeader()) items.push(['all', 'All requests', '#requests/all', 0]);
    return '<div class="w123nav"><div class="w123seg" role="tablist" aria-label="Requests">' + items.map(function (it) {
      var on = R.view === it[0];
      return '<a role="tab" aria-selected="' + on + '" tabindex="0" data-w123go="' + it[2] + '" class="' + (on ? 'on' : '') + '">' + (it[0] === 'new' ? I('plus') : '') + E(it[1]) + (it[3] ? '<span class="n' + (it[0] === 'team' ? ' hot' : '') + '">' + it[3] + '</span>' : '') + '</a>';
    }).join('') + '</div><span class="sp"></span>' + (isLeader() ? '<a class="w123gear' + (R.view === 'settings' ? ' on' : '') + '" tabindex="0" data-w123go="#requests/settings">' + I('gear') + 'Slack settings</a>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function crumb(parts) {
    return '<div class="w123crumb">' + parts.map(function (p, i) { return (i ? I('chev') : '') + (p[1] ? '<a data-w123go="' + p[1] + '">' + E(p[0]) + '</a>' : '<span>' + E(p[0]) + '</span>'); }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function teamChip(d) { return '<span class="w123tm" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '"><i></i>' + E(teamShort(d)) + '</span>'; }

  /* ---------- new request: team, then need, then the short form ---------- */
  function newHtml() {
    var n = R.nw;
    if (n.sent) return sentHtml(n.sent);
    if (n.d && n.type) return formHtml(n.d, n.type === 'other' ? null : typeOf(n.type));
    if (n.d) return typesHtml(n.d);
    return teamsHtml();
  }
  function recentTypes() {
    var seen = {}, out = [];
    R.reqs.filter(isMine).forEach(function (r) { var k = r.department + '/' + (r.type_key || 'other'); if (seen[k] || out.length >= 4) return; seen[k] = 1; out.push({ d: r.department, t: r.type_key || 'other', l: r.type_key ? typeLabel(r) : 'Something else for ' + teamShort(r.department) }); });
    return out;
  }
  function teamsHtml() {
    var m = me();
    var tiles = INBOX.map(function (d) {
      var ts = typesFor(d), ch = chanFor(d);
      return '<button type="button" class="w123team' + (m.dept === d ? ' mine' : '') + '" data-w123team="' + d + '" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '">'
        + '<span class="w123ico">' + I(TEAM_ICON[d]) + '</span><b>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</b><span class="s">' + E(TEAM_BLURB[d] || '') + '</span>'
        + '<span class="m"><span>' + (ts.length ? ts.length + (ts.length === 1 ? ' kind of request' : ' kinds of request') : 'Describe what you need') + '</span>' + (ch ? '<span>' + I('hash') + E(ch) + '</span>' : '') + '</span></button>';
    }).join('');
    var again = recentTypes();
    var q0 = R.nw.q || '';
    return '<div class="w123anim"><div class="w123card"><h2 class="w123h">Who do you need something from?</h2>'
      + '<p class="w123sub">Pick the team, then what you need. It lands in their team inbox and their Slack channel, and you can follow it in My requests.</p>'
      + '<div class="w123search">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w123q autocomplete="off" placeholder="Or search: flyer, reimbursement, time off, volunteers" value="' + E(q0) + '" aria-label="Search every kind of request"></div>'
      + (again.length ? '<div class="w123again">Again:' + again.map(function (a) { return '<a class="w123chip" data-w123go="#requests/new/' + a.d + '/' + a.t + '">' + I(a.t === 'other' ? 'spark' : typeIcon(a.t)) + E(a.l) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + '</div><div id="w123qres">' + searchHtml(q0) + '</div>'
      + '<div id="w123tiles"' + (q0 ? ' style="display:none"' : '') + '><div class="w123teams">' + tiles + '</div>' + sugsLanding() + '</div></div>';
  }
  function searchHtml(qs) {
    qs = lower(qs); if (!qs) return '';
    var words = qs.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    var hits = R.types.filter(function (t) {
      var hay = lower(t.label + ' ' + (t.description || '') + ' ' + teamLabel(t.department) + ' ' + (t.key || '').replace(/-/g, ' '));
      return words.every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; });
    });
    var teams = INBOX.filter(function (d) { var hay = lower(teamLabel(d) + ' ' + (TEAM_BLURB[d] || '')); return words.every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; }); });
    var rows = hits.map(function (t) { return typeRow(t.department, t, true); }).join('')
      + teams.map(function (d) { return '<button type="button" class="w123type other" data-w123team="' + d + '" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '"><span class="w123ico">' + I(TEAM_ICON[d]) + '</span><span><span class="t2">Team</span><b>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</b><span class="d">' + E(TEAM_BLURB[d] || '') + '</span></span>' + I('chev') + '</button>'; }).join('');
    if (!rows) return '<div class="w123types w123anim"><p class="w123empty" style="padding:18px 20px">Nothing matches &ldquo;' + E(qs) + '&rdquo;. Pick the team below and choose <b>Something else</b>: describe it in your own words.</p></div>';
    return '<div class="w123types w123anim">' + rows + '</div>';
  }
  function typeRow(d, t, withTeam) {
    return '<button type="button" class="w123type" data-w123type="' + E(t.key) + '" data-d="' + d + '" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '"><span class="w123ico">' + I(typeIcon(t.key)) + '</span><span>'
      + (withTeam ? '<span class="t2">' + E(teamShort(d)) + '</span>' : '') + '<b>' + E(t.label) + '</b>' + (t.description ? '<span class="d">' + E(t.description) + '</span>' : '') + '</span>' + I('chev') + '</button>';
  }
  function typesHtml(d) {
    var ts = typesFor(d), ch = chanFor(d);
    return '<div class="w123anim">' + crumb([['New request', '#requests/new'], [teamLabel(d)]])
      + '<div class="w123card"><span class="w123tm" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '"><i></i>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</span><h2 class="w123h" style="margin-top:6px">What do you need from ' + E(teamShort(d)) + '?</h2>'
      + '<p class="w123sub">' + (ch ? 'Requests are posted in <b>#' + E(ch) + '</b> and land in the team inbox.' : 'Requests land in the team inbox.') + ' Pick the closest one; you can add anything the form does not ask for.</p></div>'
      + '<div class="w123types">' + ts.map(function (t) { return typeRow(d, t, false); }).join('')
      + '<button type="button" class="w123type other" data-w123type="other" data-d="' + d + '"><span class="w123ico">' + I('plus') + '</span><span><b>Something else</b><span class="d">Describe what you need from ' + E(teamShort(d)) + ' in your own words.</span></span>' + I('chev') + '</button></div></div>';
  }
  function titleHint(t) {
    var H = { 'post-request': 'Instagram post for the October food drive', 'email-request': 'Newsletter: thank-you to our volunteers', 'design-request': 'Poster for the community kitchen day', 'event-coverage': 'Photos at the Rally Paper finish line', 'print-request': '200 flyers for the clothing drive', 'reimbursement': 'Gloves and containers for Sunday', 'purchase-order': 'Six folding tables for servings', 'grant-info': 'Meal numbers for the DESTA report', 'hiring-request': 'Event coordinator, part time', 'onboarding-request': 'Onboard Priya, Program Coordinator', 'access-request': 'Canva access for Joy', 'event-supplies': 'Containers and gloves for Sunday', 'volunteer-support': 'Four more servers on Sunday', 'transport-request': 'Move the coolers to the park', 'media-release': 'Photo consent line for the volunteer form' };
    return 'A short summary, e.g. ' + (H[t && t.key] || 'what you need, in a few words');
  }
  function fieldHtml(f, i, dr) {
    var id = 'w123f' + i, name = 'f' + i, v = dr[name] == null ? '' : dr[name];
    var lab = '<label for="' + id + '">' + E(f.label) + (f.required ? '<span class="rq" aria-hidden="true">*</span>' : '<span class="op">optional</span>') + '</label>';
    if (f.type === 'textarea') return lab + '<textarea id="' + id + '" name="' + name + '"' + (f.required ? ' required aria-required="true"' : '') + '>' + E(v) + '</textarea>';
    var type = { number: 'number', date: 'date', url: 'url' }[f.type] || 'text';
    return lab + '<input id="' + id + '" name="' + name + '" type="' + type + '"' + (f.required ? ' required aria-required="true"' : '') + ' value="' + E(v) + '"' + (type === 'url' ? ' placeholder="https://"' : '') + (type === 'number' ? ' inputmode="decimal" step="any"' : '') + '>';
  }
  function fieldsOf(t) { return t && Array.isArray(t.fields) ? t.fields : []; }
  function hasDateField(t) { return fieldsOf(t).some(function (f) { return f.type === 'date' && DATE_KEYS.test(f.name || ''); }); }
  function formHtml(d, t) {
    var key = t ? t.key : 'other', dr = R.nw.drafts[d + ':' + key] || {};
    var head = crumb([['New request', '#requests/new'], [teamLabel(d), '#requests/new/' + d], [t ? t.label : 'Something else']]);
    if (t && t.key === 'time-off') return '<div class="w123anim">' + head + '<div class="w123card" style="max-width:720px"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv('#B9851B') + '">' + I('sun') + '</span><h2 class="w123h" style="margin-top:12px">Time off has its own form</h2><p class="w123sub">It records the days against your time off for the year, HR confirms it, and it shows on your page. HR also sees it here, in their team inbox.</p><div class="w123acts"><button type="button" class="w123btn" data-w123timeoff>' + I('sun') + 'Open the time off form</button><a class="w123btn ghost" data-w123go="#requests/new/' + d + '">Back</a></div></div></div>';
    var fields = fieldsOf(t);
    var html = '<div class="w123anim">' + head + '<div class="w123fw"><form class="w123card w123form" data-w123form="' + E(key) + '" data-d="' + d + '" novalidate>'
      + '<span class="w123tm" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '"><i></i>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</span>'
      + '<h2 class="w123h" style="margin-top:6px">' + E(t ? t.label : 'Something else for ' + teamShort(d)) + '</h2>'
      + (t && t.description ? '<p class="w123sub">' + E(t.description) + '</p>' : (!t ? '<p class="w123sub">Say what you need in your own words. The team can ask questions on the request.</p>' : ''))
      + '<label for="w123ft">Title<span class="rq" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><input type="text" id="w123ft" name="title" maxlength="160" required aria-required="true" placeholder="' + E(titleHint(t)) + '" value="' + E(dr.title || '') + '">'
      + fields.map(function (f, i) { return fieldHtml(f, i, dr); }).join('')
      + (!t ? '<label for="w123fw">What do you need?<span class="rq" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><textarea id="w123fw" name="__what" required aria-required="true" placeholder="What, for whom, by when, and anything the team should know">' + E(dr.__what || '') + '</textarea>' : '')
      + (!hasDateField(t) ? '<label for="w123fn">Needed by<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="date" id="w123fn" name="__needed" min="' + isoDay() + '" value="' + E(dr.__needed || '') + '">' : '')
      + (fields.length ? '<label for="w123fm">Anything else<span class="op">optional</span></label><textarea id="w123fm" name="__more" placeholder="Links, context, who to talk to">' + E(dr.__more || '') + '</textarea>' : '')
      + '<label class="w123tog"><input type="checkbox" name="__urgent"' + (dr.__urgent ? ' checked' : '') + '><span><b>This is urgent</b><small>It goes to the top of the team inbox and is flagged in Slack.</small></span></label>'
      + '<p class="w123sub" data-w123err style="color:' + FLAG + ';display:none"></p>'
      + '<div class="w123acts"><button type="submit" class="w123btn" data-w123send>' + I('arrow') + 'Send to ' + E(teamShort(d)) + '</button><a class="w123btn ghost" data-w123go="#requests/new/' + d + '">Back</a></div>'
      + '</form>' + asideHtml(d, t) + '</div></div>';
    return html;
  }
  function asideHtml(d, t) {
    var ch = chanFor(d, t), owner = t && t.default_assignee;
    var st = t && Array.isArray(t.statuses) && t.statuses.length ? t.statuses : DEFAULT_STATUSES;
    return '<aside class="w123aside"><h4>Where it goes</h4>'
      + '<div class="w123ar"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '">' + I('inbox') + '</span><div><b>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '&rsquo;s team inbox</b><span>On their team page and here in Requests</span></div></div>'
      + '<div class="w123ar"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv('#4A154B') + '">' + I('hash') + '</span><div><b>' + (ch ? '#' + E(ch) : 'No Slack channel yet') + '</b><span>' + (ch ? 'Posted in the team&rsquo;s channel the moment you send it' : 'An admin can set one in Slack settings') + '</span></div></div>'
      + '<div class="w123ar"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv(GREEN) + '">' + I('user') + '</span><div><b>' + (owner ? E(owner) + ' picks it up' : 'The team picks it up') + '</b><span>' + (owner ? 'They get a Slack message too' : 'A team lead gets a Slack message and can hand it to someone') + '</span></div></div>'
      + '<div class="flow"><h4>How it moves</h4><ol class="w123vsteps">' + st.map(function (s) { return '<li>' + E(cap(s)) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol></div>'
      + '<p class="fine">You get a Slack message when it is done and when someone adds a note. Everything is in <a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#requests/mine">My requests</a>.</p></aside>';
  }
  function sentHtml(r) {
    var ch = chanFor(r.department, typeOf(r.type_key));
    return '<div class="w123card w123done w123anim"><span class="ok">' + I('check') + '</span><h2>Sent to ' + E(teamLabel(r.department)) + '</h2>'
      + '<p>It is in their team inbox now' + (r.priority === 'urgent' ? ', marked urgent' : '') + '. Follow it in My requests; you get a Slack message when it is done.</p>'
      + '<div id="w123sl" class="sl">' + I('hash') + (ch ? 'Posting in #' + E(ch) + '\u2026' : 'No Slack channel is set for this team yet') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w123acts"><a class="w123btn" data-w123go="#requests/r/' + E(r.id) + '">Track it</a><a class="w123btn ghost" data-w123go="#requests/new/' + r.department + '">Another for ' + E(teamShort(r.department)) + '</a><a class="w123btn ghost" data-w123go="#requests/new">Something for another team</a></div></div>';
  }
  var sentWatch = null;
  function watchSent(r) {
    if (!r || !(r.id > 0) || sentWatch === r.id) { if (r && r.slack) slackLine(r); return; }
    sentWatch = r.id;
    var tries = 0;
    (function poll() {
      tries++;
      setTimeout(async function () {
        if (!R.nw.sent || R.nw.sent.id !== r.id) return;
        var res = await q(db.from('dept_requests').select('id, slack').eq('id', r.id).maybeSingle());
        if (res.data && res.data.slack) { r.slack = res.data.slack; var loc = R.reqs.filter(function (x) { return x.id === r.id; })[0]; if (loc) loc.slack = res.data.slack; slackLine(r); return; }
        if (tries < 5) poll(); else slackLine(r);
      }, tries === 1 ? 2500 : 3500);
    })();
  }
  function slackLine(r) {
    var el = document.getElementById('w123sl'); if (!el) return;
    var s = r.slack || {};
    if (s.ts) { el.className = 'sl'; el.innerHTML = I('check') + 'Posted in #' + E(s.channel) + (s.url ? ' &middot; <a class="w123lnk" href="' + E(s.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">open in Slack</a>' : ''); }
    else if (s.error) { el.className = 'sl bad'; el.innerHTML = I('alert') + 'Not posted in Slack: ' + E(clip(s.error, 140)) + '. The team still has it in their inbox.'; }
    else if (s.skipped) { el.className = 'sl'; el.innerHTML = I('hash') + E(cap(s.skipped)); }
    else { el.className = 'sl'; el.innerHTML = I('clock') + 'In the team inbox; the Slack post is on its way.'; }
  }

  /* ---------- rows ---------- */
  function reqRow(r, ctx) {
    var d = r.department, need = neededOf(r), dn = need ? daysUntil(need) : null, late = !r.done && dn !== null && dn < 0;
    var meta = [];
    if (ctx !== 'team' && ctx !== 'card') meta.push(teamChip(d));
    meta.push(E(typeLabel(r)));
    if (ctx !== 'mine') meta.push('from ' + E(whoAsked(r)));
    meta.push(E(ago(r.created_at)));
    if (need && !r.done) meta.push('<span class="' + (late ? 'late' : '') + '">' + E(dueText(need)) + '</span>');
    if (r.done && r.done_at) meta.push('done ' + E(ago(r.done_at)));
    var right = '<span class="w123pill ' + pillCls(r) + '">' + E(statusText(r)) + '</span>'
      + (r.priority === 'urgent' && !r.done ? '<span class="w123pill urg">' + I('bolt') + 'Urgent</span>' : '')
      + (r.assignee ? avatar(r.assignee, 22) : '');
    return '<div class="w123row" data-w123r="' + E(r.id) + '" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="' + E(r.title) + '"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '">' + I(typeIcon(r.type_key)) + '</span>'
      + '<div style="min-width:0"><div class="t">' + E(r.title) + '</div><div class="m">' + meta.join('<span class="dot"></span>') + '</div>'
      + (ctx === 'mine' && !r.done ? '<div class="bar" title="' + E(statusText(r)) + '"><i style="width:' + pct(r) + '%"></i></div>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="r">' + right + '</div></div>';
  }
  function jevPill(m) {
    var n = m.notify || null;
    if (!n) return '';
    /* wave170: an info@ email, or one sent on from someone's inbox: passed on with what to do */
    if ((n.by === 'info' || n.by === 'forward') && n.decision === 'notify') return '<span class="w123pill jev" title="Passed on on Slack, with what to do">' + I('hash') + (n.dm ? 'Sent to ' + E(String(n.dm).split(/[\s@]/)[0]) : n.channel ? E(n.channel) : 'Passed on') + '</span>';
    if (n.by === 'info' && n.decision === 'file') return '<span class="w123pill quiet" title="' + E((n.on_thread || 'Someone on the team') + ' is already on the email') + '">Filed quietly</span>';
    if (n.decision === 'notify') return '<span class="w123pill jev" title="' + E(n.by === 'setting' ? 'The team setting says to always ping' : 'Jev decided the team needed to hear about it') + '">' + I('hash') + (n.channel ? E(n.channel) : 'Team pinged') + '</span>';
    return '<span class="w123pill quiet" title="' + E(n.by === 'setting' ? 'The team setting says never to ping' : 'Jev decided finding it here was enough') + '">Filed quietly</span>';
  }
  function mailRow(m, ctx) {
    var k = MAIL_KIND[m.kind] || MAIL_KIND.update, n = m.notify || {};
    var meta = [];
    if (ctx !== 'team' && ctx !== 'card') meta.push(teamChip(m.department));
    meta.push(E(k.l));
    meta.push('from ' + E(m.from_name || m.from_email || 'someone'));
    meta.push(E(ago(m.internal_date)));
    if (m.amount != null) meta.push('$' + E(Number(m.amount).toLocaleString('en-CA', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })));
    if (m.due_date && mailOpen(m)) { var dn = daysUntil(m.due_date); meta.push('<span class="' + (dn !== null && dn < 0 ? 'late' : '') + '">' + E(dn !== null && dn < 0 ? 'was due ' + fmtDay(m.due_date) : 'due ' + fmtDay(m.due_date)) + '</span>'); }
    var right = jevPill(m)
      + (typeof n.ask === 'number' && n.ask >= 0.6 && !m.request_id && mailOpen(m) ? '<span class="w123pill new">Looks like a request</span>' : '')
      + (m.request_id ? '<span class="w123pill prog">Tracked as a request</span>' : (!mailOpen(m) ? '<span class="w123pill done">Handled</span>' : ''))
      + (m.assignee ? avatar(m.assignee, 22) : '');
    return '<div class="w123row" data-w123m="' + E(m.id) + '" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="' + E(m.title || m.subject || 'Email') + '"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv(k.c) + '">' + I(k.icon) + '</span>'
      + '<div style="min-width:0"><div class="t">' + E(m.title || m.subject || 'Email') + '</div><div class="m">' + meta.join('<span class="dot"></span>') + '</div>'
      + (m.ask && ctx !== 'card' ? '<div class="s"><b>To do:</b> ' + E(m.ask) + (m.mailbox === 'info' ? ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">(info@)</span>' : m.forwarded_by ? ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">(from ' + E(m.forwarded_by) + ')</span>' : '') + '</div>' : (m.summary && ctx !== 'card' ? '<div class="s">' + E(m.summary) + '</div>' : '')) + '</div><div class="r">' + right + '</div></div>';   /* wave170: the ask first */
  }
  function sugRow(s, ctx) {
    return '<div class="w123row sug" data-w123s="' + E(s.id) + '"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv(GOLD) + '">' + I('spark') + '</span><div style="min-width:0"><div class="t">' + E(s.title) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="m">' + (ctx !== 'team' && ctx !== 'card' ? teamChip(s.department) + '<span class="dot"></span>' : '') + '<span>' + (s.kind === 'refine' ? 'An update to an open request' : 'Spotted in a meeting') + '</span>' + (s.confidence ? '<span class="dot"></span><span>confidence ' + E(s.confidence) + '/10</span>' : '') + (s.assignee ? '<span class="dot"></span><span>for ' + E(s.assignee) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (s.quote && ctx !== 'card' ? '<div class="q">&ldquo;' + E(clip(s.quote, 240)) + '&rdquo;</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="acts"><button type="button" class="w123btn sm" data-w123sacc="' + E(s.id) + '">' + (s.kind === 'refine' ? 'Add it to the request' : 'Accept and file it') + '</button><button type="button" class="w123btn sm ghost" data-w123sx="' + E(s.id) + '">Dismiss</button>' + (s.transcript_id ? '<a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#tx/' + E(s.transcript_id) + '" style="font-size:12.5px;align-self:center;margin-left:4px">Open the meeting</a>' : '') + '</div></div><div class="r"></div></div>';
  }
  function itemRow(it, ctx) { return it.k === 'r' ? reqRow(it.x, ctx) : it.k === 'm' ? mailRow(it.x, ctx) : sugRow(it.x, ctx); }
  function sugsLanding() {
    if (!R.sugs.length) return '';
    var list = R.sugs.slice(0, 4);
    return '<div class="w123sec"><h3>Spotted in meetings</h3><span class="c">' + R.sugs.length + ' ask' + (R.sugs.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' the Hub heard; nothing is filed until someone accepts it</span></div><div class="w123list">' + list.map(function (s) { return sugRow(s, 'all'); }).join('') + '</div>'
      + (R.sugs.length > list.length ? '<p class="w123sub">The rest wait in each team&rsquo;s inbox.</p>' : '');
  }

  /* ---------- my requests ---------- */
  function sortOpen(a, b) {
    var ha = urgentOrLate(a), hb = urgentOrLate(b); if (ha !== hb) return ha ? -1 : 1;
    var na = neededOf(a), nb = neededOf(b); if (na && nb && na !== nb) return na < nb ? -1 : 1; if (na && !nb) return -1; if (nb && !na) return 1;
    return String(b.updated_at || b.created_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.updated_at || a.created_at || ''));
  }
  function mineHtml() {
    var mine = R.reqs.filter(isMine);
    if (!mine.length) return '<div class="w123card w123anim"><h2 class="w123h">Nothing asked yet</h2><p class="w123sub">When you ask another team for something, it shows here with where it is at. You get a Slack message when it is done.</p><div class="w123acts"><a class="w123btn" data-w123go="#requests/new">' + I('plus') + 'New request</a></div></div>';
    var open = mine.filter(function (r) { return !r.done; }).sort(sortOpen);
    var done = mine.filter(function (r) { return r.done; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.done_at || b.updated_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.done_at || a.updated_at || '')); });
    var showAll = R.mineAll;
    return '<div class="w123anim">'
      + '<div class="w123sec" style="margin-top:0"><h3>Open</h3><span class="c">' + open.length + '</span></div>'
      + (open.length ? '<div class="w123list">' + open.map(function (r) { return reqRow(r, 'mine'); }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w123list"><p class="w123empty" style="padding:18px 20px">Nothing open. Everything you asked for is done.</p></div>')
      + (done.length ? '<div class="w123sec"><h3>Done</h3><span class="c">' + done.length + '</span></div><div class="w123list">' + done.slice(0, showAll ? 200 : 6).map(function (r) { return reqRow(r, 'mine'); }).join('') + '</div>' + (done.length > 6 && !showAll ? '<p class="w123sub"><a class="w123lnk" data-w123mineall>Show all ' + done.length + '</a></p>' : '') : '')
      + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the team inbox ---------- */
  function teamHtml() {
    var ds = allowedTeams();
    var d = R.tm.d && ds.indexOf(R.tm.d) >= 0 ? R.tm.d : homeTeam(); R.tm.d = d;
    var items = teamItems(d, R.tm.state);
    var nAll = items.length;
    if (R.tm.kind !== 'all') items = items.filter(function (it) { return it.k === R.tm.kind; });
    if (R.tm.mine) items = items.filter(function (it) { return it.k !== 's' && withMe(it.x); });
    var ch = chanFor(d);
    var chips = ds.map(function (x) { var n = teamItems(x, 'open').length; return '<a class="w123chip' + (x === d ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w123go="#requests/team/' + x + '">' + E(teamShort(x)) + (n ? ' <span class="n">' + n + '</span>' : '') + '</a>'; }).join('');
    var seg = function (key, opts, cur) { return '<span class="w123small">' + opts.map(function (o) { return '<a data-' + key + '="' + o[0] + '" class="' + (cur === o[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(o[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>'; };
    var empty = R.tm.state === 'open'
      ? 'Nothing waiting for <b>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</b>. New requests to the team, emails the Hub inbox files for it, and asks spotted in its meetings land here.'
      : 'Nothing handled in the last while.';
    return '<div class="w123anim"><div class="w123again" style="margin:0 0 14px">' + chips + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w123note">' + I('inbox') + '<div><b>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</b>: ' + (ch ? 'new requests are posted in <b>#' + E(ch) + '</b>. ' : '') + 'Emails the Hub inbox files for the team land here too, and Jev pings the channel only when one needs the team soon.</div></div>'
      + '<div class="w123bar">' + seg('w123tms', [['open', 'Open'], ['done', 'Done and earlier']], R.tm.state) + seg('w123tmk', [['all', 'Everything'], ['r', 'Requests'], ['m', 'Emails'], ['s', 'From meetings']], R.tm.kind)
      + '<label class="w123chip" style="cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" data-w123tmme' + (R.tm.mine ? ' checked' : '') + ' style="margin:0 4px 0 0;accent-color:' + GREEN + '"> With me</label><span class="sp"></span><a class="w123btn sm" data-w123go="#requests/new/' + d + '">' + I('plus') + 'Ask ' + E(teamShort(d)) + '</a></div>'
      + (items.length ? '<div class="w123list">' + items.slice(0, 150).map(function (it) { return itemRow(it, 'team'); }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w123list"><p class="w123empty" style="padding:18px 20px">' + (nAll && (R.tm.kind !== 'all' || R.tm.mine) ? 'Nothing with these filters.' : empty) + '</p></div>')
      + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- all requests (leaders) ---------- */
  function allHtml() {
    if (!isLeader()) return mineHtml();
    var f = R.all, ql = lower(f.q);
    var rows = R.reqs.filter(function (r) {
      if (f.d && r.department !== f.d) return false;
      if (f.state === 'open' && r.done) return false;
      if (f.state === 'done' && !r.done) return false;
      if (ql && lower(r.title + ' ' + typeLabel(r) + ' ' + (r.requested_by || '') + ' ' + (r.assignee || '') + ' ' + (typeof r.details === 'string' ? r.details : JSON.stringify(r.details || {}))).indexOf(ql) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
    rows = f.state === 'open' ? rows.sort(sortOpen) : rows;
    var byTeam = {}; R.reqs.forEach(function (r) { if (!r.done) byTeam[r.department] = (byTeam[r.department] || 0) + 1; });
    return '<div class="w123anim"><div class="w123bar"><select data-w123alld aria-label="Team"><option value="">Every team</option>' + INBOX.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (f.d === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + (byTeam[d] ? ' (' + byTeam[d] + ' open)' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<span class="w123small">' + [['open', 'Open'], ['done', 'Done'], ['any', 'Everything']].map(function (o) { return '<a data-w123alls="' + o[0] + '" class="' + (f.state === o[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + o[1] + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>'
      + '<span class="sp"></span><input type="search" data-w123allq placeholder="Search requests" value="' + E(f.q) + '" aria-label="Search requests"></div>'
      + '<div id="w123allrows">' + allRows(rows) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function allRows(rows) { return rows.length ? '<div class="w123list">' + rows.slice(0, 200).map(function (r) { return reqRow(r, 'all'); }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w123list"><p class="w123empty" style="padding:18px 20px">No requests match.</p></div>'; }

  /* ---------- Slack settings (leaders see, admins change) ---------- */
  async function loadBot() {
    R.botBusy = true;
    try {
      var a = await fx({ action: 'status' });
      if (!a || !a.teams) throw new Error('the Slack check did not answer');
      var b = null; try { b = await fx({ action: 'channels' }); } catch (e) { b = null; }
      R.bot = { teams: a.teams || {}, type_channels: a.type_channels || {}, channels: (b && b.channels) || [] };
      R.botErr = null;
    } catch (e) { R.botErr = String(e.message || e); R.bot = { teams: {}, type_channels: {}, channels: [] }; }
    R.botBusy = false;
    if (R.view === 'settings' && onPage()) paint();
  }
  function settingsHtml() {
    if (!isLeader()) return '<div class="w123card"><p class="w123empty">Slack settings are for leaders and admins.</p></div>';
    var c = R.cfgDraft || (R.cfgDraft = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(R.cfg || cfgOf(null))));
    var can = isAdmin(), bot = R.bot;
    var dis = can ? '' : ' disabled';
    var rows = INBOX.map(function (d) {
      var st = bot && bot.teams && bot.teams[d];
      var stat = !bot ? '<span class="op" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">Checking Slack\u2026</span>'
        : st && st.channel ? '<span class="ok">' + I('check') + ' Posting in #' + E(st.channel) + (st.chain && st.chain[0] !== st.channel ? ' (the first choice is not reachable)' : '') + '</span>'
        : '<span class="no">' + I('alert') + ' ' + E(((st && st.tried) || []).map(function (t) { return '#' + t.name + ': ' + t.state; }).join('; ') || 'No channel set') + '</span>';
      var mode = c.email_mode[d] || 'jev';
      return '<div class="w123set"><div><span class="w123tm" style="' + cv(teamColor(d)) + '"><i></i>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</span></div>'
        + '<div><input type="text" list="w123chl" data-w123cfgch="' + d + '" value="' + E((c.channels[d] || []).join(', ')) + '" placeholder="channel-name, fallback"' + dis + ' aria-label="Slack channel for ' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '">' + stat + '</div>'
        + '<div><select data-w123cfgem="' + d + '"' + dis + ' aria-label="Emails for ' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '"><option value="jev"' + (mode === 'jev' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Emails: Jev decides</option><option value="always"' + (mode === 'always' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Emails: always ping</option><option value="never"' + (mode === 'never' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Emails: never ping</option></select></div>'
        + '<div><button type="button" class="w123btn sm ghost" data-w123test="' + d + '">Send a test</button></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    var sw = function (key, title, sub) { return '<label class="w123sw"><input type="checkbox" data-w123cfg="' + key + '"' + (c[key] ? ' checked' : '') + dis + '><div><b>' + title + '</b><span>' + sub + '</span></div></label>'; };
    var over = R.types.filter(function (t) { return t.channel; });
    return '<div class="w123anim"><div class="w123card"><h2 class="w123h">Slack and the team inbox</h2><p class="w123sub">Where each team&rsquo;s requests are posted, and what happens to the emails the Hub inbox files for them. Channels are tried in order: if the Hub bot is not in the first one, the next one is used. To use a private channel, add the Hub app to it in Slack (channel settings, Integrations).' + (can ? '' : ' Only admins can change these.') + '</p>'
      + (R.botErr ? '<div class="w123note warn" style="margin-top:14px">' + I('alert') + '<div><b>The Slack check did not answer</b>: ' + E(R.botErr) + '</div></div>' : '')
      + '<div style="margin-top:14px">' + rows + '</div>'
      + '<datalist id="w123chl">' + ((bot && bot.channels) || []).map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + E(x.name) + '">' + (x.member ? 'bot is in it' : 'public, the bot joins') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</datalist>'
      + '<div style="margin-top:12px">' + sw('post_requests', 'Post every new request in the team&rsquo;s channel', 'With the details, who asked and when it is needed. Time off is posted without the details.')
      + sw('thread_updates', 'Reply in the request&rsquo;s thread', 'When the status changes, it is handed to someone, it is done, or someone adds a note.')
      + sw('dm_requester', 'Message the requester', 'When their request is done, and when someone adds a note to it.') + '</div>'
      + (over.length ? '<div class="w123sec"><h3>Request types with their own channel</h3></div>' + over.map(function (t) { var s2 = bot && bot.type_channels && bot.type_channels[t.key]; return '<div class="w123ar" style="margin:6px 0"><span class="w123ico" style="' + cv(teamColor(t.department)) + '">' + I(typeIcon(t.key)) + '</span><div><b>' + E(t.label) + ' &rarr; #' + E(t.channel) + '</b><span>' + (s2 ? (s2.ok ? 'The bot can post there' : 'The bot cannot post there yet; the team channel is used') : '') + '</span></div></div>'; }).join('') : '')
      + (can ? '<div class="w123acts"><button type="button" class="w123btn" data-w123save>Save</button><button type="button" class="w123btn ghost" data-w123cfgreset>Discard changes</button></div>' : '')
      + '</div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the sheet: one request or one email ---------- */
  var lastFocus = null;
  function sheetEl() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w123sheet');
    if (!s) {
      s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w123sheet';
      s.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w123close></div><div class="pane" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Details"><div class="in"></div></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(s);
    }
    return s;
  }
  function findReq(id) { return R.reqs.filter(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function findMail(id) { return R.mail.filter(function (m) { return String(m.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  async function openSheet(k, id, inPlace) {
    if (!R.sheet) lastFocus = document.activeElement;
    R.sheet = { k: k, id: String(id), inPlace: !!inPlace, busy: true };
    var s = sheetEl(); s.classList.add('on');
    try { document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'hidden'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    renderSheet();
    if (!R.loaded) await load();
    if (k === 'r' && !findReq(id)) { var f = await q(db.from('dept_requests').select('*').eq('id', id).maybeSingle()); if (f.data) { R.reqs.push(f.data); bump(); } }
    if (k === 'm' && !findMail(id)) { var g = await q(db.from('hub_mail').select(MAILCOLS).eq('id', id).maybeSingle()); if (g.data) { R.mail.push(g.data); bump(); } }
    await loadThread(k, id);
    if (!R.sheet || R.sheet.id !== String(id)) return;
    R.sheet.busy = false;
    renderSheet();
    setTimeout(function () { var x = document.querySelector('#w123sheet .x'); if (x) { try { x.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }, 30);
  }
  function closeSheet(fromRoute) {
    var s = document.getElementById('w123sheet');
    var was = R.sheet; R.sheet = null;
    if (s) { s.classList.remove('on'); var inn = s.querySelector('.in'); if (inn) inn.innerHTML = ''; }
    try { document.documentElement.style.overflow = ''; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (!fromRoute && was && !was.inPlace) {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '');
      if (/^#requests\/(r|m)\//.test(h)) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', baseHash()); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } if (onPage()) paint(); }
    }
    if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus) { try { lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    lastFocus = null;
    injectCard(true);
  }
  function baseHash() {
    if (R.view === 'mine') return '#requests/mine';
    if (R.view === 'team') return '#requests/team/' + (R.tm.d || homeTeam());
    if (R.view === 'all') return '#requests/all';
    if (R.view === 'settings') return '#requests/settings';
    if (R.nw.sent) return '#requests/new';
    return '#requests/new' + (R.nw.d ? '/' + R.nw.d + (R.nw.type ? '/' + R.nw.type : '') : '');
  }
  function renderSheet() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w123sheet'); if (!s || !R.sheet) return;
    var inn = s.querySelector('.in'); if (!inn) return;
    var keep = null, ta = inn.querySelector('textarea[data-w123note]');
    if (ta && ta.value) keep = ta.value;
    var html;
    if (R.sheet.k === 'r') { var r = findReq(R.sheet.id); html = r ? sheetReq(r) : (R.sheet.busy ? loadingSheet() : missingSheet('That request is not in the Hub any more.')); }
    else { var m = findMail(R.sheet.id); html = m ? sheetMail(m) : (R.sheet.busy ? loadingSheet() : missingSheet('That email is not in the team inbox. Leaders can find every email on the Hub inbox page.')); }
    inn.innerHTML = html;
    if (keep) { var t2 = inn.querySelector('textarea[data-w123note]'); if (t2) t2.value = keep; }
  }
  function closeBtn() { return '<button type="button" class="x" data-w123close aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button>'; }
  function loadingSheet() { return '<div class="top"><span class="sp"></span>' + closeBtn() + '</div><p class="w123empty">Loading\u2026</p>'; }
  function missingSheet(t) { return '<div class="top"><span class="sp"></span>' + closeBtn() + '</div><p class="w123empty">' + E(t) + '</p>'; }
  function linkify(v) {
    var s = String(v == null ? '' : v);
    if (/^https?:\/\/\S+$/i.test(s.trim())) return '<a class="w123lnk" href="' + E(s.trim()) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(clip(s.trim(), 70)) + '</a>';
    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s.trim())) return E(fmtLong(s.trim()));
    return E(s).replace(/(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g, function (u) { return '<a class="w123lnk" href="' + u + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + clip(u, 60) + '</a>'; });
  }
  function detailsHtml(det) {
    if (det == null || det === '' || (typeof det === 'object' && !Array.isArray(det) && !Object.keys(det).length)) return '<p class="w123sub" style="margin:0">No details were given.</p>';
    if (typeof det === 'string') return '<div class="prose">' + linkify(det) + '</div>';
    if (Array.isArray(det)) return '<div class="prose">' + linkify(det.join('\n')) + '</div>';
    return '<dl>' + Object.keys(det).map(function (k) { return '<dt>' + E(k) + '</dt><dd>' + linkify(det[k]) + '</dd>'; }).join('') + '</dl>';
  }
  function assigneeOptions(d, cur) {
    var team = teamMembers(d).map(function (m) { return m.name; });
    var rest = members().filter(function (m) { return m.department !== d && m.active !== false; }).map(function (m) { return m.name; }).sort();
    var has = function (n) { return lower(n) === lower(cur); };
    var o = '<option value="">Nobody yet</option>';
    if (team.length) o += '<optgroup label="' + E(teamShort(d)) + '">' + team.map(function (n) { return '<option value="' + E(n) + '"' + (has(n) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(n) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    if (rest.length) o += '<optgroup label="Everyone else">' + rest.map(function (n) { return '<option value="' + E(n) + '"' + (has(n) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(n) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    if (cur && !team.concat(rest).some(has)) o += '<option value="' + E(cur) + '" selected>' + E(cur) + '</option>';
    return o;
  }
  function slackBlock(r) {
    var s = r.slack || {};
    if (s.ts) return '<div class="w123slk"><span class="w123ico">' + I('hash') + '</span><div>Posted in <b>#' + E(s.channel) + '</b> ' + E(ago(s.at)) + (s.url ? ' &middot; <a class="w123lnk" href="' + E(s.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">open the thread in Slack</a>' : '') + '<br><span class="w123sub" style="font-size:12.5px;margin:0">Status changes, hand-offs and notes are added to the thread.</span></div></div>';
    var old = r.created_at && String(r.created_at) < JEV_SINCE;
    var txt = s.error ? 'Not posted: ' + clip(s.error, 180) : s.skipped ? cap(s.skipped) : old ? 'Filed before requests were posted to Slack.' : (asDate(r.created_at) && now() - asDate(r.created_at).getTime() < 120000 ? 'Posting in Slack\u2026' : 'Not posted in Slack.');
    return '<div class="w123slk' + (s.error ? ' bad' : '') + '"><span class="w123ico">' + I(s.error ? 'alert' : 'hash') + '</span><div>' + E(txt) + (r.id > 0 && staffOk() && !r.done ? ' <a class="w123lnk" data-w123repost="' + E(r.id) + '">Post it now</a>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  var LOGTXT = {
    status: function (l) { return 'Status: <b>' + E(cap(l.new_value)) + '</b>'; },
    done: function (l) { return String(l.new_value) === 'true' ? '<b>Marked done</b>' : '<b>Reopened</b>'; },
    assignee: function (l) { return l.new_value ? 'Handed to <b>' + E(l.new_value) + '</b>' : 'Unassigned'; },
    refined: function (l) { return 'Updated from a meeting: ' + E(clip(l.new_value, 120)); },
    slack: function (l) { return 'Slack: ' + E(clip(l.new_value, 140)); }
  };
  function timelineHtml(r) {
    var ev = [];
    ev.push({ at: r.created_at, first: 1, h: '<b>' + E(whoAsked(r)) + '</b> asked ' + E(teamLabel(r.department)) + (r.source === 'email' ? ' (from an email)' : r.source === 'meeting' ? ' (from a meeting)' : '') });
    (R.logs['r' + r.id] || []).forEach(function (l) { var f = LOGTXT[l.field]; if (!f) return; ev.push({ at: l.changed_at, h: f(l) + (l.changed_by && l.field !== 'slack' ? ' <span class="w">by ' + E(window.waWho ? waWho(l.changed_by) : l.changed_by) + '</span>' : '') }); });
    (R.notes[r.id] || []).forEach(function (n) { ev.push({ at: n.created_at, note: true, h: '<b>' + E(n.author) + '</b> <span class="w">added a note</span><div class="bd">' + (window.w138Mark ? window.w138Mark(linkify(n.body)) : linkify(n.body)) + '</div>' }); });
    ev.sort(function (a, b) { if (a.first || b.first) return a.first ? -1 : 1; return String(a.at || '').localeCompare(String(b.at || '')); });
    return '<ol class="w123tl">' + ev.map(function (e) { return '<li' + (e.note ? ' class="note"' : '') + '>' + e.h + ' <span class="w">' + E(fmtWhen(e.at)) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>';
  }
  function sheetReq(r) {
    var d = r.department, s = statusesOf(r), cur = stepOf(r), need = neededOf(r), st = staffOk();
    var late = need && !r.done && daysUntil(need) < 0;
    var out = '<div class="top">' + teamChip(d) + '<span class="w123pill">' + I(typeIcon(r.type_key)) + E(typeLabel(r)) + '</span>' + (r.priority === 'urgent' && !r.done ? '<span class="w123pill urg">' + I('bolt') + 'Urgent</span>' : '') + (r.done ? '<span class="w123pill done">' + I('check') + 'Done</span>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>' + closeBtn() + '</div>'
      + '<h2>' + E(r.title) + '</h2>'
      + '<div class="meta"><span>Asked by <b>' + E(whoAsked(r)) + '</b></span><span>' + E(fmtWhen(r.created_at)) + '</span>' + (need ? '<span class="' + (late ? 'late' : '') + '">' + E(r.done ? 'was needed by ' + fmtLong(need) : dueText(need)) + '</span>' : '') + (r.assignee ? '<span>With <b>' + E(r.assignee) + '</b></span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w123steps" role="list">' + s.map(function (x, i) { var cls = (r.done || i < cur) ? 'past' : (i === cur ? 'cur' : ''); return '<div class="st ' + cls + '" role="listitem"' + (st ? ' data-w123setst="' + E(x) + '" title="Set the status to ' + E(cap(x)) + '"' : '') + '><span class="c">' + (cls === 'past' ? I('check') : '') + '</span><span>' + E(cap(x)) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (st) out += '<div class="ctl"><label>Status <select data-w123st aria-label="Status">' + s.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + E(x) + '"' + (lower(x) === lower(r.status) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(cap(x)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>'
      + '<label>With <select data-w123as aria-label="Who has it">' + assigneeOptions(d, r.assignee) + '</select></label><span class="sp"></span>'
      + (r.done ? '<button type="button" class="w123btn sm ghost" data-w123reopen>' + I('undo') + 'Reopen</button>' : '<button type="button" class="w123btn sm" data-w123done>' + I('check') + 'Mark done</button>') + '</div>';
    out += '<div class="blk"><h4>What they asked for</h4>' + detailsHtml(r.details) + '</div>';
    if (r.mail_id) out += '<div class="blk"><h4>From the Hub inbox</h4><a class="w123lnk" data-w123openm="' + E(r.mail_id) + '">Open the email it came from</a></div>';
    out += '<div class="blk"><h4>Slack</h4>' + slackBlock(r) + '</div>';
    out += '<div class="blk"><h4>Activity</h4>' + timelineHtml(r)
      + (st ? '<div class="w123compose"><textarea data-w123note rows="2" placeholder="Add a note for the team and the requester. It goes into the Slack thread too." aria-label="Add a note"></textarea><button type="button" class="w123btn" data-w123addnote>Add note</button></div>' : '') + '</div>';
    return out;
  }
  function filedHtml(m) {
    var f = m.filed || {};
    if (f.type === 'task') return 'Filed as a Finance task. <a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#task/' + E(f.id) + '">Open the task</a>';
    if (f.type === 'transcript') return 'Sent through the meeting pipeline: its tasks, updates and decisions are on the boards. <a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#tx/' + E(f.id) + '">Open the meeting</a>';
    if (f.type === 'candidate') return 'Added to Recruitment in the HR centre.' + (isLeader() ? ' <a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#hrcentre/recruitment">Open Recruitment</a>' : '');
    if (f.type === 'update') return 'Filed as an update on the team page. <a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#team/' + E(m.department) + '">Open the team page</a>';
    if (f.type === 'forward') return 'Sent to the team by ' + E(f.by || m.forwarded_by || 'a colleague') + ' from their inbox.';   /* wave170 */
    if (f.type === 'info') return f.on_thread ? 'Sent to info@. ' + E(f.on_thread) + ' is already on the email, so it was filed here without a ping.' : 'Sent to info@ and passed on to ' + E(m.assignee || teamLabel(m.department)) + ' with what to do. Nothing was emailed.';   /* wave170 */
    return 'Filed by the Hub inbox.';
  }
  function jevBlock(m) {
    var n = m.notify, team = teamLabel(m.department);
    if (!n) {
      var old = String(m.internal_date || '') < JEV_SINCE;
      return '<div class="w123jevbox"><span class="w123ico">' + I('spark') + '</span><div><b>' + (old ? 'Filed before Jev started deciding on team pings' : 'Waiting for Jev') + '</b><br><span class="w123sub" style="margin:0;font-size:13px">' + (old ? 'Emails the Hub inbox files from now on get Jev&rsquo;s call.' : 'Jev decides whether ' + E(team) + ' hears about it now. It takes a minute after the email is filed.') + '</span></div></div>';
    }
    var p = typeof n.p === 'number' ? Math.round(n.p * 100) : null;
    var why = n.why ? WHY[n.why] || n.why : null;
    var head, sub;
    if (n.by === 'setting') { head = n.decision === 'notify' ? 'The team is always pinged' : 'The team is never pinged'; sub = 'That is the team&rsquo;s setting in Slack settings.'; }
    else if ((n.by === 'forward' || n.by === 'info') && n.decision === 'file') {   /* wave170: someone on the team is already on the email */
      head = 'Filed quietly: ' + E(n.on_thread || 'someone on the team') + ' is already on the email'; sub = 'Emails to info@ are passed on with what to do, unless someone on the team is already on the thread.';
    }
    else if (n.by === 'forward' || n.by === 'info') {   /* wave170: sent on by a person, or an info@ email: always passed on */
      head = n.dm ? 'Sent to ' + E(n.dm) + ' on Slack' : (n.channel ? 'Posted in #' + E(n.channel) : 'Passed on to ' + E(team));
      sub = (n.by === 'info' ? 'Emails to info@ are always passed on, with what to do. ' : 'Sent on from their inbox, with what to do. ') + (n.url ? '<a class="w123lnk" href="' + E(n.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open it in Slack</a>' : '');
    }
    else if (n.decision === 'notify') { head = 'Jev pinged ' + E(team) + (n.channel ? ' in #' + E(n.channel) : ''); sub = (why ? 'Because ' + E(why) + '. ' : '') + (n.url ? '<a class="w123lnk" href="' + E(n.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open it in Slack</a>' : ''); }
    else { head = 'Jev filed it quietly'; sub = (why ? 'It looked like ' + E(why === 'someone needs to act' ? 'something to act on, but not urgent' : why) + '. ' : '') + 'No Slack message: finding it here is enough.'; }
    if (n.by === 'rule') sub += ' Jev did not answer, so the Hub&rsquo;s rule decided (bills, applicants and anything due within ten days get a ping).';
    if (n.error) sub += ' <span style="color:' + FLAG + '">Slack: ' + E(clip(n.error, 140)) + '</span>';
    return '<div class="w123jevbox"><span class="w123ico">' + I('spark') + '</span><div><b>' + head + '</b><br><span style="font-size:13px">' + sub + '</span>'
      + (p !== null ? '<div class="pbar" title="How sure Jev was that the team needed a ping"><i style="width:' + p + '%"></i></div><span style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted)">How sure Jev was the team needed a ping: ' + p + '%</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function mayReadBody(m) {
    if (m.kind === 'application' || m.department === 'hr') { if (isLeader()) return true; return me().dept === 'hr'; }
    return true;
  }
  function sheetMail(m) {
    var k = MAIL_KIND[m.kind] || MAIL_KIND.update, n = m.notify || {}, st = staffOk(), open = mailOpen(m);
    var out = '<div class="top">' + teamChip(m.department) + '<span class="w123pill" style="' + cv(k.c) + ';background:var(--cb);color:var(--c)">' + I(k.icon) + E(k.l) + '</span>' + (!open ? '<span class="w123pill done">' + I('check') + (m.team_state === 'request' ? 'Tracked as a request' : 'Handled') + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>' + closeBtn() + '</div>'
      + '<h2>' + E(m.title || m.subject || 'Email') + '</h2>'
      + '<div class="meta"><span>From <b>' + E(m.from_name || m.from_email || 'someone') + '</b>' + (m.from_name && m.from_email ? ' &lt;' + E(m.from_email) + '&gt;' : '') + '</span><span>' + E(fmtWhen(m.internal_date)) + '</span>'
      + (m.amount != null ? '<span><b>$' + E(Number(m.amount).toLocaleString('en-CA', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })) + '</b></span>' : '') + (m.due_date ? '<span class="' + (daysUntil(m.due_date) < 0 && open ? 'late' : '') + '">due ' + E(fmtLong(m.due_date)) + '</span>' : '') + (m.assignee ? '<span>With <b>' + E(m.assignee) + '</b></span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (m.ask ? '<div class="blk"><h4>What to do</h4><div class="prose" style="white-space:normal"><b>' + E(m.ask) + '</b>' + (m.forwarded_by ? '<br><span class="w123sub" style="margin:0">From ' + E(m.forwarded_by) + ', who sent it on from their inbox' + (m.assignee ? ', for ' + E(m.assignee) : '') + '.</span>' : m.mailbox === 'info' ? '<br><span class="w123sub" style="margin:0">Sent to info@; the Hub wrote this line' + ((m.filed || {}).on_thread ? '. ' + E(m.filed.on_thread) + ' is already on the email' : m.assignee ? ' and passed it to ' + E(m.assignee) : '') + '.</span>' : '') + '</div></div>' : '')   /* wave170 */
      + '<div class="blk">' + jevBlock(m) + '</div>';
    if (st) out += '<div class="ctl"><label>With <select data-w123mas aria-label="Who has it">' + assigneeOptions(m.department, m.assignee) + '</select></label><span class="sp"></span>'
      + (m.request_id ? '<a class="w123btn sm ghost" data-w123go="#requests/r/' + E(m.request_id) + '">Open the request</a>' : '<button type="button" class="w123btn sm ghost" data-w123mconv>' + I('inbox') + 'Make it a request</button>')
      + (open ? '<button type="button" class="w123btn sm" data-w123mdone>' + I('check') + 'Mark handled</button>' : '<button type="button" class="w123btn sm ghost" data-w123mopen>' + I('undo') + 'Back to open</button>') + '</div>';
    if (typeof n.ask === 'number' && n.ask >= 0.6 && !m.request_id && open) out += '<div class="w123note" style="margin:14px 0 0">' + I('inbox') + '<div><b>This looks like a request.</b> Make it one to track it to done: it gets statuses, an owner and a Slack thread.</div></div>';
    if (m.summary) out += '<div class="blk"><h4>In short</h4><div class="prose">' + E(m.summary) + '</div></div>';
    out += '<div class="blk"><h4>What the Hub did</h4><div class="prose" style="white-space:normal">' + filedHtml(m) + '</div></div>';
    var atts = Array.isArray(m.attachments) ? m.attachments : [];
    if (atts.length) out += '<div class="blk"><h4>Attachments</h4><div class="prose" style="white-space:normal">' + atts.map(function (a) { return '<span class="w123chip" style="cursor:default">' + I('doc') + E(clip(a.filename || 'file', 40)) + '</span>'; }).join(' ') + (isLeader() ? '<br><a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#hubmail" style="font-size:12.5px">Open them from the Hub inbox page</a>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    if (mayReadBody(m)) {
      var b = R.bodies[m.id];
      out += '<div class="blk"><h4>The email</h4>' + (b === undefined ? '<a class="w123lnk" data-w123mbody="' + E(m.id) + '">Show the email</a>' : b === null ? '<p class="w123sub" style="margin:0">Loading\u2026</p>' : '<div class="prose" style="background:rgba(16,63,57,.035);border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;max-height:420px;overflow:auto">' + linkify(clip(b, 12000)) + '</div>') + '</div>';
    }
    var logs = (R.logs['m' + m.id] || []).filter(function (l) { return l.field !== 'delivery'; });
    if (logs.length) out += '<div class="blk"><h4>Activity</h4><ol class="w123tl">' + logs.map(function (l) { return '<li>' + E(cap(l.field)) + ': ' + E(clip(l.new_value, 160)) + (l.changed_by ? ' <span class="w">by ' + E(window.waWho ? waWho(l.changed_by) : l.changed_by) + '</span>' : '') + ' <span class="w">' + E(fmtWhen(l.changed_at)) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ol></div>';
    return out;
  }
  async function loadBody(id) {
    R.bodies[id] = null; renderSheet();
    var r = await q(db.from('hub_mail').select('body_text, snippet').eq('id', id).maybeSingle());
    R.bodies[id] = (r.data && (r.data.body_text || r.data.snippet)) || 'The email has no text (it may be only attachments).';
    renderSheet();
  }

  /* ---------- the Team inbox card on each department's overview page ---------- */
  function teamRoute() {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, ''); var p = h.split('/');
    if (p[0] !== 'team' || !p[1] || T.order.indexOf(p[1]) < 0) return null;
    if (p[2] && p[2] !== 'overview') return null;
    return p[1];
  }
  function teamCardHtml(d) {
    var ch = chanFor(d), body;
    if (!R.loaded) body = '<p class="w116empty">Loading the inbox\u2026</p>';
    else {
      var items = teamItems(d, 'open');
      var reqs = items.filter(function (it) { return it.k === 'r'; }), mails = items.filter(function (it) { return it.k === 'm'; }), sugs = items.filter(function (it) { return it.k === 's'; });
      var hot = reqs.filter(function (it) { return it.hot; }).length;
      body = '<div class="w123tsum"><span' + (hot ? ' class="hot"' : '') + '><b>' + reqs.length + '</b> open request' + (reqs.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + (hot ? ' (' + hot + ' urgent or late)' : '') + '</span><span><b>' + mails.length + '</b> email' + (mails.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' from the Hub inbox</span>' + (sugs.length ? '<span><b>' + sugs.length + '</b> spotted in meetings</span>' : '') + '</div>'
        + (items.length ? '<div class="w123rows">' + items.slice(0, 5).map(function (it) { return itemRow(it, 'card'); }).join('') + '</div>' + (items.length > 5 ? '<div class="w123more">And ' + (items.length - 5) + ' more in the <a class="w123lnk" data-w123go="#requests/team/' + d + '">team inbox</a>.</div>' : '')
          : '<p class="w116empty">Nothing waiting. Requests to ' + E(teamLabel(d)) + ', emails the Hub inbox files for the team, and asks spotted in its meetings land here.</p>');
    }
    return '<div class="w116card w123tcard" id="w123tcard" data-d="' + d + '" data-v="' + R.ver + '"><h3>Team inbox <span class="lnk" data-w123go="#requests/team/' + d + '">open the inbox</span></h3>' + body
      + '<div class="w116addrow"><a data-w123go="#requests/new/' + d + '">+ Ask ' + E(teamShort(d)) + ' for something</a>' + (ch ? '<span class="w123slkl">' + I('hash') + 'requests post to #' + E(ch) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  var cardBusy = false;
  function injectCard(force) {
    if (cardBusy) return;
    var d = teamRoute(); if (!d || !staffOk()) return;
    var hostEl = document.getElementById('w116host'); if (!hostEl) return;
    var col = hostEl.querySelector('.w116grid > .w116col'); if (!col) return;
    var cur = document.getElementById('w123tcard');
    if (cur && !force && cur.getAttribute('data-d') === d && cur.parentNode === col && col.firstElementChild === cur && cur.getAttribute('data-v') === String(R.ver)) return;
    cardBusy = true;
    try {
      if (cur) cur.remove();
      col.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', teamCardHtml(d));
    } finally { cardBusy = false; }
    if (!R.loaded && !R.loading) load().then(function () { injectCard(true); });
    else if (R.loaded && now() - R.loaded > 60000 && !R.loading) load(true).then(function () { injectCard(true); });
  }
  var cardT = null;
  function watchTeam() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('team'); if (!sec || sec.__w123obs) return;
    sec.__w123obs = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (cardBusy) return; clearTimeout(cardT); cardT = setTimeout(function () { injectCard(false); }, 40); }).observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- the Hub inbox page: Jev's call on each filed email ---------- */
  var HM = { notify: {}, busy: false, at: 0 };
  async function hubmailChips() {
    var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#w116mailhost .w116mr[data-mid]'); if (!rows.length) return;
    if (!HM.busy && now() - HM.at > 30000) {
      HM.busy = true;
      var r = await q(db.from('hub_mail').select('id, notify').not('notify', 'is', null).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(300));
      (r.data || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.notify) HM.notify[String(x.id)] = x.notify; });
      HM.at = now(); HM.busy = false;
    }
    rows = document.querySelectorAll('#w116mailhost .w116mr[data-mid]');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(rows, function (row) {
      var n = HM.notify[row.getAttribute('data-mid')]; if (!n) return;
      var f = row.querySelector('.b .f'); if (!f || f.querySelector('.w123jchip')) return;
      var t = n.decision === 'notify' ? '<span class="w123jchip" title="' + E(n.by === 'setting' ? 'Team setting' : 'Jev decided the team needed to hear about it') + '">' + I('hash') + (n.channel ? 'pinged #' + E(n.channel) : 'team pinged') + '</span>'
        : '<span class="w123jchip quiet" title="' + E(n.by === 'setting' ? 'Team setting' : 'Jev decided finding it in the team inbox was enough') + '">filed quietly</span>';
      f.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', t);
    });
  }
  var hmT = null;
  function watchHubmail() {
    var el = document.getElementById('hubmail'); if (!el || el.__w123obs) return;
    el.__w123obs = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(hmT); hmT = setTimeout(hubmailChips, 250); }).observe(el, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- routing ---------- */
  function parse() {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    if (h !== 'requests' && h.indexOf('requests/') !== 0) return null;
    var p = h.split('/');
    return { v: p[1] || '', a: p[2] || '', b: p[3] || '' };
  }
  function route() {
    var r = parse();
    if (!r) { if (R.sheet && !R.sheet.inPlace) closeSheet(true); return false; }
    if (!document.getElementById('requests') || !staffOk()) return false;
    var act = document.querySelector('.panel.active');
    if (!act || act.id !== 'requests') { try { showPanel('requests'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    mount();
    var v = r.v;
    if (v === '' || v === 'new') {
      R.view = 'new'; R.nw.sent = null;
      R.nw.d = INBOX.indexOf(r.a) >= 0 ? r.a : null;
      R.nw.type = R.nw.d && r.b ? r.b : null;
    } else if (v === 'sent') { R.view = 'new'; }
    else if (v === 'mine') R.view = 'mine';
    else if (v === 'team') { R.view = 'team'; if (INBOX.indexOf(r.a) >= 0) R.tm.d = r.a; }
    else if (v === 'all') R.view = isLeader() ? 'all' : 'mine';
    else if (v === 'settings') { R.view = isLeader() ? 'settings' : 'new'; R.cfgDraft = null; R.bot = null; }
    else if (v !== 'r' && v !== 'm') R.view = 'new';
    var first = !R.shown; R.shown = true;
    var run = function () {
      if (v === 'sent') { var s = findReq(r.a); if (s) R.nw.sent = s; else { R.nw.sent = null; } }
      if (v === 'new' && R.nw.d && R.nw.type && R.nw.type !== 'other') { var t = typeOf(R.nw.type); if (!t || t.department !== R.nw.d) { if (t) R.nw.d = t.department; else R.nw.type = null; } }
      if (v === 'm') { var m = findMail(r.a); if (m && (first || R.view !== 'team')) { R.view = 'team'; R.tm.d = m.department; } }
      if (v === 'r' && first) { var rq = findReq(r.a); if (rq) { if (isMine(rq)) R.view = 'mine'; else if (allowedTeams().indexOf(rq.department) >= 0) { R.view = 'team'; R.tm.d = rq.department; } } }
      if (v === 'r' || v === 'm') { if (!R.sheet || R.sheet.id !== String(r.a) || R.sheet.k !== v) openSheet(v, r.a, false); }
      else if (R.sheet && !R.sheet.inPlace) closeSheet(true);
      paint();
      if (v !== 'r' && v !== 'm') { try { var top = document.getElementById('requests'); if (top && top.getBoundingClientRect().top < -40) top.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    };
    if (R.loaded) { run(); if (now() - R.loaded > 60000) load(true).then(function () { if (onPage()) paint(); injectCard(true); }); }
    else { paint(); load().then(run); }
    return true;
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    try { route(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try { if (teamRoute()) { watchTeam(); setTimeout(function () { injectCard(false); }, 60); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  });
  if (typeof applyHash === 'function') {
    var __ah123 = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () { var r = parse(); if (r && route()) return; return __ah123.apply(this, arguments); };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp123 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      var out = __sp123.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (id === 'requests') setTimeout(function () { if (!parse()) { R.view = R.view || 'new'; } if (mount()) { if (R.loaded) paint(); else { paint(); load().then(paint); } } }, 0);

  /* ---------- the form ---------- */
  function formData(form) {
    var o = {};
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(form.elements, function (el) {
      if (!el.name) return;
      if (el.type === 'checkbox') o[el.name] = el.checked; else o[el.name] = el.value;
    });
    return o;
  }
  function saveDraft(form) { var key = form.getAttribute('data-d') + ':' + form.getAttribute('data-w123form'); R.nw.drafts[key] = formData(form); }
  async function submitForm(form) {
    var d = form.getAttribute('data-d'), key = form.getAttribute('data-w123form');
    var t = key === 'other' ? null : typeOf(key);
    var v = formData(form), fields = fieldsOf(t), missing = [], firstBad = null;
    form.querySelectorAll('.bad').forEach(function (el) { el.classList.remove('bad'); });
    var mark = function (name, label) { var el = form.querySelector('[name="' + name + '"]'); if (el) { el.classList.add('bad'); if (!firstBad) firstBad = el; } missing.push(label); };
    var title = String(v.title || '').trim();
    if (!title) mark('title', 'a title');
    fields.forEach(function (f, i) { if (f.required && !String(v['f' + i] || '').trim()) mark('f' + i, f.label.toLowerCase()); });
    if (!t && !String(v.__what || '').trim()) mark('__what', 'what you need');
    var err = form.querySelector('[data-w123err]');
    if (missing.length) {
      if (err) { err.style.display = ''; err.textContent = 'Fill in ' + missing.join(', ') + '.'; }
      if (firstBad) { try { firstBad.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      return;
    }
    var details = {}, needed = null;
    fields.forEach(function (f, i) {
      var val = String(v['f' + i] || '').trim(); if (!val) return;
      if (f.type === 'url' && !/^[a-z]+:\/\//i.test(val) && /^[\w.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}(\/|$)/i.test(val)) val = 'https://' + val;
      details[f.label] = val;
      if (f.type === 'date' && DATE_KEYS.test(f.name || '') && !needed) needed = val;
    });
    if (!t) details['What is needed'] = String(v.__what || '').trim();
    if (String(v.__more || '').trim()) details['Anything else'] = String(v.__more).trim();
    if (!needed && v.__needed) needed = v.__needed;
    var btn = form.querySelector('[data-w123send]'); if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.innerHTML = 'Sending\u2026'; }
    try {
      var saved = await createRequest({ d: d, type: t, title: title, details: details, needed: needed || null, urgent: !!v.__urgent });
      delete R.nw.drafts[d + ':' + key];
      R.nw.sent = saved; sentWatch = null;
      try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#requests/sent/' + saved.id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      paint();
      injectCard(true);
    } catch (e) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = I('arrow') + 'Send to ' + E(teamShort(d)); }
      if (err) { err.style.display = ''; err.textContent = 'It did not send: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 160) + '. Nothing was lost; try again.'; }
    }
  }
