  };
  function I(n, cls) { return '<svg class="w124i' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || P.spark) + '</svg>'; }
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase().trim(); }
  function clip(s, n) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1).trim() + '\u2026' : s; }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  /* dates built field by field (the offline harness swaps Date for a shim that mishandles 3 to 6 arguments) */
  function mk(y, m, d, h) { var x = new Date(0); x.setFullYear(Number(y), Number(m), Number(d)); x.setHours(h == null ? 12 : Number(h), 0, 0, 0); return x; }
  function pad(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function ymd(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
  function parseDay(s) { if (!s) return null; var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(s)); return m ? mk(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]), 12) : null; }
  function today() { var d = new Date(); return mk(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate(), 12); }
  function addDays(d, n) { var x = new Date(d.getTime()); x.setDate(x.getDate() + n); return x; }
  function monthStart(d) { return mk(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1, 12); }
  function addMonths(d, n) { return mk(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + n, 1, 12); }
  function sameDay(a, b) { return a && b && a.getFullYear() === b.getFullYear() && a.getMonth() === b.getMonth() && a.getDate() === b.getDate(); }
  function fmtTime(d) { return d.toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }).replace(':00', '').replace(/\s?([ap])\.?m\.?/i, function (x, p) { return p.toLowerCase() + 'm'; }); }
  function fmtDate(d, opt) { return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', opt || { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); }
  function money(n) { return '$' + Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('en-CA', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }); }
  function kMoney(n) { n = Number(n || 0); return n >= 1000 ? '$' + (Math.round(n / 100) / 10).toString().replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'K' : money(n); }
  /* wave274b: an anticipated spend, cents only when there are cents ($250, $1,250.50) */
  function spend(n) { n = Number(n || 0); var c = Math.round(n * 100) % 100 !== 0; return '$' + n.toLocaleString('en-CA', { minimumFractionDigits: c ? 2 : 0, maximumFractionDigits: c ? 2 : 0 }); }
  function budgetOf(v) { var b = v ? v.budget : null; if (b === null || b === undefined || b === '') return null; b = Number(b); return isFinite(b) ? b : null; }   /* wave274b */
  function lsGet(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function lsSet(k, v) { try { if (v === null) localStorage.removeItem(k); else localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function initials(n) { var p = String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (!p.length) return '?'; return (p[0][0] + (p.length > 1 ? p[p.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase(); }

  /* ---------- state ---------- */
  var C = {
    view: lsGet('w124view') === 'agenda' ? 'agenda' : 'month', anchor: monthStart(today()),
    layers: (function () { var o = {}; LAYERS.forEach(function (x) { o[x.k] = true; }); try { var s = JSON.parse(lsGet('w124layers') || 'null'); if (s && typeof s === 'object') Object.keys(s).forEach(function (k) { if (k in o) o[k] = !!s[k]; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return o; })(),
    fund: [], goals: null, bdays: [], projects: [], boardMeets: [], loadedBase: 0, loadingBase: null,
    range: {}, meetings: [], posts: [], planned: [], vol: [], rangeKey: '', loadingRange: null, budgetOk: null,   /* wave274b: false once the database said it has no volunteer_events.budget */
    sheet: null, mine: null, idx: {}, scrollOnce: true
  };
  function members() { try { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function me() {
    var email = ''; try { email = lower((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || ''); } catch (e) { email = ''; }
    var name = ''; try { name = (typeof who === 'function') ? String(who() || '') : ''; } catch (e) { name = ''; }
    var m = members().filter(function (x) { return email && lower(x.email) === email; })[0] || null;
    return { email: email, name: name || (m && m.name) || (email ? email.split('@')[0] : ''), dept: m ? m.department : null, id: m ? m.id : null };
  }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function boardOk() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function isLeader() { var r = role(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader'; }
  function canEditFund() { if (!staffOk()) return false; if (isLeader()) return true; return me().dept === 'fundraising'; }
  function memberByEmail(em) { em = lower(em); return members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === em; })[0] || null; }
  function q(p) { return Promise.resolve(p).then(function (r) { return r || { data: null }; }, function (e) { return { data: null, error: e }; }); }

  /* ---------- the always-there data: fundraising events, goals, birthdays ---------- */
  async function loadBase(force) {
    if (C.loadingBase) return C.loadingBase;
    if (C.loadedBase && !force && Date.now() - C.loadedBase < 60000) return;
    C.loadingBase = (async function () {
      var a = await Promise.all([
        q(db.from('fund_events').select('*').order('sort').order('start_date')),
        q(db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'fund_goals').maybeSingle()),
        staffOk() ? q(db.from('hub_birthdays').select('*')) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
        staffOk() ? q(db.from('projects').select('id, name, department, status, parent_id').eq('department', 'fundraising')) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
        staffOk() || boardOk() ? q(db.from('board_meetings').select('id, meets_at, kind, title, status, agenda_url, notes_url, note').order('meets_at')) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
        /* wave168: the Photo library and the website's past fundraisers, for the events' photos */
        staffOk() || boardOk() ? q(db.from('wa_photos').select('id, key, title, alt_text, path_web, path_thumb, focal_x, focal_y, taken_on, series, usage, orientation')) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
        staffOk() || boardOk() ? q(db.from('site_events').select('id, kind, title, starts_at, poster_url, hero_url').eq('kind', 'fundraising').order('starts_at')) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] })
      ]);
      if (a[0].data) C.fund = a[0].data;
      C.goals = (a[1].data && a[1].data.value) || C.goals || { minimum: 10000, healthy: 15000, target: 20000, notes: [], planning: [] };
      if (a[2].data) C.bdays = a[2].data;
      if (a[3].data) C.projects = a[3].data;
      if (Array.isArray(a[4].data)) C.boardMeets = a[4].data.filter(function (m) { return m && m.meets_at && lower(m.status) !== 'cancelled'; });
      if (Array.isArray(a[5].data)) C.photos = a[5].data;
      if (Array.isArray(a[6].data)) C.siteEv = a[6].data;
      C.loadedBase = Date.now();
    })();
    try { await C.loadingBase; } finally { C.loadingBase = null; }
  }
  function hasBoard() { return C.boardMeets.length > 0; }
  function myBirthday() { var em = me().email; return C.bdays.filter(function (b) { return lower(b.email) === em; })[0] || null; }

  /* ---------- the range data: meetings, posts, servings ---------- */
  function rangeFor() {
    var from, to;
    if (C.view === 'month') { var s = monthStart(C.anchor); var dow = (s.getDay() + 6) % 7; from = addDays(s, -dow); to = addDays(from, 42); }
    else { from = monthStart(C.anchor); to = addMonths(C.anchor, 1); }
    var t = today(); var sideTo = addDays(t, 35);
    var f = from < t ? from : t, e = to > sideTo ? to : sideTo;
    return { from: from, to: to, qFrom: mk(f.getFullYear(), f.getMonth(), f.getDate(), 0), qTo: mk(e.getFullYear(), e.getMonth(), e.getDate() + 1, 0) };
  }
  async function loadRange() {
    var r = rangeFor(); var key = ymd(r.qFrom) + '/' + ymd(r.qTo);
    if (C.rangeKey === key) return;
    if (C.loadingRange && C.loadingKey === key) return C.loadingRange;
    C.loadingKey = key;
    C.loadingRange = (async function () {
      var fromIso = r.qFrom.toISOString(), toIso = r.qTo.toISOString();
      var a = await Promise.all([
        q(db.rpc('calendar_my_meetings', { p_from: fromIso, p_to: toIso })),
        staffOk() || boardOk() ? q(db.from('social_posts').select('id, platform, url, kind, caption, published_at, views, likes, comments').gte('published_at', fromIso).lt('published_at', toIso).in('platform', ['instagram', 'facebook', 'linkedin', 'tiktok']).order('published_at').limit(400)) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
        staffOk() || boardOk() ? q(db.from('content_posts').select('id, title, platforms, format, scheduled_at, status, published_at, campaign, owner').gte('scheduled_at', fromIso).lt('scheduled_at', toIso).order('scheduled_at').limit(300)) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
        staffOk() ? volRange(ymd(r.qFrom), ymd(r.qTo)) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] })   /* wave274b: with the anticipated spend */
      ]);
      var meet = a[0].data;
      if (!Array.isArray(meet) || a[0].error) meet = await myMeetingsFallback(fromIso, toIso);
      C.meetings = meet || [];
      C.posts = (a[1].data || []).filter(function (p) { return p.published_at && p.published_at >= fromIso && p.published_at < toIso; });
      C.planned = (a[2].data || []).filter(function (p) { return p.scheduled_at && ['published', 'live', 'cancelled', 'deleted'].indexOf(lower(p.status)) < 0; });
      C.vol = (a[3].data || []).filter(function (v) { return v.event_date && v.event_date >= ymd(r.qFrom) && v.event_date < ymd(r.qTo); });
      C.rangeKey = key;
    })();
    try { await C.loadingRange; } finally { C.loadingRange = null; }
  }
  /* wave274b: the volunteering events with their anticipated spend (volunteer_events.budget, wa274b_budget.sql). Before the
     column exists the database answers 42703 (or PGRST204): ask again without it, and not again in this page load */
  var VOL_COLS = 'id, code, event_date, title, kind, location, status, planned_meals';
  function missingCol(e) { var s = String((e && e.code) || '') + ' ' + String((e && (e.message || e.details)) || e || ''); return /42703|PGRST204|column .*does not exist|could not find the .*column/i.test(s); }
  async function volRange(fromD, toD) {
    var ask = function (cols) { return q(db.from('volunteer_events').select(cols).gte('event_date', fromD).lt('event_date', toD).order('event_date').limit(200)); };
    if (C.budgetOk !== false) {
      var a = await ask(VOL_COLS + ', budget');
      if (!(a.error && missingCol(a.error))) { if (!a.error) C.budgetOk = true; return a; }
      C.budgetOk = false;
    }
    return ask(VOL_COLS);
  }
  /* wave274b: an app changed a volunteering event (the Toronto app's spend, date or status): read the range again on the next draw */
  document.addEventListener('wa:volevents', function () { C.rangeKey = ''; });
  /* before calendar_my_meetings exists (or in the offline harness): the same filter on this side */
  async function myMeetingsFallback(fromIso, toIso) {
    var res = await q(db.from('hub_events').select('*').gte('start_at', fromIso).lt('start_at', toIso).order('start_at').limit(500));
    var em = me().email; if (!em) return [];
    var keys = [em, em.split('@')[0].replace(/\./g, '') + '@' + em.split('@')[1]];
    return (res.data || []).filter(function (e) {
      var st = new Date(e.start_at).getTime(); if (!(st >= new Date(fromIso).getTime() && st < new Date(toIso).getTime())) return false;
      if (lower(e.status) === 'cancelled') return false;
      var att = (e.attendee_emails || []).map(lower);
      return keys.indexOf(lower(e.organizer)) >= 0 || att.some(function (x) { return keys.indexOf(x) >= 0; });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- items ---------- */
  function fundWhen(f) {
    var s = parseDay(f.start_date), e = parseDay(f.end_date);
    if (f.precision === 'day' && s) return e && !sameDay(s, e) ? fmtDate(s, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + ' to ' + fmtDate(e, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }) : fmtDate(s, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
    if (f.precision === 'month' && s) return MONTHS[s.getMonth()] + ' ' + s.getFullYear();
    if (f.precision === 'season' && s) return (f.timing ? f.timing.replace(/\s*\(([^)]*)\)$/, '') : 'Season') + ' ' + s.getFullYear() + (/\(([^)]*)\)$/.test(f.timing || '') ? ' (' + /\(([^)]*)\)$/.exec(f.timing)[1] + ')' : '');
    if (f.precision === 'range' && s && e) { var yrs = s.getFullYear() === e.getFullYear() ? ' ' + e.getFullYear() : ''; return (f.timing ? f.timing + yrs : MONTHS[s.getMonth()].slice(0, 3) + ' ' + (yrs ? '' : s.getFullYear() + ' ') + 'to ' + MONTHS[e.getMonth()].slice(0, 3) + ' ' + e.getFullYear()); }
    return f.timing || (f.precision === 'flexible' ? 'Flexible' : 'Not yet scheduled');
  }
  function items(from, to) {
    var out = [], L = C.layers, t0 = today();
    if (L.fund) C.fund.forEach(function (f) {
      if (f.precision !== 'day' || f.status === 'cancelled') return;
      var s = parseDay(f.start_date); if (!s) return; var e = parseDay(f.end_date) || s;
      for (var d = new Date(s.getTime()); d <= e; d = addDays(d, 1)) if (d >= from && d < to) out.push({ k: 'fund', id: 'f' + f.id, ref: f, day: ymd(d), allDay: true, title: f.title, sub: f.partners || '', past: d < t0 });
    });
    if (L.meet) C.meetings.forEach(function (m) {
      var s = new Date(m.start_at); if (isNaN(s.getTime())) return;
      var local = mk(s.getFullYear(), s.getMonth(), s.getDate(), 12);
      if (local < from || local >= to) return;
      out.push({ k: 'meet', id: 'm' + m.id, ref: m, day: ymd(local), at: s, allDay: !!m.all_day, time: m.all_day ? '' : fmtTime(s), title: m.summary || 'Meeting', sub: m.location || '', past: new Date(m.end_at || m.start_at) < new Date() });
    });
    if (L.post) {
      C.posts.forEach(function (p) { var s = new Date(p.published_at); var local = mk(s.getFullYear(), s.getMonth(), s.getDate(), 12); if (local < from || local >= to) return; out.push({ k: 'post', id: 'p' + p.id, ref: p, day: ymd(local), at: s, time: fmtTime(s), title: (PLATFORM[p.platform] || p.platform) + ': ' + clip(p.caption || 'Post', 60), sub: 'Published', past: true }); });
      C.planned.forEach(function (p) { var s = new Date(p.scheduled_at); var local = mk(s.getFullYear(), s.getMonth(), s.getDate(), 12); if (local < from || local >= to) return; out.push({ k: 'post', id: 'c' + p.id, ref: p, planned: true, day: ymd(local), at: s, time: fmtTime(s), title: p.title || 'Planned post', sub: (p.status ? p.status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.status.slice(1) : 'Planned') + ((p.platforms || []).length ? ' for ' + p.platforms.map(function (x) { return PLATFORM[x] || x; }).join(', ') : ''), past: s < new Date() }); });
    }
    if (L.vol) C.vol.forEach(function (v) {
      var d = parseDay(v.event_date); if (!d || d < from || d >= to) return;
      var kind = lower(v.kind || 'service');
      out.push({ k: 'vol', id: 'v' + v.id, ref: v, day: ymd(d), allDay: true, title: v.title || (kind === 'service' ? (d.getDay() === 0 ? 'Sunday serving' : 'Serving') : kind.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + kind.slice(1)) + (v.location ? ', ' + v.location : ''), sub: v.location || '', cancelled: lower(v.status) === 'cancelled', past: d < t0, budget: budgetOf(v) });   /* wave274b: budget */
    });
    if (L.board && hasBoard()) {
      C.boardMeets.forEach(function (m) {
        var s = new Date(m.meets_at); if (isNaN(s.getTime())) return;
        var local = mk(s.getFullYear(), s.getMonth(), s.getDate(), 12);
        if (local >= from && local < to) out.push({ k: 'board', id: 'bm' + m.id, ref: m, day: ymd(local), at: s, time: fmtTime(s), title: m.title || 'Board meeting', sub: lower(m.status) === 'tentative' ? 'Tentative' : '', past: s < new Date() });
        /* the committees meet the week before a board meeting that is still ahead */
        var due = addDays(local, -7);
        if (local >= t0 && due >= from && due < to) out.push({ k: 'board', id: 'bc' + m.id, ref: m, cm: true, day: ymd(due), allDay: true, title: 'Committee meetings due', sub: 'A week before the ' + (m.title || 'board meeting'), past: due < t0 });
      });
      /* the fiscal year ends on September 30 */
      for (var fy = from.getFullYear(); fy <= to.getFullYear(); fy++) {
        var fe = mk(fy, 8, 30, 12);
        if (fe >= from && fe < to) out.push({ k: 'board', id: 'fy' + fy, fy: fy, day: ymd(fe), allDay: true, title: 'Fiscal year ends', sub: 'FY' + fy + ' closes', past: fe < t0 });
      }
    }
    if (L.bday) C.bdays.forEach(function (b) {
      for (var y = from.getFullYear(); y <= to.getFullYear(); y++) {
        var d = mk(y, b.month - 1, b.day, 12); if (d.getMonth() !== b.month - 1) continue;
        if (d < from || d >= to) continue;
        var m = memberByEmail(b.email); var nm = m ? m.name : b.email.split('@')[0];
        out.push({ k: 'bday', id: 'b' + b.email + y, ref: b, person: m, day: ymd(d), allDay: true, title: nm + '\u2019s birthday', sub: '', past: d < t0, mine: lower(b.email) === me().email });
      }
    });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { if (a.day !== b.day) return a.day < b.day ? -1 : 1; var ra = a.allDay ? 0 : 1, rb = b.allDay ? 0 : 1; if (ra !== rb) return ra - rb; return (a.at ? a.at.getTime() : 0) - (b.at ? b.at.getTime() : 0); });
    return out;
  }
  /* month-long, range and season fundraising events that overlap a window: the band over the grid */
  function bands(from, to) {
    if (!C.layers.fund) return [];
    return C.fund.filter(function (f) {
      if (['month', 'range', 'season'].indexOf(f.precision) < 0 || f.status === 'cancelled') return false;
      var s = parseDay(f.start_date), e = parseDay(f.end_date) || s; if (!s) return false;
      return s < to && e >= from;
    });
  }

  /* ---------- the master calendar (#calendar) ---------- */
  function mast(id, crumb, title, blurb) {
    var sec = document.getElementById(id); if (!sec) return;
    var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w124', id); sec.insertBefore(m, sec.firstChild); }
    var html = '<div class="crumb">' + E(crumb) + '</div><h1>' + E(title) + '</h1><p>' + E(blurb) + '</p>';
    if (m.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { m.innerHTML = html; m.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
  }
  function calHost() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('calendar'); if (!sec) return null;
    /* the board has no Home: the calendar sits in Strategy, without the volunteering and birthday layers */
    var bv = boardOk() && !staffOk();
    var hb = hasBoard();
    mast('calendar', bv ? 'Board' : 'Home', 'Calendar', bv ? (hb ? 'Board meetings, fundraising events, your meetings and marketing posts, in one place.' : 'Fundraising events, your meetings and marketing posts, in one place.') : 'Fundraising events, ' + (hb ? 'board dates, ' : '') + 'your meetings, marketing posts, volunteering and birthdays, in one place.');
    var h = document.getElementById('w124cal');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w124cal'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function chipCounts(list) { var c = {}; list.forEach(function (x) { c[x.k] = (c[x.k] || 0) + 1; }); return c; }
  function evPill(it, compact) {
    var L = LAYER[it.k];
    var amt = it.budget != null ? spend(it.budget) : '';   /* wave274b: the anticipated spend: in full in the tooltip, as a short tag ($750, $1.3K) after the title where it fits (b_css.js) */
    return '<div class="w124ev' + (it.past ? ' past' : '') + (it.cancelled ? ' x' : '') + '" data-w124it="' + E(it.id) + '" style="--c:' + L.c + ';--bg:' + L.bg + ';--ink:' + L.ink + '" title="' + E(it.title + (it.time ? ', ' + it.time : '') + (amt ? ', anticipated spend ' + amt : '')) + '" tabindex="0" role="button">'
      + (it.k === 'bday' ? I('cake') : '') + (it.time && !compact ? '<b>' + E(it.time) + '</b>' : '') + '<span>' + E(it.title) + '</span>' + (amt ? '<em class="w124amt">' + E(kMoney(it.budget)) + '</em>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function monthHtml(list) {
    var s = monthStart(C.anchor), dow = (s.getDay() + 6) % 7, start = addDays(s, -dow), t = today();
    var byDay = {}; list.forEach(function (it) { (byDay[it.day] = byDay[it.day] || []).push(it); });
    var html = '<div class="w124grid">' + WD.map(function (d) { return '<div class="w124dow">' + d + '</div>'; }).join('');
    for (var i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
      var d = addDays(start, i), key = ymd(d), evs = byDay[key] || [];
      var out = d.getMonth() !== s.getMonth();
      if (i === 35 && out) break;
      var shown = evs.slice(0, 3);
      html += '<div class="w124cell' + (out ? ' out' : '') + (sameDay(d, t) ? ' today' : '') + (((i % 7) >= 5) ? ' wk' : '') + '" data-day="' + key + '"><span class="dn">' + d.getDate() + '</span>'
        + shown.map(function (it) { return evPill(it, false); }).join('')
        + (evs.length > 3 ? '<a class="w124more" data-w124day="' + key + '">+' + (evs.length - 3) + ' more</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function rowHtml(it) {
    var L = LAYER[it.k];
    /* wave274b: the anticipated spend on its line; a cancelled event says so, since the month's total leaves it out */
    var mm = [it.sub ? clip(it.sub, 120) : '', it.budget != null && it.cancelled ? 'Cancelled' : '', it.budget != null ? 'Anticipated spend ' + spend(it.budget) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ');
    return '<div class="w124row" data-w124it="' + E(it.id) + '" style="--c:' + L.c + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="tm">' + (it.allDay ? (it.k === 'bday' ? 'Birthday' : 'All day') : E(it.time || '')) + '</span><div style="min-width:0"><div class="tt"><i></i>' + E(it.title) + '</div>' + (mm ? '<div class="mm">' + E(mm) + '</div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function agendaHtml(list) {
    var t = today(), tk = ymd(t), byDay = {}, days = [];
    list.forEach(function (it) { if (!byDay[it.day]) { byDay[it.day] = []; days.push(it.day); } byDay[it.day].push(it); });
    if (!days.length) return '<div class="w124ag"><p class="w124empty" style="padding:18px 20px">Nothing in ' + E(MONTHS[C.anchor.getMonth()]) + ' with these layers on.</p></div>';
    /* this month's agenda always has a today row, so you can see where you are */
    if (!byDay[tk] && C.anchor.getMonth() === t.getMonth() && C.anchor.getFullYear() === t.getFullYear()) { byDay[tk] = []; days.push(tk); days.sort(); }
    return '<div class="w124ag">' + days.map(function (k) {
      var d = parseDay(k);
      return '<div class="w124agd' + (sameDay(d, t) ? ' today' : '') + '" id="w124d' + k + '"><div class="dd"><b>' + d.getDate() + '</b><span>' + E(fmtDate(d, { weekday: 'short' })) + (sameDay(d, t) ? ', today' : '') + '</span></div><div>' + (byDay[k].length ? byDay[k].map(rowHtml).join('') : '<p class="w124empty" style="padding:9px 0">Nothing on today.</p>') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function bandHtml(from, to) {
    var bs = bands(from, to); if (!bs.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w124band"><span class="lb">' + (C.view === 'month' ? 'This month' : 'Also this month') + '</span>' + bs.map(function (f) { return '<a data-w124fund="' + f.id + '">' + I('heart') + E(f.title) + ' <small>' + E(f.precision === 'month' ? 'all ' + MONTHS[parseDay(f.start_date).getMonth()] : (f.timing || '')) + '</small></a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function upNext() {
    var now = new Date(), t = today();
    var list = items(t, addDays(t, 35)).filter(function (it) { if (it.at) return it.at >= now || (it.day === ymd(t) && !it.past); return it.day >= ymd(t); }).slice(0, 7);
    list.forEach(function (it) { C.idx[it.id] = it; });
    if (!list.length) return '<p class="w124empty">Nothing coming up in the next five weeks with these layers on.</p>';
    return '<div class="w124up">' + list.map(function (it) { var d = parseDay(it.day); var copy = Object.assign({}, it); copy.time = (sameDay(d, t) ? 'Today' : sameDay(d, addDays(t, 1)) ? 'Tomorrow' : fmtDate(d, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + (it.time ? ' ' + it.time : ''); copy.allDay = false; return rowHtml(copy); }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function bdaysHtml() {
    var mo = C.anchor.getMonth(), m0 = me();
    var list = C.bdays.filter(function (b) { return b.month - 1 === mo; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.day - b.day; });
    var mine = myBirthday();
    var rows = list.map(function (b) { var m = memberByEmail(b.email); var nm = m ? m.name : b.email.split('@')[0]; var isMe = lower(b.email) === m0.email; return '<div class="w124bd' + (isMe ? ' me' : '') + '"' + (m ? ' data-w124person="' + m.id + '" style="cursor:pointer"' : '') + '><span class="av">' + E(initials(nm)) + '</span><b>' + E(nm) + '</b><span>' + E(MONTHS[b.month - 1].slice(0, 3) + ' ' + b.day) + (isMe ? ', you' : '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="w124card"><h3>Birthdays in ' + E(MONTHS[mo]) + '</h3>' + (rows || '<p class="w124empty">No birthdays this month yet.</p>')
      + (staffOk() ? '<p class="w124empty" style="margin-top:10px">' + (mine ? 'Yours is on ' + E(MONTHS[mine.month - 1] + ' ' + mine.day) + '. <a class="w124lnk" data-w124bday="1">Change it</a>' : '<a class="w124lnk" data-w124bday="1">+ Add your birthday</a> (month and day only; you choose who sees it).') + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function fundSideHtml() {
    var t = today();
    var next = C.fund.filter(function (f) { var s = parseDay(f.start_date), e = parseDay(f.end_date) || s; return s && e >= t && f.status !== 'cancelled' && f.status !== 'idea' && f.precision !== 'none'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.start_date).localeCompare(String(b.start_date)); }).slice(0, 4);
    return '<div class="w124card"><h3>Fundraising next<span class="lnk" data-w124go="#fundevents">events calendar</span></h3>' + (next.length ? next.map(function (f) { return '<div class="w124row" data-w124fund="' + f.id + '" style="--c:' + LAYER.fund.c + ';grid-template-columns:1fr"><div><div class="tt"><i></i>' + E(f.title) + '</div><div class="mm">' + E(fundWhen(f)) + (f.target_min ? ' \u00b7 target ' + E(kMoney(f.target_min)) + (f.target_max ? ' to ' + E(kMoney(f.target_max)) : '') : '') + '</div></div></div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w124empty">No fundraising events ahead.</p>') + '</div>';
  }
  /* wave274b: the month's anticipated spend, one quiet line under the layers while Volunteering is on: "Anticipated spend this
     month: $1,250 on 3 events (Toronto $750, Montreal $500)". Cancelled events are left out; a city is Toronto when its location
     says so, otherwise Montreal; the cities are named only when both have one. Nothing until the shown month is loaded. */
  function spendLine() {
    if (!C.layers.vol || !staffOk()) return '';
    var y = C.anchor.getFullYear(), m = C.anchor.getMonth(), k = String(C.rangeKey || '').split('/');
    if (!(k[0] && k[1] && k[0] <= ymd(monthStart(C.anchor)) && k[1] >= ymd(addMonths(C.anchor, 1)))) return '';
    var tor = { n: 0, s: 0 }, mtl = { n: 0, s: 0 };
    C.vol.forEach(function (v) {
      var d = parseDay(v.event_date), b = budgetOf(v);
      if (!d || b == null || d.getFullYear() !== y || d.getMonth() !== m || lower(v.status) === 'cancelled') return;
      var c = /toronto/i.test(String(v.location || '')) ? tor : mtl; c.n++; c.s = Math.round((c.s + b) * 100) / 100;
    });
    var n = tor.n + mtl.n; if (!n) return '';
    return '<p class="w124spend"><i aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Anticipated spend this month: <b>' + E(spend(Math.round((tor.s + mtl.s) * 100) / 100)) + '</b> on ' + n + (n === 1 ? ' event' : ' events')
      + (tor.n && mtl.n ? ' (Toronto ' + E(spend(tor.s)) + ', Montreal ' + E(spend(mtl.s)) + ')' : '') + '</span></p>';
  }
  function calPaint() {
    var h = calHost(); if (!h) return;
    var r = rangeFor(), grid = C.view === 'month';
    var from = grid ? r.from : monthStart(C.anchor), to = grid ? r.to : addMonths(C.anchor, 1);
    var list = items(from, to);
    C.idx = {}; list.forEach(function (it) { C.idx[it.id] = it; });
    var monthOnly = list.filter(function (it) { var d = parseDay(it.day); return d.getMonth() === C.anchor.getMonth(); });
    var cnt = chipCounts(monthOnly);
    var chips = LAYERS.filter(function (x) { if (x.k === 'board') return hasBoard(); return (x.k !== 'vol' && x.k !== 'bday') || staffOk(); }).map(function (x) { var on = C.layers[x.k]; return '<a class="w124chip' + (on ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w124layer="' + x.k + '" style="--c:' + x.c + ';--bg:' + x.bg + ';--ink:' + x.ink + '" role="switch" aria-checked="' + on + '" tabindex="0"><i></i>' + E(x.l) + (on && cnt[x.k] ? ' <span class="n">' + cnt[x.k] + '</span>' : '') + '</a>'; }).join('');
    var title = MONTHS[C.anchor.getMonth()] + ' ' + C.anchor.getFullYear();
    var html = '<div class="w124bar"><button type="button" class="w124btn ghost icon" data-w124nav="-1" aria-label="Previous month">' + I('chevl') + '</button><button type="button" class="w124btn ghost sm" data-w124nav="0">Today</button><button type="button" class="w124btn ghost icon" data-w124nav="1" aria-label="Next month">' + I('chevr') + '</button><h2>' + E(title) + '</h2><span class="sp"></span>'
      + '<span class="w124seg"><a data-w124view="month" class="' + (grid ? 'on' : '') + '">' + I('grid') + 'Month</a><a data-w124view="agenda" class="' + (!grid ? 'on' : '') + '">' + I('list') + 'Agenda</a></span></div>'
      + '<div class="w124layers" role="group" aria-label="Layers">' + chips + '</div>' + spendLine()   /* wave274b */
      + '<div class="w124wrap"><div style="min-width:0">' + bandHtml(monthStart(C.anchor), addMonths(C.anchor, 1)) + (grid ? monthHtml(list) : agendaHtml(monthOnly)) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w124side"><div class="w124card"><h3>Up next</h3>' + upNext() + '</div>' + fundSideHtml() + (staffOk() ? bdaysHtml() : '') + '</div></div>';
    h.innerHTML = html;
    if (!grid && sameDay(monthStart(today()), monthStart(C.anchor))) { var el = document.getElementById('w124d' + ymd(today())); if (el && el.scrollIntoView && C.scrollOnce) { C.scrollOnce = false; el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); } }
  }
  async function calShow() {
    var h = calHost(); if (!h) return;
    if (!C.loadedBase) h.innerHTML = '<div class="w124card"><p class="w124empty">Loading the calendar\u2026</p></div>';
    await loadBase();
    calPaint();
    await loadRange();
    if (onCal()) calPaint();
  }
  function onCal() { var a = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); return !!(a && a.id === 'calendar'); }
  function calItemById(id) { return (C.idx && C.idx[id]) || null; }

  /* ---------- the fundraising events calendar (#fundevents) ---------- */
  var PLAN_START = mk(2026, 9, 1, 12), PLAN_END = mk(2027, 9, 1, 12);
  function fundHost() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('fundevents'); if (!sec) return null;
    mast('fundevents', tabOk('fundraising') ? 'Fundraising' : 'Home', 'Events calendar', 'The year\u2019s fundraising events, season by season: partners, targets, and where each one stands.');
    var h = document.getElementById('w124fund');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w124fund'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function stTag(s) { var x = STATUS[s] || STATUS.planned; return '<span class="st" style="background:' + x[1] + ';color:' + x[2] + '">' + E(x[0]) + '</span>'; }
  function targetText(f) {
    if (f.target_min && f.target_max) return 'Target ' + kMoney(f.target_min) + ' to ' + kMoney(f.target_max);
    if (f.target_min) return 'Target about ' + kMoney(f.target_min);
    return '';
  }
  function pastDue(f) { var e = parseDay(f.end_date) || parseDay(f.start_date); return !!e && e < today() && (f.status === 'planned' || f.status === 'confirmed'); }
  function feCard(f) {
    var proj = f.project_id ? C.projects.filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) === String(f.project_id); })[0] : null;
    var tg = targetText(f);
    return '<div class="w124fe" data-w124fund="' + f.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button">' + cardCover(f) + '<div class="hd">' + stTag(f.status) + '<span class="sp"></span><span class="when">' + I('clock') + E(fundWhen(f)) + '</span></div>'
      + '<h4>' + E(f.title) + '</h4>'
      + (f.partners ? '<div class="pt"><b>Partners:</b> ' + E(f.partners) + '</div>' : '')
      + (tg || f.raised != null ? '<div class="tg">' + (tg ? '<span>' + E(tg) + '</span>' : '') + (f.raised != null ? '<span class="raised">' + (tg ? '\u00b7 ' : '') + 'raised ' + E(money(f.raised)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' : '')
      + (f.result_note ? '<div class="nt"><b>Result:</b> ' + E(f.result_note) + '</div>' : (f.notes ? '<div class="nt">' + E(f.notes) + '</div>' : ''))
      + (pastDue(f) ? '<div class="warn">The date has passed: record how it went.</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="ft">' + (proj ? '<a class="w124lnk" data-w124go="#project/' + proj.id + '">' + I('board') + ' Project board</a>' : '') + (f.sop_training_id ? '<a class="w124lnk" data-w124go="#training/t/' + f.sop_training_id + '">' + I('doc') + ' SOP</a>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function goalsHtml() {
    var g = C.goals || {}, target = Number(g.target || 20000), min = Number(g.minimum || 10000), healthy = Number(g.healthy || 15000);
    var inPlan = function (f) { var s = parseDay(f.start_date); return s && s >= PLAN_START && s < PLAN_END; };
    var raised = C.fund.filter(inPlan).reduce(function (a, f) { return a + Number(f.raised || 0); }, 0);
    var kick = C.fund.filter(function (f) { return f.season === 'summer-2026'; }).reduce(function (a, f) { return a + Number(f.raised || 0); }, 0);
    var planned = C.fund.filter(function (f) { return inPlan(f) && f.status !== 'cancelled' && f.status !== 'idea'; });
    var lo = planned.reduce(function (a, f) { return a + Number(f.target_min || 0); }, 0), hi = planned.reduce(function (a, f) { return a + Number(f.target_max || f.target_min || 0); }, 0);
    var pct = function (v) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, v / target * 100)); };
    var notes = (g.notes || []).map(function (n) { return '<li>' + E(n) + '</li>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="w124card"><div class="w124goals"><div><h3 style="margin-bottom:6px">Fiscal 2026-27, from October 1</h3>'
      + '<div class="big">' + E(money(raised)) + '<small>raised of the ' + E(money(target)) + ' target</small></div>'
      + '<div class="w124meter" title="Raised by the events this fiscal year"><div class="fill" style="width:' + pct(raised).toFixed(1) + '%"></div>'
      + '<div class="tick" style="left:' + pct(min) + '%"><span>' + E(kMoney(min)) + '<em> minimum</em></span></div><div class="tick" style="left:' + pct(healthy) + '%"><span>' + E(kMoney(healthy)) + '<em> healthy</em></span></div><div class="tick" style="left:calc(100% - 2px)"><span style="left:auto;right:0;transform:none">' + E(kMoney(target)) + '+<em> target</em></span></div></div>'
      + '<p class="w124empty">' + (lo ? 'The events with a target this year aim at ' + E(kMoney(lo)) + (hi > lo ? ' to ' + E(kMoney(hi)) : '') + '. ' : '') + (kick ? 'The summer 2026 kickoff raised ' + E(money(kick)) + ' (fiscal 2025-26).' : '') + '</p></div>'
      + '<div><ul class="w124gnotes">' + notes + '</ul></div></div></div>';
  }
  function stripHtml() {
    var months = []; for (var i = 0; i < 14; i++) months.push(mk(2026, 7 + i, 1, 12));
    var t = today();
    var head = months.map(function (m, i) { return '<div class="m' + (m.getMonth() === 9 ? ' fy' : '') + (m.getMonth() === t.getMonth() && m.getFullYear() === t.getFullYear() ? ' now' : '') + '" title="' + E(MONTHS[m.getMonth()] + ' ' + m.getFullYear()) + '">' + E(MONTHS[m.getMonth()].slice(0, 3)) + (m.getMonth() === 0 || i === 0 ? ' \u2019' + String(m.getFullYear()).slice(2) : '') + '</div>'; }).join('');
    var idx = function (d) { return (d.getFullYear() - 2026) * 12 + d.getMonth() - 7; };
    var evs = C.fund.filter(function (f) { return f.start_date && f.precision !== 'none' && f.precision !== 'flexible' && f.status !== 'cancelled'; }).map(function (f) {
      var s = parseDay(f.start_date), e = parseDay(f.end_date) || s; return { f: f, a: Math.max(0, idx(s)), b: Math.min(13, idx(e)) };
    }).filter(function (x) { return x.b >= 0 && x.a <= 13; }).sort(function (x, y) { return x.a - y.a || (y.b - y.a) - (x.b - x.a); });
    var lanes = [];
    evs.forEach(function (x) { for (var l = 0; l < lanes.length; l++) { if (lanes[l].every(function (o) { return o.b < x.a || o.a > x.b; })) { lanes[l].push(x); return; } } lanes.push([x]); });
    var body = lanes.map(function (ln) { return '<div class="w124lane">' + ln.map(function (x) { return '<a data-w124fund="' + x.f.id + '" class="' + (x.f.status === 'done' ? 'done' : x.f.status === 'idea' ? 'idea' : '') + '" style="grid-column:' + (x.a + 1) + ' / ' + (x.b + 2) + '" title="' + E(x.f.title + ', ' + fundWhen(x.f)) + '">' + E(x.f.title) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="w124card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>The year at a glance<span class="lnk" data-w124go="#calendar">master calendar</span></h3><div class="w124sw"><div><div class="w124strip">' + head + '</div><div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;margin-top:6px">' + body + '</div></div></div></div>';
  }
  function fundPaint() {
    var h = fundHost(); if (!h) return;
    var edit = canEditFund();
    var html = goalsHtml() + stripHtml();
    SEASONS.forEach(function (s) {
      var list = C.fund.filter(function (f) { return (f.season || 'backlog') === s[0]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
      if (s[0] === 'backlog') {
        html += '<div class="w124season"><h3>' + E(s[1]) + '</h3><span>' + E(s[2]) + '</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (edit ? '<a class="w124btn ghost sm" data-w124fadd="backlog">' + I('plus') + 'Add an idea</a>' : '') + '</div>'
          + '<div class="w124ideas">' + (list.length ? list.map(function (f) { return '<a data-w124fund="' + f.id + '" title="' + E(f.notes || '') + '">' + I('spark') + E(f.title) + '</a>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w124empty">No ideas parked yet.</p>') + '</div>';
        return;
      }
      html += '<div class="w124season"><h3>' + E(s[1]) + '</h3><span>' + E(s[2]) + '</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (edit ? '<a class="w124btn ghost sm" data-w124fadd="' + s[0] + '">' + I('plus') + 'Add an event</a>' : '') + '</div>'
        + (list.length ? '<div class="w124evs">' + list.map(feCard).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w124empty">Nothing planned for this season yet.</p>');
    });
    var other = C.fund.filter(function (f) { return f.season && !SEASONS.some(function (s) { return s[0] === f.season; }); });
    if (other.length) html += '<div class="w124season"><h3>Other events</h3></div><div class="w124evs">' + other.map(feCard).join('') + '</div>';
    var g = C.goals || {};
    if ((g.planning || []).length) html += '<div class="w124card" style="margin-top:22px"><h3>Planning notes</h3><ul class="w124gnotes">' + g.planning.map(function (n) { return '<li>' + E(n) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' + (g.source ? '<p class="w124empty" style="margin-top:6px">From ' + E(g.source) + '.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    h.innerHTML = html;
  }
  async function fundShow(sub) {
    var h = fundHost(); if (!h) return;
    if (!C.loadedBase) h.innerHTML = '<div class="w124card"><p class="w124empty">Loading the events\u2026</p></div>';
    await loadBase();
    fundPaint();
    if (sub && /^\d+$/.test(sub)) { var f = fundById(sub); if (f) openFund(f); }
  }
  function onFund() { var a = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); return !!(a && a.id === 'fundevents'); }
  function fundById(id) { return C.fund.filter(function (f) { return String(f.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }

  /* ---------- wave168: photos on the fundraising events (29/09/26) ----------
     Steel: "where possible add photos to our fundraising events pages". An event carries a cover from the Photo library
     (fund_events.cover_photo_id), a gallery (photo_series: its own photos, or last year's edition) and the past fundraisers
     like it from the website's archive (past_site_events: site_events with their posters). Cards show the cover (or the
     latest poster); an event's page opens on it, with the photos and the past fundraisers, each opening full size. Only
     photos marked ready in the Photo library, and only the Hub's own storage. */
  var PSTORE = 'https://zwnmulmnvopclzcvytce.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/content-images/';
  var OWN = /^https:\/\/zwnmulmnvopclzcvytce\.supabase\.co\/storage\/v1\/object\/public\/[\w./%-]+$/;
  var DOODLE = { star: PSTORE + 'brand/doodle-star-gold.png', ast: PSTORE + 'brand/doodle-asterisk-cream.png', flowers: PSTORE + 'brand/doodle-flowers-gold.png' };
  var BRAND_E = PSTORE + 'brand/e-large-gold-600.png';
  var DOODLES = [PSTORE + 'brand/doodle-star-gold.png', PSTORE + 'brand/doodle-flowers-gold.png', PSTORE + 'brand/doodle-heart-purple.png', PSTORE + 'brand/doodle-asterisk-cream.png'];
  /* an event with no picture yet: the brand's green, the E that moves and a doodle (one per event, so the cards differ) */
  function brandBand(f, cls) { return '<div class="' + cls + ' brand" aria-hidden="true"><img class="e" src="' + BRAND_E + '" alt=""><img class="s" src="' + DOODLES[Math.abs(Number(f.id) || 0) % DOODLES.length] + '" alt=""></div>'; }
  /* a poster at the size it is shown (Supabase's image resizing); the original if the resizing does not answer */
  function sized(u, w) { return String(u).replace('/storage/v1/object/public/', '/storage/v1/render/image/public/') + '?width=' + w + '&resize=contain&quality=72'; }
  function posterImg(u, w, alt, lazy) { return '<img src="' + E(sized(u, w)) + '" data-w168orig="' + E(u) + '" alt="' + E(alt || '') + '"' + (lazy ? ' loading="lazy"' : '') + '>'; }
  document.addEventListener('error', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || t.tagName !== 'IMG' || !t.getAttribute('data-w168orig')) return;
    var o = t.getAttribute('data-w168orig'); t.removeAttribute('data-w168orig'); if (OWN.test(o)) t.src = o;
  }, true);
  function photoRow(id) { return (C.photos || []).filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function pUrl(p, thumb) { return p ? PSTORE + String(thumb && p.path_thumb ? p.path_thumb : p.path_web).replace(/^\/+/, '') : ''; }
  function pPos(p) { return Math.round(100 * (p && p.focal_x != null ? p.focal_x : 0.5)) + '% ' + Math.round(100 * (p && p.focal_y != null ? p.focal_y : 0.5)) + '%'; }
  function yearOf(d) { var m = /^(\d{4})/.exec(String(d || '')); return m ? Number(m[1]) : null; }
  function shown(p) { return !!(p && p.path_web && p.usage === 'ready'); }
  /* the year a photo is from: when it was taken, or the year its series names (gift-baskets-2025) */
  function photoYear(p) { var y = p ? yearOf(p.taken_on) : null; if (!y && p) { var m = /(20\d\d)/.exec(String(p.series || '')); if (m) y = Number(m[1]); } return y; }
  function tidy(t) { return String(t || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }
  function evName(s) { return tidy(s.title) + ', ' + monthYear(s.starts_at); }
  function monthYear(d) { var x = parseDay(String(d || '').slice(0, 10)); return x ? MONTHS[x.getMonth()] + ' ' + x.getFullYear() : ''; }
  function mediaOf(f) {
    var cover = f.cover_photo_id ? photoRow(f.cover_photo_id) : null; if (!shown(cover)) cover = null;
    var gallery = f.photo_series ? (C.photos || []).filter(function (p) { return p.series === f.photo_series && shown(p); }) : [];
    if (!cover && gallery.length) cover = gallery[0];
    var past = (f.past_site_events || []).map(function (id) { return (C.siteEv || []).filter(function (s) { return String(s.id) === String(id); })[0]; }).filter(Boolean)
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.starts_at || '').localeCompare(String(b.starts_at || '')); });
    var last = past.length ? past[past.length - 1] : null, poster = null;
    if (last) { var u = last.poster_url || last.hero_url; if (OWN.test(String(u || ''))) poster = u; }
    var ey = yearOf(f.start_date) || yearOf(f.end_date), cy = cover ? photoYear(cover) : null;
    return { cover: cover, gallery: gallery, past: past, poster: poster, last: last, earlier: !!(cover && cy && (!ey || cy < ey)) && f.status !== 'done', year: cy };
  }
  function cardCover(f) {
    var m = mediaOf(f);
    if (m.cover) return '<div class="w168cv" style="background-image:url(\'' + E(pUrl(m.cover, true)) + '\');background-position:' + pPos(m.cover) + '">' + (m.earlier ? '<span class="lb">The ' + m.year + ' edition</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (m.poster) return '<div class="w168cv poster">' + posterImg(m.poster, 480, '', true) + '<span class="lb">From ' + E(monthYear(m.last.starts_at)) + '</span></div>';
    return brandBand(f, 'w168cv');
  }
  function sheetHero(f) {
    var m = mediaOf(f);
    if (m.cover) return '<div class="w168hero" style="background-image:url(\'' + E(pUrl(m.cover)) + '\');background-position:' + pPos(m.cover) + '" role="img" aria-label="' + E(m.cover.alt_text || m.cover.title || '') + '"><img class="d" src="' + DOODLE.star + '" alt="">' + (m.earlier ? '<span class="lb">Photo: the ' + m.year + ' edition</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (m.poster) return '<button type="button" class="w168hero poster" data-w168full="' + E(m.poster) + '" data-w168alt="' + E(evName(m.last)) + '">' + posterImg(m.poster, 900, evName(m.last)) + '<span class="lb">' + E(evName(m.last)) + '</span></button>';
    return brandBand(f, 'w168hero') + (canEditFund() ? '<p class="w168hint">No photo yet: Edit the event to choose one from the Photo library, or tick past fundraisers like it.</p>' : '');
  }
  function sheetMedia(f) {
    var m = mediaOf(f), h = '';
    if (m.gallery.length) h += '<div class="w168sec"><b>' + (m.earlier ? 'Photos from the ' + m.year + ' edition' : 'Photos') + '</b><div class="w168gal">' + m.gallery.map(function (p) {
      return '<button type="button" class="w168g" data-w168full="' + E(pUrl(p)) + '" data-w168alt="' + E(p.alt_text || p.title || '') + '" aria-label="' + E(p.title || 'A photo') + '" style="background-image:url(\'' + E(pUrl(p, true)) + '\');background-position:' + pPos(p) + '"></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div></div>';
    if (m.past.length) h += '<div class="w168sec"><b>' + (m.past.length === 1 ? 'A past fundraiser like it' : 'Past fundraisers like it') + '</b><div class="w168past">' + m.past.map(function (s) {
      var u = s.poster_url || s.hero_url; if (!OWN.test(String(u || ''))) u = '';
      return '<button type="button" class="w168pp"' + (u ? ' data-w168full="' + E(u) + '" data-w168alt="' + E(evName(s)) + '"' : '') + '>' + (u ? posterImg(u, 360, '', true) : '') + '<span><b>' + E(tidy(s.title)) + '</b>' + E(monthYear(s.starts_at)) + '</span></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div></div>';
    return h;
  }
  /* the edit form: a cover from the Photo library, the photos shown, the past editions */
  function mediaForm(f) {
    var ready = (C.photos || []).filter(function (p) { return shown(p) && p.path_thumb && (p.orientation !== 'portrait' || String(p.id) === String(f.cover_photo_id)); });
    if (f.photo_series) ready.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.series === f.photo_series ? 1 : 0) - (a.series === f.photo_series ? 1 : 0); });
    if (f.cover_photo_id) ready.sort(function (a, b) { return (String(b.id) === String(f.cover_photo_id) ? 1 : 0) - (String(a.id) === String(f.cover_photo_id) ? 1 : 0); });
    var series = {}; (C.photos || []).forEach(function (p) { if (p.series && shown(p)) series[p.series] = (series[p.series] || 0) + 1; });
    if (f.photo_series && !series[f.photo_series]) series[f.photo_series] = 0;
    var sOpts = '<option value="">None</option>' + Object.keys(series).sort().map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + E(k) + '"' + (k === f.photo_series ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(k) + (series[k] ? ' (' + series[k] + ')' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('');
    var past = (f.past_site_events || []).map(String);
    return '<div class="w168form"><label class="f">Cover photo<span class="op">from the Photo library</span></label><input type="hidden" name="cover_photo_id" value="' + E(f.cover_photo_id || '') + '">'
      + '<div class="w168pick"><button type="button" class="w168th none' + (f.cover_photo_id ? '' : ' on') + '" data-w168pick="">None</button>' + ready.map(function (p) {
        return '<button type="button" class="w168th' + (String(p.id) === String(f.cover_photo_id) ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w168pick="' + E(p.id) + '" title="' + E(p.title || '') + '" aria-label="' + E('Use ' + (p.title || 'this photo')) + '" style="background-image:url(\'' + E(pUrl(p, true)) + '\')"></button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<label class="f" for="w168ps">Photos shown<span class="op">a series of the Photo library</span></label><select id="w168ps" name="photo_series">' + sOpts + '</select>'
      + ((C.siteEv || []).length ? '<label class="f">Past fundraisers like it<span class="op">from the website&rsquo;s archive</span></label><div class="w168chk" data-w168past>' + (C.siteEv || []).map(function (s) {
        return '<label><input type="checkbox" name="past_site" value="' + E(s.id) + '"' + (past.indexOf(String(s.id)) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + '> ' + E(tidy(s.title)) + ' <span class="op">' + E(monthYear(s.starts_at)) + '</span></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w168pick]') : null; if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var form = b.closest('form'); if (!form) return;
    var inp = form.querySelector('input[name=cover_photo_id]'); if (inp) inp.value = b.getAttribute('data-w168pick');
    [].forEach.call(form.querySelectorAll('[data-w168pick]'), function (x) { x.classList.toggle('on', x === b); });
  }, true);
  /* full size, over everything; a click or Escape closes it */
  function openLb(url, alt) {
    if (!OWN.test(String(url || ''))) return;
    var old = document.getElementById('w168lb'); if (old) old.remove();
    var el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w168lb'; el.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); el.setAttribute('aria-label', alt || 'Photo');
    el.innerHTML = '<img src="' + E(url) + '" alt="' + E(alt || '') + '"><button type="button" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>';
    el.addEventListener('click', function () { el.remove(); });
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w168full]') : null; if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); openLb(b.getAttribute('data-w168full'), b.getAttribute('data-w168alt'));
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('w168lb')) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); document.getElementById('w168lb').remove(); } }, true);

  /* ---------- the sheet: details and the two forms ---------- */
  var lastFocus = null;
  function sheetEl() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w124sheet');
    if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w124sheet'; s.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w124close></div><div class="pane" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Details"></div>'; document.body.appendChild(s); }
    return s;
  }
  function openSheet(html, kind) {
    if (!C.sheet) lastFocus = document.activeElement;
    C.sheet = { kind: kind || 'view' };
    var s = sheetEl(); s.querySelector('.pane').innerHTML = html; s.classList.add('on');
    setTimeout(function () { var f = s.querySelector('.pane input, .pane select, .pane .x'); if (f) { try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }, 30);
  }
  function closeSheet() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w124sheet'); if (s) { s.classList.remove('on'); s.querySelector('.pane').innerHTML = ''; }
    C.sheet = null;
    if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus) { try { lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } lastFocus = null;
  }
  function top(tag) { return '<div class="top">' + (tag || '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="x" data-w124close aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div>'; }
  function layerTag(k) { var L = LAYER[k]; return '<span class="tag" style="--bg:' + L.bg + ';--ink:' + L.ink + '">' + I(L.icon) + E(L.l.replace(/^My /, '')) + '</span>'; }
  function kv(icon, html) { return '<span>' + I(icon) + '</span><div>' + html + '</div>'; }
  function openItem(it) {
    if (!it) return;
    if (it.k === 'fund') { openFund(it.ref); return; }
    var r = it.ref, html = top(layerTag(it.k)) + '<h2>' + E(it.title) + '</h2>', rows = [], acts = [];
    var d = parseDay(it.day);
    if (it.k === 'meet') {
      var s = new Date(r.start_at), e = r.end_at ? new Date(r.end_at) : null;
      rows.push(kv('clock', E(fmtDate(s, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })) + (r.all_day ? ', all day' : ', ' + E(fmtTime(s)) + (e ? ' to ' + E(fmtTime(e)) : ''))));
      if (r.location) rows.push(kv('pin', E(r.location)));
      var att = (r.attendee_emails || []).filter(function (x) { return !/hub@|loom@|admin@|gserviceaccount|\.test-google-a\.com$/i.test(x); });
      var names = att.map(function (x) { var m = memberByEmail(x); return m ? m.name : x.split('@')[0]; });
      if (names.length) rows.push(kv('people', E(names.slice(0, 10).join(', ')) + (names.length > 10 ? ' and ' + (names.length - 10) + ' more' : '')));
      if (r.organizer) { var om = memberByEmail(r.organizer); rows.push(kv('spark', 'Organized by ' + E(om ? om.name : r.organizer))); }
      if (r.meet_link) acts.push('<a class="w124btn" href="' + E(r.meet_link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('video') + 'Join</a>');
      if (r.html_link) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" href="' + E(r.html_link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open in Google Calendar</a>');
      if (r.department) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#team/' + E(r.department) + '/agenda">Agenda</a>');
      if (r.description) html += '<div class="note">' + E(clip(String(r.description).replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' '), 600)) + '</div>';
    } else if (it.k === 'post') {
      if (it.planned) {
        rows.push(kv('clock', E(fmtDate(it.at, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })) + ', ' + E(fmtTime(it.at))));
        rows.push(kv('megaphone', E(it.sub)));
        if (r.campaign) rows.push(kv('target', 'Campaign: ' + E(r.campaign)));
        if (r.owner) rows.push(kv('people', 'Owner: ' + E(r.owner)));
        acts.push('<a class="w124btn" data-w124go="#contentcal/' + E(r.id) + '">Open in the content calendar</a>');
      } else {
        rows.push(kv('clock', 'Published ' + E(fmtDate(it.at, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })) + ', ' + E(fmtTime(it.at))));
        var stats = [['views', 'views'], ['likes', 'likes'], ['comments', 'comments']].filter(function (x) { return r[x[0]] != null; }).map(function (x) { return Number(r[x[0]]).toLocaleString('en-CA') + ' ' + x[1]; });
        if (stats.length) rows.push(kv('spark', E(stats.join(' \u00b7 '))));
        if (r.caption) html += '<div class="note">' + E(clip(r.caption, 500)) + '</div>';
        if (r.url) acts.push('<a class="w124btn" href="' + E(r.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('link') + 'Open the post</a>');
        acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#socialstats">Analytics</a>');
      }
    } else if (it.k === 'vol') {
      rows.push(kv('clock', E(fmtDate(d, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })) + (it.cancelled ? ' (cancelled)' : '')));
      if (r.location) rows.push(kv('pin', E(r.location)));
      if (r.planned_meals) rows.push(kv('pot', E(r.planned_meals) + ' meals planned'));
      if (it.budget != null) rows.push(kv('cash', 'Anticipated spend <b>' + E(spend(it.budget)) + '</b>'));   /* wave274b: its own line */
      var toEv = String(r.kind || '').toLowerCase() === 'meal' && /toronto/i.test(String(r.location || ''));   /* wave274b: a Toronto event opens in the Toronto app */
      acts.push('<a class="w124btn" data-w124go="' + (toEv ? '#toronto/e/' : '#volunteering/e/') + E(r.id) + '">Open the event</a>');
    } else if (it.k === 'board') {
      if (it.fy) {
        rows.push(kv('clock', E(fmtDate(d, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }))));
        rows.push(kv('doc', 'The last day of FY' + it.fy + ' (October 1, ' + (it.fy - 1) + ' to September 30, ' + it.fy + '). The books close and the year-end review starts.'));
        if (staffOk()) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#finances">Finances</a>');
        acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#fundevents">Events calendar</a>');
      } else if (it.cm) {
        var bm = new Date(r.meets_at);
        rows.push(kv('clock', 'By ' + E(fmtDate(d, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' }))));
        rows.push(kv('people', 'Each committee meets before the ' + E(r.title || 'board meeting') + ' on ' + E(fmtDate(bm, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })) + ', so everyone arrives informed.'));
        acts.push('<a class="w124btn" data-w124go="#committees">Committees</a>');
        acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#governance">Board overview</a>');
      } else {
        var ms = new Date(r.meets_at);
        rows.push(kv('clock', E(fmtDate(ms, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })) + ', ' + E(fmtTime(ms))));
        var stw = { tentative: 'Tentative', scheduled: 'Scheduled', confirmed: 'Confirmed', held: 'Held' }[lower(r.status)] || '';
        if (stw) rows.push(kv('spark', E(stw)));
        if (r.note) html += '<div class="note">' + E(clip(r.note, 500)) + '</div>';
        if (r.agenda_url) acts.push('<a class="w124btn" href="' + E(r.agenda_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('doc') + 'Agenda</a>');
        if (r.notes_url) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" href="' + E(r.notes_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('doc') + 'Minutes</a>');
        acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#governance">Board overview</a>');
      }
    } else if (it.k === 'bday') {
      rows.push(kv('cake', E(fmtDate(d, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' }))));
      if (it.person) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#person/' + E(it.person.id) + '">Open their profile</a>');
      if (it.mine) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124bday="1">Change your birthday</a>');
    }
    html += '<div class="kv">' + rows.join('') + '</div>' + (acts.length ? '<div class="acts">' + acts.join('') + '</div>' : '');
    openSheet(html, 'view');
  }
  function openFund(f) {
    if (!f) return;
    var proj = f.project_id ? C.projects.filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) === String(f.project_id); })[0] : null;
    var rows = [kv('clock', E(fundWhen(f)))];
    if (f.partners) rows.push(kv('people', '<b>Partners:</b> ' + E(f.partners)));
    var tg = targetText(f); if (tg || f.target_note) rows.push(kv('target', E(tg) + (f.target_note ? (tg ? '. ' : '') + E(f.target_note) : '')));
    if (f.raised != null) rows.push(kv('check', 'Raised <b>' + E(money(f.raised)) + '</b>'));
    if (f.lead) rows.push(kv('spark', 'Lead: ' + E(f.lead)));
    var acts = [];
    if (canEditFund()) { acts.push('<a class="w124btn" data-w124fedit="' + f.id + '">' + I('pen') + 'Edit</a>'); if (f.status !== 'done' && f.status !== 'idea' && f.precision !== 'none') acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124fdone="' + f.id + '">' + I('check') + 'Record the result</a>'); }
    if (proj) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#project/' + proj.id + '">' + I('board') + 'Project board</a>');
    if (f.sop_training_id) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#training/t/' + f.sop_training_id + '">' + I('doc') + 'SOP</a>');
    if (!onFund()) acts.push('<a class="w124btn ghost" data-w124go="#fundevents">Events calendar</a>');
    var html = top('<span class="tag" style="--bg:' + STATUS[f.status][1] + ';--ink:' + STATUS[f.status][2] + '">' + E(STATUS[f.status][0]) + '</span>' + layerTag('fund')) + sheetHero(f) + '<h2>' + E(f.title) + '</h2>'
      + '<div class="kv">' + rows.join('') + '</div>'
      + (window.w261 ? window.w261.slot(f) : '')   /* wave261: Gifts collected, on an event with a campaign */
      + (f.result_note ? '<div class="note"><b>Result:</b> ' + E(f.result_note) + '</div>' : '')
      + (f.notes ? '<div class="note">' + E(f.notes) + '</div>' : '')
      + (pastDue(f) ? '<p class="err">The date has passed: record how it went, so the year\u2019s total stays right.</p>' : '')
      + sheetMedia(f)
      + (acts.length ? '<div class="acts">' + acts.join('') + '</div>' : '');
    openSheet(html, 'view');
    if (window.w261) window.w261.fill(f);
  }
  function monthVal(d) { return d ? d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) : ''; }
  function openFundForm(f, season, doneMode) {
    var isNew = !f; f = f || { title: '', season: season || 'fall-2026', precision: season === 'backlog' ? 'none' : 'month', status: season === 'backlog' ? 'idea' : 'planned' };
    var s = parseDay(f.start_date), e = parseDay(f.end_date);
    var projOpts = '<option value="">None</option>' + C.projects.filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; }).map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '"' + (String(p.id) === String(f.project_id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    var html = top('<span class="tag" style="--bg:' + LAYER.fund.bg + ';--ink:' + LAYER.fund.ink + '">' + I('heart') + (doneMode ? 'Record the result' : isNew ? 'New fundraising event' : 'Edit the event') + '</span>')
      + '<form data-w124fform="' + (f.id || '') + '" novalidate>'
      + (doneMode ? '<h2>' + E(f.title) + '</h2>' : '<label class="f" for="w124ft">Name</label><input type="text" id="w124ft" name="title" maxlength="140" value="' + E(f.title) + '" required>')
      + (doneMode ? '' : '<div class="two"><div><label class="f" for="w124fs">Season</label><select id="w124fs" name="season">' + SEASONS.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x[0] + '"' + (x[0] === f.season ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>'
        + '<div><label class="f" for="w124fst">Status</label><select id="w124fst" name="status">' + Object.keys(STATUS).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (k === f.status ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(STATUS[k][0]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div></div>'
        + '<label class="f" for="w124fp">When</label><select id="w124fp" name="precision">' + [['day', 'On a date'], ['month', 'Sometime in a month'], ['range', 'Across a few months'], ['season', 'Sometime in a season'], ['flexible', 'Flexible'], ['none', 'Not scheduled yet']].map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x[0] + '"' + (x[0] === f.precision ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + x[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
        + '<div class="two" data-w124dates><div data-p="day"><label class="f" for="w124fd1">Date</label><input type="date" id="w124fd1" name="d1" value="' + (s ? ymd(s) : '') + '"></div><div data-p="day"><label class="f" for="w124fd2">Until<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="date" id="w124fd2" name="d2" value="' + (e && s && !sameDay(s, e) ? ymd(e) : '') + '"></div>'
        + '<div data-p="month range season"><label class="f" for="w124fm1">From month</label><input type="month" id="w124fm1" name="m1" value="' + monthVal(s) + '"></div><div data-p="range season"><label class="f" for="w124fm2">To month</label><input type="month" id="w124fm2" name="m2" value="' + monthVal(e) + '"></div></div>'
        + '<label class="f" for="w124fpa">Partners<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="text" id="w124fpa" name="partners" maxlength="240" value="' + E(f.partners || '') + '">'
        + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f" for="w124fmin">Target from ($)<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="number" id="w124fmin" name="target_min" min="0" step="100" value="' + E(f.target_min == null ? '' : f.target_min) + '"></div><div><label class="f" for="w124fmax">Target up to ($)<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="number" id="w124fmax" name="target_max" min="0" step="100" value="' + E(f.target_max == null ? '' : f.target_max) + '"></div></div>'
        + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f" for="w124fl">Lead<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="text" id="w124fl" name="lead" maxlength="80" value="' + E(f.lead || '') + '" list="w124people"></div><div><label class="f" for="w124fpr">Project board<span class="op">optional</span></label><select id="w124fpr" name="project_id">' + projOpts + '</select></div></div>'
        + '<datalist id="w124people">' + members().filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; }).map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '">'; }).join('') + '</datalist>'
        + '<label class="f" for="w124fn">Notes<span class="op">optional</span></label><textarea id="w124fn" name="notes">' + E(f.notes || '') + '</textarea>'
        + mediaForm(f))
      + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f" for="w124fr">Raised ($)' + (doneMode ? '' : '<span class="op">once it has happened</span>') + '</label><input type="number" id="w124fr" name="raised" min="0" step="1" value="' + E(f.raised == null ? '' : f.raised) + '"></div><div></div></div>'
      + '<label class="f" for="w124frn">How it went<span class="op">optional</span></label><textarea id="w124frn" name="result_note" placeholder="Turnout, what worked, what to change next time">' + E(f.result_note || '') + '</textarea>'

'<button type="button" class="w124btn ghost" data-w124fdel="' + f.id + '" style="margin-left:auto;color:' + FLAG + ' !important">Delete</button>' : '') + '</div>'

  function syncDates() {
    var sel = document.querySelector('#w124sheet select[name=precision]'); var box = document.querySelector('#w124sheet [data-w124dates]');
    if (!box) return; var p = sel ? sel.value : 'day';
    box.style.display = (p === 'flexible' || p === 'none') ? 'none' : '';
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-p]').forEach(function (el) { el.style.display = el.getAttribute('data-p').split(' ').indexOf(p) >= 0 ? '' : 'none'; });
  }
  function lastOfMonth(v) { var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})$/.exec(v || ''); if (!m) return null; var d = mk(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), 0, 12); return ymd(d); }
  function firstOfMonth(v) { var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})$/.exec(v || ''); return m ? m[1] + '-' + m[2] + '-01' : null; }
  async function saveFund(form) {
    var id = form.getAttribute('data-w124fform'), f = id ? fundById(id) : null, fd = {};
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(form.elements, function (el) { if (el.name) fd[el.name] = el.value; });
    var err = form.querySelector('[data-w124ferr]'), fail = function (m) { if (err) { err.style.display = ''; err.textContent = m; } };
    var num = function (v) { v = String(v || '').trim(); return v === '' ? null : Number(v); };
    var row = {};
    if (fd.__done === '1') { row.status = 'done'; row.raised = num(fd.raised); row.result_note = String(fd.result_note || '').trim() || null; }
    else {
      row.title = String(fd.title || '').trim(); if (!row.title) { fail('Give the event a name.'); return; }
      row.season = fd.season; row.status = fd.status; row.precision = fd.precision;
      row.partners = String(fd.partners || '').trim() || null; row.lead = String(fd.lead || '').trim() || null;
      row.target_min = num(fd.target_min); row.target_max = num(fd.target_max); row.raised = num(fd.raised);
      row.result_note = String(fd.result_note || '').trim() || null; row.notes = String(fd.notes || '').trim() || null;
      row.project_id = fd.project_id ? Number(fd.project_id) : null;
      /* wave168: the photos */
      row.cover_photo_id = fd.cover_photo_id ? Number(fd.cover_photo_id) : null; row.photo_series = fd.photo_series || null;
      if (form.querySelector('[data-w168past]')) { var pastIds = Array.prototype.map.call(form.querySelectorAll('input[name=past_site]:checked'), function (x) { return Number(x.value); }); row.past_site_events = pastIds.length ? pastIds : null; }
      if (row.precision === 'day') { if (!fd.d1) { fail('Pick the date.'); return; } row.start_date = fd.d1; row.end_date = fd.d2 && fd.d2 > fd.d1 ? fd.d2 : null; row.timing = MONTHS[parseDay(fd.d1).getMonth()]; }
      else if (row.precision === 'month') { if (!fd.m1) { fail('Pick the month.'); return; } row.start_date = firstOfMonth(fd.m1); row.end_date = lastOfMonth(fd.m1); row.timing = MONTHS[Number(fd.m1.slice(5)) - 1]; }
      else if (row.precision === 'range' || row.precision === 'season') { if (!fd.m1 || !fd.m2) { fail('Pick the first and last month.'); return; } if (fd.m2 < fd.m1) { fail('The last month is before the first.'); return; } row.start_date = firstOfMonth(fd.m1); row.end_date = lastOfMonth(fd.m2); row.timing = MONTHS[Number(fd.m1.slice(5)) - 1].slice(0, 3) + ' to ' + MONTHS[Number(fd.m2.slice(5)) - 1].slice(0, 3); }
      else { row.start_date = null; row.end_date = null; row.timing = row.precision === 'flexible' ? 'Flexible' : 'Not yet scheduled'; }
      if (row.target_min != null && row.target_max != null && row.target_max < row.target_min) { fail('The target range runs backwards.'); return; }
    }
    row.updated_by = me().name; row.updated_at = new Date().toISOString();
    var res;
    if (f) { res = await q(db.from('fund_events').update(row).eq('id', f.id)); if (!res.error) Object.assign(f, row); }
    else { row.created_by = me().name; row.plan = '2026-27'; row.source = 'hub'; row.sort = 100 + C.fund.length; res = await q(db.from('fund_events').insert(row).select('*').single()); if (!res.error) C.fund.push((res.data && res.data.id) ? res.data : Object.assign({ id: -Date.now() }, row)); }
    if (res.error) { fail('It did not save: ' + String(res.error.message || res.error).slice(0, 140)); return; }
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'fund_event', entity_id: String(f ? f.id : 'new'), field: fd.__done === '1' ? 'result' : (f ? 'edited' : 'created'), old_value: null, new_value: (row.title || (f && f.title) || '') + (row.raised != null ? ' raised ' + row.raised : ''), changed_by: me().name }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
    closeSheet();
    toastS(fd.__done === '1' ? 'Result saved' : f ? 'Saved' : 'Event added');
    repaintAll();
  }
  async function deleteFund(id) {
    var f = fundById(id); if (!f) return;
    if (typeof confirm === 'function' && !confirm('Delete ' + f.title + '? This cannot be undone.')) return;
    var res = await q(db.from('fund_events').delete().eq('id', f.id));
    if (res.error) { toastS('Could not delete it'); return; }
    C.fund = C.fund.filter(function (x) { return x !== f; });
    closeSheet(); toastS('Deleted'); repaintAll();
  }

' checked' : '') + '><div><b>' + o[1] + '</b><span>' + o[2] + '</span></div></label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'

  async function saveBday(form) {
    var m = Number(form.month.value), d = Number(form.day.value), share = (form.querySelector('input[name=share]:checked') || {}).value || 'everyone';
    var err = form.querySelector('[data-w124berr]'), fail = function (t) { if (err) { err.style.display = ''; err.textContent = t; } };
    if (!m || !d) { fail('Pick the month and the day.'); return; }
    var max = [31, 29, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31][m - 1]; if (d > max) { fail(MONTHS[m - 1] + ' has ' + max + ' days.'); return; }
    var em = me().email; if (!em) { fail('Sign in again first.'); return; }
    var row = { email: em, month: m, day: d, share: share, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    var res = await q(db.from('hub_birthdays').upsert(row, { onConflict: 'email' }));
    if (res.error) { fail('It did not save: ' + String(res.error.message || res.error).slice(0, 140)); return; }
    C.bdays = C.bdays.filter(function (b) { return lower(b.email) !== em; }).concat([row]);
    closeSheet(); toastS('Birthday saved: ' + MONTHS[m - 1] + ' ' + d); repaintAll();
  }
  async function deleteBday() {
    var em = me().email; var res = await q(db.from('hub_birthdays').delete().eq('email', em));
    if (res.error) { toastS('Could not remove it'); return; }
    C.bdays = C.bdays.filter(function (b) { return lower(b.email) !== em; });
    closeSheet(); toastS('Birthday removed'); repaintAll();
  }

  /* ---------- person pages: the birthday pill ---------- */
  function personId() { var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/'); return h[0] === 'person' && h[1] ? h[1] : null; }
  var pillBusy = false;
  function personPill() {
    if (pillBusy || !staffOk()) return;
    var sec = document.querySelector('section#personpage:not([data-w116kdecoy])'); if (!sec || !sec.classList.contains('active')) return;
    var meta = sec.querySelector('.w116khead .meta'); if (!meta) return;
    var id = personId(), m0 = me();
    var m = id ? members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : members().filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === m0.email; })[0];
    if (!m) return;
    var self = lower(m.email) === m0.email && !!m0.email;
    var b = C.bdays.filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === lower(m.email); })[0];
    var html = b ? '<button type="button" class="w124bpill"' + (self ? ' data-w124bday="1" title="Change your birthday"' : '') + '>' + I('cake') + E(MONTHS[b.month - 1] + ' ' + b.day) + '</button>' : (self ? '<button type="button" class="w124bpill add" data-w124bday="1">' + I('cake') + 'Add your birthday</button>' : '');
    var cur = meta.querySelector('.w124bpill');
    var sig = (m.id || '') + '|' + html;
    if (cur && cur.getAttribute('data-sig') === sig) return;
    pillBusy = true;
    try { if (cur) cur.remove(); if (html) { meta.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html); var nb = meta.querySelector('.w124bpill'); if (nb) nb.setAttribute('data-sig', sig); } } finally { pillBusy = false; }
  }
  var ppT = null;
  function watchPerson() {
    var sec = document.querySelector('section#personpage:not([data-w116kdecoy])'); if (!sec || sec.__w124obs) return;
    sec.__w124obs = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (pillBusy) return; clearTimeout(ppT); ppT = setTimeout(personPill, 80); }).observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- menus: Calendar under Home, Events calendar in Fundraising ---------- */
  function placeTabs() {
    try {
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar) return;
      var LAY = !!(window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.layout); /* wave134: the menu layout places the calendar; this keeps the Events calendar in the Fundraising row */
      /* after Home; the board has no Home, so after Overview */
      var cal = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="calendar"]'), home = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="mypage"]');
      /* for the board (no Home): after the board pages (wave126 puts them right after Overview), else after Overview */
      var ov0 = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="overview"]'), cm0 = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="committees"]');
      var anchor = home && home.style.display !== 'none' ? home : (cm0 && ov0 && cm0.parentNode === ov0.parentNode && cm0.style.display !== 'none' ? cm0 : (ov0 || home));
      if (!LAY && cal && anchor && anchor.parentNode && anchor.nextSibling !== cal) anchor.parentNode.insertBefore(cal, anchor.nextSibling);
      var fe = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="fundevents"]');
      if (fe) {
        /* inside Fundraising when that page is yours; otherwise (the board) right after the calendar */
        var fr = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="fundraising"]'); var row = fr && fr.closest ? fr.closest('.w42d') : null; var sub = row ? row.querySelector('.w42sub') : null;
        if (sub && tabOk('fundraising')) { if (fe.parentNode !== sub) { var fin = sub.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="finances"]'); if (fin) sub.insertBefore(fe, fin); else sub.appendChild(fe); } fe.classList.add('w116tool'); }
        else if (!LAY && cal && cal.parentNode) { if (cal.nextSibling !== fe) cal.parentNode.insertBefore(fe, cal.nextSibling); fe.classList.remove('w116tool'); }
        fe.style.display = tabOk('fundevents') ? '' : 'none';
      }
      if (cal) cal.style.display = tabOk('calendar') ? '' : 'none';
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function tabOk(id) { try { return typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || (wa40Allowed() || []).indexOf(id) >= 0; } catch (e) { return true; } }
  var navT = null;
  function watchNav() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar || bar.__w124obs) return;
    bar.__w124obs = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (navT) return; navT = setTimeout(function () { navT = null; placeTabs(); }, 150); }).observe(bar, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function teamPill() {
    try {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || ''); if (h.indexOf('#team/fundraising') !== 0) return;
      var seg = document.querySelector('#team .w116seg'); if (!seg || seg.querySelector('[data-w116go="#fundevents"]')) return;
      var a = document.createElement('a'); a.setAttribute('data-w116go', '#fundevents'); a.textContent = 'Events calendar';
      var fin = seg.querySelector('[data-w116go="#finances"]'); if (fin) seg.insertBefore(a, fin); else seg.appendChild(a);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  var tpT = null;
  function watchTeam() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('team'); if (!sec || sec.__w124obs) return;
    sec.__w124obs = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(tpT); tpT = setTimeout(teamPill, 60); }).observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* people with their own tab list (hub_roles.tabs: 3 admins and 2 leaders today) never had these two pages on it:
     everyone signed in gets the calendar, and whoever can see Fundraising or Finances (or the board) gets the events calendar */
  function enrolAllowed() {
    try {
      if (typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || wa40Allowed.__w124) return;
      var __al = wa40Allowed;
      wa40Allowed = function () {
        var a = __al.apply(this, arguments) || [];
        if (role() === 'blocked') return a;
        var add = [];
