  /* ---------- state ---------- */
  var B = { data: null, loaded: 0, loading: null, me: null, meLoading: null, sheet: null, err: null, openDone: {} };
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function boardMode() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function wRole() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myName() { var n = ''; try { n = typeof who === 'function' ? String(who() || '') : ''; } catch (e) { n = ''; } return n || myEmail().split('@')[0]; }
  function isLeaderUI() { var r = wRole(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader'; }
  function isAdminUI() { return wRole() === 'admin'; }

  /* who am I on the board (board_me: director id, board person, leader, admin) */
  function fallbackMe() { return { member_id: null, board: boardMode(), leader: isLeaderUI(), admin: isAdminUI(), fallback: true }; }
  function loadMe(force) {
    if (B.me && !force) return Promise.resolve(B.me);
    if (B.meLoading) return B.meLoading;
    B.meLoading = Promise.resolve(db.rpc('board_me')).then(function (r) {
      var d = r && !r.error ? r.data : null;
      B.me = d && typeof d === 'object' ? d : fallbackMe(); B.meLoading = null; return B.me;
    }, function () { B.me = fallbackMe(); B.meLoading = null; return B.me; });
    return B.meLoading;
  }
  function canSee() { var m = B.me || fallbackMe(); return !!(m.board || m.leader || m.admin); }
  function meId() { var d = B.data; return d && d.me && d.me.member_id != null ? d.me.member_id : (B.me && B.me.member_id != null ? B.me.member_id : null); }
  function amLeader() { var d = B.data; return !!((d && d.me && (d.me.leader || d.me.admin)) || (B.me && (B.me.leader || B.me.admin)) || isLeaderUI()); }
  function amAdmin() { var d = B.data; return !!((d && d.me && d.me.admin) || (B.me && B.me.admin) || isAdminUI()); }
  function canEditItems() { return amLeader() || meId() != null; }
  function canLog() { return amAdmin() || meId() != null; }
  /* each director's progress is for the board itself and the admins, not for every leader */
  function seeDirectors() { return amAdmin() || meId() != null || !!(B.me && B.me.board) || boardMode(); }
  function canEditCommittee(c) { return amLeader() || (meId() != null && c && String(c.chair_id) === String(meId())); }

  /* ---------- loading: board_home(), or the tables when the function is not there (the offline harness) ---------- */
  function load(force) {
    if (B.loading) return B.loading;
    if (B.loaded && !force && Date.now() - B.loaded < 60000) return Promise.resolve(B.data);
    B.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = null; try { r = await db.rpc('board_home'); } catch (e) { r = null; }
        var d = r && !r.error ? r.data : null;
        if (!d || typeof d !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(d.members)) d = await fallbackHome();
        B.data = normalize(d); B.loaded = Date.now(); B.err = null;
      } catch (e) { B.err = String((e && e.message) || e); if (!B.data) B.data = normalize({}); }
      finally { B.loading = null; }
      return B.data;
    })();
    return B.loading;
  }
  function fyStartOf(d) { return d.getMonth() >= 9 ? mk(d.getFullYear(), 9, 1, 12) : mk(d.getFullYear() - 1, 9, 1, 12); }
  async function fallbackHome() {
    var q = function (p) { return Promise.resolve(p).then(function (r) { return (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; }, function () { return []; }); };
    var res = await Promise.all([
      q(db.from('board_members').select('*').order('sort')), q(db.from('board_committees').select('*').order('sort')), q(db.from('board_meetings').select('*')),
      q(db.from('board_items').select('*').order('sort')), q(db.from('board_touchpoints').select('*').order('happened_on', { ascending: false }).limit(400)),
      q(db.from('dept_tasks').select('id, title, owner, status, done, due_date, closed_at, opened_at, confidential').eq('department', 'board')),
      q(db.from('goals').select('id, title, status, progress, quarter, sort').eq('department', 'org')),
      q(db.from('fund_events').select('id, title, start_date, end_date, timing, status, raised')),
      q(db.from('hub_settings').select('key, value').eq('key', 'fund_goals')),
      q(db.from('finance_transactions').select('txn_date, amount, duplicate_of')),
      q(db.from('finance_statements').select('period_end'))
    ]);
    var em = myEmail(), mine = res[0].filter(function (m) { return (m.status === 'active' || m.status === 'adviser') && (m.emails || []).map(lower).indexOf(em) >= 0; })[0];
    var fs = fyStartOf(today()), fe = mk(fs.getFullYear() + 1, 9, 1, 12), ps = mk(fs.getFullYear() - 1, 9, 1, 12);
    var inc = 0, exp = 0, pinc = 0, pexp = 0, last = '';
    res[9].forEach(function (t) { if (t.duplicate_of) return; var d = parseDay(t.txn_date); if (!d) return; var a = Number(t.amount || 0); if (t.txn_date > last) last = t.txn_date; if (d >= fs && d < fe) { if (a > 0) inc += a; else exp -= a; } else if (d >= ps && d < fs) { if (a > 0) pinc += a; else pexp -= a; } });
    var lastSt = res[10].map(function (s) { return s.period_end || ''; }).sort().pop() || null;
    var t0 = today();
    var raised = res[7].filter(function (f) { var s = parseDay(f.start_date); return s && s >= fs && s < fe; }).reduce(function (a, f) { return a + Number(f.raised || 0); }, 0);
    var next = res[7].filter(function (f) { var e = parseDay(f.end_date) || parseDay(f.start_date); return (f.status === 'planned' || f.status === 'confirmed') && e && e >= t0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.start_date).localeCompare(String(b.start_date)); }).slice(0, 4);
    return {
      me: { member_id: mine ? mine.id : null, leader: isLeaderUI(), admin: isAdminUI(), email: em, fallback: true },
      members: res[0], committees: res[1], meetings: res[2], items: res[3], touchpoints: res[4],
      tasks: res[5].filter(function (t) { return !t.confidential; }), goals: res[6],
      finance: { fy_start: ymd(fs), income: Math.round(inc * 100) / 100, expenses: Math.round(exp * 100) / 100, prev_income: Math.round(pinc * 100) / 100, prev_expenses: Math.round(pexp * 100) / 100, last_txn: last || null, last_statement: lastSt },
      fund: { goals: (res[8][0] || {}).value || null, raised: raised, next: next }
    };
  }
  function normalize(d) {
    d = d || {};
    ['members', 'committees', 'meetings', 'items', 'touchpoints', 'tasks', 'goals'].forEach(function (k) { if (!Array.isArray(d[k])) d[k] = []; });
    d.committees.sort(function (a, b) { return ORDER.indexOf(a.key) - ORDER.indexOf(b.key); });
    d.meetings.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.meets_at || '9999').localeCompare(String(b.meets_at || '9999')); });
    d.touchpoints.sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.happened_on || '').localeCompare(String(a.happened_on || '')) || (b.id || 0) - (a.id || 0); });
    d.finance = d.finance || {}; d.fund = d.fund || {}; d.me = d.me || {};
    return d;
  }

  /* ---------- lookups ---------- */
  function D() { return B.data || normalize({}); }
  function members() { return D().members; }
  function directors() { return members().filter(function (m) { return m.status === 'active'; }); }
  function memberById(id) { return members().filter(function (m) { return String(m.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function memberName(id) { var m = memberById(id); return m ? m.name : ''; }
  function comms() { return D().committees; }
  function commByKey(k) { return comms().filter(function (c) { return c.key === k; })[0] || null; }
  function inCommittee(c, id) { return !!c && id != null && ((c.member_ids || []).map(String).indexOf(String(id)) >= 0 || String(c.chair_id) === String(id)); }
  function committeesOf(id) { return comms().filter(function (c) { return inCommittee(c, id); }); }
  function tps() { return D().touchpoints; }

  /* ---------- the cycle: from the last board meeting held to the next one ---------- */
  function boardMeetings() { return D().meetings.filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'board' || m.kind === 'special' || m.kind === 'agm'; }); }
  function nextMeeting() { var now = Date.now(); return boardMeetings().filter(function (m) { return m.meets_at && new Date(m.meets_at).getTime() >= now - 3 * 3600000 && m.status !== 'cancelled' && m.status !== 'held'; })[0] || null; }
  function lastMeeting() { var now = Date.now(); var held = boardMeetings().filter(function (m) { return m.meets_at && (m.status === 'held' || new Date(m.meets_at).getTime() < now - 3 * 3600000) && m.status !== 'cancelled'; }); return held[held.length - 1] || null; }
  function strategySession() { return D().meetings.filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'strategy' && m.status !== 'cancelled' && m.status !== 'held'; })[0] || null; }
  function cycleStart() { var l = lastMeeting(); return l ? dayOfIso(l.meets_at) : addDays(today(), -91); }
  function cycleEnd() { var n = nextMeeting(); return n ? dayOfIso(n.meets_at) : null; }
  function committeeDue() { var e = cycleEnd(); return e ? addDays(e, -7) : null; }
  function inCycle(t) { var s = cycleStart(), d = parseDay(t.happened_on); return !!d && (!s || d > s || (t.meeting_id != null && nextMeeting() && String(t.meeting_id) === String(nextMeeting().id))); }
  function cycleTps(c) { return tps().filter(function (t) { return t.committee === c.key && inCycle(t); }); }
  function isRec(t) { return t.kind === 'recommendation' || !!String(t.recommendation || '').trim(); }
  function steps(c) {
    var tp = cycleTps(c);
    var first = function (f) { return tp.filter(f).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.happened_on).localeCompare(String(b.happened_on)); })[0] || null; };
    var i = first(function (t) { return t.kind === 'internal'; }), x = first(function (t) { return t.kind === 'external'; }), r = first(isRec);
    return [
      { k: 'chair', t: 'Chair', done: !!c.chair_id, note: c.chair_id ? memberName(c.chair_id) + (c.chair_confirmed ? '' : ' (proposed)') : 'No chair yet' },
      { k: 'internal', t: 'Internal touchpoint', done: !!i, note: i ? fmtDay(parseDay(i.happened_on)) + (i.with_whom ? ', with ' + i.with_whom : '') : 'Meet the team lead' },
      { k: 'external', t: 'External touchpoint', done: !!x, note: x ? fmtDay(parseDay(x.happened_on)) + (x.with_whom ? ', with ' + x.with_whom : '') : 'A session with an outside professional' },
      { k: 'board', t: 'Bring it to the board', done: !!r, note: r ? 'Ready for the board' : 'A recommendation for the meeting' }
    ];
  }
  function stepsDone(c) { return steps(c).filter(function (s) { return s.done; }).length; }
  function myCycleTps(id, c) { return tps().filter(function (t) { return String(t.member_id) === String(id) && inCycle(t) && (!c || t.committee === c.key); }); }
  function tasksOf(m) {
    if (!m) return [];
    var re; try { re = new RegExp('(^|[^a-z])' + String(m.name || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + '([^a-z]|$)', 'i'); } catch (e) { return []; }
    return D().tasks.filter(function (t) { return re.test(String(t.owner || '')); });
  }
  function renewalState(m) {
    var d = parseDay(m.renewal_due); if (!d) return null;
    var n = daysBetween(today(), d);
    if (n < 0) return { k: 'past', t: 'Renewal was due ' + (m.renewal || fmtDay(d, true)) };
    if (n <= 60) return { k: 'soon', t: 'Renewal due ' + (m.renewal || fmtDay(d, true)) };
    return { k: 'ok', t: 'Term to ' + (m.renewal || fmtDay(d, true)) };
  }

  /* ---------- the masthead and the section nav ---------- */
  function mast(id, crumb, title, blurb) {
    var sec = document.getElementById(id); if (!sec) return;
    var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w126', id); sec.insertBefore(m, sec.firstChild); }
    var html = '<div class="crumb">' + E(crumb) + '</div><h1>' + E(title) + '</h1><p>' + E(blurb) + '</p>';
    if (m.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { m.innerHTML = html; m.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
  }
  function host(id, hid) {
    var sec = document.getElementById(id); if (!sec) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById(hid);
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = hid; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function segHtml(on) {
    var a = [['#governance', 'Overview', 'gov'], ['#committees', 'Committees', 'com']];
    if (seeDirectors()) a.push(['#governance/directors', 'Directors', 'dir']);
    if (staffOk() && isTabAllowed('board')) a.push(['#team/board', 'Team page', 'tp']);
    if (staffOk() && isTabAllowed('board')) { a.push(['#team/board/agenda', 'Agendas', 'ag']); a.push(['#team/board/tasks', 'Board tasks', 'tk']); }
    return '<nav class="w126seg" aria-label="Board">' + a.map(function (x) { return '<a data-w126go="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (x[2] === on ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</nav>';
  }
  function isTabAllowed(id) { try { return typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || (wa40Allowed() || []).indexOf(id) >= 0; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function stTag(s) { var x = ST[s] || ST.todo; return '<span class="w126st" style="background:' + x[1] + ';color:' + x[2] + '">' + E(x[0]) + '</span>'; }

  /* ---------- the next board meeting and the cycle ---------- */
  function meetHtml() {
    var n = nextMeeting(), l = lastMeeting(), s = strategySession(), due = committeeDue(), t = today();
    var nd = n ? dayOfIso(n.meets_at) : null, left = nd ? daysBetween(t, nd) : null;
    var edit = amLeader();
    return '<div class="w126card w126meet">'
      + '<div><div class="lb">Next board meeting' + (edit && n ? '<a class="ed" data-w126meet="' + n.id + '">Edit</a>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (n ? '<b>' + E(n.title) + '</b><span>' + E(fmtWhen(n.meets_at)) + (n.status === 'tentative' ? ' (tentative)' : '') + '</span>' + (left != null ? '<span class="cd">' + (left === 0 ? 'Today' : left === 1 ? 'Tomorrow' : 'In ' + left + ' days') + '</span>' : '')
        : '<b>Not scheduled yet</b><span>Four board meetings a year, plus an AGM.</span>' + (edit ? '<span><a data-w126meet="new">Schedule it</a></span>' : ''))
      + '</div>'
      + '<div><div class="lb">Committees meet by</div>' + (due ? '<b>' + E(fmtDay(due)) + '</b><span>A week before the board meeting, so everyone arrives informed.</span>' : '<b>A week before</b><span>each board meeting.</span>') + '</div>'
      + '<div><div class="lb">Last meeting</div>' + (l ? '<b>' + E(fmtDay(dayOfIso(l.meets_at), true)) + '</b><span>' + E(l.title) + (l.notes_url ? ' \u00b7 <a href="' + E(l.notes_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">minutes</a>' : '') + '</span>' : '<b>None yet</b>') + '</div>'
      + '<div><div class="lb">Strategy session' + (edit && s ? '<a class="ed" data-w126meet="' + s.id + '">Edit</a>' : '') + '</div>' + (s ? '<b>' + (s.meets_at ? E(fmtDay(dayOfIso(s.meets_at))) : 'To schedule') + '</b><span>Board and operations: assumptions, who we serve, the plan.</span>' : '<b>Every October</b><span>The fiscal year-end deep dive.</span>') + '</div>'
      + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the three jobs ---------- */
  function itemHtml(it) {
    var task = it.task_id ? D().tasks.filter(function (t) { return String(t.id) === String(it.task_id); })[0] : null;
    var meta = [it.owner, it.due ? 'Due ' + it.due : '', task ? 'Board task: ' + (task.done ? 'done' : (task.status || 'open')) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ');
    return '<div class="w126it' + (it.status === 'done' ? ' done' : '') + '" data-w126item="' + it.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button">' + stTag(it.status) + '<div><b>' + E(it.title) + '</b>' + (it.detail && it.status !== 'done' ? '<span class="d">' + E(clip(it.detail, 150)) + '</span>' : '') + (meta ? '<em>' + E(meta) + '</em>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function jobItems(k) {
    var rank = { at_risk: 0, in_progress: 1, todo: 2, watch: 3, standing: 4, done: 5 };
    return D().items.filter(function (i) { return i.job === k; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (rank[a.status] - rank[b.status]) || (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
  }
  function stewardExtra() {
    var ds = directors().filter(function (m) { return m.renewal_due || m.renewal; });
    var rows = ds.map(function (m) { var r = renewalState(m); return '<div><b>' + E(m.name) + '</b>' + (r ? '<span class="w126st ' + r.k + '">' + E(r.t) + '</span>' : '<span class="w126st ok">' + E(m.renewal || 'No renewal set') + '</span>') + '</div>'; }).join('');
    var open = members().filter(function (m) { return m.status === 'resigned'; });
    return '<div class="w126x"><h4>Terms and renewals</h4><div class="w126ren">' + (rows || '<p class="w126empty">No terms recorded yet.</p>') + '</div>'
      + (open.length ? '<p>' + E(open.length === 1 ? 'One seat is open' : open.length + ' seats are open') + ' (HR & People).</p>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function fiduciaryExtra() {
    var f = D().finance || {}, fs = parseDay(f.fy_start), net = Number(f.income || 0) - Number(f.expenses || 0), pnet = Number(f.prev_income || 0) - Number(f.prev_expenses || 0);
    var ls = parseDay(f.last_statement || f.last_txn), stale = ls ? daysBetween(ls, today()) > 45 : true;
    var fyl = fs ? 'Fiscal ' + fs.getFullYear() + '-' + String(fs.getFullYear() + 1).slice(2) : 'This fiscal year';
    var fund = D().fund || {}, g = fund.goals || {};
    return '<div class="w126x"><h4>' + E(fyl) + (ls ? ', to ' + E(fmtDay(ls)) : '') + '</h4><div class="w126fin">'
      + '<div><b>' + E(money(f.income)) + '</b><span>came in</span></div><div><b>' + E(money(f.expenses)) + '</b><span>went out</span></div><div class="' + (net >= 0 ? 'pos' : 'neg') + '"><b>' + (net >= 0 ? '+' : '') + E(money(net)) + '</b><span>net</span></div></div>'
      + (f.prev_income || f.prev_expenses ? '<p>Last fiscal year: ' + E(money(f.prev_income)) + ' in, ' + E(money(f.prev_expenses)) + ' out (' + (pnet >= 0 ? '+' : '') + E(money(pnet)) + ').</p>' : '')
      + (stale ? '<p class="warn">The statements stop at ' + (ls ? E(fmtDay(ls, true)) : 'no date yet') + ': the newer months are not in the Hub yet.</p>' : '')
      + '<p>Fundraising events raised ' + E(money(fund.raised || 0)) + ' this fiscal year' + (g.target ? '; the 2026-27 goal is ' + E(money(g.minimum || 0)) + ' minimum, ' + E(money(g.target)) + ' target' : '') + '. <a class="w126lnk" data-w126go="#fundevents">Events calendar</a></p></div>';
  }
  function strategyExtra() {
    var steps3 = D().items.filter(function (i) { return i.job === 'strategy' && /^Step \d/i.test(i.title || ''); }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)); });
    var goals = D().goals || [];
    var gst = function (s) { s = lower(s); return s === 'on track' || s === 'done' || s === 'complete' ? 'in_progress' : s === 'at risk' || s === 'off track' ? 'at_risk' : s === 'not started' ? 'todo' : 'in_progress'; };
    return '<div class="w126x"><h4>Strategy starts with shared assumptions</h4>'
      + (steps3.length ? '<div class="w126steps3">' + steps3.map(function (s) { var m = /^Step (\d)[:.]?\s*(.*)$/i.exec(s.title) || [0, '', s.title]; return '<div class="' + (s.status === 'done' ? 'done' : '') + '" data-w126item="' + s.id + '"><small>Step ' + E(m[1]) + (s.status === 'done' ? ' \u00b7 done' : '') + '</small><b>' + E(m[2].replace(/^./, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); })) + '</b></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + '</div>'
      + (goals.length ? '<div class="w126x"><h4>Organizational goals</h4>' + goals.map(function (gl) { return '<div class="w126goal"><b>' + E(gl.title) + '</b>' + stTag(gst(gl.status)).replace(/>[^<]*<\/span>$/, '>' + E(gl.status || '') + '</span>') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '<p><a class="w126lnk" data-w126go="#orggoals">Organizational goals</a></p></div>' : '');
  }
  function jobHtml(j) {
    var list = jobItems(j.k), visible = list.filter(function (i) { return i.status !== 'done' && !(j.k === 'strategy' && /^Step \d/i.test(i.title || '')); }), done = list.filter(function (i) { return i.status === 'done'; });
    var total = list.filter(function (i) { return i.status !== 'standing' && i.status !== 'watch'; }).length, nd = done.length, pct = total ? Math.round(nd / total * 100) : 0;
    var extra = j.k === 'steward' ? stewardExtra() : j.k === 'fiduciary' ? fiduciaryExtra() : strategyExtra();
    var showDone = !!B.openDone[j.k];
    return '<div class="w126card w126job" id="w126job-' + j.k + '" style="--c:' + j.c + '"><div class="jh"><div class="jn"><i>' + j.n + '</i><h3>' + E(j.t) + '</h3></div><p class="jd">' + E(j.d) + '</p>'
      + '<div class="jp"><span>' + nd + ' of ' + total + ' done</span><span class="bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></span></div></div>'
      + '<div class="jb">' + (visible.length ? visible.map(itemHtml).join('') : '<p class="w126empty" style="padding:8px">Nothing open.</p>')
      + (done.length ? '<a class="w126tog" data-w126done="' + j.k + '">' + I(showDone ? 'x' : 'check') + (showDone ? 'Hide what is done' : done.length + ' done') + '</a>' + (showDone ? done.map(itemHtml).join('') : '') : '')
      + (canEditItems() ? '<a class="w126tog" data-w126newitem="' + j.k + '">' + I('plus') + 'Add an item</a>' : '')
      + extra + '</div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- each director's progress ---------- */
  function dirCardHtml(m) {
    var cs = committeesOf(m.id), mine = String(m.id) === String(meId());
    var r = renewalState(m), my = myCycleTps(m.id), recs = my.filter(isRec).length, ts = tasksOf(m), open = ts.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }).length, dn = ts.filter(function (t) { return t.done; }).length;
    var rows = cs.map(function (c) {
      var n = stepsDone(c);
      return '<div class="row" style="--c:' + E(c.color || GREEN) + '"><b><i></i>' + E(c.name) + (String(c.chair_id) === String(m.id) ? ' <span class="ch">Chair</span>' : '') + '</b><span class="w126dots">' + [0, 1, 2, 3].map(function (i) { return '<i class="' + (i < n ? 'on' : '') + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</span><small>' + n + ' of 4</small></div>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="w126card w126dir" data-w126dir="' + m.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><div class="dh"><span class="w126av" style="background:' + (cs[0] ? E(cs[0].color || GREEN) : GREEN) + '">' + E(initials(m.full_name || m.name)) + '</span><div><b>' + E(m.full_name || m.name) + '</b><span>' + E(m.seat || 'Director') + '</span></div>' + (mine ? '<span class="me">You</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w126cm">' + (rows || '<p class="w126empty">Not on a committee yet.</p>') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="ft">' + (my.length ? '<span class="hl">' + my.length + ' logged this cycle' + (recs ? ', ' + recs + ' recommendation' + (recs === 1 ? '' : 's') : '') + '</span>' : '<span>Nothing logged this cycle</span>')
      + (ts.length ? '<span>' + open + ' open, ' + dn + ' done board tasks</span>' : '') + (r ? '<span>' + E(r.t) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function dirsHtml() {
    var ds = directors(), others = members().filter(function (m) { return m.status !== 'active'; });
    var cs = cycleStart(), ce = cycleEnd();
    return '<div class="w126sec" id="w126dirs"><h2>The directors</h2><span>' + (cs && ce ? 'This cycle: ' + E(fmtDay(cs)) + ' to ' + E(fmtDay(ce)) + '. ' : '') + 'Each committee\u2019s steps, what each director logged, their board tasks and their term.</span></div>'
      + (ds.length ? '<div class="w126dirs">' + ds.map(dirCardHtml).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w126empty">No directors recorded yet.</p>')
      + (others.length ? '<div class="w126others">' + others.map(function (m) { return '<span>' + E(m.full_name || m.name) + ': ' + E(m.status === 'adviser' ? m.seat || 'Adviser' : m.status === 'resigned' ? 'resigned' + (m.note ? ' (' + clip(m.note.replace(/;.*$/, ''), 40) + ')' : '') : m.status) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
  }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  function govPaint() {
    var h = host('governance', 'w126gov'); if (!h) return;
    mast('governance', 'Board & Governance', 'Board overview', 'Everything the board does ladders up to one of three jobs. Where each one stands, and how each director is progressing on their committees.');
    if (!canSee()) { h.innerHTML = '<div class="w126card"><p class="w126empty">The board pages are for directors, board advisers and leaders.</p></div>'; return; }
    if (!B.data) { h.innerHTML = segHtml('gov') + '<div class="w126card"><p class="w126empty">Loading the board\u2026</p></div>'; return; }
    var html = segHtml(B.dirFocus ? 'dir' : 'gov') + meetHtml()
      + '<div class="w126sec"><h2>The three jobs of a board</h2><span>Everything the board does should ladder up to one of these three.</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w126jobs">' + JOBS.map(jobHtml).join('') + '</div>'
      + (seeDirectors() ? dirsHtml() : '');
    if (h.__h !== html) { h.innerHTML = html; h.__h = html; }
  }
  async function govShow(sub) {
    var h = host('governance', 'w126gov'); if (!h) return;
    B.dirFocus = sub === 'directors';
    await loadMe();
    govPaint();
    if (!canSee()) return;
    await load();
    if (onPage('governance')) {
      govPaint();
      if (sub === 'directors') { var el = document.getElementById('w126dirs'); if (el && el.scrollIntoView) el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); }
      else if (sub && /^director\/\d+$/.test(sub)) openDirector(sub.split('/')[1]);
      else if (sub && JOB[sub]) { var j = document.getElementById('w126job-' + sub); if (j && j.scrollIntoView) j.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); }
    }
  }
  function onPage(id) { var a = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); return !!(a && a.id === id); }

  /* ---------- the committees ---------- */
  function namesOf(ids) { return (ids || []).map(memberName).filter(Boolean); }
  function stepHtml(s) { return '<div class="' + (s.done ? 'done' : '') + '"><small>' + (s.done ? I('check') : '') + E(s.t) + '</small><b>' + E(s.note) + '</b></div>'; }
  function logRowHtml(t) {
    var k = KIND[t.kind] || KIND.note, who = memberName(t.member_id) || t.logged_by_name || '';
    var line = t.kind === 'recommendation' ? (t.recommendation || t.summary) : (t.summary || t.recommendation || '');
    return '<div class="w126lg" data-w126tp="' + t.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="k ' + E(t.kind) + '">' + E(k[1].replace(/ (touchpoint|working session|for the board)$/, '')) + '</span><div><b>' + E(clip(line || (t.with_whom ? 'With ' + t.with_whom : k[1]), 120)) + '</b><span>' + E(fmtDay(parseDay(t.happened_on), true)) + (who ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(who) : '') + (t.with_whom && line ? ' \u00b7 with ' + E(t.with_whom) : '') + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function comHtml(c) {
    var st = steps(c), n = st.filter(function (s) { return s.done; }).length, dirs = (c.member_ids || []).map(memberById).filter(Boolean);
    var parts = dirs.map(function (m) {
      var mine = myCycleTps(m.id, c), kinds = {};
      mine.forEach(function (t) { kinds[t.kind] = (kinds[t.kind] || 0) + 1; });
      var txt = mine.length ? Object.keys(kinds).map(function (k) { return kinds[k] + ' ' + (KIND[k] ? KIND[k][1].toLowerCase() : k) + (kinds[k] > 1 ? 's' : ''); }).join(', ') : '';
      return '<div class="row"><span class="w126av" style="background:' + E(c.color || GREEN) + '">' + E(initials(m.full_name || m.name)) + '</span><b>' + E(m.name) + (String(c.chair_id) === String(m.id) ? ' (chair)' : '') + '</b><span class="tx' + (txt ? '' : ' none') + '">' + E(txt || 'Nothing logged this cycle') + '</span></div>';
    }).join('');
    var log = tps().filter(function (t) { return t.committee === c.key; });
    var chair = c.chair_id ? memberName(c.chair_id) : '';
    return '<div class="w126card w126com" id="w126com-' + c.key + '" style="--c:' + E(c.color || GREEN) + '">'
      + '<div class="ch"><div class="tt"><h3>' + E(c.name) + '</h3><span class="sp"></span><span class="w126st" style="background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);color:' + GREEN + '">' + n + ' of 4 this cycle</span></div>' + (c.purpose ? '<p>' + E(c.purpose) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w126ros"><div><small>Chair</small><b>' + E(chair || 'To name') + (chair && !c.chair_confirmed ? '<em>proposed</em>' : '') + '</b></div>'
      + '<div><small>Directors</small><b>' + E(namesOf(c.member_ids).join(', ') || 'None yet') + '</b></div>'
      + '<div><small>Internal leads</small><b>' + E((c.internal_leads || []).join(', ') || 'To name') + '</b></div>'
      + '<div><small>To recruit</small><b>' + E(c.external_recruit || 'An outside professional') + '</b></div></div>'
      + '<div class="w126stp">' + st.map(stepHtml).join('') + '</div>'
      + (seeDirectors() ? '<div class="w126part"><h4>Each director this cycle</h4>' + (parts || '<p class="w126empty">No directors on this committee yet.</p>') + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="w126log"><h4>The log</h4>' + (log.length ? log.slice(0, 4).map(logRowHtml).join('') + (log.length > 4 ? '<a class="w126tog" data-w126all="' + c.key + '">' + I('chev') + 'All ' + log.length + ' entries</a>' : '') : '<p class="w126empty">Nothing logged yet. Start with a touchpoint with the team lead.</p>') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="cf">' + (canLog(c) ? '<a class="w126btn sm" data-w126log="' + c.key + '">' + I('plus') + 'Log a touchpoint</a>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>'
      + (c.depts && c.depts.length && staffOk() ? '<a class="w126btn ghost sm" data-w126go="#team/' + E(c.depts[0]) + '">' + I('people') + 'The team</a>' : '')
      + (canEditCommittee(c) ? '<a class="w126btn ghost sm" data-w126cedit="' + c.key + '">' + I('pen') + 'Edit</a>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function howHtml() {
    var how = [['1', 'Chair', 'Coordinates the meetings and owns the follow-through.', GREEN], ['2', 'Touchpoint 1: internal', 'Meet the team lead to get the real picture from the ground.', '#2A6F55'], ['3', 'Touchpoint 2: external', 'A 30 to 60 minute working session with a professional from your network.', PURPLE], ['4', 'Bring it to the board', 'Arrive with informed views and a recommendation: fuel for real debate.', '#C8961F']];
    var cs = cycleStart(), ce = cycleEnd(), due = committeeDue();
    return '<div class="w126how">' + how.map(function (h) { return '<div style="--c:' + h[3] + '"><i>' + h[0] + '</i><b>' + E(h[1]) + '</b><span>' + E(h[2]) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w126cyc">' + I('clock') + '<span>' + (ce ? 'This cycle runs from the <b>' + E(fmtDay(cs)) + '</b> meeting to the <b>' + E(fmtDay(ce)) + '</b> meeting. Committees meet by <b>' + E(fmtDay(due)) + '</b>: once with the team, once with an outside professional.' : 'Committees meet twice between board meetings: once with the team, once with an outside professional.') + '</span></div>';
  }
  function stratHtml() {
    var s = strategySession(), chair = directors().filter(function (m) { return /^chair\b/i.test(String(m.seat || '').trim()); })[0];
    return '<div class="w126strat"><div class="w126card dark"><h3>Strategy: the whole board</h3><p>Strategy stays with the full board, everyone\u2019s job, run as a dedicated block at every meeting' + (chair ? ', led by the board chair (' + E(chair.name) + ')' : '') + '.</p><p>' + (s ? 'Next: ' + E(s.title) + (s.meets_at ? ', ' + E(fmtDay(dayOfIso(s.meets_at), true)) : ' (to schedule)') + '. ' : '') + '<a class="w126lnk" data-w126go="#governance/strategy">Where strategy stands</a></p></div>'
      + '<div class="w126card"><h3>Cadence</h3><ul><li>4 board meetings a year, plus an AGM</li><li>Each committee meets twice between board meetings: once with the team, once with an outside professional</li><li>Monthly for committees chasing a target</li><li>An October fiscal year-end strategy session</li></ul></div>'
      + '<div class="w126card"><h3>Recruiting beyond the board</h3><ul><li>Our networks: the fastest yes</li><li>Donors, past and standout volunteers</li><li>Professors: public policy, Indigenous studies, IT</li><li>Director networks (ICD) and working groups as on-ramps</li></ul></div></div>';
  }
  function comPaint() {
    var h = host('committees', 'w126com'); if (!h) return;
    mast('committees', 'Board & Governance', 'Committees', 'Four working committees, each chaired by a director: meet the team, bring in an outside professional, and arrive at the board with a recommendation.');
    if (!canSee()) { h.innerHTML = '<div class="w126card"><p class="w126empty">The board pages are for directors, board advisers and leaders.</p></div>'; return; }
    if (!B.data) { h.innerHTML = segHtml('com') + '<div class="w126card"><p class="w126empty">Loading the committees\u2026</p></div>'; return; }
    var cs = comms();
    var html = segHtml('com') + howHtml()
      + '<div class="w126sec"><h2>The four committees</h2><span>From reporting to engaging: each committee owns an area, gathers context and brings an informed view.</span></div>'
      + (cs.length ? '<div class="w126coms">' + cs.map(comHtml).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w126empty">No committees yet.</p>')
      + stratHtml();
    if (h.__h !== html) { h.innerHTML = html; h.__h = html; }
  }
  async function comShow(sub) {
    var h = host('committees', 'w126com'); if (!h) return;
    await loadMe();
    comPaint();
    if (!canSee()) return;
    await load();
    if (onPage('committees')) {
      comPaint();
      if (sub && commByKey(sub)) { var el = document.getElementById('w126com-' + sub); if (el && el.scrollIntoView) el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); }
    }
  }

  /* ---------- the sheet ---------- */
  function sheetEl() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w126sheet');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w126sheet'; el.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); el.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); document.body.appendChild(el); }
    return el;
  }
  function openSheet(kind, kicker, title, body, foot) {
    var el = sheetEl();
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w126close="1"></div><div class="pane"><div class="ph"><div><div class="kk">' + E(kicker) + '</div><h2>' + E(title) + '</h2></div><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="xb" data-w126close="1" aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div><div class="pb">' + body + '</div>' + (foot ? '<div class="pf">' + foot + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    el.classList.add('on'); B.sheet = kind;
    var f = el.querySelector('input:not([type=hidden]):not([type=radio]):not([type=checkbox]),textarea,select'); if (f && kind.indexOf('form') >= 0) { try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function closeSheet() { var el = document.getElementById('w126sheet'); if (el) { el.classList.remove('on'); el.innerHTML = ''; } B.sheet = null; }
  function sheetErr(m) { var e = document.querySelector('#w126sheet .err'); if (e) e.textContent = m || ''; }
  function afterSave(msg) {
    toastS(msg); closeSheet(); repaint();
    /* refresh from the database; the offline copy (no board_home) keeps what was just saved */
    if (B.data && B.data.me && B.data.me.fallback) return Promise.resolve();
    B.loaded = 0; return load(true).then(repaint);
  }
  function repaint() { if (onPage('governance')) govPaint(); if (onPage('committees')) comPaint(); }
  function nowIso() { return new Date().toISOString(); }
  async function run(p) { var r = await p; if (r && r.error) throw r.error; return r; }

  /* log a touchpoint (or change your own) */
  function openLog(key, tp) {
    var c = commByKey(key) || comms()[0]; if (!c) return;
    var t = tp || null, kind = t ? t.kind : 'internal';
    var n = nextMeeting(), meets = boardMeetings().filter(function (m) { return m.status !== 'held' && m.status !== 'cancelled'; });
    var admin = amAdmin(), me0 = meId();
    var who = admin ? '<label>On behalf of<select name="member_id">' + '<option value="">Nobody in particular</option>' + directors().map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + m.id + '"' + (String((t ? t.member_id : me0)) === String(m.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.full_name || m.name) + (inCommittee(c, m.id) ? '' : ' (not on this committee)') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' : '';
    var body = '<form data-w126logform="' + E(c.key) + '"' + (t ? ' data-id="' + t.id + '"' : '') + '>'
      + '<div class="kinds">' + KINDS.map(function (k) { return '<label><input type="radio" name="kind" value="' + k[0] + '"' + (k[0] === kind ? ' checked' : '') + '><div>' + E(k[1]) + '<span>' + E(k[2]) + '</span></div></label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="two"><label>When<input type="date" name="happened_on" value="' + E(t ? t.happened_on : ymd(today())) + '"></label><label>With whom<input type="text" name="with_whom" value="' + E(t ? t.with_whom || '' : '') + '" placeholder="For example: Amanda, the fundraising lead"></label></div>'
      + '<label>What we learned<textarea name="summary" placeholder="The real picture: what is working, what is stuck, what they need">' + E(t ? t.summary || '' : '') + '</textarea></label>'
      + '<label>Recommendation for the board <small>optional unless this is the recommendation</small><textarea name="recommendation" placeholder="What the committee recommends, and why">' + E(t ? t.recommendation || '' : '') + '</textarea></label>'
      + (meets.length ? '<label>For the board meeting<select name="meeting_id"><option value="">None in particular</option>' + meets.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + m.id + '"' + (String(t ? t.meeting_id : (n && n.id)) === String(m.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.title) + (m.meets_at ? ', ' + E(fmtDay(dayOfIso(m.meets_at), true)) : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' : '')
      + who + '<div class="err" data-w126err="1"></div></form>';
    openSheet('logform', c.name + ' committee', t ? 'Change the entry' : 'Log a touchpoint', body,
      (t ? '<button type="button" class="w126btn danger sm" data-w126tpdel="' + t.id + '">Delete</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w126btn ghost" data-w126close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w126btn" data-w126logsave="1">' + I('check') + (t ? 'Save' : 'Log it') + '</button>');
  }
  var saving = false;
  async function saveLog() {
    var f = document.querySelector('#w126sheet form[data-w126logform]'); if (!f || saving) return;
    var key = f.getAttribute('data-w126logform'), id = f.getAttribute('data-id');
    var g = function (n) { var el = f.querySelector('[name="' + n + '"]'); return el ? String(el.value || '').trim() : ''; };
    var kind = (f.querySelector('input[name=kind]:checked') || {}).value || 'note';
    var row = { committee: key, kind: kind, happened_on: g('happened_on') || ymd(today()), with_whom: g('with_whom') || null, summary: g('summary') || null, recommendation: g('recommendation') || null, meeting_id: g('meeting_id') ? Number(g('meeting_id')) : null, updated_at: nowIso() };
    if (!row.summary && !row.recommendation) { sheetErr('Write what you learned, or the recommendation.'); return; }
    if (kind === 'recommendation' && !row.recommendation) { row.recommendation = row.summary; }
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(row.happened_on)) { sheetErr('Pick the date.'); return; }
    var sel = f.querySelector('[name=member_id]');
    if (sel) row.member_id = sel.value ? Number(sel.value) : null; else if (!id) row.member_id = meId();
    saving = true;
    try {
      if (id) { await run(db.from('board_touchpoints').update(row).eq('id', Number(id))); var tpl = tps().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (tpl) Object.assign(tpl, row); }
      else { row.logged_by_name = myName(); await run(db.from('board_touchpoints').insert(row)); }
      if (!id) { /* show it right away, also where the database copy is slow to come back */ D().touchpoints.unshift(Object.assign({ id: -Date.now(), logged_by_email: myEmail(), created_at: nowIso() }, row)); }
      await afterSave(id ? 'Saved' : 'Logged. It counts toward the ' + (commByKey(key) || {}).name + ' committee\u2019s cycle.');
    } catch (e) { sheetErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    finally { saving = false; }
  }
  async function deleteTp(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this entry?')) return;
    try { await run(db.from('board_touchpoints').delete().eq('id', Number(id))); B.data.touchpoints = B.data.touchpoints.filter(function (t) { return String(t.id) !== String(id); }); await afterSave('Deleted'); }
    catch (e) { sheetErr('Could not delete it: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 100)); }
  }
  function openTp(id) {
    var t = tps().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!t) return;
    var c = commByKey(t.committee) || {}, k = KIND[t.kind] || KIND.note, own = lower(t.logged_by_email) === myEmail() || amAdmin();
    var body = '<dl class="kv"><dt>Committee</dt><dd>' + E(c.name || t.committee) + '</dd><dt>When</dt><dd>' + E(fmtDay(parseDay(t.happened_on), true)) + '</dd>'
      + (t.with_whom ? '<dt>With</dt><dd>' + E(t.with_whom) + '</dd>' : '') + '<dt>Logged by</dt><dd>' + E(memberName(t.member_id) || t.logged_by_name || t.logged_by_email || '') + '</dd></dl>'
      + (t.summary ? '<div>' + E(t.summary).replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</div>' : '')
      + (t.recommendation ? '<div class="quote"><b>Recommendation:</b> ' + E(t.recommendation).replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</div>' : '');
    openSheet('tp', c.name ? c.name + ' committee' : 'Committee', k[1], body, own ? '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w126btn ghost" data-w126tpedit="' + t.id + '">' + I('pen') + 'Change it</button>' : '');
  }

  /* a director */
  function openDirector(id) {
    var m = memberById(id); if (!m) return;
    var cs = committeesOf(m.id), my = tps().filter(function (t) { return String(t.member_id) === String(m.id); }), ts = tasksOf(m), r = renewalState(m);
    var body = '<dl class="kv"><dt>Seat</dt><dd>' + E(m.seat || 'Director') + '</dd>' + (m.renewal || r ? '<dt>Term</dt><dd>' + E(r ? r.t : m.renewal) + '</dd>' : '') + (m.note ? '<dt>Note</dt><dd>' + E(m.note) + '</dd>' : '') + '</dl>'
      + '<div><div class="kk" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted);margin-bottom:6px">Committees</div>' + (cs.length ? cs.map(function (c) { var st = steps(c); return '<div class="w126cm"><div class="row" style="--c:' + E(c.color || GREEN) + '"><b><i></i>' + E(c.name) + (String(c.chair_id) === String(m.id) ? ' <span class="ch">Chair</span>' : '') + '</b><span class="w126dots">' + st.map(function (s) { return '<i class="' + (s.done ? 'on' : '') + '" title="' + E(s.t) + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</span><small>' + stepsDone(c) + ' of 4</small></div></div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w126empty">Not on a committee yet.</p>') + '</div>'
      + '<div><div class="kk" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted);margin-bottom:6px">What ' + E(m.name) + ' logged</div>' + (my.length ? my.slice(0, 8).map(logRowHtml).join('') : '<p class="w126empty">Nothing logged yet.</p>') + '</div>'
      + (ts.length ? '<div><div class="kk" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted);margin-bottom:6px">Board tasks</div>' + ts.map(function (t) { return '<div class="w126goal"><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b>' + stTag(t.done ? 'done' : 'todo').replace(/>[^<]*<\/span>$/, '>' + E(t.done ? 'Done' : (t.status || 'Open')) + '</span>') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
    openSheet('director', m.status === 'active' ? 'Director' : 'Board', m.full_name || m.name, body, amAdmin() ? '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w126btn ghost" data-w126medit="' + m.id + '">' + I('pen') + 'Edit the seat and term</button>' : '');
  }
  function openMemberEdit(id) {
    var m = memberById(id); if (!m) return;
    var body = '<form data-w126mform="' + m.id + '"><div class="two"><label>Seat<input type="text" name="seat" value="' + E(m.seat || '') + '"></label><label>Status<select name="status">' + ['active', 'adviser', 'resigned', 'former'].map(function (s) { return '<option' + (m.status === s ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label></div>'
      + '<div class="two"><label>Term runs to <small>as said, for example Q3 2026</small><input type="text" name="renewal" value="' + E(m.renewal || '') + '"></label><label>Renewal date<input type="date" name="renewal_due" value="' + E(m.renewal_due || '') + '"></label></div>'
      + '<label>Emails they sign in with <small>comma separated</small><input type="text" name="emails" value="' + E((m.emails || []).join(', ')) + '"></label>'
      + '<label>Note<textarea name="note">' + E(m.note || '') + '</textarea></label><div class="err"></div></form>';
    openSheet('mform', 'Director', m.full_name || m.name, body, '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w126btn ghost" data-w126close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w126btn" data-w126msave="1">' + I('check') + 'Save</button>');
  }
  async function saveMember() {
    var f = document.querySelector('#w126sheet form[data-w126mform]'); if (!f || saving) return;
    var g = function (n) { var el = f.querySelector('[name="' + n + '"]'); return el ? String(el.value || '').trim() : ''; };
    var row = { seat: g('seat') || null, status: g('status') || 'active', renewal: g('renewal') || null, renewal_due: g('renewal_due') || null, emails: g('emails').split(/[,\s]+/).map(lower).filter(function (x) { return /@/.test(x); }), note: g('note') || null, updated_at: nowIso(), updated_by: myName() };
    saving = true;
    try { await run(db.from('board_members').update(row).eq('id', Number(f.getAttribute('data-w126mform')))); var m = memberById(f.getAttribute('data-w126mform')); if (m) Object.assign(m, row); await afterSave('Saved'); }
    catch (e) { sheetErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    finally { saving = false; }
  }

  /* an item under one of the three jobs */
  function openItem(id, job) {
    var it = id ? D().items.filter(function (i) { return String(i.id) === String(id); })[0] : null;
    if (id && !it) return;
    var j = JOB[(it && it.job) || job] || JOBS[0], edit = canEditItems();
    if (!edit && it) {
      var body = '<div>' + stTag(it.status) + '</div>' + (it.detail ? '<div>' + E(it.detail) + '</div>' : '') + '<dl class="kv">' + (it.owner ? '<dt>Owner</dt><dd>' + E(it.owner) + '</dd>' : '') + (it.due ? '<dt>Due</dt><dd>' + E(it.due) + '</dd>' : '') + (it.source ? '<dt>From</dt><dd>' + E(it.source) + '</dd>' : '') + '</dl>';
      openSheet('item', j.t, it.title, body, ''); return;
    }
    var st = (it && it.status) || 'todo';
    var body2 = '<form data-w126iform="' + (it ? it.id : '') + '" data-job="' + j.k + '">'
      + '<label>Title<input type="text" name="title" value="' + E(it ? it.title : '') + '"></label>'
      + '<div><div style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#4a4945;margin-bottom:6px">Where it stands</div><div class="sts">' + Object.keys(ST).map(function (k) { return '<button type="button" data-w126st="' + k + '" class="' + (k === st ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(ST[k][0]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div><input type="hidden" name="status" value="' + st + '"></div>'
      + '<label>Detail<textarea name="detail">' + E(it ? it.detail || '' : '') + '</textarea></label>'
      + '<div class="two"><label>Owner<input type="text" name="owner" value="' + E(it ? it.owner || '' : '') + '"></label><label>Due<input type="text" name="due" value="' + E(it ? it.due || '' : '') + '" placeholder="For example: Q3 board meeting"></label></div>'
      + (it && it.source ? '<p class="w126empty">From ' + E(it.source) + '.</p>' : '') + '<div class="err"></div></form>';
    openSheet('iform', j.t, it ? 'Update the item' : 'Add an item', body2, (it && amAdmin() ? '<button type="button" class="w126btn danger sm" data-w126idel="' + it.id + '">Delete</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w126btn ghost" data-w126close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w126btn" data-w126isave="1">' + I('check') + 'Save</button>');
  }
  async function saveItem() {
    var f = document.querySelector('#w126sheet form[data-w126iform]'); if (!f || saving) return;
    var g = function (n) { var el = f.querySelector('[name="' + n + '"]'); return el ? String(el.value || '').trim() : ''; };
    var id = f.getAttribute('data-w126iform');
    var row = { title: g('title'), status: g('status') || 'todo', detail: g('detail') || null, owner: g('owner') || null, due: g('due') || null, updated_at: nowIso(), updated_by: myName() };
    if (!row.title) { sheetErr('Give it a title.'); return; }
    saving = true;
    try {
      if (id) { await run(db.from('board_items').update(row).eq('id', Number(id))); var it = D().items.filter(function (i) { return String(i.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (it) Object.assign(it, row); }
      else { row.job = f.getAttribute('data-job'); row.sort = 900; row.source = 'Added in the Hub by ' + myName(); await run(db.from('board_items').insert(row)); D().items.push(Object.assign({ id: -Date.now() }, row)); }
      await afterSave('Saved');
    } catch (e) { sheetErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    finally { saving = false; }
  }
  async function deleteItem(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this item?')) return;
    try { await run(db.from('board_items').delete().eq('id', Number(id))); B.data.items = B.data.items.filter(function (i) { return String(i.id) !== String(id); }); await afterSave('Deleted'); }
    catch (e) { sheetErr('Could not delete it'); }
  }

  /* a committee's roster */

  async function saveCommittee() {
    var f = document.querySelector('#w126sheet form[data-w126cform]'); if (!f || saving) return;
    var key = f.getAttribute('data-w126cform');
    var g = function (n) { var el = f.querySelector('[name="' + n + '"]'); return el ? String(el.value || '').trim() : ''; };
    var ids = Array.prototype.map.call(f.querySelectorAll('input[name=member]:checked'), function (x) { return Number(x.value); });
    var chair = g('chair_id') ? Number(g('chair_id')) : null;
    if (chair && ids.indexOf(chair) < 0) ids.unshift(chair);
    var row = { chair_id: chair, chair_confirmed: !!(f.querySelector('input[name=chair_confirmed]') || {}).checked, member_ids: ids, internal_leads: g('internal_leads').split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean), external_recruit: g('external_recruit') || null, purpose: g('purpose') || null, updated_at: nowIso(), updated_by: myName() };
    saving = true;
    try { await run(db.from('board_committees').update(row).eq('key', key)); var c = commByKey(key); if (c) Object.assign(c, row); await afterSave('Saved'); }
    catch (e) { sheetErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    finally { saving = false; }
  }

  /* a board meeting */
  function openMeeting(id) {
    var m = id && id !== 'new' ? D().meetings.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : null;
    var d = m && m.meets_at ? new Date(m.meets_at) : null;
    var dv = d ? d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }) : '', tv = d ? d.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }) : '18:00';
    var body = '<form data-w126meetform="' + (m ? m.id : '') + '"><label>Title<input type="text" name="title" value="' + E(m ? m.title : 'Board meeting') + '"></label>'
      + '<div class="two"><label>Date<input type="date" name="date" value="' + E(dv) + '"></label><label>Time (Toronto)<input type="time" name="time" value="' + E(tv) + '"></label></div>'
      + '<div class="two"><label>Kind<select name="kind">' + [['board', 'Board meeting'], ['special', 'Special meeting'], ['agm', 'AGM'], ['strategy', 'Strategy session']].map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k[0] + '"' + ((m ? m.kind : 'board') === k[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + k[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>'
      + '<label>Status<select name="status">' + ['scheduled', 'tentative', 'to schedule', 'held', 'cancelled'].map(function (s) { return '<option' + ((m ? m.status : 'scheduled') === s ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label></div>'
      + '<div class="two"><label>Agenda link<input type="url" name="agenda_url" value="' + E(m ? m.agenda_url || '' : '') + '"></label><label>Minutes link<input type="url" name="notes_url" value="' + E(m ? m.notes_url || '' : '') + '"></label></div>'
      + '<label>Note<textarea name="note">' + E(m ? m.note || '' : '') + '</textarea></label><div class="err"></div></form>';
    openSheet('meetform', 'Board meetings', m ? 'Change the meeting' : 'Schedule a meeting', body, '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w126btn ghost" data-w126close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w126btn" data-w126meetsave="1">' + I('check') + 'Save</button>');
  }
  function torontoIso(date, time) {
    /* the offset for America/Toronto on that date (EDT or EST), without a timezone library */
    var p = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(date); if (!p) return null;
    var t = /^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(time || '18:00') || [0, '18', '00'];
    var probe = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(p[1]), Number(p[2]) - 1, Number(p[3]), 12, 0, 0));
    var tzHour = Number(probe.toLocaleString('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', hour: '2-digit', hour12: false }));
    var off = 12 - tzHour; /* 4 in summer, 5 in winter */
    return p[1] + '-' + p[2] + '-' + p[3] + 'T' + t[1] + ':' + t[2] + ':00-' + pad(off) + ':00';
  }
  async function saveMeeting() {
    var f = document.querySelector('#w126sheet form[data-w126meetform]'); if (!f || saving) return;
    var g = function (n) { var el = f.querySelector('[name="' + n + '"]'); return el ? String(el.value || '').trim() : ''; };
    var id = f.getAttribute('data-w126meetform');
    var row = { title: g('title') || 'Board meeting', kind: g('kind') || 'board', status: g('status') || 'scheduled', meets_at: g('date') ? torontoIso(g('date'), g('time')) : null, agenda_url: g('agenda_url') || null, notes_url: g('notes_url') || null, note: g('note') || null, updated_at: nowIso(), updated_by: myName() };
    if (!row.meets_at && row.status !== 'to schedule') { sheetErr('Pick the date, or set the status to "to schedule".'); return; }
    saving = true;
    try {
      if (id) { await run(db.from('board_meetings').update(row).eq('id', Number(id))); var m = D().meetings.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (m) Object.assign(m, row); }
      else { await run(db.from('board_meetings').insert(row)); D().meetings.push(Object.assign({ id: -Date.now() }, row)); }
      normalize(B.data); await afterSave('Saved');
    } catch (e) { sheetErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    finally { saving = false; }
  }

      ] };

/* =====================================================================
   WA v117 more of the brand feel (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "update hub to have more of the brand feel", "add some
   animations to the Ask the hub chat bot, make it look really cool
   filled with our branding assets", "fill the loading page with strong
   branding and sprinkle fun facts about Welfare Avenue when it loads".
   Brand source: wa_brand / wa_brand_assets in Supabase (Brand Guides
   v1 2026) and the brand/ folder in content-images.
   Pieces:
   1. Boot veil: rebuilt in 000_head_base (first paint): the vector
      wordmark with the E that moves as the loader, the descriptor line,
      doodles, a purple spray and a rotating "Did you know" fact.
   2. Sidebar wordmark: the traced vector (inline SVG) replaces the PNG.
   3. Sign-in gate: doodles and the purple spray on the green, the lockup
      in the card, the headline TOGETHER WE MOV[E] FORWARD with the E
      component.
   4. Ask the Hub: gold pill with the E icon (bars extend on hover), the
      drawer slides in, green header with the secondary mark, a purple
      spray and a twinkling star, bot messages with the WEL/FAR/E
      avatar, "Thinking..." becomes the E-that-moves loader, starter
      prompts as gold-ruled cards, rounded input with a gold send button,
      a faint E watermark behind the thread.
   5. Accents: gold active-tab mark in the sidebar, flower doodle after
      team page titles, E watermark in the green dashboard tile,
      asterisk before empty states, star before toasts, button lift on
      hover, pages fade in. Everything honours prefers-reduced-motion.
   Classic look (wa114classic) skips all of it except the veil.
   CSS rides inside #wa97css like wave114. Loads BEFORE wave110.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {

  /* ---------- 2. sidebar wordmark ---------- */
  function sidebar() {
    var a = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav .w114brand');
    if (!a || a.querySelector('.w117logo')) return;
    a.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', svgLockup('w117logo'));
  }

  /* ---------- 3. the gate ---------- */
  function gate() {
    var g = document.getElementById('gate');
    if (!g) return;
    if (!g.querySelector('.w117gd')) {
      g.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<img class="w117gd star" src="' + B + 'doodle-star-gold.png" alt=""><img class="w117gd flow" src="' + B + 'doodle-flowers-gold.png" alt=""><img class="w117gd heart" src="' + B + 'doodle-heart-purple.png" alt="">');
    }
    var card = g.querySelector('.gate-card');
    if (card && !card.querySelector('.w117lock')) card.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', svgLockup('w117lock'));
    var h2 = card && card.querySelector('h2');
    if (h2 && h2.getAttribute('data-w117') !== '1') {
      h2.setAttribute('data-w117', '1');
      h2.innerHTML = 'Together we mov' + svgE('w117e') + ' <span class="w117gold">forward</span>';
    }
  }

  /* ---------- 4. Ask the Hub ---------- */
  function pill() {
    var btn = document.getElementById('waChatBtn');
    if (!btn || btn.querySelector('.w117ei')) return;
    if (!btn.querySelector('.w75ic')) return;   /* wave75 has not decorated yet; try again later */
    btn.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', svgEBars('w117ei'));
  }
  function header() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('waChatPanel');
    var h = panel && panel.querySelector('.wac-h');
    if (!h || h.querySelector('.w117tt')) return;
    var small = h.querySelector('small');
    var subtitle = small ? small.textContent : '';
    var close = h.querySelector('#w81close');
    h.innerHTML = '';
    h.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', svgSecondary('w117mark') + '<span class="w117tt">Ask the Hub<small></small></span><i class="w117st"></i>');
    h.querySelector('small').textContent = subtitle || 'Ask anything, put in requests, add tasks. You confirm before anything is saved.';
    if (close) h.appendChild(close);
  }
  function decorateMsg(d) {
    if (!d || !d.classList || !d.classList.contains('wac-m') || !d.classList.contains('bot')) return;
    if (!d.querySelector(':scope > .w117av')) {
      var wrap = document.createElement('div');
      while (d.firstChild) wrap.appendChild(d.firstChild);
      d.appendChild(wrap);
      d.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<i class="w117av">' + svgSecondary('') + '</i>');
    }
    var span = d.querySelector('span');
    if (span && /^Thinking\.\.\.$/.test(span.textContent || '') && !d.classList.contains('w117think')) {
      d.classList.add('w117think');
      span.innerHTML = '<i class="w117load"><b></b><b></b><b></b></i>Thinking';
    } else if (!d.classList.contains('w117think')) {
      var st = document.querySelector('#waChatPanel .wac-h .w117st');
      if (st && d.getAttribute('data-w117pop') !== '1') {
        d.setAttribute('data-w117pop', '1');
        st.classList.remove('pop'); void st.offsetWidth; st.classList.add('pop');
      }
    }
  }
  var observed = false;
  function thread() {
    var m = document.getElementById('wacMsgs');
    if (!m) return;
    m.querySelectorAll('.wac-m.bot').forEach(decorateMsg);
    if (observed || typeof MutationObserver === 'undefined') return;
    observed = true;
    new MutationObserver(function (recs) {
      recs.forEach(function (r) { r.addedNodes.forEach(function (n) { try { decorateMsg(n); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }); });
    }).observe(m, { childList: true });
  }
  function chat() { pill(); header(); thread(); }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  function all() { try { sidebar(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { gate(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { chat(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', all); else all();
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa117 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa117.apply(this, arguments);
      all();
      setTimeout(all, 1200);
      setTimeout(all, 4000);
      return r;
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp117 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function () { var out = __sp117.apply(this, arguments); try { chat(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#waChatBtn, [data-w114ask], [data-w75go], [data-w75q]')) setTimeout(chat, 60);
  });
})();
