      var touches = document.querySelectorAll('link[rel="apple-touch-icon"]');
      if (touches.length) touches.forEach(function (l) { l.setAttribute('href', touch); l.removeAttribute('sizes'); });
      else { var t = document.createElement('link'); t.rel = 'apple-touch-icon'; t.href = touch; document.head.appendChild(t); }
      var m = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
      if (!m) { m = document.createElement('meta'); m.name = 'theme-color'; document.head.appendChild(m); }
      m.content = GREEN;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 3. mastheads ---------- */
  var slot = 0;
  function norm(t) { return String(t || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ').trim(); }
  function ownText(el) { var t = ''; for (var i = 0; i < el.childNodes.length; i++) if (el.childNodes[i].nodeType === 3) t += el.childNodes[i].nodeValue; return t; }
  function moveHelp(help, mast) {
    var parent = help.parentNode, idx = Array.prototype.indexOf.call(parent.childNodes, help);
    slot += 1;
    parent.setAttribute('data-w118slot', String(slot));
    help.setAttribute('data-w118from', String(slot));
    help.setAttribute('data-w118idx', String(idx));
    mast.appendChild(help);
  }
  function hideEl(el) { el.setAttribute('data-w118hide', '1'); }
  function mastHtml(crumb, title, blurb, withE) {
    var t = E(title);
    if (withE) t = t.replace(/^(EXECUTIV|Executiv)E /i, '$1' + svgE('w118e') + ' ');
    return '<div class="crumb">' + E(crumb) + '</div><h1>' + t + '</h1><p>' + E(blurb) + '</p>';
  }
  /* the page's own title heading: the first h2 whose own text is the title (glyphs and the help button aside) */
  function titleH2(panel, title) {
    var hs = panel.querySelectorAll('h2');
    for (var i = 0; i < hs.length && i < 5; i++) {
      if (hs[i].closest('.w118mast')) continue;
      var t = norm(ownText(hs[i]));
      if (t && (t === norm(title) || norm(title).indexOf(t) === 0 || t.indexOf(norm(title)) === 0)) return hs[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  /* the page's own title heading: lift its help button into the masthead, hide it (or slim it when it holds a chip), reuse its subtitle */
  function claim(panel, el, m) {
    var h2 = m[3] ? titleH2(panel, m[4]) : null;
    if (!h2) return;
    var help = h2.querySelector(':scope > .w43help');
    if (help && !el.querySelector('.w43help')) moveHelp(help, el);
    if (!h2.getAttribute('data-w118hide') && !h2.getAttribute('data-w118slim')) {
      var others = Array.prototype.filter.call(h2.children, function (c) { return !c.classList.contains('w43help') && !c.classList.contains('w118i'); });
      if (!others.length) hideEl(h2); else h2.setAttribute('data-w118slim', '1');
    }
    var nx = h2.nextElementSibling;
    if (nx && nx.classList.contains('w85p') && !nx.getAttribute('data-w118hide') && norm(nx.textContent)) {
      var p = el.querySelector(':scope > p'); if (p) p.textContent = nx.textContent.trim();
      hideEl(nx);
    }
  }
  function mast(id) {
    var panel = document.getElementById(id), m = MAST[id];
    if (!panel || !m) return;
    var el = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'w118mast';
      el.setAttribute('data-w118', id);
      el.innerHTML = mastHtml(m[0], m[1], m[2], id === 'overview');
      panel.insertBefore(el, panel.firstChild);
    } else if (panel.firstElementChild !== el) panel.insertBefore(el, panel.firstChild);
    claim(panel, el, m);
  }
  function mastheads() { Object.keys(MAST).forEach(function (id) { try { mast(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }); }

  /* ---------- 4. the admin sections ---------- */
  var admSec = ADMIN[0][0];
  function admKey(h) {
    var p = String(h || location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
    if (p[0] !== 'adminpanel') return null;
    var k = p[1] || '';
    return ADMIN.some(function (a) { return a[0] === k; }) ? k : ADMIN[0][0];
  }
  function admDef(k) { for (var i = 0; i < ADMIN.length; i++) if (ADMIN[i][0] === k) return ADMIN[i]; return ADMIN[0]; }
  function admMount() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('adminpanel');
    if (!panel) return;
    var mastEl = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!mastEl) {
      mastEl = document.createElement('div'); mastEl.className = 'w118mast'; mastEl.setAttribute('data-w118', 'adminpanel');
      panel.insertBefore(mastEl, panel.firstChild);
    }
    if (panel.firstChild !== mastEl) panel.insertBefore(mastEl, panel.firstChild);
    var sw = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118adm');
    if (!sw) {
      sw = document.createElement('nav'); sw.className = 'w118adm'; sw.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Admin sections');
      sw.innerHTML = ADMIN.map(function (a) { return '<a href="#adminpanel/' + a[0] + '" data-w118sec="' + a[0] + '">' + E(a[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('');
      panel.insertBefore(sw, mastEl.nextSibling);
    }
    if (mastEl.nextSibling !== sw) panel.insertBefore(sw, mastEl.nextSibling);
    admApply(panel, mastEl, sw);
  }
  function admApply(panel, mastEl, sw) {
    var d = admDef(admSec);
    ADMIN.forEach(function (a) {
      var card = panel.querySelector(a[2].split(',')[0].trim());
      var h = card ? card.querySelector(':scope > h2:first-child') : null;
      if (h && !h.getAttribute('data-w118hide') && norm(ownText(h)) === norm(a[1]).replace(' trail', '')) hideEl(h);
      else if (h && !h.getAttribute('data-w118hide') && h.children.length === 0) hideEl(h);
    });
    sw.setAttribute('data-sec', d[0]);
    sw.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (a) { a.classList.toggle('on', a.getAttribute('data-w118sec') === d[0]); });
    var want = mastHtml('Admin', d[1], d[3], false);
    if (mastEl.getAttribute('data-w118cur') !== d[0]) { mastEl.innerHTML = want; mastEl.setAttribute('data-w118cur', d[0]); }
    var sub = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav .w118sub');
    if (sub) sub.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (a) { a.classList.toggle('on', a.getAttribute('data-w118sec') === d[0]); });
    /* wave132: only while Admin is the page on screen (it used to rename whatever page was open) */
    try { if (panel.classList.contains('active')) document.title = d[1] + ' \u00b7 Welfare Avenue Hub'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function admNav() {
    var tab = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav .tab[data-tab="adminpanel"]');
    if (!tab) return;
    var sub = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav .w118sub');
    if (sub && tab.nextElementSibling === sub) return;
    if (sub) sub.parentNode.removeChild(sub);
    sub = document.createElement('div'); sub.className = 'w118sub';
    sub.innerHTML = ADMIN.map(function (a) { return '<a href="#adminpanel/' + a[0] + '" data-w118sec="' + a[0] + '">' + E(a[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('');
    sub.addEventListener('click', function () { try { document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
    tab.parentNode.insertBefore(sub, tab.nextSibling);
    sub.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (a) { a.classList.toggle('on', a.getAttribute('data-w118sec') === admSec); });
  }
  function admRoute(h) {
    var k = admKey(h);
    if (!k) return;
    admSec = k;
    admMount();
  }
  function admHook() {
    try {
      if (window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.pages) {
        var pg = null;
        for (var i = 0; i < WA_NAV.pages.length; i++) if (WA_NAV.pages[i].id === 'adminpanel') pg = WA_NAV.pages[i];
        if (!pg) { pg = { id: 'adminpanel' }; WA_NAV.pages.push(pg); }
        if (!pg.__wa118) { var prev = pg.onShow; pg.__wa118 = true; pg.onShow = function (h) { if (prev) { try { prev(h); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } admRoute(h); }; }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (admKey()) setTimeout(function () { admRoute(); }, 0); });
  }

  /* ---------- 5. icons: glyphs used as icons become SVG ---------- */
  var ICON_SCOPE = 'button, a, h2, h3, h4, .w111btn, .tab, .chip, .pill, span.ic, b, .w75ic, summary, label';
  function swapNode(tn) {
    var text = tn.nodeValue;
    if (!GLYPH_RE.test(text)) return false;
    var frag = document.createDocumentFragment(), last = 0, re = new RegExp(GLYPH_RE.source, 'gu'), m;
    while ((m = re.exec(text))) {
      if (m.index > last) frag.appendChild(document.createTextNode(text.slice(last, m.index)));
      var span = document.createElement('span');
      span.innerHTML = icon(GLYPHS[m[0]]);
      var svg = span.firstChild; svg.setAttribute('data-w118g', m[0]);
      frag.appendChild(svg);
      last = m.index + m[0].length;
    }
    if (last < text.length) frag.appendChild(document.createTextNode(text.slice(last)));
    tn.parentNode.replaceChild(frag, tn);
    return true;
  }
  function icons(root) {
    root = root || document.body;
    if (!root || !root.querySelectorAll) return;
    var hosts = root.matches && root.matches(ICON_SCOPE) ? [root] : [];
    root.querySelectorAll(ICON_SCOPE).forEach(function (h) { hosts.push(h); });
    hosts.forEach(function (h) {
      /* the retired classic department panels (hidden since wave116i) keep their text: other waves match on it */
      if (h.closest('#wa110veil, script, style, textarea, input, #opsmtl, #opsto, #marketing, #fundraising, #hr, #board, #progdev')) return;
      for (var i = 0; i < h.childNodes.length; i++) {
        var n = h.childNodes[i];
        if (n.nodeType === 3 && GLYPH_RE.test(n.nodeValue)) { if (swapNode(n)) i = -1; }
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 5b. the phone bar: real icons in the empty slots ---------- */
  var BAR = { mypage: 'home', overview: 'chart', departments: 'people', more: 'dots' };
  function phoneBar() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('w114bar');
    if (!bar) return;
    bar.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function (b) {
      var key = b.getAttribute('data-w114go') || b.getAttribute('data-w114grp'), name = BAR[key];
      var i = b.querySelector('i');
      if (!name || !i || i.querySelector('.w118i')) return;
      var span = document.createElement('span'); span.innerHTML = icon(name);
      var svg = span.firstChild; svg.setAttribute('data-w118g', '');
      i.appendChild(svg);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 6. copy: the assistant is "Ask the Hub" ---------- */
  var FIX = [['Ask the WA assistant', 'Ask the Hub'], ['Meet the WA assistant', 'Meet Ask the Hub'], ['The WA assistant', 'Ask the Hub'], ['the WA assistant', 'Ask the Hub'], ['WA Assistant', 'Ask the Hub'], ['WA assistant', 'Ask the Hub']];
  function fixText(root) {
    root = root || document.body;
    if (!root || !root.querySelectorAll) return;
    var walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, null);
    var todo = [], n;
    while ((n = walker.nextNode())) { if (n.nodeValue.indexOf('WA assistant') >= 0 || n.nodeValue.indexOf('WA Assistant') >= 0) todo.push(n); }
    todo.forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.parentNode && (t.parentNode.classList && t.parentNode.classList.contains('w118t'))) return;
      if (t.parentNode && t.parentNode.closest && t.parentNode.closest('script, style, textarea, #wa110veil')) return;
      var s = t.nodeValue, o = s;
      FIX.forEach(function (f) { s = s.split(f[0]).join(f[1]); });
      if (s === o) return;
      var span = document.createElement('span'); span.className = 'w118t'; span.setAttribute('data-w118o', o); span.textContent = s;
      t.parentNode.replaceChild(span, t);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 7. clean copy for the harness: undo everything above on a clone ---------- */
  function cleanNode(el) {
    var c = el.cloneNode(true);
    c.querySelectorAll('[data-w118from]').forEach(function (b) {
      var tok = b.getAttribute('data-w118from'), idx = parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-w118idx'), 10) || 0;
      var slotEl = c.querySelector('[data-w118slot="' + tok + '"]');
      b.removeAttribute('data-w118from'); b.removeAttribute('data-w118idx');
      if (slotEl) { var ref = slotEl.childNodes[idx] || null; slotEl.insertBefore(b, ref); }
    });
    c.querySelectorAll('[data-w118slot]').forEach(function (s) { s.removeAttribute('data-w118slot'); });
    c.querySelectorAll('[data-w118hide],[data-w118slim]').forEach(function (s) { s.removeAttribute('data-w118hide'); s.removeAttribute('data-w118slim'); });
    c.querySelectorAll('.w118mast, .w118adm, .w118sub, #w118ck').forEach(function (n) { n.parentNode.removeChild(n); });
    c.querySelectorAll('svg.w118i').forEach(function (s) { s.parentNode.replaceChild(document.createTextNode(s.getAttribute('data-w118g') || ''), s); });
    c.querySelectorAll('span.w118t').forEach(function (s) { s.parentNode.replaceChild(document.createTextNode(s.getAttribute('data-w118o') || ''), s); });
    c.normalize();
    return c;
  }
  function clean(el) { return cleanNode(el).outerHTML; }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  var pending = null;
  function later(root) {
    if (pending) return;
    pending = setTimeout(function () { pending = null; try { mastheads(); if (document.querySelector('#adminpanel > .w118adm')) admMount(); icons(); fixText(); phoneBar(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 60);
  }
  function boot() {
    head();
    try { mastheads(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try { admNav(); admHook(); admMount(); if (admKey()) admRoute(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try { icons(); fixText(); phoneBar(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      var need = false;
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length && !need; i++) { if (muts[i].addedNodes && muts[i].addedNodes.length) need = true; }
      if (need) later();
    });
    mo.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
      var __sp118 = showPanel;
      showPanel = function (id) {
        var r = __sp118.apply(this, arguments);
        try { if (id === 'adminpanel') { if (!admKey()) admSec = ADMIN[0][0]; admMount(); } admNav(); setTimeout(mastheads, 30); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
        return r;
      };
    }
    if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
      var __sa118 = startApp;
      startApp = async function () {
        var r = await __sa118.apply(this, arguments);
        [300, 1500, 4000].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(function () { try { admNav(); mastheads(); icons(); fixText(); if (admKey()) admRoute(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, ms); });
        return r;
      };
    }
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot); else boot();

  window.__wa118 = { version: 1, clean: clean, cleanNode: cleanNode, icons: icons, mastheads: mastheads, admin: admMount, ADMIN: ADMIN };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v118b check-in nudges: the little note under the Check in pill (25/09/26)
   WA v238 No way out of the pop-ups: the Welcome back gate over the whole Hub, the meeting check and the due-date check (Steel, 14/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "When people open the dashboard if they aren't yet checked in and
   they haven't opened it for a while prompt them to check in with a little
   notif right below the check in bar. If someone leaves from their mobile
   prompt them to check out or if it's been longer than a few hours, prompt
   them to change the check-out time so it's logged properly."
   Three notes, one at a time, anchored under the wave116j pill (#w116jck):
   1. Not checked in, and the hub has not been open for 3 hours (or ever
      on this device): "Don't forget to check in" (the wording since 26/09/26).
      Check in goes through the pill (wave116q posts to Slack). Not now
      snoozes it for 4 hours.
   2. Checked in and back after being away 20 minutes or more (the tab or
      app was hidden; on a phone that is leaving the app): "Away since
      12:40": check out as of the moment they left, check out now (the
      usual note sheet), or still working.
   3. Checked in for more than 3 hours: "Still here?", check out now, or
      set the time they actually stopped. Asked again 2 hours later.
   A time-adjusted check-out writes ended_at and minutes on the open
   hub_checkins row, then calls checkin-post {action: out} so the Slack
   line and the row's summary fields follow the same path as a normal
   check-out (the function keeps an ended_at that is already set).
   Works in both looks (this is behaviour, not skin). window.__wa118b.
   wave192 (01/10/26), The Loop: the notes sit under the I'm in button (#w192go), speak The Loop (I'm in, close the
   loop), and a close "as of" a time ends the session at that time and opens the wrap-up sheet (w192.openRow), so the
   person chooses what to share; nothing is posted on its own. The audit line keeps the time, never the hours.
   wave217 (07/10/26), Steel: "After an hour, hour and a half, 2 hours, and 2.5 hours being in, I want you to do a light
   check to confirm if they are still in." His choices: the check is in the Hub (plus one Slack DM to the person at
   3 hours, sent by checkin-post's still_dm from the cron wa-loop-still-dm); nothing happens when nobody answers; after
   2.5 hours it keeps asking every half hour. So note 3 now comes an hour in and at every half hour after (1h, 1.5h,
   2h, 2.5h, 3h ...), once per mark: shown is asked (the x waits for the next mark). Still in, here or in the Welcome
   back note, writes hub_checkins.still_in_at (the DM skips someone who said so in the last 40 minutes), which also
   tells the person's other devices that mark is answered. The DM's links land here: ?loop=still answers Still in,
   ?loop=close opens the wrap-up sheet.
   wave238 (14/10/26), the gate. Steel: "I don't want people to exit out of closing their tasks or change due dates on the
   pop up - they must do it to use the hub." Welcome back (note 2) is now a gate over the whole Hub (#w238loop, a dialog
   with no x, no Escape and no click outside): someone who comes back with a loop still open from before they left must
   close it as of the time they left, set the time, or, when they were away less than three hours on the same day, say
   they are still working. The decision is kept (w118bHid stays until it is answered), so a reload or a new tab brings
   the same gate back. The meeting pop-up (177) and the due-date check (213) wait for it. Still in? (note 3) keeps no x
   either: it stays until Still in, Close the loop or Set the time. The first note (Starting some work?) is unchanged.
   ===================================================================== */

  function num(k) { try { return Number(localStorage.getItem(k) || 0) || 0; } catch (e) { return 0; } }
  function put(k, v) { try { localStorage.setItem(k, String(v)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function drop(k) { try { localStorage.removeItem(k); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function ck() { return window.w116jCk || null; }
  function pill() { return document.getElementById('w192go') || document.getElementById('w116jck'); }
  function open() { var c = ck(); try { return c ? c.open() : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }

  function fmtMins(m) { var c = ck(); try { if (c && c.fmtMins) return c.fmtMins(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } var h = Math.floor(m / 60), r = Math.round(m % 60); return (h ? h + 'h ' : '') + (r || !h ? r + 'm' : ''); }
  function clock(t) { return new Date(t).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); }
  function dayWord(t) { var d = new Date(t), n = new Date(); return d.toDateString() === n.toDateString() ? 'today' : (n - d < 2 * 86400000 ? 'yesterday' : d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })); }
  function hhmm(t) { var d = new Date(t); return ('0' + d.getHours()).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + d.getMinutes()).slice(-2); }
  function gateUp() { var g = document.getElementById('gate'); return g && g.style.display !== 'none'; }

  function myName() { try { var em = myEmail(); var m = (DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.email || '').toLowerCase() === em; })[0]; return (m && m.name) || (USER && USER.name) || em; } catch (e) { return ''; } }

  /* ---------- the note ---------- */
  function hide() {

  }

    var left = r.left;
    if (left + w > window.innerWidth - 12) { left = Math.max(12, r.right - w); n.classList.add('r'); } else n.classList.remove('r');
    n.style.top = Math.round(top) + 'px'; n.style.left = Math.round(left) + 'px';
  }

  /* ---------- the three notes ---------- */
  function nudgeIn() {
    var last = num(K.seen);
    if (open() || !pill() || gateUp()) return false;
    if (num(K.snooze) > Date.now()) return false;
    if (last && Date.now() - last < AWAY_MS) return false;
    /* wave130: two short lines, so the note under the pill stays clear of the page (the tiles on Home sit 90 px below the pill) */
    /* Steel (26/09/26): "instead it should say don't forget to check-in" */

    return true;
  }
  /* wave238: Welcome back is a gate over the Hub. Away less than three hours on the same day: Still working is an answer too;
     longer, or another day: the loop closes as of the time they left, or the time they set. */
  function nudgeBack(row, leftAt) {
    hide();
    var away = Date.now() - leftAt, sameDay = new Date(leftAt).toDateString() === new Date().toDateString();
    var canStill = away < 3 * 3600000 && sameDay;
    var g = document.createElement('div'); g.id = 'w238loop'; g.setAttribute('data-kind', 'back');
    g.innerHTML = '<div class="box" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w238k">'
      + '<p class="k" id="w238k">The Loop</p><b>Welcome back' + (myFirst() ? ', ' + E(myFirst()) : '') + '</b>'
      + '<p>Your loop has been open since ' + E(clock(row.started_at)) + ' ' + E(dayWord(row.started_at)) + ', and you were away from ' + E(clock(leftAt)) + ' ' + E(dayWord(leftAt)) + '. '
      + (canStill ? 'Finished then? Close the loop as of that time and share what moved. Still at it? Say so.' : 'Close the loop as of that time and share what moved, or set the time you finished.') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="row"><button class="go" data-w118b="asof" data-t="' + leftAt + '">Close as of ' + E(clock(leftAt)) + '</button>'
      + '<button class="ghost" data-w118b="settime">Set the time</button>'
      + (canStill ? '<button class="link" data-w118b="still">Still working</button>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<p class="note">Hours are yours and HR\u2019s only; what you share is the note. This stays until you answer.</p></div>';
    g.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === g) { e.stopPropagation(); } });
    document.body.appendChild(g); S.kind = 'back';
    document.addEventListener('keydown', gateKey, true);
    try { var b = g.querySelector('button.go'); if (b) b.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* wave217: the marks. -1 before the first hour, then 0 at 1h, 1 at 1.5h, 2 at 2h, 3 at 2.5h ... */
  function markAt(row, t) { var age = t - new Date(row.started_at).getTime(); return age < FIRST_MS ? -1 : Math.floor((age - FIRST_MS) / EVERY_MS); }
  /* the last mark this session has had: shown on this device, or answered Still in on any device (still_in_at) */
  function markDone(row) {
    var done = -1;
    try { var v = String(localStorage.getItem(K.mark) || '').split(':'); if (v[0] === String(row.id)) done = Number(v[1]); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (row.still_in_at) done = Math.max(done, markAt(row, new Date(row.still_in_at).getTime()));
    return isNaN(done) ? -1 : done;
  }
  function nudgeLong(row, mark) {
    var mins = (Date.now() - new Date(row.started_at).getTime()) / 60000;
    show('long', '<b><i></i>Still in?</b><p>' + E(fmtMins(Math.round(mins))) + ' so far (only you see this). Finished earlier? Set the time, then close the loop.</p>'
      + '<div class="row"><button class="ghost" data-w118b="still">Still in</button><button class="go" data-w118b="out">Close the loop</button><button class="link" data-w118b="settime">Set the time</button></div>');
    S.mark = mark; put(K.mark, row.id + ':' + mark); put(K.asked, Date.now());
  }
  /* Still in: the session row says so (the 3-hour DM skips someone who just answered, and other devices see the mark is done) */
  function stillIn(row) {
    if (!row || !row.id) return;
    var at = new Date().toISOString();
    row.still_in_at = at;
    put(K.mark, row.id + ':' + Math.max(markAt(row, Date.now()), markDone(row)));
    try { db.from('hub_checkins').update({ still_in_at: at }).eq('id', row.id).is('ended_at', null).then(function () { /* fine */ }, function () { /* fine */ }); } catch (e) { /* the note is answered either way */ }
  }
  function timeForm(row) {
    var gate = document.getElementById('w238loop');
    var n = gate ? gate.querySelector('.box') : document.getElementById('w118ck'); if (!n) return;
    var guess = num(K.hid) && num(K.hid) > new Date(row.started_at).getTime() ? num(K.hid) : (num(K.seen) && num(K.seen) > new Date(row.started_at).getTime() ? num(K.seen) : Date.now());
    /* wave238: inside the gate there is no Cancel out of the gate, only back to its choices */
    n.innerHTML = (gate ? '<p class="k" id="w238k">The Loop</p>' : '<button class="x" data-w118b="x" title="Close" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>') + '<b>' + (gate ? '' : '<i></i>') + 'When did you finish?</b><p>You were in from ' + E(clock(row.started_at)) + ' ' + E(dayWord(row.started_at)) + '. Your session ends at this time; then you share what moved.</p>'
      + '<div class="when"><input type="time" id="w118bt" value="' + hhmm(guess) + '" step="60"><span>' + E(dayWord(guess)) + '</span></div><div class="err" id="w118berr"></div>'
      + '<div class="row"><button class="go" data-w118b="save" data-day="' + new Date(guess).toDateString() + '">Close at that time</button><button class="link" data-w118b="' + (gate ? 'back' : 'x') + '">' + (gate ? 'Back' : 'Cancel') + '</button></div>';
    if (!gate) place();
  }

  /* ---------- writing a check-out at a chosen moment ---------- */
  async function checkOutAt(row, endMs) {
    var start = new Date(row.started_at).getTime();
    if (endMs <= start) return 'That is before you were in (' + clock(row.started_at) + ').';
    if (endMs > Date.now() + 60000) return 'That is in the future.';
    var mins = Math.max(1, Math.round((endMs - start) / 60000));
    var endIso = new Date(endMs).toISOString();
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_checkins').update({ ended_at: endIso, minutes: mins }).eq('id', row.id).is('ended_at', null);
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
    } catch (e) { return 'Could not end the session (' + ((e && e.message) || e) + ').'; }
    /* wave192: no audit of the hours (change_log is read by staff), and nothing posted: the wrap-up sheet opens for this session */
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'checkin', entity_id: myEmail(), field: 'out', old_value: null, new_value: 'closed the loop as of ' + clock(endMs) + ' (time set by hand)', changed_by: myName() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
    var c = ck(); try { if (c && c.clear) c.clear(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (c && c.reload) await c.reload(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (c && c.render) c.render(); if (c && c.presence) c.presence(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var ended = { id: row.id, email: row.email, started_at: row.started_at, ended_at: endIso, minutes: mins };
    if (window.w192 && window.w192.openRow) window.w192.openRow(ended);
    else {
      try { await db.functions.invoke('checkin-post', { body: { action: 'out', checkin_id: row.id, bullets: [], close_task_ids: [], skipped: true } }); } catch (e) { /* the session is ended either way */ }
      toastS('Session ended as of ' + clock(endMs) + '.');
    }
    return '';
  }

  /* ---------- decide what to show ---------- */
  function decide(reason) {
    if (gateUp() || !pill()) return;
    var row = open();
    if (!row) { if (reason !== 'tick') nudgeIn(); return; }
    var hid = num(K.hid);
    /* wave238: the moment they left is kept until the gate is answered (a reload brings the same gate back) */
    if (hid && Date.now() - hid > GONE_MS && hid > new Date(row.started_at).getTime()) { if (!document.getElementById('w238loop')) nudgeBack(row, hid); return; }
    if (document.getElementById('w238loop')) return;
    /* wave217: Still in? at each mark not had yet; an unanswered note just moves on to the next mark */
    var mark = markAt(row, Date.now());
    if (mark >= 0 && mark > markDone(row) && !(S.kind === 'long' && S.mark === mark) && S.kind !== 'back') nudgeLong(row, mark);
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w118b]') : null; if (!t) return;
    var a = t.getAttribute('data-w118b'), row = open();
    if (a === 'x' || a === 'still') { hide(); if (a === 'still') { drop(K.hid); put(K.asked, Date.now()); stillIn(row); } return; }
    if (a === 'later') { put(K.snooze, Date.now() + SNOOZE_MS); hide(); return; }
    if (a === 'in') { hide(); var p = pill(); if (p) p.click(); return; }
    if (a === 'out') { hide(); drop(K.hid); put(K.asked, Date.now()); var p2 = pill(); if (p2) p2.click(); return; }
    if (a === 'settime') { if (row) timeForm(row); return; }
    if (a === 'back') { if (row && num(K.hid)) nudgeBack(row, num(K.hid)); else hide(); return; }   /* wave238: back to the gate's choices */
    if (a === 'asof') { if (!row) { hide(); return; } t.disabled = true; checkOutAt(row, Number(t.getAttribute('data-t'))).then(function (err) { if (err) { t.disabled = false; var ge = document.getElementById('w118berr'); if (ge) ge.textContent = err; else toastS(err); } else { drop(K.hid); hide(); } }); return; }
    if (a === 'save') {
      if (!row) { hide(); return; }
      var inp = document.getElementById('w118bt'), err = document.getElementById('w118berr');
      var v = inp && inp.value ? inp.value.split(':') : null;
      if (!v || v.length < 2) { if (err) err.textContent = 'Pick a time.'; return; }
      var d = new Date(t.getAttribute('data-day') || new Date().toDateString()); d.setHours(Number(v[0]), Number(v[1]), 0, 0);
      t.disabled = true;
      checkOutAt(row, d.getTime()).then(function (msg) { if (msg) { t.disabled = false; if (err) err.textContent = msg; } else { drop(K.hid); hide(); } });
      return;
    }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- presence: when the hub was last open, when it went out of sight ---------- */
  function seen() { put(K.seen, Date.now()); }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
    if (document.hidden) { if (open()) put(K.hid, Date.now()); seen(); }
    else { setTimeout(function () { decide('back'); seen(); }, 400); }
  });
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', function () { if (open()) put(K.hid, Date.now()); seen(); });
  window.addEventListener('pageshow', function (ev) { if (ev.persisted) setTimeout(function () { decide('back'); }, 400); });
  /* wave217: the 3-hour DM's links. ?loop=still answers Still in; ?loop=close opens the wrap-up sheet. The query is taken off the address. */
  function linkAsk() { var m = /[?&]loop=(still|close)(?:&|$)/.exec(location.search || ''); return m ? m[1] : ''; }
  async function fromLink(ask) {
    try { var u = new URL(location.href); u.searchParams.delete('loop'); history.replaceState(history.state, '', u.pathname + u.search + u.hash); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var c = ck(); try { if (c && c.reload) await c.reload(); } catch (e) { /* the row we have */ }
    var row = open();
    if (!row) { toastS('Your loop is already closed.'); return; }
    hide();
    if (ask === 'still') { stillIn(row); toastS('Got it, you\u2019re still in.'); return; }
    var p = pill(); if (p) p.click();
  }
  function boot() {
    /* the first decision waits for wave116j to have loaded the open check-in */
    var tries = 0, ask = linkAsk();
    (function wait() {
      tries += 1;
      var c = ck();
      if ((c && pill()) || tries > 40) { if (ask) fromLink(ask).then(function () { seen(); }); else { decide('open'); seen(); } }
      else setTimeout(wait, 500);
    })();
    setInterval(function () { if (!document.hidden) { seen(); decide('tick'); } }, 60000);
    /* the check-in pill changing state means a check-in or check-out happened: the note is stale */
    var p = pill();
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { if (S.kind && S.kind !== 'long') hide(); else if (S.kind === 'long' && !open()) hide(); });
    /* wave192: the state is the button's class (in or not); its text carries a minute counter that must not dismiss a note */
    if (p) mo.observe(p, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
    else { var mo2 = new MutationObserver(function () { var q = pill(); if (q) { mo.observe(q, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] }); mo2.disconnect(); } }); mo2.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); }
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa118b = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var r = await __sa118b.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(boot, 3500); return r; };
  } else if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () { setTimeout(boot, 3500); });
  else setTimeout(boot, 3500);

  window.__wa118b = { version: 2, decide: decide, show: show, hide: hide, checkOutAt: checkOutAt, K: K, markAt: markAt, markDone: markDone, fromLink: fromLink, nudgeBack: nudgeBack, state: function () { return { kind: S.kind, mark: S.mark, gate: !!document.getElementById('w238loop') }; } };
})();

   Steel: "Okay I want to label all of these photos and host them in the
   Supabase storage. Can you set it up for successful use."
   - Storage and labels: every photo is three JPEGs in the public
     content-images bucket, photos/full (2400 px), photos/web (1600 px)
     and photos/thumb (640 px), sRGB, no location or camera data, cached
     a week (?v=<version> busts the cache). One row per photo in
     public.wa_photos: title, alt text, caption, description, date,
     season, place, event, scene, tags, what it is cleared for and why,
     people by role, partners, branding era, focal point, colour, best
     uses, sizes, credit and source. The view wa_photos_list adds the
     public URLs. Staff read and write, board viewers read, admins delete.
   - The page (#photos, under More): search, usage chips (Ready, Crop
     first, Consent needed), seasons, scenes, sets and picks; a grid
     cropped on each photo's focal point, grouped by year; a side panel
     with the photo, what it is cleared for, the alt text and caption to
     copy, three download sizes and the details. Deep links:
     #photos/<key>, #photos/new (add photos), #photos/review.
   - Staff edit the labels (focal point with crop previews included),
     add photos (the browser makes the three sizes, reads the date from
     the file, uploads with the person's session, and asks the llm route
     photo_label for first labels that a person confirms), and archive.
     Admins can delete an archived photo for good.

     graphic) and Use in a post (the content calendar composer opens with
     the photo attached); both screens get a "Pick from the photo library"
     link. Photos show in Search everything, and the Marketing and
     Fundraising team pages list the library in their tools.

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function board() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function allowed() { return staff() || board(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return String((USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function admin() {
    if (['steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com', 'steelmacdonald@gmail.com', 'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com'].indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0) return true;
    try { return !!(WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'); } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  /* a page this person may open (per-person tab lists win) */
  function tabOk(id) { try { if (typeof wa40Allowed === 'function') return (wa40Allowed() || []).indexOf(id) >= 0; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return true; }
  function me() { try { return (typeof who === 'function' && who()) || (USER && (USER.name || USER.email)) || 'Someone'; } catch (e) { return 'Someone'; } }
  function pad(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function ymd(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
  function ddmmyy(d) { d = d || new Date(); return pad(d.getDate()) + '/' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '/' + String(d.getFullYear()).slice(2); }
  function dateOf(ds) { return ds ? new Date(String(ds).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00') : null; }
  function niceDate(ds) { var d = dateOf(ds); if (!d || isNaN(d)) return ''; try { return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(ds); } }
  function monthYear(ds) { var d = dateOf(ds); if (!d || isNaN(d)) return ''; try { return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(ds).slice(0, 7); } }
  function seasonOf(ds) { var m = Number(String(ds || '').slice(5, 7)); if (!m) return null; return m === 12 || m <= 2 ? 'winter' : m <= 5 ? 'spring' : m <= 8 ? 'summer' : 'autumn'; }
  function label(list, v) { for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i][0] === v) return list[i][1]; return v || ''; }
  function bytes(n) { n = Number(n); if (!n) return ''; if (n >= 1048576) return (n / 1048576).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + ' MB'; return Math.max(1, Math.round(n / 1024)) + ' KB'; }
  function arr(v) {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(function (x) { return String(x == null ? '' : x).trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
    if (typeof v === 'string') return v.split(/[,;\n]/).map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
    return [];
  }
  function clean(s, max) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\s*[\u2014\u2013]\s*/g, ', ').replace(/,\s*,/g, ',').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return max ? s.slice(0, max) : s; }
  function slug(s) {
    s = String(s || '').toLowerCase();
    try { s = s.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, ''); } catch (e) { /* old browsers */ }
    return s.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
  }
  function uclass(p) { return p.status === 'archived' ? 'u-arch' : p.status === 'needs_labels' ? 'u-needs' : 'u-' + (USAGE[p.usage] ? p.usage : 'check'); }
  function ulabel(p) { return p.status === 'archived' ? 'Archived' : p.status === 'needs_labels' ? 'Needs a look' : (USAGE[p.usage] || USAGE.check).label; }
  function badge(p) { return '<span class="w118cub ' + uclass(p) + '"><i></i>' + E(ulabel(p)) + '</span>'; }
  function focalCss(p) { return 'object-position:' + Math.round((p.focal_x == null ? 0.5 : p.focal_x) * 100) + '% ' + Math.round((p.focal_y == null ? 0.5 : p.focal_y) * 100) + '%;'; }
  function url(p, size) {
    if (!p) return '';
    var k = size === 'full' ? 'url_full' : size === 'thumb' ? 'url_thumb' : 'url_web';
    if (p[k]) return p[k];
    var path = p['path_' + (size || 'web')];
    return path ? BASE + path + '?v=' + (p.version || 1) : '';
  }
  function dlUrl(p, size) { var u = url(p, size); if (!u) return ''; return u + (u.indexOf('?') >= 0 ? '&' : '?') + 'download=' + encodeURIComponent(p.key + (size === 'full' ? '' : '-' + size) + '.jpg'); }
  function hay(p) { return [p.title, p.alt_text, p.caption, p.description, p.place, p.event, p.people, p.series, p.notes, p.credit, p.key, label(SCENES, p.scene), label(SEASONS, p.season), arr(p.tags).join(' '), arr(p.partner).join(' '), String(p.taken_on || '').slice(0, 4)].join(' ').toLowerCase(); }
  function byKey(k) { for (var i = 0; i < P.rows.length; i++) if (P.rows[i].key === k) return P.rows[i]; return null; }
  function norm(r) {
    r = Object.assign({}, r);
    r.tags = arr(r.tags); r.partner = arr(r.partner); r.best_for = arr(r.best_for);
    if (r.focal_x == null) r.focal_x = 0.5; if (r.focal_y == null) r.focal_y = 0.5;
    r.status = r.status || 'ready'; r.usage = r.usage || 'check';
    ['full', 'web', 'thumb'].forEach(function (s) { if (!r['url_' + s] && r['path_' + s]) r['url_' + s] = BASE + r['path_' + s] + '?v=' + (r.version || 1); });
    return r;
  }
  /* on the same day: confirmed before new, ready before crop before consent, picks first */
  function rank(p) { var u = p.status === 'needs_labels' ? 8 : USAGE_ORDER.indexOf(p.usage); if (u < 0) u = 9; return u * 2 + (p.is_pick ? 0 : 1); }
  function sortRows(list) {
    var s = P.sort;
    return list.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      if (s === 'added') return String(b.created_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.created_at || '')) || a.key.localeCompare(b.key);
      if (s === 'title') return String(a.title || '').localeCompare(String(b.title || ''));
      var x = a.taken_on || '', y = b.taken_on || '';
      if (x === y) return rank(a) - rank(b) || a.key.localeCompare(b.key);
      if (!x) return 1; if (!y) return -1;
      return s === 'taken_asc' ? x.localeCompare(y) : y.localeCompare(x);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function load(force) {
    if (P.loading) return P.loading;
    if (P.loaded && !force && Date.now() - P.at < 120000) return;
    P.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.from('wa_photos_list').select('*').order('taken_on', { ascending: false, nullsFirst: false }).limit(2000);
        if (r.error) throw r.error;
        P.rows = (r.data || []).map(norm); P.loaded = true; P.err = null; P.at = Date.now();
      } catch (e) { P.err = (e && e.message) || String(e); }
      P.loading = null;
    })();
    return P.loading;
  }
  function put(row) { row = norm(row); var i = P.rows.findIndex(function (x) { return x.key === row.key; }); if (i >= 0) P.rows[i] = Object.assign({}, P.rows[i], row); else P.rows.unshift(row); return byKey(row.key); }
  async function update(key, patch, what) {
    var old = byKey(key); patch = Object.assign({}, patch, { updated_by: me() });
    var r = await db.from('wa_photos').update(patch).eq('key', key).select().maybeSingle();
    if (r.error) throw r.error;
    var row = put(Object.assign({}, old || {}, r.data && r.data.key ? r.data : patch, { key: key }));
    if (what) { try { if (typeof logChange === 'function') logChange('photo', key, what.field, what.from, what.to); } catch (e) { /* audit is best effort */ } }
    return row;
  }
  function diffText(a, b, keys) {
    var from = [], to = [];
    var fmt = function (x) { if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.join(', '); if (typeof x === 'number') return String(Math.round(x * 1000) / 1000); return x == null ? '' : String(x); };
    keys.forEach(function (k) {
      var x = fmt(a[k]), y = fmt(b[k]);
      if (x !== y) { from.push(k + ': ' + x.slice(0, 80)); to.push(k + ': ' + y.slice(0, 80)); }
    });
    return { from: from.join('; ').slice(0, 900), to: to.join('; ').slice(0, 900), n: to.length };
  }

  /* ---------- images in the browser ---------- */
  function loadImg(src, cors) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var im = new Image(); if (cors) im.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'; im.decoding = 'async';
      im.onload = function () { res(im); }; im.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('Could not read this image')); };
      im.src = src;
    });
  }
  function drawTo(src, max) {
    var w = src.naturalWidth || src.width, h = src.naturalHeight || src.height;
    var s = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(w, h)); var cw = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * s)), ch = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * s));
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = cw; c.height = ch;
    var x = c.getContext('2d'); x.imageSmoothingEnabled = true; x.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
    x.fillStyle = '#FFFAF2'; x.fillRect(0, 0, cw, ch); x.drawImage(src, 0, 0, cw, ch);
    return c;
  }
  function blobOf(c, q) { return new Promise(function (res, rej) { c.toBlob(function (b) { if (b) res(b); else rej(new Error('Could not make the JPEG')); }, 'image/jpeg', q); }); }
  function avgColor(c) {
    try {
      var t = document.createElement('canvas'); t.width = 12; t.height = 12; var x = t.getContext('2d'); x.drawImage(c, 0, 0, 12, 12);
      var d = x.getImageData(0, 0, 12, 12).data, r = 0, g = 0, b = 0, n = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) { r += d[i]; g += d[i + 1]; b += d[i + 2]; n++; }
      var hx = function (v) { return ('0' + Math.round(v / n).toString(16)).slice(-2); };
      return ('#' + hx(r) + hx(g) + hx(b)).toUpperCase();
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  /* the date (and camera) from a JPEG's EXIF block; nothing else is read or kept */
  function exif(buf) {
    var out = {};
    try {
      var v = new DataView(buf), len = v.byteLength;
      if (len < 12 || v.getUint16(0) !== 0xFFD8) return out;
      var off = 2;
      while (off + 10 < len) {
        var mk = v.getUint16(off), size = v.getUint16(off + 2);
        if (mk === 0xFFE1 && v.getUint32(off + 4) === 0x45786966) {
          var t = off + 10, le = v.getUint16(t) === 0x4949;
          var g16 = function (o) { return v.getUint16(o, le); }, g32 = function (o) { return v.getUint32(o, le); };
          var str = function (o, n) { var s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < n && o + i < len; i++) { var ch = v.getUint8(o + i); if (!ch) break; s += String.fromCharCode(ch); } return s.trim(); };
          var ifd = function (at, want) {
            var res = {}; if (t + at + 2 > len) return res; var n = g16(t + at);
            for (var i = 0; i < n && i < 400; i++) {
              var e = t + at + 2 + i * 12; if (e + 12 > len) break;
              var tag = g16(e), type = g16(e + 2), cnt = g32(e + 4);
              if (want.indexOf(tag) < 0) continue;
              if (type === 2) res[tag] = str(cnt > 4 ? t + g32(e + 8) : e + 8, Math.min(cnt, 64));
              else if (type === 3) res[tag] = g16(e + 8);
              else if (type === 4) res[tag] = g32(e + 8);
            }
            return res;
          };
          var i0 = ifd(g32(t + 4), [0x010F, 0x0110, 0x0132, 0x8769]);
          var dt = i0[0x0132];
          if (i0[0x8769]) { var ex = ifd(i0[0x8769], [0x9003, 0x9004]); dt = ex[0x9003] || ex[0x9004] || dt; }
          if (dt && /^(19[89]\d|20\d\d):[01]\d:[0-3]\d/.test(dt) && dt.slice(0, 10) !== '0000:00:00') { out.date = dt.slice(0, 10).replace(/:/g, '-'); out.time = dt.slice(11, 16); }
          var make = clean(i0[0x010F] || '', 40), model = clean(i0[0x0110] || '', 40);
          if (model) out.camera = model.toLowerCase().indexOf(make.toLowerCase().split(' ')[0]) === 0 || !make ? model : make + ' ' + model;
          return out;
        }
        if (mk === 0xFFDA || (mk & 0xFF00) !== 0xFF00) break;
        off += 2 + size;
      }
    } catch (e) { /* no date then */ }
    return out;
  }
  async function b64For(p, canvas) {
    var c = canvas;
    if (!c) { var im = await loadImg(url(p, 'web'), true); c = im; }
    var s = drawTo(c, 1024);
    var d = s.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
    return d.slice(d.indexOf(',') + 1);
  }

  /* ---------- AI: first labels from the photo_label route ---------- */
  function normSuggestion(inp) {
    inp = inp || {}; var o = {};
    ['title', 'alt_text', 'caption', 'description', 'usage_note', 'people'].forEach(function (k) { if (inp[k] != null && String(inp[k]).trim()) o[k] = clean(inp[k], k === 'title' ? 160 : k === 'alt_text' ? 400 : 1200); });
    if (o.title) o.title = o.title.replace(/[.]+$/, '');
    if (SEASONS.some(function (s) { return s[0] === inp.season; })) o.season = inp.season;
    if (SCENES.some(function (s) { return s[0] === inp.scene; })) o.scene = inp.scene;
    if (USAGE[inp.usage]) o.usage = inp.usage;
    if (BRANDING.some(function (s) { return s[0] === inp.branding; })) o.branding = inp.branding;
    var tags = arr(inp.tags).map(function (x) { return x.toLowerCase().replace(/^#/, ''); }).filter(function (x, i, a) { return x && a.indexOf(x) === i; }).slice(0, 12);
    if (tags.length) o.tags = tags;
    var bf = arr(inp.best_for).map(function (x) { return x.toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]+/g, '_'); }).filter(function (x, i, a) { return BEST.some(function (b) { return b[0] === x; }) && a.indexOf(x) === i; });
    if (bf.length) o.best_for = bf;
    var fx = Number(inp.focal_x), fy = Number(inp.focal_y);
    if (isFinite(fx) && isFinite(fy)) { o.focal_x = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, fx)); o.focal_y = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, fy)); }
    return o;
  }
  async function suggest(p, canvas, hints) {
    var data = await b64For(p, canvas);
    var known = [];
    if (hints && hints.file) known.push('file name ' + hints.file);
    if (p.taken_on) known.push('taken ' + niceDate(p.taken_on));
    if (p.place) known.push('place: ' + p.place);
    if (p.event) known.push('event: ' + p.event);
    if (p.series) known.push('part of the set ' + p.series);
    var body = { max_tokens: 1200, system: SPEC.system, tools: [SPEC.tool], tool_choice: { type: 'tool', name: SPEC.tool.name },
      messages: [{ role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'image', source: { type: 'base64', media_type: 'image/jpeg', data: data } }, { type: 'text', text: 'Label this photo.' + (known.length ? ' What we know: ' + known.join('; ') + '.' : '') }] }] };
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('llm', { headers: { 'x-wa-task': 'photo_label', 'x-wa-caller': 'photos' }, body: body });
    if (r.error) { var m = (r.error && r.error.message) || String(r.error); if (/401|unauthor/i.test(m)) m = 'Suggested labels from the Hub are not open to this account'; throw new Error(m); }
    var tu = ((r.data && r.data.content) || []).filter(function (c) { return c && c.type === 'tool_use'; })[0];
    if (!tu || !tu.input) throw new Error((r.data && r.data.error && r.data.error.message) || 'nothing came back');
    var o = normSuggestion(tu.input);
    if (!o.title && !o.alt_text) throw new Error('nothing usable came back');
    return { fields: o, model: (r.data._wa && r.data._wa.model) || r.data.model || null, at: new Date().toISOString() };
  }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  var INTRO = 'Our photos, labelled and cleared for use. Copy the alt text and caption, download the size you need, or send a photo straight to the Create studio or a post.';
  var INTRO_RO = 'Our photos, labelled and cleared for use. Copy the alt text and caption, or download the size you need.';
  function panel() {
    var main = document.querySelector('main'); if (!main) return null;
    var sec = document.getElementById('photos');
    if (!sec) { sec = document.createElement('section'); sec.id = 'photos'; sec.className = 'panel'; main.appendChild(sec); }
    sec.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1');
    if (!sec.querySelector('.w118chost')) {
      sec.innerHTML = '<div class="w116ptop"><div class="crumb">Other Tools</div><h2>Photo library</h2><p>' + E(INTRO) + '</p><button class="w43help" data-w118chelp="1" type="button">&#63; How this page works</button></div><div class="w118chost"><p class="read">Loading the photo library...</p></div>';
    }
    return sec;
  }
  function live() { return P.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'archived'; }); }
  function base(except) {
    /* every filter except one, so each chip shows what it would add */
    var f = P.f;
    return P.rows.filter(function (p) {
      if (f.status === 'archived') { if (p.status !== 'archived') return false; }
      else { if (p.status === 'archived') return false; if (except !== 'status' && f.status === 'needs_labels' && p.status !== 'needs_labels') return false; }
      if (except !== 'usage' && f.usage && (p.usage !== f.usage || p.status === 'needs_labels')) return false;
      if (except !== 'season' && f.season && p.season !== f.season) return false;
      if (except !== 'scene' && f.scene && p.scene !== f.scene) return false;
      if (except !== 'picks' && f.picks && !p.is_pick) return false;
      if (f.series && p.series !== f.series) return false;
      if (f.tag && arr(p.tags).indexOf(f.tag) < 0) return false;
      if (f.q) { var h = hay(p); var terms = f.q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); for (var i = 0; i < terms.length; i++) if (h.indexOf(terms[i]) < 0) return false; }
      return true;
    });
  }
  function shown() { return sortRows(base(null)); }
  function chip(attr, val, text, on, count, dot, extra) {
    var off = count === 0 && !on;
    return '<span data-' + attr + '="' + E(val) + '" class="' + (on ? 'on' : '') + (off ? ' off' : '') + (extra ? ' ' + extra : '') + '" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-pressed="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' + (dot || '') + E(text) + (count != null ? ' <em>' + count + '</em>' : '') + '</span>';
  }
  function toolbar() {
    var f = P.f, st = staff();
    var bu = base('usage'), bs = base('season'), bp = base('picks');
    var cu = function (u) { return bu.filter(function (p) { return p.usage === u && p.status !== 'needs_labels'; }).length; };
    var h = '<div class="pbar"><div class="pr1">' +
      '<label class="srch">' + ic('search') + '<input type="search" id="w118cq" placeholder="Search titles, tags, places, people..." value="' + E(f.q) + '" aria-label="Search the photos"></label>' +
      '<select class="sl" id="w118cscene" aria-label="Scene"><option value="">Any scene</option>' + SCENES.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + s[0] + '"' + (f.scene === s[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select class="sl" id="w118csort" aria-label="Order"><option value="taken"' + (P.sort === 'taken' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Newest first</option><option value="taken_asc"' + (P.sort === 'taken_asc' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Oldest first</option><option value="added"' + (P.sort === 'added' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Recently added</option><option value="title"' + (P.sort === 'title' ? ' selected' : '') + '>By title</option></select>' +
      '<span class="sp"></span>' +
      (st ? '<button class="add" data-w118cgo="new" type="button">' + ic('upload') + 'Add photos</button>' : '') +
      '</div><div class="chips">' +
      chip('w118cu', '', 'All', !f.usage, bu.length) +
      USAGE_ORDER.filter(function (u) { return u === 'ready' || u === 'crop' || u === 'consent' || cu(u) > 0 || f.usage === u; }).map(function (u) { return chip('w118cu', u, USAGE[u].label, f.usage === u, cu(u), '<i class="w118cdot u-' + u + '"></i>'); }).join('') +
      '<b class="dv"></b>' +
      SEASONS.map(function (s) { return chip('w118cs', s[0], s[1], f.season === s[0], bs.filter(function (p) { return p.season === s[0]; }).length); }).join('') +
      '<b class="dv"></b>' +
      chip('w118cpk', '1', 'Picks', f.picks, bp.filter(function (p) { return p.is_pick; }).length, ic('star'), 'pk');
    if (st) {
      var nl = P.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'needs_labels'; }).length, ar = P.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'archived'; }).length;
      if (nl || f.status === 'needs_labels') h += '<b class="dv"></b>' + chip('w118cst', 'needs_labels', 'Needs a look', f.status === 'needs_labels', nl, '<i class="w118cdot u-needs"></i>');
      if (ar || f.status === 'archived') h += (nl ? '' : '<b class="dv"></b>') + chip('w118cst', 'archived', 'Archived', f.status === 'archived', ar);
    }
    if (f.series) h += '<b class="dv"></b><span class="x on" data-w118cclr="series" role="button" tabindex="0">Set: ' + E(f.series) + ' &nbsp;&#215;</span>';
    if (f.tag) h += (f.series ? '' : '<b class="dv"></b>') + '<span class="x on" data-w118cclr="tag" role="button" tabindex="0">Tag: ' + E(f.tag) + ' &nbsp;&#215;</span>';
    h += '</div><div class="legend"><i class="w118cdot u-ready"></i><b>Ready</b> for posts, the website, decks and reports. &nbsp; <i class="w118cdot u-crop"></i><b>Crop first:</b> crop the edge in the note before it goes public. &nbsp; <i class="w118cdot u-consent"></i><b>Consent needed:</b> someone we serve or a child is recognizable, so inside the team, or public with their consent.</div></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function card(p) {
    var meta = [monthYear(p.taken_on) || 'Date unknown', label(SEASONS, p.season), label(SCENES, p.scene)].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ');
    return '<button type="button" class="ph' + (p.status === 'archived' ? ' arch' : '') + '" data-w118cop="' + E(p.key) + '" aria-label="' + E(p.title + ', ' + ulabel(p)) + '">' +
      '<span class="im" style="background:' + E(p.color || '#d9d6cc') + '"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" src="' + E(url(p, 'thumb')) + '" alt="' + E(p.alt_text || p.title) + '" style="' + focalCss(p) + '">' + badge(p) + (p.is_pick ? '<span class="w118cstar" title="One of our picks">' + ic('star') + '</span>' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="tx"><b>' + E(p.title) + '</b><em>' + E(meta) + '</em></span></button>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var sec = panel(); if (!sec) return;
    var host = sec.querySelector('.w118chost'); if (!host) return;
    var intro = sec.querySelector('.w116ptop p'); if (intro) { var want = staff() ? INTRO : INTRO_RO; if (intro.textContent !== want) intro.textContent = want; }
    if (!allowed()) { host.innerHTML = '<div class="w116empty">The photo library is open to staff and board accounts.</div>'; return; }
    if (!P.loaded) { host.innerHTML = P.err ? '<div class="w116empty">Could not load the photos: ' + E(P.err) + '</div>' : '<p class="read">Loading the photo library...</p>'; return; }
    var qEl = document.getElementById('w118cq'), hadFocus = qEl && document.activeElement === qEl, caret = hadFocus ? qEl.selectionStart : null;
    var list = shown(), html = toolbar();
    var nLive = live().length;
    if (!P.rows.length) {
      html += '<div class="w116empty" style="margin-top:14px">No photos yet.' + (staff() ? ' Add the first ones with Add photos: the hub makes the sizes and suggests the labels.' : '') + '</div>';
    } else if (!list.length) {
      html += '<div class="w116empty" style="margin-top:14px">No photo matches that. <a data-w118cclr="all" style="cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;font-weight:700">Clear the filters</a></div>';
    } else {
      var picked = list.slice(0, P.limit);
      html += '<div class="sum">' + (list.length === nLive && P.f.status !== 'archived' ? nLive + ' photo' + (nLive === 1 ? '' : 's') : list.length + ' of ' + nLive + ' photo' + (nLive === 1 ? '' : 's')) + (P.f.status === 'archived' ? ' (archived)' : '') + '</div>';
      if (P.sort === 'taken' || P.sort === 'taken_asc') {
        var groups = [], cur = null;
        picked.forEach(function (p) { var y = p.taken_on ? String(p.taken_on).slice(0, 4) : 'Date unknown'; if (!cur || cur.y !== y) { cur = { y: y, list: [] }; groups.push(cur); } cur.list.push(p); });
        groups.forEach(function (g) {
          var all = list.filter(function (p) { return (p.taken_on ? String(p.taken_on).slice(0, 4) : 'Date unknown') === g.y; }).length;
          html += '<div class="grp"><h3>' + E(g.y) + '</h3><span>' + all + ' photo' + (all === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span></div><div class="grid">' + g.list.map(card).join('') + '</div>';
        });
      } else {
        html += '<div class="grid" style="margin-top:14px">' + picked.map(card).join('') + '</div>';
      }
      if (list.length > picked.length) html += '<button class="more" data-w118cmore="1" type="button">Show ' + Math.min(120, list.length - picked.length) + ' more</button>';
    }
    html += '<div class="foot">Files live in Supabase storage, <code>content-images/photos/</code> (full, web and thumb), and the labels in the <code>wa_photos</code> table.' + (staff() ? ' Every change is in the Activity log.' : '') + '</div>';
    host.innerHTML = html;
    if (hadFocus) { var q2 = document.getElementById('w118cq'); if (q2) { q2.focus(); try { q2.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }

  /* ---------- the side panel: one photo ---------- */
  function drawerEl() { var d = document.getElementById('w118cdr'); if (!d) { d = document.createElement('div'); d.id = 'w118cdr'; d.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1'); document.body.appendChild(d); } return d; }
  function neighbours() { var list = shown(); var i = list.findIndex(function (x) { return x.key === P.open; }); return { list: list, i: i }; }
  function openPhoto(key, fromRoute) {
    var p = byKey(key); if (!p) { toastS('That photo is not in the library'); return; }
    P.open = key; P.mode = 'view'; P.edit = null; paintDrawer();
    if (!fromRoute) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#photos/' + encodeURIComponent(key)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function closeDrawer(force) {
    if (!P.mode) return;
    if (!force && P.mode === 'edit' && P.edit && P.edit.dirty && !confirm('Close without saving the label changes?')) return;
    if (!force && P.mode === 'upload' && P.up && P.up.running) toastS('Still adding photos in the background');
    P.open = null; P.mode = null; P.edit = null;
    var d = document.getElementById('w118cdr'); if (d) d.remove();
    try { if (/^#photos\/./.test(location.hash)) history.replaceState(null, '', '#photos'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function step(dir) {
    if (P.mode !== 'view') return;
    var n = neighbours(); if (n.i < 0) return;
    var nx = n.list[n.i + dir]; if (nx) openPhoto(nx.key);
  }
  function copyBox(lbl, text, key, sub) {
    return '<div class="sec"><div class="lbl">' + E(lbl) + (sub ? ' <small>' + E(sub) + '</small>' : '') + '</div><div class="copy"><p' + (text ? '' : ' class="none"') + '>' + (text ? E(text) : 'Not written yet.') + '</p>' + (text ? '<button type="button" data-w118ccopy="' + key + '">' + ic('copy') + 'Copy</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function fact(dt, dd) { return dd ? '<dt>' + E(dt) + '</dt><dd>' + dd + '</dd>' : ''; }
  function viewHtml(p) {
    var st = staff(), n = neighbours(), u = USAGE[p.usage] || USAGE.check;
    var useTitle = p.status === 'archived' ? 'Archived' : p.status === 'needs_labels' ? 'Needs a look' : u.long;
    var useText = p.status === 'archived' ? 'Hidden from the library. ' + (st ? 'Restore it to use it again.' : '') : p.status === 'needs_labels' ? 'New, with first labels from the Hub that nobody has confirmed yet. ' + (p.usage && p.usage !== 'check' ? 'The Hub thinks: ' + u.long.toLowerCase() + '.' : '') : u.tip;
    var h = '<div class="bk" data-w118cx="1"></div><aside class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="' + E(p.title) + '">' +
      '<header><h3 title="' + E(p.title) + '">' + E(p.title) + '</h3>' + (n.i >= 0 ? '<span class="ct">' + (n.i + 1) + ' of ' + n.list.length + '</span>' : '') +
      '<button class="ib" data-w118cstep="-1" title="Previous (left arrow)" aria-label="Previous photo"' + (n.i > 0 ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + ic('left') + '</button>' +
      '<button class="ib" data-w118cstep="1" title="Next (right arrow)" aria-label="Next photo"' + (n.i >= 0 && n.i < n.list.length - 1 ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + ic('right') + '</button>' +
      '<button class="ib" data-w118cx="1" title="Close (Esc)" aria-label="Close">' + ic('close') + '</button></header>' +
      '<div class="bd">' +
      '<figure class="hero" style="margin:0 0 14px;background:' + E(p.color || '#d9d6cc') + '"><span class="fw"><img src="' + E(url(p, 'web')) + '" alt="' + E(p.alt_text || p.title) + '"></span></figure>' +
      '<div class="use ' + uclass(p) + '"><b>' + E(useTitle) + '</b>' + E(useText) + (p.usage_note ? '<p>' + E(p.usage_note) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="acts">' +
      (st && p.status !== 'archived' && tabOk('create') ? '<button type="button" class="pri" data-w118cuse="create">' + ic('brush') + 'Use in the Create studio</button>' : '') +
      (st && p.status !== 'archived' && typeof window.w121Compose === 'function' && tabOk('contentcal') ? '<button type="button" class="pri" data-w118cuse="post">' + ic('calendar') + 'Use in a post</button>' : '') +
      '<button type="button" data-w118ccopy="link">' + ic('link') + 'Copy the link</button>' +
      '<a href="' + E(dlUrl(p, 'web')) + '" download>' + ic('download') + 'Download</a>' +
      '</div>' +
      copyBox('Alt text', p.alt_text, 'alt', 'for screen readers, paste it in the alt text field') +
      copyBox('Caption', p.caption, 'caption', 'a starting line for a post or a report') +
      (p.description ? '<div class="sec"><div class="lbl">About this photo</div><p class="desc">' + E(p.description) + '</p></div>' : '') +
      '<div class="sec"><div class="lbl">Download</div><div class="dl">' + SIZES.map(function (s) {
        var w = p.width, hh = p.height, big = Math.max(w || 0, hh || 0), sc = big ? Math.min(1, s[1] / big) : 0;
        var dims = s[0] === 'full' && w ? w + ' x ' + hh : sc ? Math.round(w * sc) + ' x ' + Math.round(hh * sc) : '';
        return '<a href="' + E(dlUrl(p, s[0])) + '" download title="' + E(s[4]) + '">' + ic('download') + E(s[3]) + ' <span>' + E([dims, bytes(p['bytes_' + s[0]])].filter(Boolean).join(', ')) + '</span></a>';
      }).join('') + '</div><div class="dlh">Full for print and big screens, Web for posts, the website and decks, Thumbnail for emails. No location or camera data in any of them.</div></div>' +
      (arr(p.tags).length ? '<div class="sec"><div class="lbl">Tags</div><div class="tags">' + arr(p.tags).map(function (t) { return '<span data-w118ctag="' + E(t) + '" title="Show every photo tagged ' + E(t) + '">' + E(t) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>' : '') +
      '<div class="sec"><div class="lbl">Details</div><dl class="facts">' +
      fact('Taken', p.taken_on ? E(niceDate(p.taken_on)) + (p.date_note ? '<small>' + E(p.date_note) + '</small>' : '') : (p.date_note ? E(p.date_note) : 'Date unknown')) +
      fact('Season', E(label(SEASONS, p.season))) +
      fact('Place', E(p.place || '')) +
      fact('Event', E(p.event || '')) +
      fact('Scene', E(label(SCENES, p.scene))) +
      fact('People', E(p.people || '')) +
      fact('Partners', E(arr(p.partner).join(', '))) +
      fact('Branding', E(label(BRANDING, p.branding))) +
      fact('Set', p.series ? '<a data-w118cseries="' + E(p.series) + '">' + E(p.series) + '</a> <small>' + P.rows.filter(function (x) { return x.series === p.series && x.status !== 'archived'; }).length + ' photos in this set</small>' : '') +
      fact('Best for', arr(p.best_for).length ? '<span class="tags">' + arr(p.best_for).map(function (b) { return '<span class="bf">' + E(label(BEST, b)) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</span>' : '') +
      fact('Size', p.width ? E(p.width + ' x ' + p.height + ' px') + (p.orientation ? ', ' + E(p.orientation) : '') : '') +
      fact('Credit', E(p.credit || '')) +
      fact('Notes', E(p.notes || '')) +
      fact('Source', E(p.source || '')) +
      fact('Labels', E(p.labelled_by || '') + (p.updated_by && p.updated_by !== p.labelled_by ? '<small>Last change by ' + E(p.updated_by) + '</small>' : '')) +
      fact('File name', '<code>' + E(p.key) + '.jpg</code>') +
      '</dl></div>' +
      '</div>';
    var foot = '';
    if (st) {
      foot = '<footer>' +
        (p.status === 'archived' ? '<button type="button" class="b" data-w118cact="restore">' + ic('restore') + 'Restore</button>' : '<button type="button" class="b go" data-w118cact="edit">' + ic('pen') + (p.status === 'needs_labels' ? 'Check the labels' : 'Edit the labels') + '</button>') +
        '<span class="sp"></span>' +
        (p.status === 'archived' ? (admin() ? '<button type="button" class="lnk del" data-w118cact="delete">' + ic('trash') + 'Delete for good</button>' : '') : '<button type="button" class="lnk" data-w118cact="archive">' + ic('archive') + 'Archive</button>') +
        '</footer>';
    }
    return h + foot + '</aside>';
  }

  /* ---------- editing the labels ---------- */
  var FIELDS = ['title', 'alt_text', 'caption', 'description', 'usage', 'usage_note', 'people', 'taken_on', 'date_note', 'season', 'place', 'event', 'scene', 'series', 'tags', 'partner', 'branding', 'best_for', 'is_pick', 'focal_x', 'focal_y', 'credit', 'notes'];
  function startEdit(p) {
    var v = {}; FIELDS.forEach(function (k) { v[k] = Array.isArray(p[k]) ? p[k].slice() : p[k]; });
    P.mode = 'edit'; P.edit = { key: p.key, v: v, dirty: false, ai: null, aiBusy: false, aiErr: null, flag: {} };
    if (p.status === 'needs_labels' && p.ai_suggestion && p.ai_suggestion.fields) { P.edit.ai = p.ai_suggestion; }
    paintDrawer();
  }
  function val(k) { var v = P.edit.v[k]; return v == null ? '' : v; }
  function aiHint(k) {
    var e = P.edit; if (!e || !e.ai || !e.ai.fields || e.ai.fields[k] == null) return '';
    if (k === 'focal_x') {
      var ax = Number(e.ai.fields.focal_x), ay = Number(e.ai.fields.focal_y);
      if (Math.abs(Number(e.v.focal_x) - ax) < 0.01 && Math.abs(Number(e.v.focal_y) - ay) < 0.01) return '';
      return '<div class="aih">' + ic('sparkle') + '<span>The Hub suggests: the main subject sits ' + Math.round(ax * 100) + '% across and ' + Math.round(ay * 100) + '% down</span><a data-w118cai="focal_x">Use this</a></div>';
    }
    var s = e.ai.fields[k], cur = e.v[k];
    var same = Array.isArray(s) ? arr(cur).join('|') === s.join('|') : String(cur == null ? '' : cur) === String(s);
    if (same) return '';
    var shownV = Array.isArray(s) ? s.map(function (x) { return k === 'best_for' ? label(BEST, x) : x; }).join(' \u00b7 ') : k === 'usage' ? USAGE[s].label : k === 'season' ? label(SEASONS, s) : k === 'scene' ? label(SCENES, s) : k === 'branding' ? label(BRANDING, s) : String(s);
    return '<div class="aih">' + ic('sparkle') + '<span>The Hub suggests: ' + E(shownV) + '</span><a data-w118cai="' + k + '">Use this</a></div>';
  }
  function inp(k, lab, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var id = 'w118cf-' + k, v = val(k), fl = P.edit.flag[k] ? ' aiflag' : '';
    var cnt = opts.max ? '<span class="cnt' + (String(v).length > opts.max ? ' bad' : '') + '" data-w118ccnt="' + k + '">' + String(v).length + ' / ' + opts.max + '</span>' : '';
    var field;
    if (opts.area) field = '<textarea id="' + id + '" data-w118cf="' + k + '" class="' + fl + '" rows="' + (opts.rows || 2) + '" placeholder="' + E(opts.ph || '') + '">' + E(v) + '</textarea>';
    else if (opts.select) field = '<select id="' + id + '" data-w118cf="' + k + '" class="' + fl + '">' + (opts.blank ? '<option value="">' + E(opts.blank) + '</option>' : '') + opts.select.map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + E(o[0]) + '"' + (String(v) === String(o[0]) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(o[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';
    else field = '<input type="' + (opts.date ? 'date' : 'text') + '" id="' + id + '" data-w118cf="' + k + '" class="' + fl + '" value="' + E(Array.isArray(v) ? v.join(', ') : v) + '" placeholder="' + E(opts.ph || '') + '">';
    return '<div class="' + (opts.full ? 'full' : '') + '"><label class="fl" for="' + id + '">' + E(lab) + cnt + '</label>' + field + (opts.hint ? '<div class="hint">' + E(opts.hint) + '</div>' : '') + aiHint(k) + '</div>';
  }
  function cropsHtml(p) {
    var fx = Number(val('focal_x') || 0.5), fy = Number(val('focal_y') || 0.5), pos = 'object-position:' + Math.round(fx * 100) + '% ' + Math.round(fy * 100) + '%;';
    return '<div class="crops">' + CROPS.map(function (c) { var hgt = 86, wd = Math.round(hgt * c[1]); return '<div><div class="cv" style="width:' + wd + 'px;height:' + hgt + 'px;background:' + E(p.color || '#ddd') + '"><img src="' + E(url(p, 'thumb')) + '" alt="" style="' + pos + '"></div>' + E(c[0]) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function editHtml(p) {
    var e = P.edit, fx = Number(val('focal_x') || 0.5), fy = Number(val('focal_y') || 0.5);
    var h = '<div class="bk" data-w118cx="1"></div><aside class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Edit the labels">' +
      '<header><h3>' + (p.status === 'needs_labels' ? 'Check the labels' : 'Edit the labels') + '</h3><button class="ib" data-w118cx="1" title="Close (Esc)" aria-label="Close">' + ic('close') + '</button></header><div class="bd">' +
      '<figure class="hero pickable" style="margin:0 0 8px;background:' + E(p.color || '#d9d6cc') + '"><span class="fw" data-w118cfocal="1"><img src="' + E(url(p, 'web')) + '" alt="' + E(p.alt_text || p.title) + '" draggable="false"><span class="fm" style="left:' + (fx * 100) + '%;top:' + (fy * 100) + '%"></span></span></figure>' +
      '<p class="note" style="margin:0 0 6px">' + ic('target') + ' Click the photo on the main subject: every crop keeps that point in view.</p>' + cropsHtml(p) + aiHint('focal_x') +
      '<div style="height:16px"></div>' +
      (e.aiBusy ? '<div class="busy"><i></i>Looking at the photo...</div>' : '') +
      (e.aiErr ? '<div class="use u-consent" style="margin:0 0 14px"><b>No suggestion from the Hub</b>' + E(e.aiErr) + '</div>' : '') +
      (e.ai && e.ai.fields && !e.aiBusy ? '<div class="use u-check" style="margin:0 0 14px"><b>The Hub\u2019s suggestion' + (e.ai.model ? ' (' + E(String(e.ai.model).replace(/^[a-z-]+\//, '')) + ')' : '') + '</b>Fields the Hub would change show a purple note: Use this takes it. <a data-w118cai="*" style="font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">Use every suggestion</a></div>' : '') +
      '<div class="fs"><h4>What it shows</h4><div class="fg">' +
      inp('title', 'Title', { full: true, ph: 'A few words, no date' }) +
      inp('alt_text', 'Alt text', { full: true, area: true, max: 250, ph: 'Who (by role) and what is visible, and where', hint: 'Read aloud by screen readers. One sentence; describe people by role, not by name.' }) +
      inp('caption', 'Caption', { full: true, area: true, ph: 'One warm line about the moment' }) +
      inp('description', 'Description', { full: true, area: true, rows: 3, ph: 'Two or three sentences for the library' }) +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="fs"><h4>Can we use it</h4><div class="fg">' +
      inp('usage', 'Cleared for', { select: USAGE_ORDER.map(function (u) { return [u, USAGE[u].label + ': ' + USAGE[u].long.toLowerCase()]; }) }) +
      inp('people', 'People in it', { ph: 'Three volunteers in green vests' }) +
      inp('usage_note', 'Why', { full: true, area: true, ph: 'Who is recognizable, what to crop, whose consent we have', hint: 'When someone gives consent, set it to Ready and note who agreed and when.' }) +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="fs"><h4>When and where</h4><div class="fg">' +
      inp('taken_on', 'Taken on', { date: true }) +
      inp('season', 'Season', { select: SEASONS, blank: 'Not set' }) +
      inp('date_note', 'About the date', { full: true, ph: 'From the camera; or roughly, from the event' }) +
      inp('place', 'Place', { ph: 'Montreal' }) +
      inp('event', 'Event', { ph: 'Sunday serving' }) +
      inp('scene', 'Scene', { select: SCENES, blank: 'Not set' }) +
      inp('series', 'Set', { ph: 'serving-2024-10', hint: 'Photos from the same moment share a set name.' }) +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="fs"><h4>Finding it</h4><div class="fg">' +
      inp('tags', 'Tags', { full: true, ph: 'volunteers, tent, summer', hint: 'Separate with commas.' }) +
      inp('partner', 'Partners in view', { ph: 'VCSA' }) +
      inp('branding', 'Branding', { select: BRANDING, blank: 'Not set' }) +
      '<div class="full"><label class="fl">Best for</label><div class="opts">' + BEST.map(function (b) { return '<span data-w118cbest="' + b[0] + '" class="' + (arr(val('best_for')).indexOf(b[0]) >= 0 ? 'on' : '') + '" role="button" tabindex="0">' + E(b[1]) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' + aiHint('best_for') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="full"><label class="ck"><input type="checkbox" data-w118cf="is_pick"' + (val('is_pick') ? ' checked' : '') + '> One of our picks (the best of a set, first choice for decks and posts)</label></div>' +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="fs"><h4>Credit and notes</h4><div class="fg">' +
      inp('credit', 'Credit', { ph: 'Photographer, if we should credit one' }) +
      inp('notes', 'Notes', { area: true, ph: 'Anything the next person should know' }) +
      '</div></div>' +
      '</div><footer>' +
      '<button type="button" class="b" data-w118cact="cancel">Cancel</button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="b ai" data-w118cact="suggest"' + (e.aiBusy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + ic('sparkle') + (e.ai ? 'Ask the Hub again' : 'Suggest with the Hub') + '</button>' +
      '<span class="sp"></span>' +
      '<button type="button" class="b go" data-w118cact="save">' + ic('check') + (p.status === 'needs_labels' ? (P.rows.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'needs_labels' && x.key !== p.key; }).length ? 'Save and next' : 'Save and confirm') : 'Save') + '</button>' +
      '</footer></aside>';
    return h;
  }
  function readForm() {
    var e = P.edit; if (!e) return;
    document.querySelectorAll('#w118cdr [data-w118cf]').forEach(function (el) {
      var k = el.getAttribute('data-w118cf');
      if (k === 'is_pick') { e.v[k] = !!el.checked; return; }
      var v = el.value;
      if (k === 'tags') e.v[k] = arr(v).map(function (x) { return x.toLowerCase(); });
      else if (k === 'partner') e.v[k] = arr(v);
      else e.v[k] = v;
    });
  }
  function paintDrawer() {
    if (!P.mode) return;
    var d = drawerEl(), bd = d.querySelector('.bd'), scroll = bd ? bd.scrollTop : 0, prevMode = d.getAttribute('data-mode'), prevKey = d.getAttribute('data-key');
    if (P.mode === 'upload') { d.innerHTML = uploadHtml(); }
    else {
      var p = byKey(P.open); if (!p) { closeDrawer(true); return; }
      d.innerHTML = P.mode === 'edit' ? editHtml(p) : viewHtml(p);
    }
    var same = prevMode === P.mode && prevKey === String(P.open);
    d.setAttribute('data-mode', P.mode); d.setAttribute('data-key', String(P.open));
    var nb = d.querySelector('.bd'); if (nb && same) nb.scrollTop = scroll;
    if (!same) { var f = d.querySelector(P.mode === 'edit' ? '#w118cf-title' : '.ib[data-w118cx]'); if (f) try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  async function saveEdit() {
    readForm();
    var e = P.edit, p = byKey(e && e.key); if (!e || !p) return;
    var v = e.v, title = clean(v.title, 160);
    if (!title) { toastS('Give the photo a title'); return; }
    if (String(v.alt_text || '').length > 400) { toastS('Alt text stops at 400 characters'); return; }
    var patch = {
      title: title, alt_text: clean(v.alt_text, 400) || null, caption: clean(v.caption) || null, description: clean(v.description) || null,
      usage: USAGE[v.usage] ? v.usage : 'check', usage_note: clean(v.usage_note) || null, people: clean(v.people) || null,
      taken_on: /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(v.taken_on || '')) ? v.taken_on : null, date_note: clean(v.date_note) || null,
      season: v.season || null, place: clean(v.place) || null, event: clean(v.event) || null, scene: v.scene || null,
      series: slug(v.series) || null, tags: arr(v.tags), partner: arr(v.partner), branding: v.branding || null, best_for: arr(v.best_for),
      is_pick: !!v.is_pick, focal_x: Math.round(Number(v.focal_x || 0.5) * 1000) / 1000, focal_y: Math.round(Number(v.focal_y || 0.5) * 1000) / 1000,
      credit: clean(v.credit) || null, notes: clean(v.notes) || null
    };
    if (!patch.season && patch.taken_on) patch.season = seasonOf(patch.taken_on);
    var wasNew = p.status === 'needs_labels';
    if (wasNew && patch.usage === 'check' && !confirm('It is still marked Not checked. Save it that way?')) return;
    var d = diffText(p, patch, FIELDS);
