    if (!d.n && !wasNew) { P.mode = 'view'; P.edit = null; paintDrawer(); toastS('No changes'); return; }
    patch.labelled_by = me() + ', ' + ddmmyy();
    if (wasNew) patch.status = 'ready';
    var btn = document.querySelector('#w118cdr [data-w118cact="save"]'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      await update(p.key, patch, { field: wasNew ? 'labels confirmed' : 'labels', from: d.from, to: d.to });
      toastS(wasNew ? 'Labels confirmed' : 'Labels saved');
      paint();
      if (wasNew) {
        var nx = sortRows(P.rows.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'needs_labels'; }))[0];
        if (nx) { openPhoto(nx.key); startEdit(nx); return; }
        if (P.f.status === 'needs_labels') { P.f.status = ''; paint(); }
      }
      P.mode = 'view'; P.edit = null; paintDrawer();
    } catch (x) { if (btn) btn.disabled = false; toastS('Could not save: ' + ((x && x.message) || x)); }
  }
  async function aiEdit() {
    readForm();
    var e = P.edit, p = byKey(e && e.key); if (!e || !p || e.aiBusy) return;
    e.aiBusy = true; e.aiErr = null; paintDrawer();
    try {
      var s = await suggest(Object.assign({}, p, { taken_on: e.v.taken_on || p.taken_on, place: e.v.place, event: e.v.event, series: e.v.series }), null, null);
      if (P.edit !== e) return;
      e.ai = s;
      /* fill what is empty or still the upload default; everything else waits for Use this */
      var f = s.fields, filled = [];
      Object.keys(f).forEach(function (k) {
        if (k === 'focal_x' || k === 'focal_y') return;
        var cur = e.v[k], empty = cur == null || cur === '' || (Array.isArray(cur) && !cur.length) || (k === 'usage' && cur === 'check') || (k === 'title' && /^New photo\b/.test(String(cur)));
        if (empty) { e.v[k] = Array.isArray(f[k]) ? f[k].slice() : f[k]; e.flag[k] = true; filled.push(k); }
      });
      if (f.focal_x != null && Number(e.v.focal_x) === 0.5 && Number(e.v.focal_y) === 0.5) { e.v.focal_x = f.focal_x; e.v.focal_y = f.focal_y; }
      e.dirty = e.dirty || filled.length > 0;
      try { await db.from('wa_photos').update({ ai_suggestion: s }).eq('key', p.key); p.ai_suggestion = s; } catch (x) { /* the form still has it */ }
    } catch (x) { if (P.edit === e) e.aiErr = String((x && x.message) || x).slice(0, 200); }
    if (P.edit === e) { e.aiBusy = false; paintDrawer(); }
  }
  function useAi(k) {
    var e = P.edit; if (!e || !e.ai || !e.ai.fields) return;
    readForm();
    var f = e.ai.fields, keys = k === '*' ? Object.keys(f) : k === 'focal_x' ? ['focal_x', 'focal_y'] : [k];
    keys.forEach(function (x) { if (f[x] == null) return; e.v[x] = Array.isArray(f[x]) ? f[x].slice() : f[x]; e.flag[x] = true; });
    e.dirty = true; paintDrawer();
  }

  /* ---------- adding photos ---------- */
  function openUpload() {
    if (!staff()) return;
    if (!P.up) P.up = { items: [], running: false };
    P.open = null; P.mode = 'upload'; P.edit = null; paintDrawer();
    try { if (location.hash !== '#photos/new') history.replaceState(null, '', '#photos/new'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function uploadHtml() {
    var u = P.up || { items: [] }, items = u.items;
    var done = items.filter(function (i) { return i.state === 'done'; }).length, bad = items.filter(function (i) { return i.state === 'error'; }).length, left = items.length - done - bad;
    var review = P.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'needs_labels'; }).length;
    var h = '<div class="bk" data-w118cx="1"></div><aside class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Add photos">' +
      '<header><h3>Add photos</h3><button class="ib" data-w118cx="1" title="Close (Esc)" aria-label="Close">' + ic('close') + '</button></header><div class="bd">' +
      '<div class="drop" id="w118cdrop">' + ic('camera') + '<b>Drop photos here</b>or <a data-w118cchoose="1">choose files</a> (JPEG or PNG, up to 25 at a time).<br>The hub makes the three sizes, keeps the date, drops the location and camera data, and asks the Hub for first labels.<input type="file" id="w118cfile" multiple accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/heic,image/heif" style="display:none"></div>' +
      '<p class="note">Photos arrive marked <b>Needs a look</b>: open each one, fix what the Hub got wrong (above all who is recognizable), set the focal point, and save. Describe people by role, not by name.</p>';
    if (items.length) {
      h += '<div class="ups">' + items.map(function (it, n) {
        var pct = it.state === 'done' || it.state === 'error' ? 100 : Math.round((it.pct || 0) * 100);
        return '<div class="it' + (it.state === 'error' ? ' err' : it.state === 'done' ? ' ok' : '') + '">' + (it.thumb ? '<img src="' + E(it.thumb) + '" alt="">' : '<span class="ph0"></span>') +
          '<div><b title="' + E(it.name) + '">' + E(it.name) + '</b><em>' + E(it.msg || '') + '</em>' + (it.state !== 'done' && it.state !== 'error' ? '<div class="bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>' : '') + '</div>' +
          (it.state === 'done' && it.key ? '<a data-w118copen="' + E(it.key) + '">Check it</a>' : '') + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    h += '</div><footer>' + (items.length ? '<span class="note" style="margin:0">' + (left ? 'Working: ' + left + ' to go' : done + ' added' + (bad ? ', ' + bad + ' could not be added' : '')) + '</span>' : '') +
      '<span class="sp"></span>' +
      (items.length && !left ? '<button type="button" class="b" data-w118cact="clearup">Clear the list</button>' : '') +
      (review ? '<button type="button" class="b go" data-w118cact="review">' + ic('check') + 'Check the labels (' + review + ')</button>' : '') +
      '</footer></aside>';
    return h;
  }
  function paintUp() { if (P.mode === 'upload') paintDrawer(); }
  function keyFor(date, season, name) {
    var b = slug(String(name || '').replace(/\.[a-z0-9]{2,5}$/i, ''));
    var digits = (b.match(/\d{3,}/g) || []).pop();
    if (!b || /^(img|dsc|dscn|dscf|pxl|mvimg|image|photo|picture|screenshot|screen-shot|whatsapp|signal|fullsizerender|p\d{6,}|\d{6,})/.test(b)) b = 'photo' + (digits ? '-' + digits.slice(-6) : '');
    var k = ((date ? date.slice(0, 7) + '-' + (season || '') : 'undated') + '-' + b).replace(/-+/g, '-').slice(0, 72).replace(/-+$/, '');
    if (!/^[a-z0-9]/.test(k)) k = 'p' + k;
    var taken = {}; P.rows.forEach(function (r) { taken[r.key] = 1; }); ((P.up && P.up.items) || []).forEach(function (i) { if (i.key) taken[i.key] = 1; });
    var out = k, n = 2; while (taken[out]) out = k + '-' + (n++);
    return out;
  }
  function addFiles(files) {
    if (!staff()) return;
    var list = Array.prototype.slice.call(files || []);
    if (!P.up) P.up = { items: [], running: false };
    var isPhoto = function (f) { return /^image\//.test(f.type) || /\.(jpe?g|png|webp|heic|heif)$/i.test(f.name || ''); };
    var ok = list.filter(isPhoto);
    if (list.length && !ok.length) { toastS('Those are not photos: JPEG or PNG please'); return; }
    list.filter(function (f) { return !isPhoto(f); }).forEach(function (f) { P.up.items.push({ name: f.name || 'file', state: 'error', msg: 'Not a photo: JPEG or PNG please' }); });
    if (ok.length > 25) { toastS('25 at a time: the first 25 are on their way'); ok = ok.slice(0, 25); }
    ok.forEach(function (f) {
      if (f.size > 40 * 1024 * 1024) { P.up.items.push({ name: f.name, state: 'error', msg: 'Over 40 MB: export a smaller copy first' }); return; }
      P.up.items.push({ file: f, name: f.name || 'photo.jpg', state: 'wait', msg: 'Waiting', pct: 0 });
    });
    paintUp();
    run();
  }
  async function run() {
    var u = P.up; if (!u || u.running) return;
    u.running = true;
    try {
      for (var i = 0; i < u.items.length; i++) {
        var it = u.items[i]; if (it.state !== 'wait') continue;
        try { await addOne(it); } catch (x) { it.state = 'error'; it.msg = String((x && x.message) || x).slice(0, 160); it.file = null; paintUp(); }
      }
    } finally { u.running = false; paintUp(); paint(); }
  }
  async function addOne(it) {
    var f = it.file;
    it.state = 'read'; it.msg = 'Reading'; it.pct = 0.05; paintUp();
    var ex = {}; try { ex = exif(await f.slice(0, 262144).arrayBuffer()); } catch (e) { ex = {}; }
    var src = URL.createObjectURL(f), img;
    try { img = await loadImg(src, false); } catch (e) { URL.revokeObjectURL(src); throw new Error(/heic|heif/i.test(f.name + f.type) ? 'This browser cannot open HEIC: export it as a JPEG (or use Safari)' : 'Could not read this image'); }
    var w0 = img.naturalWidth, h0 = img.naturalHeight;
    if (!w0 || !h0) { URL.revokeObjectURL(src); throw new Error('Could not read this image'); }
    it.msg = 'Making the sizes'; it.pct = 0.2; paintUp();
    var cFull = drawTo(img, SIZES[0][1]); URL.revokeObjectURL(src); img = null;
    var cWeb = drawTo(cFull, SIZES[1][1]), cThumb = drawTo(cWeb, SIZES[2][1]);
    var bFull = await blobOf(cFull, SIZES[0][2]), bWeb = await blobOf(cWeb, SIZES[1][2]), bThumb = await blobOf(cThumb, SIZES[2][2]);
    it.thumb = URL.createObjectURL(bThumb);
    var date = ex.date || null, season = date ? seasonOf(date) : null;
    var key = keyFor(date, season, f.name); it.key = key;
    var wf = cFull.width, hf = cFull.height;
    it.msg = 'Uploading'; it.pct = 0.4; paintUp();
    var files = [['full', bFull], ['web', bWeb], ['thumb', bThumb]], done = [];
    try {
      for (var j = 0; j < files.length; j++) {
        var path = 'photos/' + files[j][0] + '/' + key + '.jpg';
        var r = await db.storage.from(BUCKET).upload(path, files[j][1], { contentType: 'image/jpeg', cacheControl: '604800', upsert: false });
        if (r && r.error) throw new Error('Upload failed: ' + (r.error.message || r.error));
        done.push(path); it.pct = 0.4 + 0.15 * (j + 1); paintUp();
      }
      var row = {
        key: key, title: 'New photo' + (date ? ', ' + (dateOf(date).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })) : ''),
        taken_on: date, date_note: date ? 'From the camera' + (ex.time ? ', ' + ex.time : '') + '.' : 'No date in the file; add it if you know it.', season: season,
        usage: 'check', status: 'needs_labels', tags: [], partner: [], best_for: [], is_pick: false, focal_x: 0.5, focal_y: 0.5,
        orientation: wf / hf > 1.05 ? 'landscape' : wf / hf < 0.95 ? 'portrait' : 'square', width: wf, height: hf, color: avgColor(cThumb),
        path_full: done[0], path_web: done[1], path_thumb: done[2], bytes_full: bFull.size, bytes_web: bWeb.size, bytes_thumb: bThumb.size, version: 1,
        source: 'Uploaded by ' + me() + ', ' + ddmmyy() + ' (original ' + w0 + ' x ' + h0 + (ex.camera ? ', ' + ex.camera : '') + ')',
        created_by: me(), updated_by: me()
      };
      var ins = await db.from('wa_photos').insert(row).select().maybeSingle();
      if (ins.error) throw new Error(ins.error.message || String(ins.error));
      put(Object.assign({}, row, ins.data && ins.data.key ? ins.data : {}, { created_at: (ins.data && ins.data.created_at) || new Date().toISOString() }));
    } catch (x) {
      if (done.length) { try { await db.storage.from(BUCKET).remove(done); } catch (e) { /* leave it */ } }
      throw x;
    }
    try { if (typeof logChange === 'function') logChange('photo', key, 'added', null, f.name); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
    it.file = null; it.pct = 0.85; it.msg = 'Asking the Hub for first labels'; paintUp(); paint();
    try {
      var s = await suggest(byKey(key), cWeb, { file: f.name });
      var fl = s.fields, patch = { ai_suggestion: s };
      ['title', 'alt_text', 'caption', 'description', 'usage', 'usage_note', 'people', 'scene', 'branding', 'tags', 'best_for', 'focal_x', 'focal_y'].forEach(function (k) { if (fl[k] != null) patch[k] = fl[k]; });
      if (!season && fl.season && fl.season !== 'unknown') patch.season = fl.season;
      await update(key, patch, null);
      it.state = 'done'; it.msg = 'Added, with the Hub\u2019s labels to check';
    } catch (x) {
      it.state = 'done'; it.msg = 'Added; no labels from the Hub (' + String((x && x.message) || x).slice(0, 80) + '), label it by hand';
    }
    it.pct = 1; paintUp(); paint();
  }

  /* ---------- the picker: choose a library photo from another screen ---------- */
  function pick(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    if (!allowed()) return;
    P.pick = { onPick: opts.onPick, title: opts.title || 'Pick a photo', ready: opts.readyOnly !== false, q: '', why: opts.why || '' };
    var el = document.getElementById('w118cpk');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w118cpk'; el.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1'); document.body.appendChild(el); }
    paintPick();
    load(false).then(paintPick);
    setTimeout(function () { var q = document.getElementById('w118cpq'); if (q) try { q.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 40);
  }
  function pickList() {
    var k = P.pick; if (!k) return [];
    var terms = k.q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    return sortRows(P.rows.filter(function (p) {
      if (p.status === 'archived') return false;
      if (k.ready && (p.usage !== 'ready' || p.status !== 'ready')) return false;
      var h = hay(p); for (var i = 0; i < terms.length; i++) if (h.indexOf(terms[i]) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    }));
  }
  function paintPick() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w118cpk'), k = P.pick; if (!el || !k) return;
    var list = pickList(), keepQ = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'w118cpq';
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w118cpx="1"></div><div class="box" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="' + E(k.title) + '">' +
      '<header><h3>' + E(k.title) + '</h3><input type="search" id="w118cpq" placeholder="Search the photos" value="' + E(k.q) + '" aria-label="Search the photos">' +
      '<span class="tg' + (k.ready ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w118cpr="1" role="button" tabindex="0">Ready for public use only</span>' +
      '<button class="x" data-w118cpx="1" aria-label="Close">' + ic('close') + '</button></header>' +
      '<div class="pg">' + (!P.loaded ? '<div class="none">Loading the photos...</div>' : list.length ? list.map(function (p) {
        return '<button type="button" class="pi" data-w118cpp="' + E(p.key) + '" title="' + E(p.title + (p.usage_note && p.usage !== 'ready' ? ': ' + p.usage_note : '')) + '"><span class="im" style="background:' + E(p.color || '#d9d6cc') + '"><img loading="lazy" src="' + E(url(p, 'thumb')) + '" alt="' + E(p.alt_text || p.title) + '" style="' + focalCss(p) + '"></span>' + badge(p) + '<b>' + E(p.title) + '</b></button>';
      }).join('') : '<div class="none">' + (k.ready ? 'No ready photo matches. Turn off Ready for public use only to see the rest.' : 'No photo matches.') + '</div>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="ft">' + (k.why ? E(k.why) + ' ' : '') + (k.ready ? 'Showing the photos cleared for public use.' : 'Showing every photo: check the badge before anything goes public.') + ' <a href="#photos" data-w118cpx="1" style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700">Open the photo library</a></div></div>';
    if (keepQ) { var q = document.getElementById('w118cpq'); if (q) { q.focus(); try { q.setSelectionRange(q.value.length, q.value.length); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function closePick() { P.pick = null; var el = document.getElementById('w118cpk'); if (el) el.remove(); }
  function choose(key) {
    var k = P.pick, p = byKey(key); if (!k || !p) return;
    if ((p.usage !== 'ready' || p.status !== 'ready') && !confirm((USAGE[p.usage] || USAGE.check).label + ': ' + (p.usage_note || (USAGE[p.usage] || USAGE.check).tip) + '\n\nUse it anyway?')) return;
    var cb = k.onPick; closePick();
    try { if (cb) cb(p); } catch (e) { toastS('Could not use that photo: ' + ((e && e.message) || e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- hand-off 1: the Create studio (a photo behind a social graphic) ---------- */
  var CD_HINT = 'A photo sits behind the text with a dark overlay so the words stay readable.';
  function cdEnsure() {
    var hint = document.getElementById('cdImgHint'); if (!hint || !staff()) return null;
    var box = document.getElementById('w118ccd');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w118ccd'; box.className = 'w118ccd'; box.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1'); box.innerHTML = 'Or <a data-w118cpick="cd">pick one from the photo library</a>, labelled and cleared for use.<div class="sel" style="display:none"></div>'; hint.parentNode.insertBefore(box, hint.nextSibling); }
    return box;
  }
  function cdShow(p) {
    var box = cdEnsure(); if (!box) return;
    var sel = box.querySelector('.sel');
    if (!p) { sel.style.display = 'none'; sel.innerHTML = ''; box.removeAttribute('data-key'); return; }
    box.setAttribute('data-key', p.key);
    sel.innerHTML = '<img src="' + E(url(p, 'thumb')) + '" alt="" style="' + focalCss(p) + '"><span>' + E(p.title) + '<small>' + E(p.status === 'ready' && p.usage === 'ready' ? 'From the photo library, ready for public use' : ulabel(p) + (p.usage_note ? ': ' + p.usage_note : '')) + '</small></span><button type="button" data-w118ccdx="1">Remove</button>';
    sel.style.display = 'flex';
  }
  function cdApply(p) {
    var t = document.getElementById('cdType'), hint = document.getElementById('cdImgHint');
    if (!t || !hint) return false;
    try { WA_CD_IMG = url(p, 'web'); } catch (e) { return false; }
    if (t.value !== 'graphic') { t.value = 'graphic'; try { if (typeof waCdSyncGfx === 'function') waCdSyncGfx(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    var fi = document.getElementById('cdImg'); if (fi) fi.value = '';
    hint.textContent = 'Photo attached from the library. It will sit behind the words on the tile.';
    cdShow(p);
    var brief = document.getElementById('cdBrief') || document.querySelector('#create textarea');
    try { var o = document.getElementById('cdGfxOpts'); if (o && o.scrollIntoView) o.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'smooth' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (brief && !brief.value) { try { brief.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    return true;
  }
  function toCreate(p) {
    W.pendingCd = p; location.hash = '#create';
    var n = 0, t = setInterval(function () {
      n++;
      if (W.pendingCd && cdApply(W.pendingCd)) { W.pendingCd = null; clearInterval(t); toastS('Photo attached in the Create studio: write the brief and Write it'); return; }
      if (n > 40) { clearInterval(t); W.pendingCd = null; toastS('Open the Create studio and use Pick one from the photo library'); }
    }, 150);
  }
  /* when the studio resets or takes an uploaded file, the library chip goes */
  function cdWatch() {
    var hint = document.getElementById('cdImgHint'); if (!hint || hint.__w118c) return;
    hint.__w118c = true;
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      var box = document.getElementById('w118ccd'); if (!box || !box.getAttribute('data-key')) return;
      var p = byKey(box.getAttribute('data-key')); var cur = null; try { cur = WA_CD_IMG; } catch (e) { cur = null; }
      if (!p || cur !== url(p, 'web')) cdShow(null);
    }).observe(hint, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true });
  }

  /* ---------- hand-off 2: the content calendar composer (wave121) ---------- */
  async function fileOf(p) {
    var r = await fetch(url(p, 'web'), { mode: 'cors' }); if (!r.ok) throw new Error('could not fetch the photo (' + r.status + ')');
    var b = await r.blob();
    return new File([b], p.key + '.jpg', { type: 'image/jpeg' });
  }
  async function intoComposer(p) {
    var input = document.querySelector('#w121dr #w121file');
    if (!input) throw new Error('the post composer is not open');
    var file = await fileOf(p);
    var dt = new DataTransfer(); dt.items.add(file);
    input.files = dt.files;
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
  }
  function toPost(p) {
    if (typeof window.w121Compose !== 'function') { toastS('The content calendar is not available'); return; }
    closeDrawer(true);
    var note = 'Photo from the library: ' + p.title + '.' + (p.alt_text ? ' Alt text: ' + p.alt_text : '') + (p.usage !== 'ready' || p.status !== 'ready' ? ' Usage: ' + ulabel(p) + (p.usage_note ? ', ' + p.usage_note : '') : '');
    window.w121Compose({ title: p.title, notes: note });
    var n = 0, t = setInterval(function () {
      n++;
      if (document.querySelector('#w121dr #w121file')) { clearInterval(t); intoComposer(p).then(function () { toastS('Photo added to the post'); }).catch(function (x) { toastS('Could not attach the photo: ' + ((x && x.message) || x)); }); return; }
      if (n > 60) { clearInterval(t); toastS('Open a post and use From the photo library'); }
    }, 150);
  }
  function calLink() {
    var drop = document.querySelector('#w121dr #w121drop'); if (!drop || drop.querySelector('[data-w118cpick]') || !staff()) return;
    var a = document.createElement('a'); a.setAttribute('data-w118cpick', 'cal'); a.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1'); a.textContent = 'pick from the photo library';
    var span = document.createElement('span'); span.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1'); span.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' Or ')); span.appendChild(a); span.appendChild(document.createTextNode('.'));
    var input = drop.querySelector('#w121file'); drop.insertBefore(span, input || null);
  }
  var calMo = null;
  function calWatch() {
    var dr = document.getElementById('w121dr');
    if (!dr) { if (calMo) { calMo.disconnect(); calMo = null; } return; }
    if (calMo && calMo.__el === dr) { calLink(); return; }
    if (calMo) calMo.disconnect();
    calMo = new MutationObserver(function () { calLink(); }); calMo.__el = dr;
    calMo.observe(dr, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    calLink();
  }

  /* ---------- hand-off 3: team tools and Search everything ---------- */
  function teamTools() {
    try {
      var tools = document.querySelector('#team .w116tools'); if (!tools || tools.querySelector('[data-w116go="#photos"]') || !allowed()) return;
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || ''); if (!/^#team\/(marketing|fundraising)\b/.test(h)) return;
      var a = document.createElement('a'); a.setAttribute('data-w116go', '#photos'); a.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1'); a.innerHTML = ic('image') + ' Photo library';
      var after = tools.querySelector('[data-w116go="#create"]');
      if (after && after.nextSibling) tools.insertBefore(a, after.nextSibling); else tools.insertBefore(a, tools.firstChild);
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }
  var searchSeq = 0;
  async function searchPhotos(q) {
    var seq = ++searchSeq;
    try {
      q = String(q || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!allowed() || q.length < 3) return;
      await load(false);
      if (seq !== searchSeq) return;
      var terms = q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
      var hits = P.rows.filter(function (p) { if (p.status === 'archived') return false; var h = hay(p); return terms.every(function (t) { return h.indexOf(t) >= 0; }); }).slice(0, 4);
      var pop = document.getElementById('searchResults');
      if (!hits.length || !pop || pop.style.display === 'none' || pop.querySelector('[data-w118cs]')) return;
      var empty = !pop.querySelector('.sr[data-hash], .sr[data-w107]') ? pop.querySelector('.sr') : null;
      if (empty && (empty.textContent || '').indexOf('No matches') >= 0) empty.remove();
      hits.forEach(function (p) {
        var div = document.createElement('div'); div.className = 'sr'; div.setAttribute('data-w118cs', p.key);
        div.innerHTML = '<span class="sr-type w118cst">Photo</span><img src="' + E(url(p, 'thumb')) + '" alt="">' + E(p.title);
        div.addEventListener('click', function () { pop.style.display = 'none'; var gs = document.getElementById('globalSearch'); if (gs) gs.value = ''; location.hash = '#photos/' + encodeURIComponent(p.key); });
        pop.appendChild(div);
      });
    } catch (e) { /* search stays as it was */ }
  }
  if (typeof renderSearch === 'function') {
    var __rs118c = renderSearch, tS = null;
    renderSearch = function (q) { var r = __rs118c.apply(this, arguments); clearTimeout(tS); tS = setTimeout(function () { searchPhotos(q); }, 280); return r; };
  }

  /* ---------- the guide ---------- */
  function help() {
    if (typeof openModal !== 'function') return;
    openModal('<h3>Photo library</h3><div class="w43hm">' +
      '<p>Every photo we can use, with the words that go with it. Search or filter, open a photo, and copy what you need.</p>' +
      '<p><b>The badges.</b> Ready: fine for posts, the website, decks and reports. Crop first: crop the edge named in the note before it goes public. Consent needed: someone we serve or a child is recognizable, so use it inside the team, or publicly only with their consent. Internal only: keep it inside the team. Needs a look: new, with first labels from the Hub that a person has not confirmed yet.</p>' +
      '<p><b>Sizes.</b> Full (2400 px) for print and big screens, Web (1600 px) for posts, the website and decks, Thumbnail (640 px) for emails and documents. None of them carry location or camera data.</p>' +
      '<p><b>Using a photo.</b> Use in the Create studio puts it behind a social graphic. Use in a post opens the content calendar with the photo attached. Paste the alt text into the alt text field wherever the photo goes. Both screens also have a Pick from the photo library link.</p>' +
      '<p><b>Adding photos</b> (staff). Add photos, drop the files, and the hub makes the three sizes, keeps the date, uploads them and asks the Hub for first labels. Open each one marked Needs a look, fix what is off (above all who is recognizable), click the photo on its main subject, and save. Describe people by role, not by name. When someone gives consent, set the photo to Ready and note who agreed and when.</p>' +
      '<p><b>Where it lives.</b> Files: Supabase storage, content-images/photos (full, web and thumb). Labels: the wa_photos table. Archive hides a photo; an admin can delete an archived photo for good. Every change goes to the Activity log.</p>' +
      '</div><div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w118chc">Got it</button></div>');
    var c = document.getElementById('w118chc'); if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  function copyText(t, btn) {
    var ok = function () { if (btn) { var o = btn.innerHTML; btn.classList.add('done'); btn.innerHTML = ic('check') + 'Copied'; setTimeout(function () { btn.classList.remove('done'); btn.innerHTML = o; }, 1600); } else toastS('Copied'); };
    try { if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(ok, function () { legacy(); }); return; } } catch (e) { /* below */ }
    legacy();
    function legacy() { try { var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = t; ta.setAttribute('readonly', ''); ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0'; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove(); ok(); } catch (e) { toastS('Could not copy: select the text instead'); } }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118chelp]'))) { e.preventDefault(); help(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cpick]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      var where = el.getAttribute('data-w118cpick');
      if (where === 'cd') pick({ title: 'Pick a photo for the graphic', why: 'It sits behind the words on the tile.', onPick: function (p) { cdApply(p); } });
      else if (where === 'cal') pick({ title: 'Add a photo to the post', why: 'It is added to this post like an upload.', onPick: function (p) { intoComposer(p).then(function () { toastS('Photo added to the post'); }).catch(function (x) { toastS('Could not attach the photo: ' + ((x && x.message) || x)); }); } });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118ccdx]'))) { e.preventDefault(); try { WA_CD_IMG = null; } catch (x) { /* fine */ } var hint = document.getElementById('cdImgHint'); if (hint) hint.textContent = CD_HINT; cdShow(null); return; }
    var pk = t.closest('#w118cpk');
    if (pk) {
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cpx]'))) { if (el.tagName !== 'A') e.preventDefault(); closePick(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cpr]'))) { P.pick.ready = !P.pick.ready; paintPick(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cpp]'))) { choose(el.getAttribute('data-w118cpp')); return; }
      return;
    }
    var inPage = t.closest('#photos'), inDr = t.closest('#w118cdr');
    if (!inPage && !inDr) return;
    if (inPage) {
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cop]'))) { openPhoto(el.getAttribute('data-w118cop')); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cgo]'))) { openUpload(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cu]'))) { if (el.classList.contains('off')) return; P.f.usage = el.getAttribute('data-w118cu'); P.limit = 120; paint(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cs]'))) { if (el.classList.contains('off')) return; var s = el.getAttribute('data-w118cs'); P.f.season = P.f.season === s ? '' : s; P.limit = 120; paint(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cpk]'))) { if (el.classList.contains('off')) return; P.f.picks = !P.f.picks; P.limit = 120; paint(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cst]'))) { var v = el.getAttribute('data-w118cst'); P.f.status = P.f.status === v ? '' : v; if (P.f.status) P.f.usage = ''; P.limit = 120; paint(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cclr]'))) { var c = el.getAttribute('data-w118cclr'); if (c === 'all') { P.f = blankF(); } else P.f[c] = ''; paint(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cmore]'))) { P.limit += 120; paint(); return; }
      return;
    }
    /* the side panel */
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cx]'))) { e.preventDefault(); if (P.mode === 'edit') { if (P.edit && P.edit.dirty && !confirm('Close without saving the label changes?')) return; closeDrawer(true); return; } closeDrawer(false); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cstep]'))) { step(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w118cstep'))); return; }
    var p = byKey(P.open);
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118ccopy]')) && p) {
      var what = el.getAttribute('data-w118ccopy');
      copyText(what === 'alt' ? p.alt_text : what === 'caption' ? p.caption : url(p, 'web'), what === 'link' ? null : el);
      if (what === 'link') toastS('Link copied (the web size)');
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cuse]')) && p) { var u = el.getAttribute('data-w118cuse'); if (u === 'create') { closeDrawer(true); toCreate(p); } else toPost(p); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118ctag]'))) { P.f = blankF(); P.f.tag = el.getAttribute('data-w118ctag'); closeDrawer(true); paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cseries]'))) { P.f = blankF(); P.f.series = el.getAttribute('data-w118cseries'); P.sort = 'taken_asc'; closeDrawer(true); paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118copen]'))) { var k = el.getAttribute('data-w118copen'), q = byKey(k); if (q) { openPhoto(k); if (q.status === 'needs_labels') startEdit(q); } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cchoose]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var fi = document.getElementById('w118cfile'); if (fi) fi.click(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cbest]')) && P.edit) { readForm(); var b = el.getAttribute('data-w118cbest'), list = arr(P.edit.v.best_for); var ix = list.indexOf(b); if (ix >= 0) list.splice(ix, 1); else list.push(b); P.edit.v.best_for = list; P.edit.dirty = true; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cai]'))) { e.preventDefault(); useAi(el.getAttribute('data-w118cai')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cfocal]')) && P.edit) {
      var img = el.querySelector('img'); if (!img) return;
      var r = img.getBoundingClientRect(); if (!r.width || !r.height) return;
      readForm();
      P.edit.v.focal_x = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width));
      P.edit.v.focal_y = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height));
      P.edit.dirty = true; paintDrawer(); return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w118cact]'))) {
      var act = el.getAttribute('data-w118cact');
      if (act === 'edit' && p) { startEdit(p); return; }
      if (act === 'cancel') { if (P.edit && P.edit.dirty && !confirm('Drop the label changes?')) return; P.mode = 'view'; P.edit = null; paintDrawer(); return; }
      if (act === 'save') { await saveEdit(); return; }
      if (act === 'suggest') { await aiEdit(); return; }
      if (act === 'clearup') { if (P.up && !P.up.running) P.up.items = []; paintDrawer(); return; }
      if (act === 'review') { P.f = blankF(); P.f.status = 'needs_labels'; P.sort = 'added'; paint(); var first = sortRows(P.rows.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'needs_labels'; }))[0]; if (first) { openPhoto(first.key); startEdit(first); } else closeDrawer(true); return; }
      if (act === 'archive' && p) {
        if (!confirm('Archive "' + p.title + '"? It leaves the library and the pickers; you can restore it from Archived.')) return;
        try { await update(p.key, { status: 'archived' }, { field: 'status', from: p.status, to: 'archived' }); toastS('Archived'); closeDrawer(true); paint(); } catch (x) { toastS('Could not archive: ' + ((x && x.message) || x)); }
        return;
      }
      if (act === 'restore' && p) {
        var to = p.labelled_by && p.usage !== 'check' ? 'ready' : 'needs_labels';
        try { await update(p.key, { status: to }, { field: 'status', from: 'archived', to: to }); toastS('Restored'); paint(); paintDrawer(); } catch (x) { toastS('Could not restore: ' + ((x && x.message) || x)); }
        return;
      }
      if (act === 'delete' && p) {
        if (!admin()) return;
        if (!confirm('Delete "' + p.title + '" for good? The three files and the labels go, and this cannot be undone.')) return;
        try {
          var d = await db.from('wa_photos').delete().eq('key', p.key).select();
          if (d.error) throw d.error;
          if (Array.isArray(d.data) && !d.data.length) throw new Error('only a hub admin can delete');
          var paths = [p.path_full, p.path_web, p.path_thumb].filter(Boolean);
          if (paths.length) { try { await db.storage.from(BUCKET).remove(paths); } catch (x) { /* the row is gone; files can be swept later */ } }
          try { if (typeof logChange === 'function') logChange('photo', p.key, 'deleted', p.title, null); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
          P.rows = P.rows.filter(function (x) { return x.key !== p.key; });
          closeDrawer(true); paint(); toastS('Deleted');
        } catch (x) { toastS('Could not delete: ' + ((x && x.message) || x)); }
        return;
      }
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#photos [role="button"]') && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { e.preventDefault(); e.target.click(); return; }
    if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w118cdr .opts [role="button"], #w118cpk [role="button"]') && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { e.preventDefault(); e.target.click(); return; }
    if (P.pick) { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); closePick(); } return; }
    if (!P.mode) return;
    var typing = e.target && /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(e.target.tagName);
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); if (P.mode === 'edit') { if (P.edit && P.edit.dirty && !confirm('Close without saving the label changes?')) return; P.mode = 'view'; P.edit = null; paintDrawer(); return; } closeDrawer(false); return; }
    if (typing) { if (P.mode === 'edit' && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); saveEdit(); } return; }
    if (P.mode === 'view' && e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); step(-1); }
    if (P.mode === 'view' && e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); step(1); }
  });

      var cnt = document.querySelector('#w118cdr [data-w118ccnt="' + k + '"]'); if (cnt) { var max = Number((cnt.textContent.split('/')[1] || '0').trim()); cnt.textContent = t.value.length + ' / ' + max; cnt.classList.toggle('bad', t.value.length > max); }
    }
  });

    }
  });
  /* drop files on the Add photos panel */

  /* ---------- the page in the hub: route, menu, permissions ---------- */
  async function show(h) {
    var sec = panel(); if (!sec) return;
    try { document.title = 'Photo library \u00b7 Welfare Avenue Hub'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var sub = decodeURIComponent(String(h || '').split('/').slice(1).join('/'));
    if (!P.loaded) paint();
    await load(false);
    paint();
    if (!allowed()) return;
    if (sub === 'new') { if (staff()) openUpload(); return; }
    if (sub === 'review') { P.f = blankF(); P.f.status = 'needs_labels'; P.sort = 'added'; paint(); return; }
    if (sub) { if (byKey(sub)) { if (P.open !== sub || P.mode !== 'view') openPhoto(sub, true); } else toastS('That photo is not in the library'); return; }
    if (P.mode && P.mode !== 'upload') closeDrawer(true);
  }
  function register() {
    if (!window.WA_NAV || !WA_NAV.register) return;
    var known = (WA_NAV.pages || []).some(function (p) { return p && p.id === 'photos'; });
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'), has = bar && bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="photos"]');
    if (known && has) return;
    WA_NAV.register({ id: 'photos', label: 'Photo library', group: 'more', dot: GOLD, gate: 'member', noRoute: known, onShow: function (h) { show(h); } });
  }
  function place() {
    try {
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar) return;
      var b = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="photos"]'); if (!b) return;
      b.setAttribute('data-w118c', '1');
      var host = bar.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="more"] .tab-group-p'); if (!host) return;
      var br = host.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="brain"]');
      if (br) { if (br.nextSibling !== b) host.insertBefore(b, br.nextSibling); }
      else if (b.parentNode !== host) host.appendChild(b);
      /* remember when the group shows only because of this tab (board accounts), for the harness */
      var others = false; host.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function (x) { if (x !== b && x.style.display !== 'none') others = true; });
      var grp = host.closest('.tab-group'); if (grp) { if (!others) { if (grp.getAttribute('data-w118cg') !== '1') grp.setAttribute('data-w118cg', '1'); } else if (grp.hasAttribute('data-w118cg')) grp.removeAttribute('data-w118cg'); }
